================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/artifacts/router_report.py
================================================
"""Export bounded, Git-friendly saliency panels for every drift condition."""

import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont

from fyp_drift.data.clear100 import MEAN, STD, open_dataset
from fyp_drift.data.corruptions import (
    REALISTIC_BENCHMARK_SCENARIOS,
    SCENARIOS,
    drift_transform,
    scenario_components,
)
from fyp_drift.models.routing import apply_policy
from fyp_drift.models.siamese import ViTTwinRouter
from fyp_drift.models.vision_transformer import TemporalViT

SALIENCY_OVERLAY_STRENGTH = 0.28
SALIENCY_COLOR = (213, 94, 0)


def figure_font(size=14, bold=False):
    names = (
        ("timesbd.ttf", "DejaVuSerif-Bold.ttf")
        if bold
        else ("times.ttf", "DejaVuSerif.ttf")
    )
    roots = (Path("C:/Windows/Fonts"), Path("/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu"))
    for root, name in zip(roots, names):
        candidate = root / name
        if candidate.is_file():
            return ImageFont.truetype(candidate, size)
    return ImageFont.load_default(size=size)


def rgb(tensor):
    value = (
        tensor.cpu() * torch.tensor(STD)[:, None, None]
        + torch.tensor(MEAN)[:, None, None]
    )
    return Image.fromarray(
        (value.clamp(0, 1).permute(1, 2, 0).numpy() * 255).astype(np.uint8)
    )


def overlay(base, weights):
    heat = weights.reshape(16, 16).detach().cpu().numpy()
    heat = Image.fromarray(
        (255 * heat / max(heat.max(), 1e-8)).astype(np.uint8)
    ).resize(base.size)
    return Image.composite(
        Image.new("RGB", base.size, SALIENCY_COLOR),
        base,
        heat.point(lambda value: int(value * SALIENCY_OVERLAY_STRENGTH)),
    )


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--base-checkpoint", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--run-dir", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument(
        "--output", type=Path, default=Path("runs/reports/router")
    )
    parser.add_argument("--indices", type=int, nargs="+", default=(0, 777, 1777))
    parser.add_argument(
        "--scenarios",
        nargs="+",
        choices=SCENARIOS,
        default=list(REALISTIC_BENCHMARK_SCENARIOS),
    )
    parser.add_argument("--long-horizon", action="store_true")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    device = "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    base = torch.load(args.base_checkpoint, map_location=device, weights_only=True)
    classes = base["classifier.weight"].shape[0]
    old, new = (
        TemporalViT("xai", classes).to(device),
        TemporalViT("xai", classes).to(device),
    )
    old.load_state_dict(base)
    new.load_state_dict(
        torch.load(
            args.run_dir / "models/best.pt", map_location=device, weights_only=True
        )
    )
    router = ViTTwinRouter(old, new).eval()
    policy = json.loads((args.run_dir / "metrics/router_policy.json").read_text())
    dataset = open_dataset()
    records = []
    years = (2012, 2013, 2014) if args.long_horizon else (2014,)
    split = "test_source" if args.long_horizon else "test"
    for scenario in args.scenarios:
        for severity, year in enumerate(years, 1):
            clean = dataset.get_subset(
                split, transform=drift_transform("clean", severity)
            )
            shifted = dataset.get_subset(
                split, transform=drift_transform(scenario, severity)
            )
            year_indices = torch.where(clean.metadata_array[:, 0] == year - 2002)[
                0
            ].tolist()
            folder = args.output / scenario / str(year)
            folder.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            for offset in args.indices:
                index = year_indices[offset % len(year_indices)]
                clean_image, label, _ = clean[index]
                image, _, _ = shifted[index]
                with torch.no_grad():
                    comparison = router.compare(image[None].to(device))
                    routed, flag, use_new, probability, features = apply_policy(
                        comparison, policy
                    )
                clean_rgb, shifted_rgb = rgb(clean_image), rgb(image)
                panels = [
                    clean_rgb,
                    shifted_rgb,
                    overlay(shifted_rgb, comparison["old_map"][0]),
                    overlay(shifted_rgb, comparison["new_map"][0]),
                ]
                canvas = Image.new("RGB", (896, 282), "white")
                names = (
                    "(a) Clean reference",
                    f"(b) {scenario.replace('_', ' ')}",
                    "(c) Historical evidence",
                    "(d) Updated evidence",
                )
                draw = ImageDraw.Draw(canvas)
                for position, (panel, name) in enumerate(zip(panels, names)):
                    canvas.paste(panel, (position * 224, 28))
                    draw.text(
                        (position * 224 + 6, 6),
                        name,
                        font=figure_font(14),
                        fill="black",
                    )
                footer = (
                    f"sample {index}   true {int(label)}   old {int(comparison['old_logits'].argmax(1)[0])}   "
                    f"new {int(comparison['new_logits'].argmax(1)[0])}   routed {int(routed[0])}   "
                    f"routing score {float(probability[0]):.3f}"
                )
                draw.text((6, 258), footer, font=figure_font(13), fill="black")
                path = folder / f"sample_{offset:04d}.png"
                canvas.save(path, optimize=True)
                records.append(
                    {
                        "scenario": scenario,
                        "year": year,
                        "severity": 0 if scenario == "clean" else severity,
                        "index": index,
                        "image": path.as_posix(),
                        "drift_components": list(
                            scenario_components(scenario, index, severity, seed=42)
                        ),
                        "true_class": int(label),
                        "old_class": int(comparison["old_logits"].argmax(1)[0]),
                        "new_class": int(comparison["new_logits"].argmax(1)[0]),
                        "routed_class": int(routed[0]),
                        "update_flag": bool(flag[0]),
                        "routed_to_new": bool(use_new[0]),
                        "route_probability": float(probability[0]),
                        **{
                            name: float(features[0, i])
                            for i, name in enumerate(policy["feature_names"])
                        },
                    }
                )
    (args.output / "samples.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(records, indent=2), encoding="utf-8"
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/artifacts/run_store.py
================================================
"""Isolated run directories and explicit checkpoint metadata."""

import json
import re
import shutil
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from pathlib import Path

import torch


class RunStore:
    def __init__(self, root, method, run_id=None):
        run_id = run_id or datetime.now(UTC).strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ")
        if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9_-]+", run_id):
            raise ValueError(
                "run_id must contain only letters, numbers, underscore, or dash"
            )
        self.path = Path(root) / method / run_id
        if self.path.exists():
            raise FileExistsError(f"Run already exists: {self.path}")
        for folder in ("logs", "metrics", "models", "manual"):
            (self.path / folder).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    @classmethod
    def from_existing(cls, path):
        path = Path(path)
        if not (path / "config.json").is_file():
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"Existing run is missing config.json: {path}")
        store = cls.__new__(cls)
        store.path = path
        return store

    def log(self, event):
        with (self.path / "logs" / "events.jsonl").open(
            "a", encoding="utf-8"
        ) as handle:
            handle.write(json.dumps(event, default=str) + "\n")

    def save_model(self, model, kind, epoch, metric_name, metric_value):
        if kind not in ("best", "last"):
            raise ValueError(kind)
        destination = self.path / "models" / f"{kind}.pt"
        torch.save(model.state_dict(), destination)
        self.write_json(
            f"models/{kind}.json",
            {
                "epoch": epoch,
                "selection_metric": metric_name,
                "metric_value": metric_value,
                "saved_utc": datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(),
            },
        )
        return destination

    def save_training_checkpoint(self, model, optimizer, epoch, best_score):
        """Atomically save enough state to resume after the latest epoch."""
        destination = self.path / "models" / "resume.pt"
        temporary = destination.with_suffix(".tmp")
        torch.save(
            {
                "model": model.state_dict(),
                "optimizer": optimizer.state_dict(),
                "epoch": epoch,
                "best_score": best_score,
            },
            temporary,
        )
        temporary.replace(destination)
        self.write_json(
            "models/resume.json",
            {
                "epoch": epoch,
                "best_score": best_score,
                "saved_utc": datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(),
            },
        )
        return destination

    def write_json(self, relative, value):
        path = self.path / relative
        path.write_text(json.dumps(value, indent=2, default=str), encoding="utf-8")


def manual_snapshot(run_dir, name, which="best"):
    if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9_-]+", name):
        raise ValueError("name must contain only letters, numbers, underscore, or dash")
    run_dir = Path(run_dir)
    source = run_dir / "models" / f"{which}.pt"
    metadata = run_dir / "models" / f"{which}.json"
    if which not in ("best", "last") or not source.is_file() or not metadata.is_file():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"No {which} checkpoint and metadata in {run_dir}")
    destination = run_dir / "manual" / f"{name}.pt"
    if destination.exists():
        raise FileExistsError(destination)
    shutil.copy2(source, destination)
    details = json.loads(metadata.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    details.update(
        {
            "source": str(source),
            "manual_name": name,
            "saved_utc": datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(),
        }
    )
    (run_dir / "manual" / f"{name}.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(details, indent=2), encoding="utf-8"
    )
    return destination



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/artifacts/snapshots.py
================================================
"""Manually preserve a selected run checkpoint with its selection metadata."""

import argparse
from pathlib import Path

from fyp_drift.artifacts.run_store import manual_snapshot


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--run-dir", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--name", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--which", choices=("best", "last"), default="best")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    print(manual_snapshot(args.run_dir, args.name, args.which))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/acdc_screen.py
================================================
"""Screen historical and adapted ViT representations on paired ACDC views."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import json
import random
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch
from PIL import Image
from torch.utils.data import DataLoader, Dataset
from torchvision.transforms import Compose, Normalize, Resize, ToTensor
from tqdm.auto import tqdm

from fyp_drift.data.clear100 import MEAN, STD
from fyp_drift.data.acdc import validate_rows
from fyp_drift.evaluation.adaptation_evidence import (
    compare_checkpoints,
    compare_feature_archives,
)
from fyp_drift.evaluation.drift_metrics import DEFAULT_PERMUTATIONS
from fyp_drift.models.vision_transformer import TemporalViT


class AcdcPairs(Dataset):
    def __init__(self, manifest: Path, limit=None, seed=42):
        with Path(manifest).open(newline="", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
            self.rows = list(csv.DictReader(handle))
        validate_rows(self.rows)
        if limit and limit < len(self.rows):
            by_condition = {}
            for row in self.rows:
                by_condition.setdefault(row["condition"], []).append(row)
            generator = random.Random(seed)
            conditions = sorted(by_condition)
            for rows in by_condition.values():
                generator.shuffle(rows)
            selected = []
            while len(selected) < limit:
                for condition in conditions:
                    if by_condition[condition]:
                        selected.append(by_condition[condition].pop())
                        if len(selected) == limit:
                            break
            self.rows = selected
        if len(self.rows) < 10:
            raise ValueError("The ACDC screen needs at least ten paired scenes")
        self.transform = Compose(
            [
                Resize((224, 224), antialias=True),
                ToTensor(),
                Normalize(mean=MEAN, std=STD),
            ]
        )

    def __len__(self):
        return len(self.rows)

    def __getitem__(self, index):
        row = self.rows[index]
        with Image.open(row["normal_image"]) as image:
            normal = self.transform(image.convert("RGB"))
        with Image.open(row["adverse_image"]) as image:
            adverse = self.transform(image.convert("RGB"))
        return normal, adverse, row["pair_id"], row["condition"]


def load_model(checkpoint: Path, method, device):
    state = torch.load(checkpoint, map_location="cpu", weights_only=True)
    state = state.get("model", state)
    classes = state["classifier.weight"].shape[0]
    model = TemporalViT(method=method, num_classes=classes)
    model.load_state_dict(state)
    return model.to(device).eval()


@torch.inference_mode()
def extract(model, loader, device, label):
    collected = {
        "normal_features": [],
        "adverse_features": [],
        "normal_probabilities": [],
        "adverse_probabilities": [],
        "normal_saliency": [],
        "adverse_saliency": [],
        "pair_ids": [],
        "conditions": [],
    }
    for normal, adverse, pair_ids, conditions in tqdm(loader, desc=label, unit="batch"):
        normal_features, normal_saliency, _, _ = model.encode(normal.to(device))
        adverse_features, adverse_saliency, _, _ = model.encode(adverse.to(device))
        collected["normal_features"].append(normal_features.cpu().numpy())
        collected["adverse_features"].append(adverse_features.cpu().numpy())
        collected["normal_probabilities"].append(
            model.classifier(normal_features).softmax(1).cpu().numpy()
        )
        collected["adverse_probabilities"].append(
            model.classifier(adverse_features).softmax(1).cpu().numpy()
        )
        collected["normal_saliency"].append(normal_saliency.cpu().numpy())
        collected["adverse_saliency"].append(adverse_saliency.cpu().numpy())
        collected["pair_ids"].extend(pair_ids)
        collected["conditions"].extend(conditions)
    return {
        name: np.concatenate(values, axis=0)
        if name not in ("pair_ids", "conditions")
        else np.asarray(values, dtype=str)
        for name, values in collected.items()
    }


def subset_archive(archive, indices):
    return {
        name: values[indices]
        for name, values in archive.items()
        if name not in ("pair_ids", "conditions")
    }


def parse_args(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Audit paired ACDC representations for two XAI ViT checkpoints."
    )
    parser.add_argument("--manifest", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--historical-checkpoint", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--adapted-checkpoint", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument(
        "--historical-method", choices=("baseline", "xai"), default="xai"
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--adapted-method", choices=("baseline", "xai"), default="xai"
    )
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--batch-size", type=int, default=16)
    parser.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=0)
    parser.add_argument("--limit", type=int)
    parser.add_argument("--permutations", type=int, default=DEFAULT_PERMUTATIONS)
    parser.add_argument("--permutation-limit", type=int, default=200)
    parser.add_argument("--bootstrap-repetitions", type=int, default=1000)
    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=42)
    parser.add_argument("--alpha", type=float, default=0.05)
    parser.add_argument("--collapse-ratio-floor", type=float, default=0.8)
    return parser.parse_args(argv)


def main(argv=None):
    args = parse_args(argv)
    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)
    np.random.seed(args.seed)
    device = "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    dataset = AcdcPairs(args.manifest, args.limit, args.seed)
    loader = DataLoader(
        dataset,
        batch_size=args.batch_size,
        num_workers=args.workers,
        shuffle=False,
    )
    args.output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    archives = {}
    for name, checkpoint, method in (
        ("historical", args.historical_checkpoint, args.historical_method),
        ("adapted", args.adapted_checkpoint, args.adapted_method),
    ):
        model = load_model(checkpoint, method, device)
        archives[name] = extract(model, loader, device, name)
        np.savez_compressed(args.output / f"{name}_features.npz", **archives[name])
        del model
        if device == "cuda":
            torch.cuda.empty_cache()

    report = compare_feature_archives(
        archives["historical"],
        archives["adapted"],
        saliency_aligned=False,
        permutations=args.permutations,
        permutation_limit=args.permutation_limit,
        seed=args.seed,
        bootstrap_repetitions=args.bootstrap_repetitions,
        alpha=args.alpha,
        collapse_ratio_floor=args.collapse_ratio_floor,
    )
    report["parameter_update"] = compare_checkpoints(
        args.historical_checkpoint, args.adapted_checkpoint
    )
    conditions = sorted(set(archives["historical"]["conditions"].tolist()))
    report["by_condition"] = {}
    for condition in conditions:
        indices = np.flatnonzero(archives["historical"]["conditions"] == condition)
        report["by_condition"][condition] = compare_feature_archives(
            subset_archive(archives["historical"], indices),
            subset_archive(archives["adapted"], indices),
            saliency_aligned=False,
            permutations=args.permutations,
            permutation_limit=args.permutation_limit,
            seed=args.seed,
            bootstrap_repetitions=args.bootstrap_repetitions,
            alpha=args.alpha,
            collapse_ratio_floor=args.collapse_ratio_floor,
        )
    report["dataset"] = {
        "name": "ACDC",
        "pairs": len(dataset),
        "conditions": conditions,
        "scope": (
            "This is a paired representation screen for CLEAR-trained classifiers. "
            "It does not measure ACDC semantic-segmentation accuracy."
        ),
    }
    report["models"] = {
        "historical_method": args.historical_method,
        "adapted_method": args.adapted_method,
    }
    report_path = args.output / "adaptation_evidence.json"
    report_path.write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps(report, indent=2))
    print(f"Saved: {report_path}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/adaptation_evidence.py

"""Measure whether an update reduces condition shift without feature collapse."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch

from fyp_drift.evaluation.drift_metrics import (
    DEFAULT_PERMUTATIONS,
    distribution_shift_metrics,
    median_bandwidth,
)
from fyp_drift.evaluation.metrics import classification_metrics


REQUIRED_ARRAYS = ("normal_features", "adverse_features")


def _matrix(values, name):
    matrix = np.asarray(values, dtype=np.float64)
    if matrix.ndim != 2 or len(matrix) < 5:
        raise ValueError(f"{name} must be a matrix with at least five rows")
    return matrix


def paired_cosine_distance(first, second):
    first = _matrix(first, "first")
    second = _matrix(second, "second")
    if first.shape != second.shape:
        raise ValueError("Paired feature matrices must have the same shape")
    denominator = np.linalg.norm(first, axis=1) * np.linalg.norm(second, axis=1)
    similarity = np.sum(first * second, axis=1) / np.maximum(denominator, 1e-12)
    return np.clip(1.0 - similarity, 0.0, 2.0)


def unit_rows(values):
    values = np.asarray(values, dtype=np.float64)
    return values / np.maximum(np.linalg.norm(values, axis=1, keepdims=True), 1e-12)


def row_jensen_shannon(first, second):
    first = _matrix(first, "first probabilities")
    second = _matrix(second, "second probabilities")
    if first.shape != second.shape:
        raise ValueError("Paired probability matrices must have the same shape")
    first = first / np.maximum(first.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True), 1e-12)
    second = second / np.maximum(second.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True), 1e-12)
    midpoint = 0.5 * (first + second)
    first_term = np.where(
        first > 0, first * np.log(first / np.maximum(midpoint, 1e-12)), 0
    )
    second_term = np.where(
        second > 0, second * np.log(second / np.maximum(midpoint, 1e-12)), 0
    )
    return 0.5 * (first_term.sum(axis=1) + second_term.sum(axis=1))


def feature_health(features):
    features = _matrix(features, "features")
    centred = features - features.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    singular_values = np.linalg.svd(centred, compute_uv=False)
    energy = singular_values**2
    probabilities = energy / max(float(energy.sum()), 1e-12)
    entropy = -np.sum(
        np.where(probabilities > 0, probabilities * np.log(probabilities), 0)
    )
    return {
        "mean_feature_variance": float(np.var(features, axis=0).mean()),
        "effective_rank": float(np.exp(entropy)),
    }


def bootstrap_mean_change(historical, adapted, seed=42, repetitions=1000):
    historical = np.asarray(historical, dtype=np.float64)
    adapted = np.asarray(adapted, dtype=np.float64)
    if historical.shape != adapted.shape:
        raise ValueError("Paired metric arrays must have the same shape")
    generator = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    changes = np.empty(repetitions, dtype=np.float64)
    for index in range(repetitions):
        sample = generator.integers(0, len(historical), len(historical))
        changes[index] = np.mean(historical[sample] - adapted[sample])
    return {
        "historical_minus_adapted_mean": float(np.mean(historical - adapted)),
        "bootstrap_95_ci": [
            float(np.quantile(changes, 0.025)),
            float(np.quantile(changes, 0.975)),
        ],
        "bootstrap_repetitions": repetitions,
    }


def audit_features(
    arrays,
    *,
    permutations=DEFAULT_PERMUTATIONS,
    permutation_limit=200,
    seed=42,
    bandwidth=None,
):
    normal = _matrix(arrays["normal_features"], "normal_features")
    adverse = _matrix(arrays["adverse_features"], "adverse_features")
    if normal.shape != adverse.shape:
        raise ValueError("Normal and adverse feature matrices must be paired")
    distances = paired_cosine_distance(normal, adverse)
    return {
        "distribution_shift": distribution_shift_metrics(
            unit_rows(normal),
            unit_rows(adverse),
            permutations=permutations,
            permutation_limit=permutation_limit,
            seed=seed,
            paired=True,
            bandwidth=bandwidth,
        ),
        "paired_feature_cosine_distance": {
            "mean": float(distances.mean()),
            "median": float(np.median(distances)),
        },
        "normal_feature_health": feature_health(normal),
        "adverse_feature_health": feature_health(adverse),
        "distribution_feature_scaling": "row-wise L2 normalisation",
    }, distances


def optional_pair_metric(arrays, first_name, second_name):
    if first_name not in arrays or second_name not in arrays:
        return None
    values = row_jensen_shannon(arrays[first_name], arrays[second_name])
    return {"mean": float(values.mean()), "median": float(np.median(values))}, values


def optional_task_metrics(arrays):
    required = (
        "normal_labels",
        "adverse_labels",
        "normal_probabilities",
        "adverse_probabilities",
    )
    if not all(name in arrays for name in required):
        return None
    normal_labels = np.asarray(arrays["normal_labels"], dtype=np.int64)
    adverse_labels = np.asarray(arrays["adverse_labels"], dtype=np.int64)
    normal_probabilities = _matrix(
        arrays["normal_probabilities"], "normal_probabilities"
    )
    adverse_probabilities = _matrix(
        arrays["adverse_probabilities"], "adverse_probabilities"
    )
    if len(normal_labels) != len(normal_probabilities) or len(adverse_labels) != len(
        adverse_probabilities
    ):
        raise ValueError("Task labels and probability rows must have matching lengths")
    classes = normal_probabilities.shape[1]
    if adverse_probabilities.shape[1] != classes:
        raise ValueError("Normal and adverse probabilities must use the same classes")
    return {
        "normal": classification_metrics(
            normal_labels, normal_probabilities.argmax(axis=1), classes
        ),
        "adverse": classification_metrics(
            adverse_labels, adverse_probabilities.argmax(axis=1), classes
        ),
    }


def evidence_conclusions(payload, *, alpha, collapse_ratio_floor, clean_tolerance):
    historical_shift = payload["historical"]["distribution_shift"]
    adapted_shift = payload["adapted"]["distribution_shift"]
    change = payload["change"]
    distance_ci = change["paired_feature_distance"]["bootstrap_95_ci"]
    baseline_detected = historical_shift["mmd_permutation_p"] <= alpha
    residual_detected = adapted_shift["mmd_permutation_p"] <= alpha
    representation_supported = all(
        (
            change["mmd_squared_reduction"] > 0,
            change["domain_auc_reduction"] > 0,
            distance_ci[0] > 0,
            change["adverse_variance_ratio"] >= collapse_ratio_floor,
            change["adverse_effective_rank_ratio"] >= collapse_ratio_floor,
        )
    )

    conclusions = {
        "baseline_drift": {
            "detected": baseline_detected,
            "test": "RBF MMD permutation test",
            "alpha": alpha,
            "p_value": historical_shift["mmd_permutation_p"],
            "domain_classifier_roc_auc": historical_shift[
                "domain_classifier_roc_auc"
            ],
        },
        "representation_mitigation": {
            "supported": baseline_detected and representation_supported,
            "collapse_ratio_floor": collapse_ratio_floor,
            "criteria": {
                "mmd_reduced": change["mmd_squared_reduction"] > 0,
                "domain_separability_reduced": change["domain_auc_reduction"] > 0,
                "paired_distance_ci_above_zero": distance_ci[0] > 0,
                "variance_retained": change["adverse_variance_ratio"]
                >= collapse_ratio_floor,
                "effective_rank_retained": change["adverse_effective_rank_ratio"]
                >= collapse_ratio_floor,
            },
        },
        "residual_drift": {
            "detected": residual_detected,
            "test": "RBF MMD permutation test on adapted representations",
            "alpha": alpha,
            "p_value": adapted_shift["mmd_permutation_p"],
        },
        "task_mitigation": {"status": "not_assessed"},
    }

    if "task_performance" in payload["historical"]:
        old_task = payload["historical"]["task_performance"]
        new_task = payload["adapted"]["task_performance"]
        adverse_gain = (
            new_task["adverse"]["macro_f1"] - old_task["adverse"]["macro_f1"]
        )
        normal_change = (
            new_task["normal"]["macro_f1"] - old_task["normal"]["macro_f1"]
        )
        conclusions["task_mitigation"] = {
            "status": "supported"
            if adverse_gain > 0 and normal_change >= -clean_tolerance
            else "not_supported",
            "adverse_macro_f1_change": adverse_gain,
            "normal_macro_f1_change": normal_change,
            "normal_retention_tolerance": clean_tolerance,
        }

    task_status = conclusions["task_mitigation"]["status"]
    if not baseline_detected:
        overall = "baseline_drift_not_verified"
    elif not representation_supported:
        overall = "mitigation_not_supported"
    elif task_status == "not_supported":
        overall = "representation_improved_but_task_evidence_failed"
    elif task_status == "not_assessed":
        overall = "representation_mitigation_supported_task_not_assessed"
    elif residual_detected:
        overall = "partial_mitigation_supported_with_residual_drift"
    else:
        overall = "mitigation_supported_no_residual_drift_detected"
    conclusions["overall"] = overall
    return conclusions


def compare_feature_archives(
    historical,
    adapted,
    *,
    saliency_aligned=False,
    permutations=DEFAULT_PERMUTATIONS,
    permutation_limit=200,
    seed=42,
    bootstrap_repetitions=1000,
    alpha=0.05,
    collapse_ratio_floor=0.8,
    clean_tolerance=0.01,
):
    for name in REQUIRED_ARRAYS:
        if name not in historical or name not in adapted:
            raise KeyError(f"Both archives must contain {name}")

    pooled_normal = np.concatenate(
        (
            unit_rows(historical["normal_features"]),
            unit_rows(adapted["normal_features"]),
        )
    )
    pooled_adverse = np.concatenate(
        (
            unit_rows(historical["adverse_features"]),
            unit_rows(adapted["adverse_features"]),
        )
    )
    common_bandwidth = median_bandwidth(pooled_normal, pooled_adverse)

    old_metrics, old_distances = audit_features(
        historical,
        permutations=permutations,
        permutation_limit=permutation_limit,
        seed=seed,
        bandwidth=common_bandwidth,
    )
    new_metrics, new_distances = audit_features(
        adapted,
        permutations=permutations,
        permutation_limit=permutation_limit,
        seed=seed,
        bandwidth=common_bandwidth,
    )
    payload = {
        "historical": old_metrics,
        "adapted": new_metrics,
        "change": {
            "mmd_squared_reduction": old_metrics["distribution_shift"]["mmd_squared"]
            - new_metrics["distribution_shift"]["mmd_squared"],
            "domain_auc_reduction": old_metrics["distribution_shift"][
                "domain_classifier_roc_auc"
            ]
            - new_metrics["distribution_shift"]["domain_classifier_roc_auc"],
            "paired_feature_distance": bootstrap_mean_change(
                old_distances,
                new_distances,
                seed=seed,
                repetitions=bootstrap_repetitions,
            ),
            "adverse_variance_ratio": new_metrics["adverse_feature_health"][
                "mean_feature_variance"
            ]
            / max(
                old_metrics["adverse_feature_health"]["mean_feature_variance"],
                1e-12,
            ),
            "adverse_effective_rank_ratio": new_metrics["adverse_feature_health"][
                "effective_rank"
            ]
            / max(old_metrics["adverse_feature_health"]["effective_rank"], 1e-12),
        },
    }

    old_prediction = optional_pair_metric(
        historical, "normal_probabilities", "adverse_probabilities"
    )
    new_prediction = optional_pair_metric(
        adapted, "normal_probabilities", "adverse_probabilities"
    )
    if old_prediction and new_prediction:
        old_summary, old_rows = old_prediction
        new_summary, new_rows = new_prediction
        payload["historical"]["paired_prediction_js"] = old_summary
        payload["adapted"]["paired_prediction_js"] = new_summary
        payload["change"]["paired_prediction_js"] = bootstrap_mean_change(
            old_rows,
            new_rows,
            seed=seed,
            repetitions=bootstrap_repetitions,
        )

    old_task = optional_task_metrics(historical)
    new_task = optional_task_metrics(adapted)
    if bool(old_task) != bool(new_task):
        raise ValueError("Task labels and probabilities must be present in both archives")
    if old_task and new_task:
        payload["historical"]["task_performance"] = old_task
        payload["adapted"]["task_performance"] = new_task

    has_saliency = all(
        name in historical and name in adapted
        for name in ("normal_saliency", "adverse_saliency")
    )
    if saliency_aligned and has_saliency:
        old_summary, old_rows = optional_pair_metric(
            historical, "normal_saliency", "adverse_saliency"
        )
        new_summary, new_rows = optional_pair_metric(
            adapted, "normal_saliency", "adverse_saliency"
        )
        payload["historical"]["paired_saliency_js"] = old_summary
        payload["adapted"]["paired_saliency_js"] = new_summary
        payload["change"]["paired_saliency_js"] = bootstrap_mean_change(
            old_rows,
            new_rows,
            seed=seed,
            repetitions=bootstrap_repetitions,
        )
    elif has_saliency:
        payload["saliency_note"] = (
            "Saliency arrays were present but were not compared spatially. ACDC "
            "normal/adverse views require correspondence warping and valid-region "
            "masking before patch locations are comparable."
        )
    payload["conclusions"] = evidence_conclusions(
        payload,
        alpha=alpha,
        collapse_ratio_floor=collapse_ratio_floor,
        clean_tolerance=clean_tolerance,
    )
    return payload


def _state_dict(path):
    state = torch.load(path, map_location="cpu", weights_only=True)
    return state.get("model", state) if isinstance(state, dict) else state


def compare_checkpoints(historical_path, adapted_path):
    historical = _state_dict(historical_path)
    adapted = _state_dict(adapted_path)
    common = sorted(set(historical) & set(adapted))
    rows = []
    squared_change = 0.0
    squared_reference = 0.0
    changed = 0
    total = 0
    for name in common:
        first, second = historical[name], adapted[name]
        if (
            not torch.is_tensor(first)
            or not torch.is_tensor(second)
            or first.shape != second.shape
        ):
            continue
        first = first.detach().float()
        second = second.detach().float()
        change = torch.linalg.vector_norm(second - first).item()
        reference = torch.linalg.vector_norm(first).item()
        count = first.numel()
        changed_count = int(torch.count_nonzero((second - first).abs() > 1e-8))
        squared_change += change**2
        squared_reference += reference**2
        changed += changed_count
        total += count
        rows.append(
            {
                "parameter": name,
                "relative_l2_change": change / max(reference, 1e-12),
                "changed_fraction": changed_count / count,
            }
        )
    rows.sort(key=lambda row: row["relative_l2_change"], reverse=True)
    return {
        "common_parameter_tensors": len(rows),
        "relative_l2_change": np.sqrt(squared_change)
        / max(np.sqrt(squared_reference), 1e-12),
        "changed_parameter_fraction": changed / max(total, 1),
        "largest_relative_changes": rows[:20],
        "interpretation": (
            "Parameter movement confirms that optimisation changed the model. It "
            "does not establish useful adaptation without task and representation checks."
        ),
    }


def parse_args(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Compare historical and adapted representations on paired views."
    )
    parser.add_argument("--historical-features", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--adapted-features", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--historical-checkpoint", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--adapted-checkpoint", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--permutations", type=int, default=DEFAULT_PERMUTATIONS)
    parser.add_argument("--permutation-limit", type=int, default=200)
    parser.add_argument("--bootstrap-repetitions", type=int, default=1000)
    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=42)
    parser.add_argument("--alpha", type=float, default=0.05)
    parser.add_argument("--collapse-ratio-floor", type=float, default=0.8)
    parser.add_argument("--clean-retention-tolerance", type=float, default=0.01)
    parser.add_argument(
        "--saliency-aligned",
        action="store_true",
        help="Compare saliency maps only after correspondence warping and masking.",
    )
    return parser.parse_args(argv)


def main(argv=None):
    args = parse_args(argv)
    if bool(args.historical_checkpoint) != bool(args.adapted_checkpoint):
        raise ValueError("Provide both checkpoints or neither checkpoint")
    with np.load(args.historical_features) as historical, np.load(
        args.adapted_features
    ) as adapted:
        payload = compare_feature_archives(
            historical,
            adapted,
            saliency_aligned=args.saliency_aligned,
            permutations=args.permutations,
            permutation_limit=args.permutation_limit,
            seed=args.seed,
            bootstrap_repetitions=args.bootstrap_repetitions,
            alpha=args.alpha,
            collapse_ratio_floor=args.collapse_ratio_floor,
            clean_tolerance=args.clean_retention_tolerance,
        )
    if args.historical_checkpoint:
        payload["parameter_update"] = compare_checkpoints(
            args.historical_checkpoint, args.adapted_checkpoint
        )
    payload["interpretation"] = {
        "positive_reduction": (
            "A positive MMD, domain-AUC, paired-feature, prediction-JS or saliency-JS "
            "reduction means that the adapted model is less condition-sensitive."
        ),
        "claim_boundary": (
            "Reduced discrepancy supports adaptation only when adverse-condition task "
            "performance improves, normal-condition performance is retained, and feature "
            "variance and effective rank do not collapse."
        ),
    }
    args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    args.output.write_text(json.dumps(payload, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/audit.py
================================================
"""Audit when the drift-adapted ViT corrects or harms historical predictions.

The audit keeps sample identities, stratifies old/new/router outcomes, and tests
whether deleting the most salient patches changes true-class confidence more
than deleting an equal number of least salient patches.  This is a bounded
faithfulness check, not proof that a saliency map is an object segmentation.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import json
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont
from torch.utils.data import DataLoader, Dataset
from tqdm.auto import tqdm

from fyp_drift.data.clear100 import MEAN, STD, balanced_indices, open_dataset
from fyp_drift.data.corruptions import SCENARIOS, drift_transform, scenario_components
from fyp_drift.models.routing import FEATURES, apply_policy
from fyp_drift.models.siamese import ViTTwinRouter
from fyp_drift.models.vision_transformer import TemporalViT

SALIENCY_OVERLAY_STRENGTH = 0.58


def figure_font(size=14, bold=False):
    names = (
        ("timesbd.ttf", "DejaVuSerif-Bold.ttf")
        if bold
        else ("times.ttf", "DejaVuSerif.ttf")
    )
    roots = (Path("C:/Windows/Fonts"), Path("/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu"))
    for root, name in zip(roots, names):
        candidate = root / name
        if candidate.is_file():
            return ImageFont.truetype(candidate, size)
    return ImageFont.load_default(size=size)


class IndexedDataset(Dataset):
    def __init__(self, subset, indices):
        self.subset = subset
        self.indices = indices

    def __len__(self):
        return len(self.indices)

    def __getitem__(self, offset):
        index = self.indices[offset]
        image, label, metadata = self.subset[index]
        return image, label, metadata, index


def rgb(tensor):
    mean = torch.tensor(MEAN)[:, None, None]
    std = torch.tensor(STD)[:, None, None]
    value = tensor.detach().cpu() * std + mean
    return Image.fromarray(
        (value.clamp(0, 1).permute(1, 2, 0).numpy() * 255).astype(np.uint8)
    )


def normalized_saliency(weights):
    side = round(weights.numel() ** 0.5)
    heat = weights.detach().cpu().reshape(side, side).numpy().astype(np.float32)
    low, high = np.quantile(heat, (0.05, 0.95))
    return np.clip((heat - low) / max(float(high - low), 1e-8), 0, 1)


def color_map(values, size, name):
    if name == "inferno":
        positions = np.array([0.0, 0.25, 0.50, 0.75, 1.0])
        colors = np.array(
            [(0, 0, 4), (87, 16, 110), (188, 55, 84), (249, 142, 8), (252, 255, 164)]
        )
    elif name == "coolwarm":
        positions = np.array([0.0, 0.50, 1.0])
        colors = np.array([(59, 76, 192), (221, 221, 221), (180, 4, 38)])
    else:
        raise ValueError(f"Unknown colour map: {name}")
    rgb_values = np.stack(
        [np.interp(values, positions, colors[:, channel]) for channel in range(3)],
        axis=-1,
    ).astype(np.uint8)
    return Image.fromarray(rgb_values).resize(size, Image.Resampling.BILINEAR)


def overlay(base, weights):
    """Render a robustly normalised heatmap strongly enough to inspect spatial evidence."""
    heat = color_map(normalized_saliency(weights), base.size, "inferno")
    return Image.blend(base, heat, SALIENCY_OVERLAY_STRENGTH)


def saliency_change(old_weights, new_weights, size):
    """Show signed patch-evidence redistribution with a symmetric per-sample scale."""
    side = round(old_weights.numel() ** 0.5)
    old = old_weights.detach().cpu().reshape(side, side).numpy().astype(np.float32)
    new = new_weights.detach().cpu().reshape(side, side).numpy().astype(np.float32)
    difference = new - old
    scale = max(float(np.quantile(np.abs(difference), 0.95)), 1e-8)
    signed = np.clip(0.5 + 0.5 * difference / scale, 0, 1)
    return color_map(signed, size, "coolwarm")


def delete_patches(image, saliency, highest=True, fraction=0.20):
    """Replace an equal number of highest/lowest-saliency patches with mean pixels."""
    patch_side = round(saliency.numel() ** 0.5)
    count = max(1, round(saliency.numel() * fraction))
    order = saliency.argsort(descending=highest)[:count]
    mask = torch.zeros_like(saliency, dtype=torch.float32)
    mask[order] = 1
    mask = mask.reshape(1, 1, patch_side, patch_side)
    mask = torch.nn.functional.interpolate(mask, size=image.shape[-2:], mode="nearest")[
        0
    ].bool()
    changed = image.clone()
    changed[mask.expand_as(changed)] = 0.0  # zero is the normalized dataset mean
    return changed


@torch.no_grad()
def deletion_metrics(model, image, saliency, label):
    base_probability = model(image[None])[0].softmax(1)[0, label]
    salient = delete_patches(image, saliency, highest=True)
    context = delete_patches(image, saliency, highest=False)
    salient_probability = model(salient[None])[0].softmax(1)[0, label]
    context_probability = model(context[None])[0].softmax(1)[0, label]
    salient_drop = float(base_probability - salient_probability)
    context_drop = float(base_probability - context_probability)
    return (
        {
            "true_class_probability": float(base_probability),
            "salient_deletion_drop": salient_drop,
            "context_deletion_drop": context_drop,
            "faithfulness_margin": salient_drop - context_drop,
        },
        salient,
        context,
    )


def outcome(row):
    old_correct = row["old_class"] == row["true_class"]
    new_correct = row["new_class"] == row["true_class"]
    routed = row["routed_to_new"]
    if not old_correct and new_correct:
        return "helpful_routed" if routed else "missed_helpful"
    if old_correct and not new_correct:
        return "harmful_routed" if routed else "protected_old"
    if old_correct and new_correct:
        return "both_correct"
    return "both_wrong"


def save_panel(
    path, clean_image, drift_image, comparison, new_salient, new_context, row
):
    drift_rgb = rgb(drift_image)
    panels = [
        rgb(clean_image),
        drift_rgb,
        overlay(drift_rgb, comparison["old_map"][0]),
        overlay(drift_rgb, comparison["new_map"][0]),
        saliency_change(
            comparison["old_map"][0], comparison["new_map"][0], drift_rgb.size
        ),
        rgb(new_salient),
        rgb(new_context),
    ]
    shifted_name = row["scenario"].replace("_", " ")
    if row.get("drift_components"):
        shifted_name += ": " + row["drift_components"].replace("+", ", ")
    names = (
        "(a) Clean",
        f"(b) {shifted_name}",
        "(c) Historical saliency",
        "(d) Updated saliency",
        "(e) Change: blue old / red new",
        "(f) Remove top 20%",
        "(g) Remove bottom 20%",
    )
    canvas = Image.new("RGB", (224 * len(panels), 316), "white")
    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(canvas)
    for position, (panel, name) in enumerate(zip(panels, names)):
        canvas.paste(panel, (position * 224, 30))
        draw.text((position * 224 + 6, 7), name, font=figure_font(13), fill="black")
    summary = (
        f"{row['outcome']} | sample {row['dataset_index']} | true {row['true_class']} | "
        f"old {row['old_class']} | new {row['new_class']} | routed {row['routed_class']} | "
        f"p(route) {row['route_probability']:.3f} | JS {row['saliency_js']:.4f} | "
        f"new deletion margin {row['new_faithfulness_margin']:+.3f}"
    )
    draw.line((0, 258, canvas.width, 258), fill="#BBBBBB", width=1)
    draw.text((6, 267), summary, font=figure_font(13), fill="black")
    canvas.save(path, optimize=True)


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--base-checkpoint", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--run-dir", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--data-root", default="data")
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--year", type=int, default=2014)
    parser.add_argument("--scenario", choices=SCENARIOS, default="realistic_mix")
    parser.add_argument("--severity", type=int, default=3)
    parser.add_argument("--batch-size", type=int, default=16)
    parser.add_argument("--top-k", type=int, default=8)
    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=42)
    parser.add_argument("--limit", type=int)
    args = parser.parse_args()

    device = "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    base_state = torch.load(
        args.base_checkpoint, map_location=device, weights_only=True
    )
    classes = base_state["classifier.weight"].shape[0]
    old = TemporalViT("xai", classes).to(device)
    new = TemporalViT("xai", classes).to(device)
    old.load_state_dict(base_state)
    new.load_state_dict(
        torch.load(
            args.run_dir / "models" / "best.pt", map_location=device, weights_only=True
        )
    )
    old.eval()
    new.eval()
    router = ViTTwinRouter(old, new).eval()
    policy = json.loads((args.run_dir / "metrics" / "router_policy.json").read_text())

    dataset = open_dataset(args.data_root)
    clean = dataset.get_subset(
        "test_source", transform=drift_transform("clean", 1, args.seed)
    )
    shifted = dataset.get_subset(
        "test_source",
        transform=drift_transform(args.scenario, args.severity, args.seed),
    )
    indices = torch.where(shifted.metadata_array[:, 0] == args.year - 2002)[0].tolist()
    if args.limit:
        indices = balanced_indices(
            shifted.rows,
            indices,
            min(args.limit, len(indices)),
            args.seed,
        )
    loader = DataLoader(IndexedDataset(shifted, indices), batch_size=args.batch_size)

    rows = []
    with torch.no_grad():
        for images, labels, _, batch_indices in tqdm(
            loader, desc="counterexample audit", unit="batch"
        ):
            comparison = router.compare(images.to(device))
            routed, needs_update, use_new, probabilities, features = apply_policy(
                comparison, policy
            )
            old_class = comparison["old_logits"].argmax(1)
            new_class = comparison["new_logits"].argmax(1)
            old_conf = comparison["old_logits"].softmax(1).max(1).values
            new_conf = comparison["new_logits"].softmax(1).max(1).values
            for position in range(len(labels)):
                row = {
                    "dataset_index": int(batch_indices[position]),
                    "year": args.year,
                    "scenario": args.scenario,
                    "severity": args.severity,
                    "drift_components": "+".join(
                        scenario_components(
                            args.scenario,
                            int(batch_indices[position]),
                            args.severity,
                            args.seed,
                        )
                    ),
                    "true_class": int(labels[position]),
                    "old_class": int(old_class[position]),
                    "new_class": int(new_class[position]),
                    "routed_class": int(routed[position]),
                    "old_confidence": float(old_conf[position]),
                    "new_confidence": float(new_conf[position]),
                    "saliency_js": float(comparison["saliency_gap"][position]),
                    "route_probability": float(probabilities[position]),
                    "needs_update": bool(needs_update[position]),
                    "routed_to_new": bool(use_new[position]),
                    "old_evidence": float(comparison["old_evidence"][position]),
                    "new_evidence": float(comparison["new_evidence"][position]),
                }
                row.update(
                    {
                        name: float(features[position, feature_index])
                        for feature_index, name in enumerate(FEATURES)
                    }
                )
                row["outcome"] = outcome(row)
                rows.append(row)

    groups = {
        name: [row for row in rows if row["outcome"] == name]
        for name in (
            "helpful_routed",
            "missed_helpful",
            "harmful_routed",
            "protected_old",
            "both_correct",
            "both_wrong",
        )
    }
    args.output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    audited = (
        groups["helpful_routed"]
        + groups["missed_helpful"]
        + groups["harmful_routed"]
        + groups["protected_old"]
    )
    for row in tqdm(audited, desc="faithfulness audit", unit="image"):
        index = row["dataset_index"]
        drift_image, label, _ = shifted[index]
        drift_device = drift_image.to(device)
        with torch.no_grad():
            comparison = router.compare(drift_device[None])
            old_metrics, _, _ = deletion_metrics(
                old, drift_device, comparison["old_map"][0], int(label)
            )
            new_metrics, new_salient, new_context = deletion_metrics(
                new, drift_device, comparison["new_map"][0], int(label)
            )
        row.update({f"old_{key}": value for key, value in old_metrics.items()})
        row.update({f"new_{key}": value for key, value in new_metrics.items()})

    selected = []
    for name in ("helpful_routed", "missed_helpful", "harmful_routed"):
        # Prefer clear, high-probability decisions, then examples whose own
        # saliency deletion response is measurable. Missed corrections are
        # ordered from the cases closest to the route threshold.
        ranked = sorted(
            groups[name],
            key=lambda row: (
                row["route_probability"],
                row["new_faithfulness_margin"],
                row["saliency_js"],
            ),
            reverse=name != "missed_helpful",
        )
        selected.extend(ranked[: args.top_k])

    for row in tqdm(selected, desc="counterexample panels", unit="image"):
        index = row["dataset_index"]
        clean_image, _, _ = clean[index]
        drift_image, _, _ = shifted[index]
        drift_device = drift_image.to(device)
        with torch.no_grad():
            comparison = router.compare(drift_device[None])
            _, new_salient, new_context = deletion_metrics(
                new, drift_device, comparison["new_map"][0], row["true_class"]
            )
        folder = args.output / row["outcome"]
        folder.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        image_path = folder / f"sample_{index:05d}.png"
        save_panel(
            image_path,
            clean_image,
            drift_image,
            comparison,
            new_salient.cpu(),
            new_context.cpu(),
            row,
        )
        row["panel"] = image_path.relative_to(args.output).as_posix()

    fieldnames = sorted(
        {key for row in rows for key in row} | {key for row in selected for key in row}
    )
    with (args.output / "all_decisions.csv").open(
        "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8"
    ) as handle:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(handle, fieldnames=fieldnames, extrasaction="ignore")
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(rows)
    with (args.output / "selected_cases.csv").open(
        "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8"
    ) as handle:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(handle, fieldnames=fieldnames, extrasaction="ignore")
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(selected)

    faithfulness = {}
    for name in ("helpful_routed", "missed_helpful", "harmful_routed", "protected_old"):
        values = groups[name]
        faithfulness[name] = {
            "samples": len(values),
            "mean_old_margin": float(
                np.mean([row["old_faithfulness_margin"] for row in values])
            ),
            "mean_new_margin": float(
                np.mean([row["new_faithfulness_margin"] for row in values])
            ),
            "new_positive_margin_fraction": float(
                np.mean([row["new_faithfulness_margin"] > 0 for row in values])
            ),
        }
    summary = {
        "scope": {
            "year": args.year,
            "scenario": args.scenario,
            "severity": args.severity,
            "samples": len(rows),
            "device": device,
        },
        "counts": {name: len(group) for name, group in groups.items()},
        "router": {
            "helpful_routes": len(groups["helpful_routed"]),
            "harmful_routes": len(groups["harmful_routed"]),
            "missed_helpful": len(groups["missed_helpful"]),
            "net_corrections": len(groups["helpful_routed"])
            - len(groups["harmful_routed"]),
        },
        "faithfulness_by_outcome": faithfulness,
        "selected_cases": selected,
        "interpretation_limit": (
            "Deletion margins test local prediction sensitivity. They do not establish object localization "
            "or prove that saliency caused the model update."
        ),
    }
    (args.output / "counterexample_audit.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(summary, indent=2), encoding="utf-8"
    )
    print(json.dumps(summary["router"] | summary["counts"], indent=2))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/camelyon_replication.py

"""Compare matched ViT heads across Camelyon17 hospitals."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import json
from collections import defaultdict
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch
from torch.utils.data import DataLoader, Dataset
from torchvision.transforms import Compose, Normalize, Resize, ToTensor
from transformers import Dinov2Model

from fyp_drift.data.clear100 import MEAN, STD
from fyp_drift.evaluation.drift_metrics import (
    DEFAULT_PERMUTATIONS,
    distribution_shift_metrics,
)
from fyp_drift.evaluation.wilds_replication import (
    classification_scores,
    extract_features,
    fit_linear_probe,
    train_architecture,
)
from fyp_drift.models.vision_transformer import BACKBONE


DATASET_NAME = "wltjr1007/Camelyon17-WILDS"


class ImageRows(Dataset):
    def __init__(self, rows, transform, domain):
        self.rows = rows
        self.transform = transform
        self.domain = domain
        self.labels = np.asarray([row[1] for row in rows], dtype=np.int64)

    def __len__(self):
        return len(self.rows)

    def __getitem__(self, index):
        image, label = self.rows[index]
        return self.transform(image), label, torch.tensor([self.domain])


def balanced_rows(stream, limit, seed):
    """Take an equal number of examples per class from a shuffled stream."""
    if limit % 2:
        raise ValueError("Camelyon sample limits must be even")
    quota = limit // 2
    rows = defaultdict(list)
    for example in stream.shuffle(seed=seed, buffer_size=20_000):
        label = int(example["label"])
        if len(rows[label]) < quota:
            rows[label].append((example["image"].convert("RGB").copy(), label))
        if len(rows[0]) == quota and len(rows[1]) == quota:
            break
    if len(rows[0]) != quota or len(rows[1]) != quota:
        raise ValueError("The stream did not contain enough examples from both classes")
    return [row for pair in zip(rows[0], rows[1]) for row in pair]


def balanced_source_rows(stream, train_limit, eval_limit, seed):
    """Create class-balanced source splits with disjoint patients."""
    quotas = {
        "train": train_limit // 2,
        "id": eval_limit // 2,
    }
    if train_limit % 2 or eval_limit % 2:
        raise ValueError("Camelyon sample limits must be even")
    rows = {name: defaultdict(list) for name in quotas}
    complete = False
    for example in stream.shuffle(seed=seed, buffer_size=20_000):
        patient = f"{example['center']}:{example['patient']}:{seed}"
        digest = hashlib.sha256(patient.encode()).digest()
        split = "id" if int.from_bytes(digest[:2]) % 5 == 0 else "train"
        label = int(example["label"])
        if len(rows[split][label]) < quotas[split]:
            image = example["image"].convert("RGB").copy()
            rows[split][label].append((image, label))
        complete = all(
            len(rows[name][label]) == quota
            for name, quota in quotas.items()
            for label in (0, 1)
        )
        if complete:
            break
    if not complete:
        raise ValueError("The source stream did not fill both patient-disjoint splits")
    return {
        name: [row for pair in zip(values[0], values[1]) for row in pair]
        for name, values in rows.items()
    }


def parse_args(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Screen matched ViT heads on Camelyon17 hospital shift."
    )
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--train-limit", type=int, default=5000)
    parser.add_argument("--eval-limit", type=int, default=1000)
    parser.add_argument("--batch-size", type=int, default=32)
    parser.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=0)
    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=42)
    parser.add_argument("--permutations", type=int, default=DEFAULT_PERMUTATIONS)
    parser.add_argument("--permutation-limit", type=int, default=200)
    parser.add_argument("--epochs", type=int, default=2)
    parser.add_argument("--learning-rate", type=float, default=5e-5)
    parser.add_argument("--faithfulness-weight", type=float, default=0.1)
    return parser.parse_args(argv)


def image_transform():
    return Compose(
        [
            Resize((224, 224), antialias=True),
            ToTensor(),
            Normalize(mean=MEAN, std=STD),
        ]
    )


def main(argv=None):
    from datasets import load_dataset

    args = parse_args(argv)
    if args.train_limit < 100 or args.eval_limit < 100:
        raise ValueError("The train and evaluation limits must each be at least 100")

    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)
    np.random.seed(args.seed)
    device = "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    source = load_dataset(DATASET_NAME, split="train", streaming=True)
    target = load_dataset(DATASET_NAME, split="validation", streaming=True)

    source_rows = balanced_source_rows(
        source, args.train_limit, args.eval_limit, args.seed
    )
    datasets = {
        "train": ImageRows(source_rows["train"], image_transform(), 0),
        "id": ImageRows(source_rows["id"], image_transform(), 0),
        "ood": ImageRows(
            balanced_rows(target, args.eval_limit, args.seed + 1),
            image_transform(),
            1,
        ),
    }
    loaders = {
        name: DataLoader(
            dataset,
            batch_size=args.batch_size,
            num_workers=args.workers,
            shuffle=False,
        )
        for name, dataset in datasets.items()
    }

    backbone = Dinov2Model.from_pretrained(BACKBONE).to(device).eval()
    extracted = {
        name: extract_features(backbone, loader, device, name)
        for name, loader in loaders.items()
    }
    del backbone
    if device == "cuda":
        torch.cuda.empty_cache()

    probe = fit_linear_probe(*extracted["train"], args.seed)
    id_scores = classification_scores(probe, *extracted["id"])
    ood_scores = classification_scores(probe, *extracted["ood"])
    drift = distribution_shift_metrics(
        extracted["id"][0],
        extracted["ood"][0],
        permutations=args.permutations,
        permutation_limit=args.permutation_limit,
        seed=args.seed,
    )
    architecture = {
        method: train_architecture(method, 2, loaders, args, device)
        for method in ("baseline", "xai")
    }
    architecture["xai_minus_baseline_ood_macro_f1_points"] = 100 * (
        architecture["xai"]["out_of_distribution"]["macro_f1"]
        - architecture["baseline"]["out_of_distribution"]["macro_f1"]
    )

    payload = {
        "dataset": "Camelyon17-WILDS",
        "source": DATASET_NAME,
        "feature_extractor": BACKBONE,
        "seed": args.seed,
        "limits": {
            "training": len(datasets["train"]),
            "source_hospital_holdout": len(datasets["id"]),
            "validation_hospital": len(datasets["ood"]),
        },
        "linear_probe": {
            "in_distribution": id_scores,
            "out_of_distribution": ood_scores,
            "macro_f1_change_percentage_points": 100
            * (ood_scores["macro_f1"] - id_scores["macro_f1"]),
        },
        "drift_detection": drift,
        "architecture_screen": architecture,
        "scope": (
            "Camelyon17-WILDS tests acquisition shift across hospitals. The "
            "Hugging Face mirror groups source hospitals in its train split, "
            "so this screen uses a patient-disjoint balanced source holdout for ID "
            "evaluation and the validation hospital for OOD evaluation."
        ),
    }
    args.output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    output_path = args.output / "camelyon17_replication.json"
    output_path.write_text(json.dumps(payload, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2))
    print(f"Saved: {output_path}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/cifar_replication.py
