"""Compare matched ViT heads on CIFAR-10 and its recollected test set."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.request import urlretrieve

import numpy as np
import torch
from PIL import Image
from torch.utils.data import DataLoader, Dataset, Subset
from torchvision.datasets import CIFAR10
from torchvision.transforms import Compose, Normalize, Resize, ToTensor
from transformers import Dinov2Model

from fyp_drift.data.clear100 import MEAN, STD
from fyp_drift.evaluation.drift_metrics import (
    DEFAULT_PERMUTATIONS,
    distribution_shift_metrics,
)
from fyp_drift.evaluation.wilds_replication import (
    classification_scores,
    extract_features,
    fit_linear_probe,
    stratified_sample,
    train_architecture,
)
from fyp_drift.models.vision_transformer import BACKBONE


CIFAR101_BASE_URL = (
    "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/modestyachts/CIFAR-10.1/"
    "master/datasets"
)
CIFAR10_MIRROR_URL = (
    "https://huggingface.co/datasets/MIT-OL-AI-D/cifar-10-python/"
    "resolve/main/cifar-10-python.tar.gz"
)


class WithMetadata(Dataset):
    """Return the metadata placeholder expected by the shared evaluation code."""

    def __init__(self, dataset):
        self.dataset = dataset
        self.labels = np.asarray(dataset.targets, dtype=np.int64)

    def __len__(self):
        return len(self.dataset)

    def __getitem__(self, index):
        image, label = self.dataset[index]
        return image, label, torch.tensor([0])


class CIFAR101(Dataset):
    """Load the class-balanced CIFAR-10.1 v6 release from NumPy files."""

    def __init__(self, root, transform):
        root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        data_path = root / "cifar10.1_v6_data.npy"
        labels_path = root / "cifar10.1_v6_labels.npy"
        for path in (data_path, labels_path):
            if not path.exists():
                temporary = path.with_suffix(path.suffix + ".part")
                urlretrieve(f"{CIFAR101_BASE_URL}/{path.name}", temporary)
                temporary.replace(path)

        self.data = np.load(data_path, allow_pickle=False)
        labels = np.load(labels_path, allow_pickle=False)
        if self.data.shape != (2000, 32, 32, 3):
            raise ValueError(f"Unexpected CIFAR-10.1 image shape: {self.data.shape}")
        if self.data.dtype != np.uint8:
            raise ValueError(f"Unexpected CIFAR-10.1 image dtype: {self.data.dtype}")
        if labels.shape != (2000,):
            raise ValueError(f"Unexpected CIFAR-10.1 label shape: {labels.shape}")
        if not np.issubdtype(labels.dtype, np.integer):
            raise ValueError(f"Unexpected CIFAR-10.1 label dtype: {labels.dtype}")
        self.labels = labels.astype(np.int64)
        if not np.array_equal(np.bincount(self.labels, minlength=10), np.full(10, 200)):
            raise ValueError("CIFAR-10.1 v6 should contain 200 examples per class")
        self.transform = transform

    def __len__(self):
        return len(self.labels)

    def __getitem__(self, index):
        image = Image.fromarray(self.data[index])
        return self.transform(image), int(self.labels[index]), torch.tensor([1])


def parse_args(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Screen matched ViT heads on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-10.1."
    )
    parser.add_argument("--data-root", type=Path, default=Path("data/cifar"))
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--train-limit", type=int, default=5000)
    parser.add_argument("--eval-limit", type=int, default=1000)
    parser.add_argument("--batch-size", type=int, default=32)
    parser.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=0)
    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=42)
    parser.add_argument("--permutations", type=int, default=DEFAULT_PERMUTATIONS)
    parser.add_argument("--permutation-limit", type=int, default=200)
    parser.add_argument("--epochs", type=int, default=2)
    parser.add_argument("--learning-rate", type=float, default=5e-5)
    parser.add_argument("--faithfulness-weight", type=float, default=0.1)
    return parser.parse_args(argv)


def image_transform():
    return Compose(
        [
            Resize((224, 224), antialias=True),
            ToTensor(),
            Normalize(mean=MEAN, std=STD),
        ]
    )


def sampled_loader(dataset, limit, seed, batch_size, workers):
    indices = stratified_sample(dataset.labels, limit, seed)
    return DataLoader(
        Subset(dataset, indices),
        batch_size=batch_size,
        num_workers=workers,
        shuffle=False,
    )


def main(argv=None):
    args = parse_args(argv)
    if args.train_limit < 100 or args.eval_limit < 100:
        raise ValueError("The train and evaluation limits must each be at least 100")

    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)
    np.random.seed(args.seed)
    device = "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    transform = image_transform()
    # The original Toronto host is rate-limited on the project server. This
    # mirror serves the unmodified archive; torchvision still checks its MD5.
    CIFAR10.url = CIFAR10_MIRROR_URL
    datasets = {
        "train": WithMetadata(
            CIFAR10(args.data_root, train=True, download=True, transform=transform)
        ),
        "id": WithMetadata(
            CIFAR10(args.data_root, train=False, download=True, transform=transform)
        ),
        "ood": CIFAR101(args.data_root / "cifar-10.1", transform),
    }
    loaders = {
        "train": sampled_loader(
            datasets["train"], args.train_limit, args.seed, args.batch_size, args.workers
        ),
        "id": sampled_loader(
            datasets["id"],
            args.eval_limit,
            args.seed + 1,
            args.batch_size,
            args.workers,
        ),
        "ood": sampled_loader(
            datasets["ood"],
            args.eval_limit,
            args.seed + 2,
            args.batch_size,
            args.workers,
        ),
    }

    backbone = Dinov2Model.from_pretrained(BACKBONE).to(device).eval()
    extracted = {
        name: extract_features(backbone, data_loader, device, name)
        for name, data_loader in loaders.items()
    }
    del backbone
    if device == "cuda":
        torch.cuda.empty_cache()

    probe = fit_linear_probe(*extracted["train"], args.seed)
    id_scores = classification_scores(probe, *extracted["id"])
    ood_scores = classification_scores(probe, *extracted["ood"])
    drift = distribution_shift_metrics(
        extracted["id"][0],
        extracted["ood"][0],
        permutations=args.permutations,
        permutation_limit=args.permutation_limit,
        seed=args.seed,
    )
    architecture = {
        method: train_architecture(method, 10, loaders, args, device)
        for method in ("baseline", "xai")
    }
    architecture["xai_minus_baseline_ood_macro_f1_points"] = 100 * (
        architecture["xai"]["out_of_distribution"]["macro_f1"]
        - architecture["baseline"]["out_of_distribution"]["macro_f1"]
    )

    payload = {
        "dataset": "CIFAR-10.1 v6",
        "source_dataset": "CIFAR-10",
        "feature_extractor": BACKBONE,
        "seed": args.seed,
        "limits": {
            "training": len(loaders["train"].dataset),
            "in_distribution_test": len(loaders["id"].dataset),
            "recollected_test": len(loaders["ood"].dataset),
        },
        "sampling": "class-balanced without replacement",
        "linear_probe": {
            "in_distribution": id_scores,
            "recollected": ood_scores,
            "macro_f1_change_percentage_points": 100
            * (ood_scores["macro_f1"] - id_scores["macro_f1"]),
        },
        "drift_detection": drift,
        "architecture_screen": architecture,
        "scope": (
            "CIFAR-10.1 is a naturally recollected reproduction test. It tests "
            "dataset shift across collections, not temporal adaptation within a "
            "deployment and not complex industrial imagery."
        ),
    }
    args.output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    output_path = args.output / "cifar10_1_replication.json"
    output_path.write_text(json.dumps(payload, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2))
    print(f"Saved: {output_path}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/classifier.py
================================================
"""Classification metrics by temporal split, upload year, and class."""

import argparse
import csv
import json
import time
from pathlib import Path

import torch
from tqdm.auto import tqdm

from fyp_drift.data.clear100 import make_loaders
from fyp_drift.evaluation.metrics import grouped_classification
from fyp_drift.models.vision_transformer import TemporalViT


@torch.no_grad()
def evaluate(
    model, loaders, year_column, device, splits=("id_val", "id_test", "val", "test")
):
    model.eval()
    report, records = {}, []
    for split in splits:
        rows = []
        if device == "cuda":
            torch.cuda.synchronize()
            gpu_start, gpu_end = (
                torch.cuda.Event(enable_timing=True),
                torch.cuda.Event(enable_timing=True),
            )
            gpu_start.record()
        wall_start = time.perf_counter()
        for images, labels, metadata in tqdm(
            loaders[split], desc=f"evaluate {split}", unit="batch", leave=False
        ):
            probabilities = model(images.to(device))[0].softmax(1)
            confidence, prediction = probabilities.max(1)
            for year, actual, predicted, score in zip(
                metadata[:, year_column].tolist(),
                labels.tolist(),
                prediction.cpu().tolist(),
                confidence.cpu().tolist(),
            ):
                rows.append(
                    {
                        "split": split,
                        "year": 2002 + year if year >= 0 else -1,
                        "true_class": actual,
                        "prediction": predicted,
                        "confidence": round(score, 6),
                    }
                )
        if device == "cuda":
            gpu_end.record()
            torch.cuda.synchronize()
            gpu_seconds = gpu_start.elapsed_time(gpu_end) / 1000
        else:
            gpu_seconds = None
        wall_seconds = time.perf_counter() - wall_start
        report[split] = grouped_classification(rows)
        report[split]["_timing"] = {
            "samples": len(rows),
            "batches": len(loaders[split]),
            "wall_seconds": round(wall_seconds, 4),
            "gpu_seconds": round(gpu_seconds, 4) if gpu_seconds is not None else None,
            "images_per_second": round(len(rows) / wall_seconds, 2),
            "milliseconds_per_image": round(1000 * wall_seconds / len(rows), 4),
        }
        records.extend(rows)
    return report, records


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--checkpoint", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--method", choices=("baseline", "xai"), required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--data-root", default="data")
    parser.add_argument("--batch-size", type=int, default=8)
    parser.add_argument("--limit-eval", type=int)
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    device = "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    checkpoint = torch.load(args.checkpoint, map_location=device, weights_only=True)
    model = TemporalViT(
        args.method, num_classes=checkpoint["classifier.weight"].shape[0]
    ).to(device)
    model.load_state_dict(checkpoint)
    loaders, year_column = make_loaders(
        args.data_root, args.batch_size, limit_eval=args.limit_eval
    )
    report, records = evaluate(model, loaders, year_column, device)
    if args.output:
        args.output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        (args.output / "evaluation.json").write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2))
        with (args.output / "predictions.csv").open("w", newline="") as handle:
            writer = csv.DictWriter(handle, fieldnames=list(records[0]))
            writer.writeheader()
            writer.writerows(records)
    print(
        json.dumps(
            {
                split: {
                    "accuracy": values["all"]["accuracy"],
                    "macro_f1": values["all"]["macro_f1"],
                    "macro_iou": values["all"]["macro_iou"],
                }
                for split, values in report.items()
            },
            indent=2,
        )
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/classwise.py
================================================
"""Export readable class-level comparisons from router decision logs."""

import argparse
import csv
import json
from collections import defaultdict
from pathlib import Path

from fyp_drift.evaluation.metrics import classwise_model_comparison


def _read_decisions(path):
    integer_fields = {
        "severity",
        "dataset_index",
        "year",
        "true_class",
        "old_class",
        "new_class",
        "routed_class",
    }
    boolean_fields = {"needs_update", "routed_to_new"}
    with path.open(newline="", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        rows = list(csv.DictReader(handle))
    for row in rows:
        for field in integer_fields & row.keys():
            row[field] = int(row[field])
        for field in boolean_fields & row.keys():
            row[field] = row[field].lower() == "true"
    return rows


def _read_class_names(path):
    if path is None:
        return None
    names = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    if not isinstance(names, list) or not all(isinstance(name, str) for name in names):
        raise ValueError("Class names must be a JSON list of strings")
    return names


def build_report(decisions, class_names=None, min_support=20):
    groups = defaultdict(list)
    for row in decisions:
        scenario = row.get("scenario", "clean")
        groups[(scenario, str(row["year"]))].append(row)
        groups[(scenario, "all")].append(row)

    report = {
        "metric_definition": "One-versus-rest F1 and IoU for each true class",
        "stratification": (
            "Classes with sufficient support are ranked by historical F1 and divided "
            "into four equally sized groups from weakest to strongest"
        ),
        "groups": {},
    }
    for (scenario, year), rows in sorted(groups.items()):
        comparison = classwise_model_comparison(rows, min_support=min_support)
        if class_names:
            for class_row in comparison["classes"]:
                class_row["class_name"] = class_names[class_row["class_id"]]
        report["groups"][f"{scenario}:{year}"] = {
            "scenario": scenario,
            "year": year,
            "sample_count": len(rows),
            **comparison,
        }
    return report


def write_tables(report, output_dir):
    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    summary = {
        **report,
        "groups": {
            name: {key: value for key, value in group.items() if key != "classes"}
            for name, group in report["groups"].items()
        },
    }
    (output_dir / "classwise_summary.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(summary, indent=2), encoding="utf-8"
    )

    class_fields = [
        "scenario",
        "year",
        "class_id",
        "class_name",
        "support",
        "sufficient_support",
        "old_f1",
        "new_f1",
        "routed_f1",
        "new_f1_delta_pp",
        "routed_f1_delta_pp",
        "old_iou",
        "new_iou",
        "routed_iou",
        "new_iou_delta_pp",
        "routed_iou_delta_pp",
        "route_rate",
        "helpful_routes",
        "harmful_routes",
        "beneficial_available",
        "beneficial_missed",
        "harmful_available",
        "harmful_avoided",
    ]
    with (output_dir / "classwise_metrics.csv").open(
        "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8"
    ) as handle:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(handle, fieldnames=class_fields)
        writer.writeheader()
        for group in report["groups"].values():
            for row in group["classes"]:
                routing = row.get("routing", {})
                writer.writerow(
                    {
                        "scenario": group["scenario"],
                        "year": group["year"],
                        "class_id": row["class_id"],
                        "class_name": row.get("class_name", ""),
                        "support": row["support"],
                        "sufficient_support": row["sufficient_support"],
                        "old_f1": row["models"]["old"]["f1"],
                        "new_f1": row["models"]["new"]["f1"],
                        "routed_f1": row["models"]["routed"]["f1"],
                        "new_f1_delta_pp": row["f1_delta_pp"]["new"],
                        "routed_f1_delta_pp": row["f1_delta_pp"]["routed"],
                        "old_iou": row["models"]["old"]["iou"],
                        "new_iou": row["models"]["new"]["iou"],
                        "routed_iou": row["models"]["routed"]["iou"],
                        "new_iou_delta_pp": row["iou_delta_pp"]["new"],
                        "routed_iou_delta_pp": row["iou_delta_pp"]["routed"],
                        "route_rate": routing.get("route_rate", ""),
                        "helpful_routes": routing.get("helpful", ""),
                        "harmful_routes": routing.get("harmful", ""),
                        "beneficial_available": routing.get("beneficial_available", ""),
                        "beneficial_missed": routing.get("beneficial_missed", ""),
                        "harmful_available": routing.get("harmful_available", ""),
                        "harmful_avoided": routing.get("harmful_avoided", ""),
                    }
                )

    strata_fields = [
        "scenario",
        "year",
        "stratum",
        "description",
        "class_count",
        "historical_f1_min",
        "historical_f1_max",
        "old_mean_f1",
        "new_mean_f1",
        "routed_mean_f1",
        "new_mean_f1_delta_pp",
        "routed_mean_f1_delta_pp",
    ]
    with (output_dir / "classwise_strata.csv").open(
        "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8"
    ) as handle:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(handle, fieldnames=strata_fields)
        writer.writeheader()
        for group in report["groups"].values():
            for row in group["historical_f1_rank_strata"]:
                writer.writerow(
                    {
                        "scenario": group["scenario"],
                        "year": group["year"],
                        "stratum": row["stratum"],
                        "description": row["description"],
                        "class_count": row["class_count"],
                        "historical_f1_min": row["historical_f1_range"][0],
                        "historical_f1_max": row["historical_f1_range"][1],
                        "old_mean_f1": row["mean_f1"]["old"],
                        "new_mean_f1": row["mean_f1"]["new"],
                        "routed_mean_f1": row["mean_f1"]["routed"],
                        "new_mean_f1_delta_pp": row["mean_f1_delta_pp"]["new"],
                        "routed_mean_f1_delta_pp": row["mean_f1_delta_pp"]["routed"],
                    }
                )


def main(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--decisions", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--output-dir", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--class-names", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--minimum-support", type=int, default=20)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)

    decisions = _read_decisions(args.decisions)
    report = build_report(
        decisions,
        class_names=_read_class_names(args.class_names),
        min_support=args.minimum_support,
    )
    write_tables(report, args.output_dir)
    print(args.output_dir / "classwise_summary.json")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/compare_runs.py
================================================
"""Create a compact, reviewable comparison from completed router runs."""

import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path


def read_events(path):
    rows = []
    with path.open(encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        for line in handle:
            event = json.loads(line)
            if "epoch" in event and "adapt_loss" in event:
                rows.append(event)
    return rows


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument(
        "--run",
        action="append",
        required=True,
        help="NAME=RUN_DIR; repeat for every run",
    )
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    report = {"metric": "macro_f1", "runs": {}}
    controls = (
        "epochs",
        "batch_size",
        "seed",
        "adapt_year",
        "calibration_year",
        "test_years",
        "long_horizon",
    )
    for specification in args.run:
        name, raw_path = specification.split("=", 1)
        path = Path(raw_path)
        config = json.loads((path / "config.json").read_text())
        drift = json.loads((path / "metrics" / "future_drift.json").read_text())
        policy = json.loads((path / "metrics" / "router_policy.json").read_text())
        scenarios = {}
        for scenario, years in drift["scenarios"].items():
            scenarios[scenario] = {}
            for year, payload in years.items():
                result = payload["results"]["all"]
                scenarios[scenario][year] = {
                    key: result[key]["macro_f1"] for key in ("old", "new", "routed")
                } | {
                    key: result[key]
                    for key in ("helpful_routes", "harmful_routes", "routed_to_new")
                }
                if "classwise_comparison" in result:
                    classwise = result["classwise_comparison"]
                    scenarios[scenario][year]["classwise"] = {
                        "minimum_support": classwise["minimum_support"],
                        "eligible_classes": classwise["eligible_classes"],
                        "comparison": classwise["comparison"],
                        "historical_f1_rank_strata": classwise[
                            "historical_f1_rank_strata"
                        ],
                    }
        report["runs"][name] = {
            "path": str(path),
            "config": {
                key: config.get(key)
                for key in controls + ("adaptation_profile", "disable_saliency_loss")
            },
            "epochs": read_events(path / "logs" / "events.jsonl"),
            "policy": policy,
            "scenarios": scenarios,
        }
    report["comparability"] = {
        key: {
            "matched": len(
                {
                    json.dumps(run["config"].get(key), sort_keys=True)
                    for run in report["runs"].values()
                }
            )
            == 1,
            "values": {
                name: run["config"].get(key) for name, run in report["runs"].items()
            },
        }
        for key in controls
    }
    args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    args.output.write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    print(args.output)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/drift.py
================================================
"""Evaluate a frozen ViT under progressive, reproducible temporal corruptions."""

import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path

import torch
from torch.utils.data import DataLoader

from fyp_drift.data.clear100 import balanced_indices, open_dataset
from fyp_drift.data.corruptions import (
    REALISTIC_BENCHMARK_SCENARIOS,
    SCENARIOS,
    drift_transform,
)
from fyp_drift.evaluation.classifier import evaluate
from fyp_drift.models.vision_transformer import TemporalViT


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--checkpoint", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--method", choices=("baseline", "xai"), required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--data-root", default="data")
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--batch-size", type=int, default=16)
    parser.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=0)
    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=42)
    parser.add_argument("--limit", type=int)
    parser.add_argument(
        "--scenarios",
        nargs="+",
        choices=SCENARIOS,
        default=list(REALISTIC_BENCHMARK_SCENARIOS),
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--long-horizon",
        action="store_true",
        help="Evaluate frozen checkpoints on test-source years 2012-2014",
    )
    args = parser.parse_args()

    device = "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    state = torch.load(args.checkpoint, map_location=device, weights_only=True)
    model = TemporalViT(args.method, state["classifier.weight"].shape[0]).to(device)
    model.load_state_dict(state)
    dataset = open_dataset(args.data_root)
    year_column = dataset.metadata_fields.index("year")
    plan = (
        ((2012, "test_source", 1), (2013, "test_source", 2), (2014, "test_source", 3))
        if args.long_horizon
        else ((2012, "val", 1), (2013, "val", 2), (2014, "test", 3))
    )
    results = {
        "protocol": {
            "training_images_modified": False,
            "progression": {str(year): severity for year, _, severity in plan},
        },
        "scenarios": {},
    }
    for scenario in args.scenarios:
        results["scenarios"][scenario] = {}
        for year, split, severity in plan:
            subset = dataset.get_subset(
                split, transform=drift_transform(scenario, severity, args.seed)
            )
            year_id = year - 2002
            indices = torch.where(subset.metadata_array[:, year_column] == year_id)[
                0
            ].tolist()
            if args.limit:
                indices = balanced_indices(
                    subset.rows,
                    indices,
                    min(args.limit, len(indices)),
                    args.seed,
                )
            loader = DataLoader(
                torch.utils.data.Subset(subset, indices),
                batch_size=args.batch_size,
                num_workers=args.workers,
            )
            report, _ = evaluate(
                model, {"drift": loader}, year_column, device, splits=("drift",)
            )
            results["scenarios"][scenario][str(year)] = {
                "severity": 0 if scenario == "clean" else severity,
                "metrics": report["drift"]["all"],
                "timing": report["drift"]["_timing"],
            }
    args.output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    destination = args.output / "drift_evaluation.json"
    destination.write_text(json.dumps(results, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    print(
        json.dumps(
            {
                scenario: {
                    year: {
                        "macro_f1": row["metrics"]["macro_f1"],
                        "images_per_second": row["timing"]["images_per_second"],
                    }
                    for year, row in years.items()
                }
                for scenario, years in results["scenarios"].items()
            },
            indent=2,
        )
    )
    print(f"Saved: {destination}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/drift_metrics.py
================================================
"""Distribution-level metrics for verifying image drift in frozen features."""

from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
from sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression
from sklearn.metrics import roc_auc_score
from sklearn.model_selection import (
    StratifiedGroupKFold,
    StratifiedKFold,
    cross_val_predict,
)
from sklearn.pipeline import make_pipeline
from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler


# The default of 499 randomisations gives a minimum p-value of 0.002 while
# keeping repeated drift audits practical on the project server.
DEFAULT_PERMUTATIONS = 499
PERMUTATION_BATCH_SIZE = 256


def _as_matrix(values):
    matrix = np.asarray(values, dtype=np.float64)
    if matrix.ndim != 2 or len(matrix) < 2:
        raise ValueError("Each feature sample must be a matrix with at least two rows")
    return matrix


def squared_distances(first, second):
    first = _as_matrix(first)
    second = _as_matrix(second)
    distances = (
        np.sum(first * first, axis=1)[:, None]
        + np.sum(second * second, axis=1)[None, :]
        - 2 * first @ second.T
    )
    return np.maximum(distances, 0.0)


def median_bandwidth(first, second):
    combined = np.concatenate((_as_matrix(first), _as_matrix(second)), axis=0)
    distances = squared_distances(combined, combined)
    positive = distances[np.triu_indices_from(distances, k=1)]
    positive = positive[positive > 0]
    if not len(positive):
        return 1.0
    return float(np.sqrt(np.median(positive)))


def rbf_kernel(first, second, bandwidth):
    if bandwidth <= 0:
        raise ValueError("bandwidth must be positive")
    return np.exp(-squared_distances(first, second) / (2 * bandwidth**2))


def maximum_mean_discrepancy(first, second, bandwidth=None):
    """Return the non-negative, biased RBF MMD squared estimate."""
    first, second = _as_matrix(first), _as_matrix(second)
    bandwidth = bandwidth or median_bandwidth(first, second)
    value = (
        rbf_kernel(first, first, bandwidth).mean()
        + rbf_kernel(second, second, bandwidth).mean()
        - 2 * rbf_kernel(first, second, bandwidth).mean()
    )
    return max(0.0, float(value)), float(bandwidth)


def mmd_permutation_test(
    first,
    second,
    permutations=DEFAULT_PERMUTATIONS,
    seed=42,
    permutation_limit=200,
    paired=False,
    bandwidth=None,
):
    """Test equality of feature distributions with a fixed-bandwidth MMD.

    Paired image views are permuted by swapping the domain label within each
    image pair. Unpaired samples use the ordinary pooled-label permutation.
    """
    first, second = _as_matrix(first), _as_matrix(second)
    if paired and len(first) != len(second):
        raise ValueError("Paired MMD requires the same number of rows per domain")
    full_mmd, full_bandwidth = maximum_mean_discrepancy(first, second, bandwidth)
    generator = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    if paired and len(first) > permutation_limit:
        indices = generator.choice(len(first), permutation_limit, replace=False)
        first, second = first[indices], second[indices]
    elif not paired:
        if len(first) > permutation_limit:
            first = first[
                generator.choice(len(first), permutation_limit, replace=False)
            ]
        if len(second) > permutation_limit:
            second = second[
                generator.choice(len(second), permutation_limit, replace=False)
            ]

    combined = np.concatenate((first, second), axis=0)
    first_size = len(first)
    second_size = len(second)
    kernel = rbf_kernel(combined, combined, full_bandwidth)

    observed_weights = np.concatenate(
        (
            np.full(first_size, 1 / first_size),
            np.full(second_size, -1 / second_size),
        )
    )
    observed = max(0.0, float(observed_weights @ kernel @ observed_weights))
    exceedances = 0
    completed = 0
    while completed < permutations:
        batch_size = min(PERMUTATION_BATCH_SIZE, permutations - completed)
        if paired:
            pair_signs = generator.choice(
                (-1.0, 1.0), size=(batch_size, first_size)
            )
            weights = np.concatenate((pair_signs, -pair_signs), axis=1)
            weights /= first_size
        else:
            random_order = generator.random((batch_size, len(combined)))
            first_indices = np.argpartition(
                random_order, first_size - 1, axis=1
            )[:, :first_size]
            weights = np.full(
                (batch_size, len(combined)), -1 / second_size, dtype=np.float64
            )
            np.put_along_axis(
                weights,
                first_indices,
                np.full_like(first_indices, 1 / first_size, dtype=np.float64),
                axis=1,
            )
        permuted = np.einsum(
            "bi,ij,bj->b", weights, kernel, weights, optimize=True
        )
        exceedances += int(np.count_nonzero(permuted >= observed))
        completed += batch_size
    p_value = (exceedances + 1) / (permutations + 1)
    return {
        "mmd_squared": full_mmd,
        "mmd_bandwidth": full_bandwidth,
        "mmd_permutation_p": float(p_value),
        "mmd_permutations": int(permutations),
        "mmd_permutation_resolution": float(1 / (permutations + 1)),
        "mmd_permutation_samples_per_group": int(min(permutation_limit, len(first))),
        "mmd_permutation_scheme": "paired_within_image_swap"
        if paired
        else "pooled_label_permutation",
    }


def domain_classifier_auc(first, second, seed=42, folds=5, paired=False):
    """Estimate how easily a linear classifier separates two domains."""
    first, second = _as_matrix(first), _as_matrix(second)
    if paired and len(first) != len(second):
        raise ValueError(
            "Paired domain classification requires the same number of rows per domain"
        )
    features = np.concatenate((first, second), axis=0)
    labels = np.concatenate((np.zeros(len(first)), np.ones(len(second))))
    fold_count = min(folds, len(first), len(second))
    if fold_count < 2:
        raise ValueError("Domain classification requires at least two folds")
    groups = None
    if paired:
        groups = np.concatenate((np.arange(len(first)), np.arange(len(second))))
        splitter = StratifiedGroupKFold(
            n_splits=fold_count,
            shuffle=True,
            random_state=seed,
        )
    else:
        splitter = StratifiedKFold(
            n_splits=fold_count,
            shuffle=True,
            random_state=seed,
        )
    classifier = make_pipeline(
        StandardScaler(),
        LogisticRegression(class_weight="balanced", max_iter=1000, random_state=seed),
    )
    probabilities = cross_val_predict(
        classifier,
        features,
        labels,
        cv=splitter,
        groups=groups,
        method="predict_proba",
    )[:, 1]
    return float(roc_auc_score(labels, probabilities))


def distribution_shift_metrics(
    first,
    second,
    *,
    permutations=DEFAULT_PERMUTATIONS,
    permutation_limit=200,
    seed=42,
    paired=False,
    bandwidth=None,
):
    metrics = mmd_permutation_test(
        first,
        second,
        permutations,
        seed,
        permutation_limit,
        paired,
        bandwidth,
    )
    metrics["domain_classifier_roc_auc"] = domain_classifier_auc(
        first, second, seed, paired=paired
    )
    metrics["domain_classifier_cv"] = (
        "pair_grouped_stratified" if paired else "stratified"
    )
    return metrics



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/drift_sample_size.py
================================================
"""Measure how unlabelled CLEAR drift detection changes with sample size.

All images are embedded once with the frozen pretrained DINOv2 backbone.
Nested, unlabelled random 100/300/500-image samples make detector sensitivity
visible. Year metadata selects the comparison periods; class labels are never
read for sampling or by either detector.
"""

import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch
from transformers import Dinov2Model

from fyp_drift.data.clear100 import open_dataset
from fyp_drift.data.corruptions import drift_transform
from fyp_drift.evaluation.drift_metrics import distribution_shift_metrics
from fyp_drift.evaluation.drift_verification import (
    extract_features,
    loader,
)
from fyp_drift.models.vision_transformer import BACKBONE

DEFAULT_SIZES = (100, 300, 500)
CONTROLLED = ("motion_blur", "realistic_mix")
YEARS = (2009, 2012, 2013, 2014)


def parse_args(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--data-root", type=Path, default=Path("data"))
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--sizes", nargs="+", type=int, default=DEFAULT_SIZES)
    parser.add_argument("--permutations", type=int, default=499)
    parser.add_argument("--batch-size", type=int, default=32)
    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=42)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    if args.output.exists():
        parser.error("--output already exists")
    if not args.sizes or any(size < 20 for size in args.sizes):
        parser.error("Each size must be at least 20")
    if len(set(args.sizes)) != len(args.sizes):
        parser.error("Sample sizes must be unique")
    if args.permutations < 19:
        parser.error("At least 19 permutations are required")
    return args


def unlabelled_indices(subset, year, count, seed):
    """Draw a fixed random order using only year metadata and image indices."""
    candidates = torch.where(subset.metadata_array[:, 0] == year - 2002)[0].tolist()
    if len(candidates) < count:
        raise ValueError(f"Year {year} has only {len(candidates)} images")
    np.random.default_rng(seed + year).shuffle(candidates)
    return candidates[:count]


def _select(features, maximum_indices, wanted_indices):
    positions = np.searchsorted(maximum_indices, wanted_indices)
    if not np.array_equal(maximum_indices[positions], wanted_indices):
        raise ValueError("Nested sample is outside the extracted maximum")
    return features[positions]


def _compare(first, second, *, paired, size, seed, permutations):
    return distribution_shift_metrics(
        first, second, paired=paired, seed=seed,
        permutations=permutations, permutation_limit=size,
    )


def main(argv=None):
    args = parse_args(argv)
    sizes = sorted(args.sizes)
    maximum = sizes[-1]
    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)
    np.random.seed(args.seed)
    device = "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    backbone = Dinov2Model.from_pretrained(BACKBONE).to(device).eval()
    dataset = open_dataset(args.data_root)
    clean = drift_transform("clean", 1, args.seed)
    subset = dataset.get_subset("test_source", transform=clean)

    ordered = {}
    indices = {}
    features = {}
    for year in YEARS:
        count = maximum * (2 if year == 2014 else 1)
        ordered[year] = unlabelled_indices(subset, year, count, args.seed)
        indices[year] = sorted(ordered[year])
        data_loader = loader(
            dataset, "test_source", year, clean, indices[year],
            args.batch_size, 0,
        )
        features[year] = extract_features(
            backbone, data_loader, device, f"clean {year} max sample"
        )
        indices[year] = np.asarray(indices[year])

    adverse = {}
    controlled_indices = sorted(ordered[2014][:maximum])
    for scenario in CONTROLLED:
        data_loader = loader(
            dataset, "test_source", 2014,
            drift_transform(scenario, 3, args.seed), controlled_indices,
            args.batch_size, 0,
        )
        adverse[scenario] = extract_features(
            backbone, data_loader, device, f"{scenario} max sample"
        )
    controlled_indices = np.asarray(controlled_indices)

    comparisons = []
    for size in sizes:
        reference = _select(
            features[2009], indices[2009],
            sorted(ordered[2009][:size]),
        )
        for year in YEARS[1:]:
            future = _select(
                features[year], indices[year],
                sorted(ordered[year][:size]),
            )
            comparisons.append({
                "sample_size_per_group": size,
                "comparison": f"clean_2009_vs_clean_{year}",
                "paired": False,
                **_compare(reference, future, paired=False, size=size,
                           seed=args.seed, permutations=args.permutations),
            })
        first_indices = sorted(ordered[2014][:size])
        second_indices = sorted(ordered[2014][maximum:maximum + size])
        if set(first_indices) & set(second_indices):
            raise ValueError("Split-half null groups overlap")
        first = _select(features[2014], indices[2014], first_indices)
        second = _select(features[2014], indices[2014], second_indices)
        comparisons.append({
            "sample_size_per_group": size,
            "comparison": "clean_2014_disjoint_null",
            "paired": False,
            **_compare(first, second, paired=False, size=size,
                       seed=args.seed, permutations=args.permutations),
        })
        for scenario in CONTROLLED:
            shifted = _select(
                adverse[scenario], controlled_indices, first_indices
            )
            comparisons.append({
                "sample_size_per_group": size,
                "comparison": f"clean_2014_vs_{scenario}",
                "paired": True,
                **_compare(first, shifted, paired=True, size=size,
                           seed=args.seed, permutations=args.permutations),
            })
    report = {
        "dataset": "CLEAR-100 test_source",
        "extractor": BACKBONE,
        "input": "raw images embedded into frozen class-token features",
        "class_labels_used_by_detector": False,
        "class_labels_used_for_sampling": False,
        "sample_sizes_per_group": sizes,
        "permutations": args.permutations,
        "seed": args.seed,
        "interpretation": (
            "Non-detection at one sample size does not establish no drift. "
            "MMD and domain AUC describe distributional differences, not "
            "task harm. Unlabelled random sampling can change class mixture, "
            "so a retrospective class-balanced audit may help diagnose that "
            "confound but cannot be assumed in deployment."
        ),
        "comparisons": comparisons,
    }
    args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    args.output.write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps(comparisons, indent=2), flush=True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/drift_verification.py
================================================
"""Verify natural and controlled image drift with frozen DINOv2 features."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import json
import random
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch
from sklearn.model_selection import train_test_split
from torch.utils.data import DataLoader, Subset
from tqdm import tqdm
from transformers import Dinov2Model

from fyp_drift.data.clear100 import NUM_CLASSES, open_dataset
from fyp_drift.data.corruptions import (
    EVIDENCE_RANKED_MIX_SOURCES,
    REALISTIC_BENCHMARK_SCENARIOS,
    drift_transform,
)
from fyp_drift.evaluation.drift_metrics import (
    DEFAULT_PERMUTATIONS,
    distribution_shift_metrics,
)
from fyp_drift.models.vision_transformer import BACKBONE


def parse_args(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--data-root", default="data")
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--batch-size", type=int, default=32)
    parser.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=0)
    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=42)
    parser.add_argument("--limit", type=int, default=500)
    parser.add_argument("--permutations", type=int, default=DEFAULT_PERMUTATIONS)
    parser.add_argument("--permutation-limit", type=int, default=200)
    parser.add_argument("--reference-year", type=int, default=2009)
    parser.add_argument("--future-years", type=int, nargs="+", default=(2012, 2013, 2014))
    parser.add_argument(
        "--scenarios",
        nargs="+",
        choices=REALISTIC_BENCHMARK_SCENARIOS,
        default=list(REALISTIC_BENCHMARK_SCENARIOS[1:]),
    )
    return parser.parse_args(argv)


def stratified_indices(subset, year, limit, seed):
    year_id = year - 2002
    candidates = torch.where(subset.metadata_array[:, 0] == year_id)[0].tolist()
    if not candidates:
        raise ValueError(f"No CLEAR-100 samples are available for {year}")
    by_class = {}
    for index in candidates:
        by_class.setdefault(int(subset.rows[index]["label"]), []).append(index)
    for label, indices in by_class.items():
        random.Random(seed + 1009 * label).shuffle(indices)
    missing = sorted(set(range(NUM_CLASSES)) - set(by_class))
    if missing:
        raise ValueError(f"CLEAR-100 year {year} is missing classes: {missing}")
    if limit < len(by_class):
        raise ValueError(
            f"Sample limit {limit} cannot represent all {len(by_class)} classes"
        )

    selected = []
    while len(selected) < min(limit, len(candidates)):
        added = False
        for label in sorted(by_class):
            if by_class[label]:
                selected.append(by_class[label].pop())
                added = True
                if len(selected) == min(limit, len(candidates)):
                    break
        if not added:
            break
    return sorted(selected)


def loader(dataset, split, year, transform, indices, batch_size, workers):
    subset = dataset.get_subset(split, transform=transform)
    if indices is None:
        indices = stratified_indices(subset, year, len(subset), 42)
    return DataLoader(
        Subset(subset, indices),
        batch_size=batch_size,
        num_workers=workers,
        shuffle=False,
    )


@torch.inference_mode()
def extract_features(model, data_loader, device, label):
    rows = []
    for images, _, _ in tqdm(data_loader, desc=label, unit="batch"):
        tokens = model(pixel_values=images.to(device)).last_hidden_state[:, 0]
        rows.append(tokens.cpu().numpy())
    return np.concatenate(rows, axis=0)


def comparison_row(group, reference, target, first, second, args, paired):
    metrics = distribution_shift_metrics(
        first,
        second,
        permutations=args.permutations,
        permutation_limit=args.permutation_limit,
        seed=args.seed,
        paired=paired,
    )
    return {
        "group": group,
        "reference": reference,
        "target": target,
        "reference_samples": len(first),
        "target_samples": len(second),
        "paired": paired,
        **metrics,
    }


def write_csv(path, rows):
    with path.open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(handle, fieldnames=list(rows[0]))
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(rows)


def main(argv=None):
    args = parse_args(argv)
    if args.limit < NUM_CLASSES:
        raise ValueError(f"--limit must be at least {NUM_CLASSES}")
    if args.permutations < 19:
        raise ValueError("--permutations must be at least 19")

    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)
    np.random.seed(args.seed)
    device = "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    model = Dinov2Model.from_pretrained(BACKBONE).to(device).eval()
    dataset = open_dataset(args.data_root)
    clean_transform = drift_transform("clean", 1, args.seed)

    # Hold the archive partition constant so this comparison only changes year.
    reference_subset = dataset.get_subset("test_source", transform=clean_transform)
    reference_indices = stratified_indices(
        reference_subset, args.reference_year, args.limit, args.seed
    )
    reference_loader = loader(
        dataset,
        "test_source",
        args.reference_year,
        clean_transform,
        reference_indices,
        args.batch_size,
        args.workers,
    )
    reference_features = extract_features(
        model, reference_loader, device, f"clean {args.reference_year}"
    )

    rows = []
    for year in args.future_years:
        future_subset = dataset.get_subset("test_source", transform=clean_transform)
        indices = stratified_indices(future_subset, year, args.limit, args.seed)
        future_loader = loader(
            dataset,
            "test_source",
            year,
            clean_transform,
            indices,
            args.batch_size,
            args.workers,
        )
        features = extract_features(model, future_loader, device, f"clean {year}")
        rows.append(
            comparison_row(
                "natural_temporal",
                f"clean_{args.reference_year}",
                f"clean_{year}",
                reference_features,
                features,
                args,
                paired=False,
            )
        )

    evaluation_year = max(args.future_years)
    evaluation_subset = dataset.get_subset("test_source", transform=clean_transform)
    evaluation_indices = stratified_indices(
        evaluation_subset, evaluation_year, args.limit, args.seed
    )
    clean_loader = loader(
        dataset,
        "test_source",
        evaluation_year,
        clean_transform,
        evaluation_indices,
        args.batch_size,
        args.workers,
    )
    clean_features = extract_features(
        model, clean_loader, device, f"clean {evaluation_year} reference"
    )

    evaluation_labels = np.asarray(
        [int(evaluation_subset.rows[index]["label"]) for index in evaluation_indices]
    )
    first_indices, second_indices = train_test_split(
        np.arange(len(clean_features)),
        test_size=0.5,
        random_state=args.seed,
        stratify=evaluation_labels,
    )
    first_half = clean_features[first_indices]
    second_half = clean_features[second_indices]
    rows.append(
        comparison_row(
            "null_control",
            f"clean_{evaluation_year}_half_a",
            f"clean_{evaluation_year}_half_b",
            first_half,
            second_half,
            args,
            paired=False,
        )
    )

    severity = 3
    for scenario in args.scenarios:
        shifted_loader = loader(
            dataset,
            "test_source",
            evaluation_year,
            drift_transform(scenario, severity, args.seed),
            evaluation_indices,
            args.batch_size,
            args.workers,
        )
        shifted_features = extract_features(
            model, shifted_loader, device, f"{scenario} severity {severity}"
        )
        rows.append(
            comparison_row(
                "controlled_paired",
                f"clean_{evaluation_year}",
                f"{scenario}_severity_{severity}",
                clean_features,
                shifted_features,
                args,
                paired=True,
            )
        )

    args.output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    payload = {
        "protocol": {
            "feature_extractor": BACKBONE,
            "reference_year": args.reference_year,
            "natural_comparison_source": "held-out test archive for every year",
            "future_years": list(args.future_years),
            "controlled_year": evaluation_year,
            "controlled_severity": severity,
            "realistic_mix_sources": list(EVIDENCE_RANKED_MIX_SOURCES),
            "realistic_mix_selection": {
                "year": 2011,
                "criterion": "mean descending rank across MMD squared and domain-classifier ROC AUC",
                "top_n": len(EVIDENCE_RANKED_MIX_SOURCES),
            },
            "sample_limit": args.limit,
            "permutations": args.permutations,
            "permutation_samples_per_group": args.permutation_limit,
            "seed": args.seed,
            "interpretation": {
                "mmd_squared": "larger values indicate greater feature-distribution separation",
                "mmd_permutation_p": "small values reject exchangeability under the permutation test",
                "mmd_permutation_resolution": "the smallest Monte Carlo p-value allowed by the configured randomisation count",
                "domain_classifier_roc_auc": "0.5 is chance and 1.0 is complete linear separability",
            },
        },
        "comparisons": rows,
    }
    json_path = args.output / "drift_verification.json"
    csv_path = args.output / "drift_verification.csv"
    json_path.write_text(json.dumps(payload, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    write_csv(csv_path, rows)
    print(json.dumps(rows, indent=2))
    print(f"Saved: {json_path}")
    print(f"Saved: {csv_path}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/extra_metrics.py
================================================
"""Extra evaluation metrics beyond accuracy and macro-F1.

  saved  (CPU, from predictions already on disk)
         balanced accuracy, worst-class F1, bottom-quartile class F1, worst-region accuracy (WILDS-style
         continents), clean-to-cloud retention, McNemar tests against learned attention and LoRA rank 8,
         router risk-coverage, route regret and the either-model-correct oracle.
  heads  (GPU, re-trains the attention heads on cached tokens and keeps their outputs)
         expected calibration error, Brier score, negative log-likelihood; box IoU of the top-weighted
         patches; deletion and insertion curves with their areas. Probabilities and patch weights are saved
         per run (npz) for figures and later analysis.

Class-level IoU (Jaccard) is not reported separately: per class it equals F1 / (2 - F1), so it carries no
information beyond macro-F1. Spatial IoU here means attention-vs-object-box overlap.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import json
from collections import defaultdict
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace

import numpy as np
import torch
from scipy.stats import binomtest
from sklearn.metrics import balanced_accuracy_score, f1_score

from fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_dinov2l_study import DEFAULT_MANIFEST, load_rows, log, scores, study_splits, write_json
from fyp_drift.evaluation.saliency_adapters import SPLITS, batches
from fyp_drift.evaluation.saliency_attention_study import ARMS, box_patch_mask, train

import os
RUNS = Path(os.environ.get("FYP_RUNS", "/mnt/sdd/FYP/runs/today"))
TOKENS = Path(os.environ.get("FYP_TOKENS", "/mnt/sdd/FYP/cache/adapter_tokens"))
SEEDS = (42, 43, 44)
CONTINENT_TO_REGION = {"NA": "Americas", "SA": "Americas", "EU": "Europe", "AS": "Asia", "AF": "Africa", "OC": "Oceania"}
GUIDE = {"clean": 0.3, "cloud": 0.1}


def region_of(country):
    try:
        import pycountry_convert as pc
        alpha2 = pc.country_alpha3_to_country_alpha2(country) if len(country) == 3 else country
        return CONTINENT_TO_REGION.get(pc.country_alpha2_to_continent_code(alpha2), "Other")
    except Exception:
        return "Other"


def classification_metrics(labels, predictions, regions):
    present = np.unique(labels)
    per_class = f1_score(labels, predictions, labels=present, average=None, zero_division=0)
    supported = np.array([(labels == c).sum() >= 5 for c in present])
    region_accuracy = {r: float((predictions[regions == r] == labels[regions == r]).mean()) for r in np.unique(regions) if (regions == r).sum() >= 30}
    return {"accuracy": float((predictions == labels).mean()), "macro_f1": float(per_class.mean()),
            "balanced_accuracy": float(balanced_accuracy_score(labels, predictions)),
            "worst_class_f1": float(per_class[supported].min()), "bottom_quartile_class_f1": float(np.sort(per_class)[: max(1, len(per_class) // 4)].mean()),
            "region_accuracy": region_accuracy, "worst_region": min(region_accuracy, key=region_accuracy.get),
            "worst_region_accuracy": min(region_accuracy.values())}


def mcnemar(correct_a, correct_b):
    only_a, only_b = int((correct_a & ~correct_b).sum()), int((~correct_a & correct_b).sum())
    p = binomtest(min(only_a, only_b), only_a + only_b, 0.5).pvalue if only_a + only_b else 1.0
    return {"only_a_correct": only_a, "only_b_correct": only_b, "p_value": float(p)}


def prediction_sources():
    """(method, condition, seed) -> predictions array, for every run saved today."""
    sources = {}
    for condition in ("clean", "cloud"):
        frozen = RUNS / "main/arms" / f"frozen_{condition}.json"
        if frozen.exists():
            for seed in SEEDS:
                sources[("frozen", condition, seed)] = np.array(json.loads(frozen.read_text())["predictions"])
        for seed in SEEDS:
            for mode, lam in (("plain", 0.0), ("uniform", 1.0), ("shuffled", 1.0), ("saliency", 1.0)):
                path = RUNS / "main/arms" / f"{condition}_{mode}_l{lam}_s{seed}.json"
                if path.exists():
                    sources[(f"lora_r8_{mode}", condition, seed)] = np.array(json.loads(path.read_text())["predictions"])
            for rank in (2, 1):
                path = RUNS / "lora_rank_sweep" / f"{condition}_plain_r{rank}_s{seed}.json"
                if path.exists():
                    sources[(f"lora_r{rank}_plain", condition, seed)] = np.array(json.loads(path.read_text())["predictions"])
            for arm in ARMS:
                path = RUNS / "saliency_attention" / f"{condition}_{arm}_s{seed}.json"
                if path.exists():
                    sources[(f"head_{arm}", condition, seed)] = np.array(json.loads(path.read_text())["predictions"])
            for arm in ("input", "gated"):
                path = RUNS / "saliency_router" / f"{condition}_{arm}_s{seed}_route_log.csv"
                if path.exists():
                    with open(path) as handle:
                        sources[(f"router_{arm}", condition, seed)] = np.array([int(r["final_pred"]) for r in csv.DictReader(handle)])
    return sources


def risk_coverage(path, labels):
    with open(path) as handle:
        rows = list(csv.DictReader(handle))
    old, new = np.array([int(r["historical_pred"]) for r in rows]), np.array([int(r["updated_pred"]) for r in rows])
    p, routed = np.array([float(r["p_update"]) for r in rows]), np.array([int(r["routed"]) for r in rows])
    disagree = np.flatnonzero(old != new)
    order = disagree[np.argsort(-p[disagree])]
    curve = []
    for fraction in np.linspace(0, 1, 11):
        chosen = order[: round(fraction * len(order))]
        final = old.copy()
        final[chosen] = new[chosen]
        curve.append({"coverage": float(len(chosen) / len(labels)), "accuracy": float((final == labels).mean())})
    final = np.where(routed == 1, new, old)
    return {"curve": curve, "area_accuracy_over_disagreement_fraction": float(np.mean([c["accuracy"] for c in curve])),
            "historical_accuracy": float((old == labels).mean()), "updated_accuracy": float((new == labels).mean()),
            "routed_accuracy": float((final == labels).mean()), "route_regret_points": float(100 * ((new == labels).mean() - (final == labels).mean())),
            "oracle_accuracy": float(((old == labels) | (new == labels)).mean())}


def run_saved(output):
    rows, _ = load_rows(DEFAULT_MANIFEST)
    test = study_splits(rows)["future_test"]
    labels = np.array([r["class_id"] for r in test])
    regions = np.array([region_of(r["country"]) for r in test])
    log("regions", {r: int((regions == r).sum()) for r in np.unique(regions)}, "sample country codes", sorted({r["country"] for r in test})[:8])
    sources = prediction_sources()
    per_run = {f"{m}/{c}/s{s}": classification_metrics(labels, p, regions) for (m, c, s), p in sources.items()}
    table = {}
    for method in sorted({m for m, _, _ in sources}):
        for condition in ("clean", "cloud"):
            runs = [per_run[f"{method}/{condition}/s{s}"] for s in SEEDS if f"{method}/{condition}/s{s}" in per_run]
            if runs:
                table[f"{method}/{condition}"] = {k: float(np.mean([r[k] for r in runs])) for k in ("accuracy", "macro_f1", "balanced_accuracy", "worst_class_f1", "bottom_quartile_class_f1", "worst_region_accuracy")} | {"seeds": len(runs), "worst_region": runs[0]["worst_region"]}
    retention = {}
    for method in sorted({m for m, _, _ in sources}):
        drops = [per_run[f"{method}/clean/s{s}"]["macro_f1"] - per_run[f"{method}/cloud/s{s}"]["macro_f1"] for s in SEEDS
                 if f"{method}/clean/s{s}" in per_run and f"{method}/cloud/s{s}" in per_run]
        if drops:
            retention[method] = {"clean_minus_cloud_macro_f1_points": float(100 * np.mean(drops)), "seeds": len(drops)}
    tests = []
    for (method, condition, seed), predictions in sources.items():
        for reference in ("head_attention", "lora_r8_plain"):
            other = sources.get((reference, condition, seed))
            if other is not None and method != reference:
                tests.append({"method": method, "reference": reference, "condition": condition, "seed": seed, **mcnemar(predictions == labels, other == labels)})
    routers = {}
    for path in sorted((RUNS / "saliency_router").glob("*_route_log.csv")):
        routers[path.name.replace("_route_log.csv", "")] = risk_coverage(path, labels)
    write_json(output / "saved_metrics.json", {"table": table, "retention": retention, "mcnemar": tests, "router": routers, "per_run": per_run})
    for k, v in table.items():
        log(k, {m: round(x, 4) if isinstance(x, float) else x for m, x in v.items()})
    log("retention", json.dumps(retention))


def calibration(probabilities, labels, bins=15):
    confidence, predicted = probabilities.max(1), probabilities.argmax(1)
    correct = (predicted == labels).astype(float)
    edges, ece = np.linspace(0, 1, bins + 1), 0.0
    for low, high in zip(edges[:-1], edges[1:]):
        inside = (confidence > low) & (confidence <= high)
        if inside.any():
            ece += inside.mean() * abs(correct[inside].mean() - confidence[inside].mean())
    onehot = np.eye(probabilities.shape[1])[labels]
    return {"ece": float(ece), "brier": float(((probabilities - onehot) ** 2).sum(1).mean()),
            "nll": float(-np.log(np.clip(probabilities[np.arange(len(labels)), labels], 1e-12, 1)).mean())}


@torch.no_grad()
def head_outputs(model, tokens, labels, box_masks):
    model.eval()
    generator = torch.Generator().manual_seed(7)
    probabilities, weights = [], []
    deletion, insertion = defaultdict(list), defaultdict(list)
    fractions_delete, fractions_insert = np.round(np.arange(0.0, 1.0, 0.1), 1), np.round(np.arange(0.1, 1.01, 0.1), 1)
    for x, y in batches(tokens, labels, 256):
        logits, w, _ = model(x, generator)
        probabilities.append(logits.softmax(1).cpu())
        weights.append(w.cpu())
        order = w.argsort(1, descending=True)
        for f in fractions_delete:
            keep = torch.ones_like(w).scatter_(1, order[:, : round(f * w.shape[1])], 0.0)
            kept = w * keep
            deletion[f].append((model.classify(x, kept / kept.sum(1, keepdim=True).clamp_min(1e-8)).argmax(1) == y).cpu())
        for f in fractions_insert:
            keep = torch.zeros_like(w).scatter_(1, order[:, : round(f * w.shape[1])], 1.0)
            kept = w * keep
            insertion[f].append((model.classify(x, kept / kept.sum(1, keepdim=True).clamp_min(1e-8)).argmax(1) == y).cpu())
    probabilities, weights = torch.cat(probabilities).numpy(), torch.cat(weights)
    deletion = {float(f): float(torch.cat(v).float().mean()) for f, v in deletion.items()}
    insertion = {float(f): float(torch.cat(v).float().mean()) for f, v in insertion.items()}
    boxes = torch.stack(box_masks)
    k_box = boxes.sum(1).long()
    order = weights.argsort(1, descending=True)
    ranks = torch.empty_like(order)
    ranks.scatter_(1, order, torch.arange(weights.shape[1]).expand_as(order).contiguous())
    top_box = ranks < k_box[:, None]
    top_quarter = ranks < weights.shape[1] // 4
    iou = lambda top: ((top & boxes).sum(1).float() / (top | boxes).sum(1).float()).mean().item()
    return probabilities, weights, {"deletion_curve": deletion, "insertion_curve": insertion,
                                    "deletion_area": float(np.mean(list(deletion.values()))), "insertion_area": float(np.mean(list(insertion.values()))),
                                    "box_iou_top_k_equal_box": iou(top_box), "box_iou_top_quarter": iou(top_quarter)}


def run_heads(output, epochs):
    rows, classes = load_rows(DEFAULT_MANIFEST)
    splits = study_splits(rows)
    test = splits["future_test"]
    labels = np.array([r["class_id"] for r in test])
    box_masks = [box_patch_mask(r) for r in test]
    head_state = torch.load(RUNS / "main/head.pt", weights_only=True)
    width = head_state["weight"].shape[1]
    train_args = SimpleNamespace(epochs=epochs, batch=128, lr=1e-3)
    heads_dir = output / "heads"
    heads_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    for condition in ("clean", "cloud"):
        data = {s: (np.load(TOKENS / f"{condition}_{s}.npy", mmap_mode="r"), np.array([r["class_id"] for r in splits[s]])) for s in SPLITS}
        for seed in SEEDS:
            for arm in ARMS:
                stem = f"{condition}_{arm}_s{seed}"
                if (heads_dir / f"{stem}.json").exists():
                    continue
                model, best = train(arm, seed, head_state, width, classes, data, GUIDE[condition], train_args)
                probabilities, weights, extra = head_outputs(model, *data["future_test"], box_masks)
                predictions = probabilities.argmax(1)
                np.savez_compressed(heads_dir / f"{stem}.npz", probabilities=probabilities.astype(np.float16), weights=weights.numpy().astype(np.float16))
                result = {"arm": arm, "condition": condition, "seed": seed, "best_epoch": best["epoch"],
                          "future_test": scores(labels, predictions, classes), **calibration(probabilities.astype(np.float64), labels), **extra}
                write_json(heads_dir / f"{stem}.json", result)
                log(f"{stem}: f1={result['future_test']['macro_f1']:.4f} ece={result['ece']:.3f} iou_k={result['box_iou_top_k_equal_box']:.3f} "
                    f"del_area={result['deletion_area']:.3f} ins_area={result['insertion_area']:.3f}")
    runs = [json.loads(p.read_text()) for p in sorted(heads_dir.glob("*.json"))]
    table = {}
    for condition in ("clean", "cloud"):
        for arm in ARMS:
            rs = [r for r in runs if (r["condition"], r["arm"]) == (condition, arm)]
            if rs:
                table[f"{condition}/{arm}"] = {k: float(np.mean([r[k] for r in rs])) for k in ("ece", "brier", "nll", "box_iou_top_k_equal_box", "box_iou_top_quarter", "deletion_area", "insertion_area")} | {
                    "macro_f1": float(np.mean([r["future_test"]["macro_f1"] for r in rs])), "seeds": len(rs)}
    write_json(output / "head_metrics.json", table)
    log("head summary", json.dumps(table, indent=1))


def main(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    parser.add_argument("part", choices=("saved", "heads"))
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, default=RUNS / "extra_metrics")
    parser.add_argument("--epochs", type=int, default=15)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    args.output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    run_saved(args.output) if args.part == "saved" else run_heads(args.output, args.epochs)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/fmow_dinov2l_study.py

"""DINOv2-L FMoW study: harm gate, cloud calibration and matched anchoring arms.

Contract
- Splits are fixed by location group (country:UTM): historical 2010-13 train/val,
  adapt 2014-15 train/val, and the future 2016-17 test half used by study B.
- Every choice (head checkpoint, cloud alpha, anchor lambda) uses historical-val or
  adapt-val. Future-test labels are read only to score finished models.
- Arms differ only in the patch weights of the feature anchor:
  plain (no anchor), uniform, shuffled (saliency-sized random patches) and saliency
  (top-40% true-class evidence of the frozen model).

Run as `python -m fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_dinov2l_study --output DIR`. Each stage
writes its result file and is skipped when that file exists, so a run can resume.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import os
import hashlib
import json
import math
import time
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch
from PIL import Image
from sklearn.metrics import accuracy_score, balanced_accuracy_score, f1_score
from sklearn.model_selection import GroupShuffleSplit
from torch import nn
from torch.nn import functional as F
from torch.utils.data import DataLoader, Dataset

Image.MAX_IMAGE_PIXELS = None
MEAN = torch.tensor([0.485, 0.456, 0.406])[:, None, None]
STD = torch.tensor([0.229, 0.224, 0.225])[:, None, None]
PERIODS = ("historical_2010_13", "adapt_2014_15", "future_2016_17")
# FYP_ANCHOR_MODES="" trains plain LoRA only (v2 time budget); default keeps all three anchors
ANCHOR_MODES = tuple(m for m in os.environ.get("FYP_ANCHOR_MODES", "uniform,shuffled,saliency").split(",") if m)
SALIENT_FRACTION = 0.4
# v2 run settings come from the environment so every downstream script picks them up unchanged.
DEFAULT_MANIFEST = Path(os.environ.get("FYP_MANIFEST", "/mnt/sdd/FYP/data/fmow_v1.1/subset_7k_country_matched/manifest_downloaded.csv"))
DEFAULT_CACHE = Path(os.environ.get("FYP_CACHE", "/mnt/sdd/FYP/cache/fmow21k_u8_224.npy"))
DEFAULT_METADATA = Path(os.environ.get("FYP_METADATA", "/mnt/sdd/FYP/data/fmow_v1.1/subset_7k_country_matched/acquisition_metadata.csv"))
IMAGE_SIZE = int(os.environ.get("FYP_IMG", "224"))      # input side in pixels (multiple of 14)
CROP_CONTEXT = float(os.environ.get("FYP_CTX", "0"))     # 0 = whole scene; k = square crop of k x the object box


def log(*parts):
    print(time.strftime("%H:%M:%S"), *parts, flush=True)


def write_json(path, obj):
    temporary = Path(f"{path}.tmp")
    temporary.write_text(json.dumps(obj, indent=2, default=float))
    temporary.replace(path)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- data
def load_rows(manifest, metadata=DEFAULT_METADATA):
    """Downloaded manifest rows with a dense class id, row index, location group and, when present, acquisition metadata."""
    with open(manifest, newline="", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        rows = [r for r in csv.DictReader(handle) if r.get("local_path") and Path(r["local_path"]).exists()]
    if Path(metadata).exists():
        with open(metadata, newline="", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
            acquisition = {m["key"]: m for m in csv.DictReader(handle)}
        for row in rows:
            extra = acquisition.get(row["key"], {})
            row.update({k: float(v) for k, v in extra.items() if k != "key" and v not in ("", None)})
    if CROP_CONTEXT > 0:
        apply_object_crop(rows, Path(manifest).with_name("image_sizes.json"))
    classes = sorted({r["category"] for r in rows})
    class_id = {name: i for i, name in enumerate(classes)}
    for i, row in enumerate(rows):
        row.update(class_id=class_id[row["category"]], idx=i, group=f"{row['country']}:{row['utm']}")
    return rows, len(classes)


def apply_object_crop(rows, sizes_path):
    """Square crop of CROP_CONTEXT x the object box (at least a quarter of the shorter side), kept inside the image.

    Box fields are rewritten as fractions of the crop, so cloud placement and grounding tests stay aligned.
    """
    sizes = json.loads(sizes_path.read_text()) if sizes_path.exists() else {}
    missing = [r for r in rows if r["key"] not in sizes]
    for r in missing:
        with Image.open(r["local_path"]) as image:
            sizes[r["key"]] = image.size
    if missing:
        sizes_path.write_text(json.dumps(sizes))
    for r in rows:
        width, height = sizes[r["key"]]
        bw, bh, cx, cy = r["box_w"] * width, r["box_h"] * height, r["box_cx"] * width, r["box_cy"] * height
        side = min(max(max(bw, bh) * CROP_CONTEXT, 0.25 * min(width, height)), min(width, height))
        x0 = min(max(cx - side / 2, 0), width - side)
        y0 = min(max(cy - side / 2, 0), height - side)
        r["crop"] = (x0, y0, x0 + side, y0 + side)
        r.update(box_cx=(cx - x0) / side, box_cy=(cy - y0) / side, box_w=min(1.0, bw / side), box_h=min(1.0, bh / side))


def split_by_group(rows, held_out_fraction, seed):
    groups = [r["group"] for r in rows]
    kept, held = next(GroupShuffleSplit(n_splits=1, test_size=held_out_fraction, random_state=seed).split(rows, groups=groups))
    return [rows[i] for i in kept], [rows[i] for i in held]


def study_splits(rows):
    by_period = {p: sorted((r for r in rows if r["period"] == p), key=lambda r: r["local_path"]) for p in PERIODS}
    hist_train, hist_val = split_by_group(by_period[PERIODS[0]], 0.15, 42)
    adapt_train, adapt_val = split_by_group(by_period[PERIODS[1]], 0.1, 42)
    _, future_test = split_by_group(by_period[PERIODS[2]], 0.5, 43)
    return {"hist_train": hist_train, "hist_val": hist_val, "adapt_train": adapt_train,
            "adapt_val": adapt_val, "future_test": future_test, "future_all": by_period[PERIODS[2]]}


def image_cache(rows, path, workers):
    """uint8 224x224 RGB array indexed by row idx; decoded once, then memory-mapped."""
    path = Path(path)
    finished = path.with_suffix(".done")
    if finished.exists():
        return np.load(path, mmap_mode="r")
    cache = np.lib.format.open_memmap(path, mode="w+", dtype=np.uint8, shape=(len(rows), IMAGE_SIZE, IMAGE_SIZE, 3))

    class Decode(Dataset):
        def __len__(self):
            return len(rows)

        def __getitem__(self, i):
            with Image.open(rows[i]["local_path"]) as image:
                if "crop" in rows[i]:
                    image = image.convert("RGB").crop(tuple(int(round(v)) for v in rows[i]["crop"]))
                else:
                    image.draft("RGB", (2 * IMAGE_SIZE, 2 * IMAGE_SIZE))
                pixels = np.asarray(image.convert("RGB").resize((IMAGE_SIZE, IMAGE_SIZE), Image.BICUBIC), dtype=np.uint8)
            return i, torch.from_numpy(pixels.copy())

    for ids, images in DataLoader(Decode(), batch_size=64, num_workers=workers):
        cache[ids.numpy()] = images.numpy()
    cache.flush()
    finished.write_text("ok")
    return np.load(path, mmap_mode="r")


CLOUD_REGIONS = ("off_object", "on_object")


def cloud_mask(key, seed=42, size=IMAGE_SIZE):
    """Main-run cloud: the top 20% of a smoothed per-image noise field."""
    digest = hashlib.sha256(f"{seed}:{key}".encode()).digest()
    noise = torch.rand((1, 1, 16, 16), generator=torch.Generator().manual_seed(int.from_bytes(digest[:8], "little")))
    smooth = F.avg_pool2d(noise, 5, stride=1, padding=2)
    mask = (smooth >= torch.quantile(smooth, 0.8)).float()
    return F.interpolate(mask, size=(size, size), mode="bilinear", align_corners=False)[0]


def add_cloud(image, key, alpha, seed=42):
    """Blend the main-run near-white cloud into a normalized image; same key, same cloud."""
    mask = cloud_mask(key, seed, image.shape[-1])
    white = (1 - MEAN) / STD
    return image * (1 - alpha * mask) + white * (alpha * mask)


def fractal_field(key, size=IMAGE_SIZE, octaves=5, seed=42):
    """Multi-octave value noise in [0, 1], the usual cumulus-like texture for simulated clouds."""
    generator = torch.Generator().manual_seed(int.from_bytes(hashlib.sha256(f"{seed}:{key}:fbm".encode()).digest()[:8], "little"))
    field = torch.zeros(1, 1, size, size)
    for octave in range(octaves):
        grid = torch.rand((1, 1, 4 * 2 ** octave, 4 * 2 ** octave), generator=generator)
        field += 0.5 ** octave * F.interpolate(grid, size=(size, size), mode="bicubic", align_corners=False)
    field = field[0, 0]
    return (field - field.min()) / (field.max() - field.min())


def object_box_mask(row, size=IMAGE_SIZE, margin=0.1):
    """1 inside the image's labelled object box (plus a margin), 0 elsewhere."""
    mask = torch.zeros(size, size)
    half_w, half_h = (row["box_w"] / 2 + margin) * size, (row["box_h"] / 2 + margin) * size
    cx, cy = row["box_cx"] * size, row["box_cy"] * size
    mask[max(0, int(cy - half_h)):min(size, int(cy + half_h) + 1), max(0, int(cx - half_w)):min(size, int(cx + half_w) + 1)] = 1
    return mask


def cumulus_opacity(row, region, size=IMAGE_SIZE, clouds=6, seed=42):
    """Opacity of a few puffy cumulus clouds: soft round envelopes carrying fractal texture.

    Cloud centres are drawn outside the labelled object box for off_object and inside it
    for on_object, so clouds keep natural rounded edges instead of being clipped to the box.
    """
    generator = torch.Generator().manual_seed(int.from_bytes(hashlib.sha256(f"{seed}:{row['key']}:cumulus".encode()).digest()[:8], "little"))
    box = object_box_mask(row, size).bool()
    candidates = (~box if region == "off_object" else box).nonzero()
    if len(candidates) == 0:
        return torch.zeros(size, size)
    ys, xs = torch.meshgrid(torch.arange(size).float(), torch.arange(size).float(), indexing="ij")
    envelope = torch.zeros(size, size)
    for _ in range(clouds):
        cy, cx = candidates[torch.randint(len(candidates), (1,), generator=generator)].float()[0]
        radius = 6 + 10 * torch.rand(1, generator=generator).item()
        for _ in range(4):  # irregular lobes around each centre, as in cumulus tops
            ly, lx = cy + radius * torch.randn(1, generator=generator).item(), cx + radius * torch.randn(1, generator=generator).item()
            ry, rx = (radius * (0.5 + torch.rand(2, generator=generator))).tolist()
            envelope = torch.maximum(envelope, torch.exp(-((ys - ly) ** 2 / (2 * ry ** 2) + (xs - lx) ** 2 / (2 * rx ** 2))))
    texture = fractal_field(row["key"], size, seed=seed)
    return ((envelope * (0.4 + 1.2 * texture) - 0.5) / 0.3).clamp(0, 1)


def shifted(field, dy, dx):
    """Translate a 2-D field by (dy, dx) pixels, filling uncovered pixels with zero (no wrap-around)."""
    out = torch.zeros_like(field)
    h, w = field.shape
    out[max(0, dy):h + min(0, dy), max(0, dx):w + min(0, dx)] = field[max(0, -dy):h - max(0, dy), max(0, -dx):w - max(0, dx)]
    return out


def realistic_cloud(pixels, row, strength, region, seed=42):
    """Composite cumulus clouds with thin edges and a sun-consistent shadow onto [0, 1] pixels.

    The shadow is the cloud opacity displaced away from the recorded sun azimuth by an
    offset that grows as the recorded sun elevation falls, and it darkens the ground beneath.
    """
    opacity = cumulus_opacity(row, region, pixels.shape[-1], seed=seed) * strength
    elevation = math.radians(max(5.0, float(row["sun_elevation"])))
    offset = min(40.0, 14.0 / math.tan(elevation)) * pixels.shape[-1] / 224
    azimuth = math.radians(float(row["sun_azimuth"]))
    dx, dy = int(round(-offset * math.sin(azimuth))), int(round(offset * math.cos(azimuth)))
    shadow = shifted(opacity, dy, dx) * (1 - opacity)
    shaded = pixels * (1 - 0.6 * shadow)
    brightness = 0.88 + 0.12 * fractal_field(row["key"] + ":shade", pixels.shape[-1], seed=seed)
    cloud_colour = torch.stack([0.96 * brightness, 0.97 * brightness, brightness])
