    return shaded * (1 - opacity) + cloud_colour * opacity


class CachedImages(Dataset):
    """Cached images, optionally with the main-run cloud (region None) or a realistic regional cloud."""

    def __init__(self, cache, rows, cloud_alpha, cloud_region=None):
        self.cache, self.rows, self.cloud_alpha, self.cloud_region = cache, rows, cloud_alpha, cloud_region

    def __len__(self):
        return len(self.rows)

    def __getitem__(self, i):
        row = self.rows[i]
        pixels = torch.from_numpy(np.array(self.cache[row["idx"]])).permute(2, 0, 1).float() / 255.0
        if self.cloud_alpha > 0 and self.cloud_region:
            pixels = realistic_cloud(pixels, row, self.cloud_alpha, self.cloud_region)
        image = (pixels - MEAN) / STD
        if self.cloud_alpha > 0 and not self.cloud_region:
            image = add_cloud(image, row["local_path"], self.cloud_alpha)
        return image, row["class_id"], i


def image_loader(cache, rows, batch_size, workers, cloud_alpha=0.0, shuffle_seed=None, cloud_region=None):
    """Evaluation order when shuffle_seed is None; otherwise a seeded shuffle that drops the last batch."""
    shuffle = shuffle_seed is not None
    generator = torch.Generator().manual_seed(shuffle_seed) if shuffle else None
    return DataLoader(CachedImages(cache, rows, cloud_alpha, cloud_region), batch_size=batch_size, shuffle=shuffle, drop_last=shuffle,
                      num_workers=workers, pin_memory=True, generator=generator)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- model
class LoRALinear(nn.Module):
    """Frozen linear layer plus a zero-initialised low-rank update that can be switched off."""

    def __init__(self, base, rank):
        super().__init__()
        self.base = base
        self.down = nn.Linear(base.in_features, rank, bias=False)
        self.up = nn.Linear(rank, base.out_features, bias=False)
        nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.down.weight, a=math.sqrt(5))
        nn.init.zeros_(self.up.weight)
        self.enabled = True

    def forward(self, x):
        output = self.base(x)
        return output + 2.0 * self.up(self.down(x)) if self.enabled else output


class Backbone(nn.Module):
    """DINOv2 whose first blocks run without gradients and whose last blocks carry LoRA.

    With adapters disabled the model is exactly the frozen backbone, so the anchor
    reference reuses the same weights and the same early-block activations.
    """

    def __init__(self, name, trainable_blocks=0, rank=8):
        super().__init__()
        from transformers import Dinov2Model
        self.model = Dinov2Model.from_pretrained(name)
        self.model.requires_grad_(False)
        self.width = self.model.config.hidden_size
        blocks = list(self.model.encoder.layer)
        self.frozen_blocks, self.adapted_blocks = blocks[:len(blocks) - trainable_blocks], blocks[len(blocks) - trainable_blocks:]
        self.adapters = []
        for block in self.adapted_blocks:
            attention = block.attention.attention
            sites = [(attention, "query"), (attention, "key"), (attention, "value"),
                     (block.attention.output, "dense"), (block.mlp, "fc1"), (block.mlp, "fc2")]
            for parent, name_ in sites:
                adapter = LoRALinear(getattr(parent, name_), rank)
                setattr(parent, name_, adapter)
                self.adapters.append(adapter)

    def use_adapters(self, enabled):
        for adapter in self.adapters:
            adapter.enabled = enabled

    def early_features(self, images):
        with torch.no_grad():
            hidden = self.model.embeddings(images)
            for block in self.frozen_blocks:
                hidden = _block_output(block, hidden)
        return hidden

    def late_features(self, hidden):
        for block in self.adapted_blocks:
            hidden = _block_output(block, hidden)
        return self.model.layernorm(hidden)

    def forward(self, images):
        return self.late_features(self.early_features(images))


def _block_output(block, hidden):
    output = block(hidden)
    return output[0] if isinstance(output, (tuple, list)) else output


def patch_mean(tokens):
    return tokens[:, 1:].float().mean(1)


def scores(labels, predictions, classes):
    counts = np.bincount(predictions, minlength=classes)
    return {"samples": int(len(labels)), "accuracy": float(accuracy_score(labels, predictions)),
            "macro_f1": float(f1_score(labels, predictions, labels=np.arange(classes), average="macro", zero_division=0)),
            "balanced_accuracy": float(balanced_accuracy_score(labels, predictions)),
            "unique_predictions": int((counts > 0).sum()), "max_class_share": float(counts.max() / max(1, len(predictions)))}


@torch.no_grad()
def evaluate(backbone, head, data, classes):
    backbone.eval()
    labels, predictions = [], []
    for images, targets, _ in data:
        with torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=torch.float16):
            tokens = backbone(images.cuda(non_blocking=True))
        predictions.append(head(patch_mean(tokens)).argmax(1).cpu())
        labels.append(targets)
    labels, predictions = torch.cat(labels).numpy(), torch.cat(predictions).numpy()
    return scores(labels, predictions, classes), predictions


def cluster_bootstrap(values, groups, repetitions=2000, seed=0):
    """Mean of per-image values with a 95% interval from resampling whole location groups."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    unique = np.unique(groups)
    by_group = {g: values[groups == g] for g in unique}
    draws = [np.concatenate([by_group[g] for g in rng.choice(unique, len(unique))]).mean() for _ in range(repetitions)]
    return {"mean": float(values.mean()), "ci95": [float(np.percentile(draws, 2.5)), float(np.percentile(draws, 97.5))]}


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- stages
def frozen_features(args, cache, rows):
    path = args.output / "features.npz"
    if not path.exists():
        backbone = Backbone(args.backbone).cuda().eval()
        means = []
        with torch.no_grad():
            for images, _, _ in image_loader(cache, rows, args.eval_batch, args.workers):
                with torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=torch.float16):
                    means.append(patch_mean(backbone(images.cuda(non_blocking=True))).half().cpu())
        np.savez(path, mean=torch.cat(means).numpy())
        del backbone
        torch.cuda.empty_cache()
    return np.load(path)["mean"].astype(np.float32)


def fit_linear_head(features, labels, classes, val_features, val_labels, epochs=300):
    """Class-weighted linear head; keeps the checkpoint with the best validation macro-F1."""
    torch.manual_seed(42)
    x, y = torch.tensor(features).cuda(), torch.tensor(labels).cuda()
    xv = torch.tensor(val_features).cuda()
    counts = torch.bincount(y, minlength=classes).float().clamp_min(1)
    head = nn.Linear(x.shape[1], classes).cuda()
    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(head.parameters(), lr=1e-3, weight_decay=1e-4)
    best_f1, best_state = -1.0, None
    for epoch in range(epochs):
        for batch in torch.randperm(len(x), device="cuda").split(512):
            loss = F.cross_entropy(head(x[batch]), y[batch], weight=counts.sum() / (classes * counts))
            optimizer.zero_grad()
            loss.backward()
            optimizer.step()
        if epoch % 10 != 9:
            continue
        with torch.no_grad():
            val_f1 = f1_score(val_labels, head(xv).argmax(1).cpu().numpy(), average="macro", zero_division=0)
        if val_f1 > best_f1:
            best_f1, best_state = val_f1, {k: v.clone() for k, v in head.state_dict().items()}
    head.load_state_dict(best_state)
    return head.eval(), best_f1


def drift_statistics(features, index, permutations):
    """MMD and domain AUC on 1,000 frozen features per group, with a historical split-half null."""
    from fyp_drift.evaluation.drift_metrics import distribution_shift_metrics
    rng = np.random.default_rng(42)
    historical = features[rng.choice(np.concatenate([index["hist_train"], index["hist_val"]]), 2000, replace=False)]
    future = features[rng.choice(index["future_all"], 1000, replace=False)]
    adapt = features[rng.choice(np.concatenate([index["adapt_train"], index["adapt_val"]]), 1000, replace=False)]
    pairs = {"historical_vs_future": (historical[:1000], future), "historical_vs_adapt": (historical[:1000], adapt),
             "historical_split_half_null": (historical[:1000], historical[1000:])}
    results = []
    for name, (first, second) in pairs.items():
        metrics = distribution_shift_metrics(first, second, permutations=permutations, permutation_limit=1000, seed=42)
        results.append({"comparison": name, **{k: v for k, v in metrics.items() if not isinstance(v, (list, dict))}})
    return results


def run_harm_gate(args, features, splits, classes):
    path = args.output / "gate.json"
    if path.exists():
        return json.loads(path.read_text()), torch.load(args.output / "head.pt", weights_only=True)
    index = {k: np.array([r["idx"] for r in v]) for k, v in splits.items()}
    labels = {k: np.array([r["class_id"] for r in v]) for k, v in splits.items()}
    head, val_f1 = fit_linear_head(features[index["hist_train"]], labels["hist_train"], classes,
                                   features[index["hist_val"]], labels["hist_val"])
    torch.save(head.state_dict(), args.output / "head.pt")
    with torch.no_grad():
        predicted = {k: head(torch.tensor(features[index[k]]).cuda()).argmax(1).cpu().numpy() for k in index}
    window_scores = {k: scores(labels[k], predicted[k], classes) for k in ("hist_val", "adapt_val", "adapt_train", "future_test", "future_all")}
    gap = accuracy_gap(splits, predicted, labels)
    gate = {"backbone": args.backbone, "head_hist_val_macro_f1": val_f1, "scores": window_scores,
            "drift": drift_statistics(features, index, args.permutations),
            "accuracy_gap_hist_val_minus_future": gap, "counts": {k: len(v) for k, v in splits.items()},
            "harm_gate_passed": bool(gap["mean"] >= 0.03 and gap["ci95"][0] > 0)}
    write_json(path, gate)
    return gate, head.state_dict()


def accuracy_gap(splits, predicted, labels, repetitions=1000):
    """Historical-val minus future-test accuracy, resampling location groups in each set independently."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
    sides = []
    for name in ("hist_val", "future_test"):
        groups = np.array([r["group"] for r in splits[name]])
        correct = (predicted[name] == labels[name]).astype(float)
        sides.append((np.unique(groups), {g: correct[groups == g] for g in np.unique(groups)}))
    draws = []
    for _ in range(repetitions):
        means = [np.concatenate([by_group[g] for g in rng.choice(unique, len(unique))]).mean() for unique, by_group in sides]
        draws.append(means[0] - means[1])
    return {"mean": float(np.mean(draws)), "ci95": [float(np.percentile(draws, 2.5)), float(np.percentile(draws, 97.5))]}


def calibrate_cloud(args, cache, splits, head_state, classes):
    """Pick the cloud alpha whose historical-val macro-F1 drop is closest to 7.5 points."""
    path = args.output / "calib.json"
    if path.exists():
        return json.loads(path.read_text())
    backbone = Backbone(args.backbone).cuda().eval()
    head = nn.Linear(backbone.width, classes).cuda()
    head.load_state_dict(head_state)
    candidates = []
    for alpha in (0.0, 0.2, 0.35, 0.5, 0.7, 0.85):
        metrics, _ = evaluate(backbone, head, image_loader(cache, splits["hist_val"], args.eval_batch, args.workers, alpha), classes)
        candidates.append({"alpha": alpha, **metrics})
    clean_f1 = candidates[0]["macro_f1"]
    chosen = min(candidates[1:], key=lambda c: abs((clean_f1 - c["macro_f1"]) - 0.075))
    drop = clean_f1 - chosen["macro_f1"]
    result = {"candidates": candidates, "selected_alpha": chosen["alpha"], "selected_drop": drop,
              "within_5_10_points": bool(0.05 <= drop <= 0.10)}
    write_json(path, result)
    del backbone
    torch.cuda.empty_cache()
    return result


def anchor_weights(reference_tokens, head, labels, mode, generator):
    """Per-patch anchor weights summing to one for each image."""
    patch_count = reference_tokens.shape[1]
    if mode == "uniform":
        return torch.full(reference_tokens.shape[:2], 1.0 / patch_count, device=reference_tokens.device)
    evidence = (reference_tokens * head.weight[labels][:, None, :]).sum(-1)
    salient = round(SALIENT_FRACTION * patch_count)
    weights = torch.zeros_like(evidence).scatter_(1, evidence.topk(salient, dim=1).indices, 1.0 / salient)
    if mode == "shuffled":
        weights = weights.gather(1, torch.rand(weights.shape, generator=generator).argsort(1).to(weights.device))
    return weights


def train_arm(args, cache, splits, head, classes, mode, anchor_lambda, cloud_alpha, seed, extra_tests=None):
    torch.manual_seed(seed)
    np.random.seed(seed)
    backbone = Backbone(args.backbone, args.trainable_blocks, args.rank).cuda()
    parameters = [p for p in backbone.parameters() if p.requires_grad]
    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(parameters, lr=args.lr, weight_decay=0.01)
    scaler = torch.amp.GradScaler("cuda")
    generator = torch.Generator().manual_seed(seed + 7)
    accumulation = max(1, args.effective_batch // args.batch)
    step0 = step_zero_gap(backbone, head, cache, splits, cloud_alpha)
    history = []
    for epoch in range(args.epochs):
        backbone.train()
        losses = {"ce": 0.0, "anchor": 0.0}
        batches = image_loader(cache, splits["adapt_train"], args.batch, args.workers, cloud_alpha, shuffle_seed=seed + epoch)
        optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
        for step, (images, targets, _) in enumerate(batches):
            images, targets = images.cuda(non_blocking=True), targets.cuda(non_blocking=True)
            with torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=torch.float16):
                early = backbone.early_features(images)
                tokens = backbone.late_features(early)[:, 1:].float()
                ce = F.cross_entropy(head(tokens.mean(1)), targets)
                anchor = anchor_penalty(backbone, head, early, tokens, targets, mode, generator) if mode != "plain" else torch.zeros((), device="cuda")
                loss = (ce + anchor_lambda * anchor) / accumulation
            scaler.scale(loss).backward()
            if (step + 1) % accumulation == 0:
                scaler.step(optimizer)
                scaler.update()
                optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
            losses["ce"] += float(ce)
            losses["anchor"] += float(anchor)
        val, _ = evaluate(backbone, head, image_loader(cache, splits["adapt_val"], args.eval_batch, args.workers, cloud_alpha), classes)
        history.append({"epoch": epoch + 1, "ce": losses["ce"] / (step + 1), "anchor_mse": losses["anchor"] / (step + 1), "adapt_val": val})
        log(f"  {mode} lambda={anchor_lambda} alpha={cloud_alpha} seed={seed} epoch={epoch + 1} val_f1={val['macro_f1']:.4f}")
    test, predictions = evaluate(backbone, head, image_loader(cache, splits["future_test"], args.eval_batch, args.workers, cloud_alpha), classes)
    shifted = {}
    for name, (alpha, region) in (extra_tests or {}).items():
        metrics, shifted_predictions = evaluate(backbone, head, image_loader(cache, splits["future_test"], args.eval_batch, args.workers, alpha, cloud_region=region), classes)
        shifted[name] = {"future_test": metrics, "predictions": shifted_predictions.tolist()}
    peak_gib = torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated() / 2**30
    del backbone, optimizer
    torch.cuda.empty_cache()
    torch.cuda.reset_peak_memory_stats()
    return {"mode": mode, "lambda": anchor_lambda, "cloud_alpha": cloud_alpha, "seed": seed, "step0_max_logit_diff": step0,
            "trainable_parameters": int(sum(p.numel() for p in parameters)), "history": history, "adapt_val": history[-1]["adapt_val"],
            "future_test": test, "predictions": predictions.tolist(), "shifted_tests": shifted, "peak_gpu_gib": peak_gib}


def anchor_penalty(backbone, head, early, tokens, targets, mode, generator):
    """Weighted squared distance between adapted and frozen patch tokens."""
    with torch.no_grad():
        backbone.use_adapters(False)
        reference = backbone.late_features(early)[:, 1:].float()
        backbone.use_adapters(True)
        weights = anchor_weights(reference, head, targets, mode, generator)
    return (weights * (tokens - reference).square().mean(-1)).sum(1).mean()


def step_zero_gap(backbone, head, cache, splits, cloud_alpha):
    """Largest logit difference between adapters off and freshly initialised adapters on (should be 0)."""
    images = next(iter(image_loader(cache, splits["adapt_val"][:8], 8, 0, cloud_alpha)))[0].cuda()
    with torch.no_grad(), torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=torch.float16):
        backbone.use_adapters(False)
        frozen = head(patch_mean(backbone(images)))
        backbone.use_adapters(True)
        adapted = head(patch_mean(backbone(images)))
    return float((frozen - adapted).abs().max())


def run_arms(args, cache, splits, head_state, classes, cloud_alpha):
    head =nn.Linear(head_state["weight"].shape[1], classes).cuda().eval()
    head.load_state_dict(head_state)
    head.requires_grad_(False)
    arms = args.output / "arms"
    arms.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    conditions = {"clean": 0.0, "cloud": cloud_alpha}
    score_frozen_model(args, cache, splits, head, classes, conditions, arms)
    for seed in args.seeds:
        for condition, alpha in conditions.items():
            for mode, anchor_lambda in [("plain", 0.0)] + [(m, l) for m in ANCHOR_MODES for l in args.lambdas]:
                path = arms / f"{condition}_{mode}_l{anchor_lambda}_s{seed}.json"
                if path.exists():
                    continue
                started = time.time()
                result = train_arm(args, cache, splits, head, classes, mode, anchor_lambda, alpha, seed)
                write_json(path, {**result, "condition": condition, "seconds": time.time() - started})
                log(f"done {path.name}: test_f1={result['future_test']['macro_f1']:.4f} peak={result['peak_gpu_gib']:.1f}GiB")


def score_frozen_model(args, cache, splits, head, classes, conditions, arms):
    backbone = Backbone(args.backbone).cuda().eval()
    for condition, alpha in conditions.items():
        path = arms / f"frozen_{condition}.json"
        if path.exists():
            continue
        metrics, predictions = evaluate(backbone, head, image_loader(cache, splits["future_test"], args.eval_batch, args.workers, alpha), classes)
        write_json(path, {"mode": "frozen", "condition": condition, "future_test": metrics, "predictions": predictions.tolist()})
    del backbone
    torch.cuda.empty_cache()


def summarize(args, splits):
    """Lambda per (condition, mode) chosen on mean adapt-val F1; paired deltas on future-test."""
    results = [json.loads(p.read_text()) for p in sorted((args.output / "arms").glob("*_s*.json"))]
    labels = np.array([r["class_id"] for r in splits["future_test"]])
    groups = np.array([r["group"] for r in splits["future_test"]])
    chosen = {}
    for condition in ("clean", "cloud"):
        for mode in ANCHOR_MODES:
            by_lambda = {}
            for r in results:
                if r["condition"] == condition and r["mode"] == mode:
                    by_lambda.setdefault(r["lambda"], []).append(r["adapt_val"]["macro_f1"])
            if by_lambda:
                chosen[(condition, mode)] = max(by_lambda, key=lambda l: np.mean(by_lambda[l]))

    def selected(condition, mode, seed):
        anchor_lambda = 0.0 if mode == "plain" else chosen.get((condition, mode))
        return next((r for r in results if (r["condition"], r["mode"], r["seed"], r["lambda"]) == (condition, mode, seed, anchor_lambda)), None)

    means, paired = {}, []
    for condition in ("clean", "cloud"):
        for mode in ("plain",) + ANCHOR_MODES:
            runs = [selected(condition, mode, s) for s in args.seeds if selected(condition, mode, s)]
            if runs:
                f1s = [r["future_test"]["macro_f1"] for r in runs]
                means[f"{condition}/{mode}"] = {"lambda": runs[0]["lambda"], "seeds": len(f1s), "mean_test_macro_f1": float(np.mean(f1s)),
                                                "sd": float(np.std(f1s, ddof=1)) if len(f1s) > 1 else None}
        for a, b in (("saliency", "uniform"), ("saliency", "shuffled"), ("saliency", "plain"), ("uniform", "plain")):
            for seed in args.seeds:
                first, second = selected(condition, a, seed), selected(condition, b, seed)
                if first and second:
                    accuracy_difference = (np.array(first["predictions"]) == labels).astype(float) - (np.array(second["predictions"]) == labels).astype(float)
                    paired.append({"condition": condition, "a": a, "b": b, "seed": seed,
                                   "delta_macro_f1": first["future_test"]["macro_f1"] - second["future_test"]["macro_f1"],
                                   "delta_accuracy": cluster_bootstrap(accuracy_difference, groups)})
    table = [{"condition": r["condition"], "mode": r["mode"], "lambda": r["lambda"], "seed": r["seed"],
              "adapt_val_f1": r["adapt_val"]["macro_f1"], **{f"test_{k}": r["future_test"][k] for k in ("macro_f1", "accuracy", "unique_predictions")}}
             for r in results]
    write_json(args.output / "summary.json", {"chosen_lambda": {f"{c}/{m}": l for (c, m), l in chosen.items()},
                                              "means": means, "paired": paired, "all_runs": table})
    return means


def calibrate_region_clouds(args, cache, splits, head, classes):
    """Per region, the cloud strength whose frozen historical-val macro-F1 drop is closest to 7.5 points."""
    path = args.output / "calib_regions.json"
    if path.exists():
        return json.loads(path.read_text())
    missing = [r["key"] for r in splits["hist_val"] + splits["future_test"] if "sun_elevation" not in r or "box_cx" not in r]
    if missing:
        raise RuntimeError(f"{len(missing)} images lack acquisition metadata; run fyp_drift.data.fmow_metadata first")
    backbone = Backbone(args.backbone).cuda().eval()
    result = {}
    for region in ("off_object", "on_object"):
        candidates = []
        for alpha in (0.0, 0.4, 0.6, 0.8, 1.0):
            metrics, _ = evaluate(backbone, head, image_loader(cache, splits["hist_val"], args.eval_batch, args.workers, alpha, cloud_region=region), classes)
            candidates.append({"alpha": alpha, **metrics})
        clean_f1 = candidates[0]["macro_f1"]
        chosen = min(candidates[1:], key=lambda c: abs((clean_f1 - c["macro_f1"]) - 0.075))
        result[region] = {"candidates": candidates, "selected_alpha": chosen["alpha"], "selected_drop": clean_f1 - chosen["macro_f1"]}
        log("region calib", region, result[region]["selected_alpha"], round(result[region]["selected_drop"], 4))
    write_json(path, result)
    del backbone
    torch.cuda.empty_cache()
    return result


def run_unseen_cloud_arms(args, cache, splits, head_state, classes):
    """Adapt on clean images with each arm's adapt-val lambda, then score on unseen realistic clouds off and on the object."""
    head = nn.Linear(head_state["weight"].shape[1], classes).cuda().eval()
    head.load_state_dict(head_state)
    head.requires_grad_(False)
    chosen = json.loads((args.output / "summary.json").read_text())["chosen_lambda"]
    regions = calibrate_region_clouds(args, cache, splits, head, classes)
    tests = {region: (regions[region]["selected_alpha"], region) for region in regions}
    out = args.output / "unseen_cloud"
    out.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    backbone = Backbone(args.backbone).cuda().eval()
    for name, (alpha, region) in tests.items():
        path = out / f"frozen_{name}.json"
        if not path.exists():
            metrics, predictions = evaluate(backbone, head, image_loader(cache, splits["future_test"], args.eval_batch, args.workers, alpha, cloud_region=region), classes)
            write_json(path, {"mode": "frozen", "condition": name, "future_test": metrics, "predictions": predictions.tolist()})
    del backbone
    torch.cuda.empty_cache()
    for seed in args.seeds:
        for mode in ("plain",) + ANCHOR_MODES:
            anchor_lambda = 0.0 if mode == "plain" else float(chosen.get(f"clean/{mode}", 1.0))  # v1 chose 1.0 for every anchor
            path = out / f"{mode}_l{anchor_lambda}_s{seed}.json"
            if path.exists():
                continue
            result = train_arm(args, cache, splits, head, classes, mode, anchor_lambda, 0.0, seed, extra_tests=tests)
            write_json(path, {**result, "condition": "clean_adapt_unseen_cloud"})
            log(f"done {path.name}: clean={result['future_test']['macro_f1']:.4f} " + " ".join(f"{k}={v['future_test']['macro_f1']:.4f}" for k, v in result["shifted_tests"].items()))
    summarize_unseen(args, splits)


def summarize_unseen(args, splits):
    labels = np.array([r["class_id"] for r in splits["future_test"]])
    groups = np.array([r["group"] for r in splits["future_test"]])
    runs = [json.loads(p.read_text()) for p in sorted((args.output / "unseen_cloud").glob("*_s*.json"))]
    frozen = {p.stem.replace("frozen_", ""): json.loads(p.read_text())["future_test"] for p in (args.output / "unseen_cloud").glob("frozen_*.json")}
    means, paired = {}, []
    tests = ("clean",) + tuple(runs[0]["shifted_tests"]) if runs else ()
    for test in tests:
        def f1(run):
            return run["future_test"]["macro_f1"] if test == "clean" else run["shifted_tests"][test]["future_test"]["macro_f1"]

        def predictions(run):
            return np.array(run["predictions"] if test == "clean" else run["shifted_tests"][test]["predictions"])
        for mode in ("plain",) + ANCHOR_MODES:
            values = [f1(r) for r in runs if r["mode"] == mode]
            if values:
                means[f"{test}/{mode}"] = {"seeds": len(values), "mean_test_macro_f1": float(np.mean(values)), "sd": float(np.std(values, ddof=1)) if len(values) > 1 else None}
        for a, b in (("saliency", "uniform"), ("saliency", "shuffled"), ("saliency", "plain")):
            for seed in args.seeds:
                first = next((r for r in runs if r["mode"] == a and r["seed"] == seed), None)
                second = next((r for r in runs if r["mode"] == b and r["seed"] == seed), None)
                if first and second:
                    difference = (predictions(first) == labels).astype(float) - (predictions(second) == labels).astype(float)
                    paired.append({"test": test, "a": a, "b": b, "seed": seed, "delta_macro_f1": f1(first) - f1(second),
                                   "delta_accuracy": cluster_bootstrap(difference, groups)})
    write_json(args.output / "summary_unseen_cloud.json", {"frozen": frozen, "means": means, "paired": paired})
    log("unseen-cloud summary", json.dumps(means, indent=1))


def parse_args(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    parser.add_argument("--manifest", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_MANIFEST)
    parser.add_argument("--cache", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_CACHE)
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--backbone", default="facebook/dinov2-large")
    parser.add_argument("--stages", nargs="+", choices=("gate", "calib", "arms", "summary", "unseen_cloud"), default=["gate", "calib", "arms", "summary"])
    parser.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=4)
    parser.add_argument("--eval-batch", type=int, default=64)
    parser.add_argument("--batch", type=int, default=16)
    parser.add_argument("--effective-batch", type=int, default=32)
    parser.add_argument("--epochs", type=int, default=2)
    parser.add_argument("--lr", type=float, default=1e-4)
    parser.add_argument("--rank", type=int, default=8)
    parser.add_argument("--trainable-blocks", type=int, default=6)
    parser.add_argument("--lambdas", type=float, nargs="+", default=[1.0, 10.0])
    parser.add_argument("--seeds", type=int, nargs="+", default=[42, 43, 44])
    parser.add_argument("--permutations", type=int, default=499)
    parser.add_argument("--limit", type=int, default=0, help="cap every split for an execution check")
    return parser.parse_args(argv)


def main(argv=None):
    args = parse_args(argv)
    args.output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    write_json(args.output / "config.json", {k: str(v) for k, v in vars(args).items()})
    rows, classes = load_rows(args.manifest)
    splits = study_splits(rows)
    if args.limit:
        splits = {k: v[:args.limit] for k, v in splits.items()}
    cache = image_cache(rows, args.cache, args.workers)
    gate, head_state = run_harm_gate(args, frozen_features(args, cache, rows), splits, classes)
    log("gate", gate["harm_gate_passed"], gate["accuracy_gap_hist_val_minus_future"])
    if "calib" in args.stages or "arms" in args.stages:
        cloud = calibrate_cloud(args, cache, splits, head_state, classes)
    if "arms" in args.stages:
        run_arms(args, cache, splits, head_state, classes, cloud["selected_alpha"])
    if "summary" in args.stages:
        log("summary", json.dumps(summarize(args, splits), indent=1))
    if "unseen_cloud" in args.stages:
        run_unseen_cloud_arms(args, cache, splits, head_state, classes)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/fmow_layer_selection.py
================================================
"""Choose DINOv2 blocks using frozen-head patch evidence and target images."""

from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np

from fyp_drift.evaluation.drift_metrics import maximum_mean_discrepancy
from fyp_drift.training.saliency_lora_pilot import LoRALinear


GROUPS = {"E": range(0, 4), "M": range(4, 8), "L": range(8, 12)}


def evidence_weights(scores: np.ndarray, mode: str, seed: int, keep_fraction: float = 0.4) -> np.ndarray:
    """Equal-area saliency and shuffled controls; rows are images, columns patches."""
    scores = np.asarray(scores)
    if scores.ndim != 2 or not 0 < keep_fraction <= 1:
        raise ValueError("scores must be N x P and keep_fraction must be in (0, 1]")
    n, patches = scores.shape
    if mode == "uniform":
        return np.full((n, patches), 1 / patches, dtype=np.float32)
    if mode not in ("saliency", "shuffled"):
        raise ValueError(mode)
    count = max(1, round(keep_fraction * patches))
    indices = np.argpartition(-scores, count - 1, axis=1)[:, :count]
    weights = np.zeros((n, patches), dtype=np.float32)
    np.put_along_axis(weights, indices, 1 / count, axis=1)
    if mode == "shuffled":
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        weights = np.take_along_axis(weights, np.argsort(rng.random(weights.shape), axis=1), axis=1)
    return weights


def layer_features(tokens: np.ndarray, weights: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """Pool each block's patch tokens with one fixed map per image."""
    tokens = np.asarray(tokens, dtype=np.float32)
    weights = np.asarray(weights, dtype=np.float32)
    if tokens.ndim != 4 or tokens.shape[0] != len(weights) or tokens.shape[2] != weights.shape[1]:
        raise ValueError("expected tokens N x 12 x P x D and weights N x P")
    return np.einsum("np,nlpd->nld", weights, tokens, optimize=True)


def profile(source_tokens, target_tokens, source_scores, target_scores, seed=42) -> dict:
    """MMD profile and locked E/M/L pick for each weighting mode."""
    result = {}
    for mode in ("saliency", "uniform", "shuffled"):
        source = layer_features(source_tokens, evidence_weights(source_scores, mode, seed))
        target = layer_features(target_tokens, evidence_weights(target_scores, mode, seed + 1))
        layer_mmd = [maximum_mean_discrepancy(source[:, i], target[:, i])[0]
                     for i in range(source.shape[1])]
        if len(layer_mmd) != 12:
            raise ValueError("expected twelve DINOv2 blocks")
        group_scores = {name: float(np.mean([layer_mmd[i] for i in indices]))
                        for name, indices in GROUPS.items()}
        pick = max(GROUPS, key=lambda name: group_scores[name])
        result[mode] = {"layer_mmd_squared": layer_mmd, "group_scores": group_scores, "pick": pick}
    return result


def add_group_lora(backbone, group: str, rank: int = 8, alpha: int = 16) -> int:
    """Freeze DINOv2 and train query/key/value/projection/MLP LoRA plus block norms."""
    if group not in (*GROUPS, "ALL"):
        raise ValueError(group)
    for parameter in backbone.parameters():
        parameter.requires_grad = False
    indices = range(12) if group == "ALL" else GROUPS[group]
    blocks = backbone.encoder.layer
    if len(blocks) != 12:
        raise ValueError("expected a 12-block DINOv2 model")
    for index in indices:
        block = blocks[index]
        attention = block.attention.attention
        for name in ("query", "key", "value"):
            setattr(attention, name, LoRALinear(getattr(attention, name), rank, alpha))
        block.attention.output.dense = LoRALinear(block.attention.output.dense, rank, alpha)
        block.mlp.fc1 = LoRALinear(block.mlp.fc1, rank, alpha)
        block.mlp.fc2 = LoRALinear(block.mlp.fc2, rank, alpha)
        for name in ("norm1", "norm2"):
            for parameter in getattr(block, name).parameters():
                parameter.requires_grad = True
    return sum(parameter.numel() for parameter in backbone.parameters() if parameter.requires_grad)



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/fmow_saliency_layer_pilot.py
================================================
"""Small FMoW pilot: use patch evidence to choose which ViT blocks to adapt.

The diagnostic sees only historical images and unlabeled future_a. All E/M/L
arms use the same label-free objective. Future_test labels are opened only for
the final score. This is an architecture pilot, not the full study protocol.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import copy
import json
import random
import subprocess
import time
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch
from PIL import Image
from sklearn.metrics import accuracy_score, f1_score, recall_score
from sklearn.model_selection import GroupShuffleSplit
from torch import nn
from torch.nn import functional as F
from torch.utils.data import DataLoader, Dataset
from torchvision.transforms import functional as TVF
from tqdm import tqdm
from transformers import Dinov2Model

from fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_layer_selection import add_group_lora, profile
from fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_temporal_pilot import (
    FMoWImages, limit_rows, load_manifest,
)
from fyp_drift.data.clear100 import MEAN, STD
from fyp_drift.models.vision_transformer import BACKBONE


class UnlabeledImages(Dataset):
    """Keep future_a labels out of the diagnostic and adaptation loaders."""

    def __init__(self, rows):
        self.rows = rows

    def __len__(self):
        return len(self.rows)

    def __getitem__(self, index):
        with Image.open(self.rows[index]["local_path"]) as opened:
            opened.draft("RGB", (224, 224))
            image = opened.convert("RGB")
        return TVF.normalize(TVF.to_tensor(TVF.resize(image, (224, 224))), MEAN, STD)


def divide_groups(rows, test_fraction, seed):
    groups = [f"{row['country']}:{row['utm']}" for row in rows]
    train, test = next(GroupShuffleSplit(n_splits=1, test_size=test_fraction,
                                         random_state=seed).split(rows, groups=groups))
    left, right = [rows[i] for i in train], [rows[i] for i in test]
    assert not ({groups[i] for i in train} & {groups[i] for i in test})
    return left, right


def loader(rows, batch_size, workers, labeled):
    dataset = FMoWImages(rows) if labeled else UnlabeledImages(rows)
    return DataLoader(dataset, batch_size=batch_size, shuffle=False,
                      num_workers=workers, pin_memory=torch.cuda.is_available())


@torch.inference_mode()
def head_features(backbone, data, device):
    features, labels = [], []
    backbone.eval()
    for images, target, _ in tqdm(data, desc="head features", leave=False):
        with torch.autocast(device_type=device, enabled=device == "cuda"):
            tokens = backbone(pixel_values=images.to(device)).last_hidden_state[:, 1:]
        features.append(tokens.mean(1).float().cpu())
        labels.append(target)
    return torch.cat(features), torch.cat(labels)


def fit_head(train_features, train_labels, val_features, val_labels, classes, seed):
    torch.manual_seed(seed)
    head = nn.Linear(train_features.shape[1], classes)
    counts = torch.bincount(train_labels, minlength=classes).float()
    weights = 1 / counts.clamp_min(1)
    weights /= weights[counts > 0].mean()
    weights = weights.clamp_max(10)
    weights[counts == 0] = 0
    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(head.parameters(), lr=1e-3, weight_decay=1e-4)
    best, best_state, stale = -1.0, None, 0
    generator = torch.Generator().manual_seed(seed)
    for _ in range(30):
        head.train()
        for indices in torch.randperm(len(train_labels), generator=generator).split(256):
            logits = head(train_features[indices])
            loss = F.cross_entropy(logits, train_labels[indices], weight=weights)
            optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
            loss.backward()
            optimizer.step()
        head.eval()
        with torch.no_grad():
            prediction = head(val_features).argmax(1).numpy()
        score = f1_score(val_labels.numpy(), prediction, labels=np.arange(classes),
                         average="macro", zero_division=0)
        if score > best:
            best, best_state, stale = score, copy.deepcopy(head.state_dict()), 0
        else:
            stale += 1
            if stale >= 5:
                break
    head.load_state_dict(best_state)
    return head, float(best)


@torch.inference_mode()
def diagnostic_features(backbone, head, data, device):
    blocks, scores = [], []
    backbone.eval()
    head.eval()
    for images in tqdm(data, desc="diagnostic features", leave=False):
        with torch.autocast(device_type=device, enabled=device == "cuda"):
            output = backbone(pixel_values=images.to(device), output_hidden_states=True)
        final = output.last_hidden_state[:, 1:].float()
        logits = head(final.mean(1))
        predicted = logits.argmax(1)
        weights = head.weight.to(device)[predicted]
        evidence = torch.einsum("bpd,bd->bp", final, weights) / final.shape[1]
        reconstructed = evidence.sum(1) + head.bias.to(device)[predicted]
        chosen_logit = logits.gather(1, predicted[:, None]).squeeze(1)
        if not torch.allclose(reconstructed, chosen_logit, atol=1e-4, rtol=1e-4):
            raise AssertionError("patch evidence does not reconstruct the head logit")
        layer_tokens = torch.stack([state[:, 1:] for state in output.hidden_states[1:]], dim=1)
        blocks.append(layer_tokens.half().cpu().numpy())
        scores.append(evidence.cpu().numpy())
    return np.concatenate(blocks), np.concatenate(scores)


def adapt(backbone, head, data, device, epochs, lr):
    backbone.train()
    head.eval()
    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW((p for p in backbone.parameters() if p.requires_grad),
                                  lr=lr, weight_decay=0.01)
    scaler = torch.amp.GradScaler("cuda", enabled=device == "cuda")
    history = []
    for epoch in range(epochs):
        losses = []
        for images in tqdm(data, desc=f"adapt epoch {epoch + 1}", leave=False):
            with torch.autocast(device_type=device, enabled=device == "cuda"):
                tokens = backbone(pixel_values=images.to(device)).last_hidden_state[:, 1:]
                logits = head(tokens.mean(1).float())
                probabilities = logits.softmax(1)
                entropy = -(probabilities * probabilities.clamp_min(1e-8).log()).sum(1).mean()
                mean_prob = probabilities.mean(0)
                diversity = (mean_prob * mean_prob.clamp_min(1e-8).log()).sum()
                loss = entropy + diversity
            optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
            scaler.scale(loss).backward()
            scaler.step(optimizer)
            scaler.update()
            losses.append(float(loss.detach()))
        history.append({"epoch": epoch + 1, "loss": float(np.mean(losses))})
    return history


@torch.inference_mode()
def evaluate(backbone, head, data, device):
    backbone.eval()
    head.eval()
    labels, predictions = [], []
    for images, target, _ in tqdm(data, desc="future_test", leave=False):
        with torch.autocast(device_type=device, enabled=device == "cuda"):
            tokens = backbone(pixel_values=images.to(device)).last_hidden_state[:, 1:]
        logits = head(tokens.mean(1).float()).cpu()
        labels.extend(target.tolist())
        predictions.extend(logits.argmax(1).tolist())
    truth, predicted = np.asarray(labels), np.asarray(predictions)
    classes = np.arange(head.out_features)
    metrics = {"samples": len(truth),
               "accuracy": float(accuracy_score(truth, predicted)),
               "macro_f1": float(f1_score(truth, predicted, labels=classes,
                                          average="macro", zero_division=0)),
               "balanced_accuracy": float(recall_score(truth, predicted, labels=classes,
                                                        average="macro", zero_division=0)),
               "per_class_f1": f1_score(truth, predicted, labels=classes,
                                        average=None, zero_division=0).tolist()}
    return metrics, predictions


def save_json(path, payload):
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path.write_text(json.dumps(payload, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")


def main(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--manifest", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, default=Path("results/fmow-saliency-layer-pilot"))
    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=42)
    parser.add_argument("--limit-per-period", type=int, default=0,
                        help="smoke-test limit; 0 uses the full manifest")
    parser.add_argument("--diagnostic-limit", type=int, default=256)
    parser.add_argument("--test-limit", type=int, default=0,
                        help="smoke-test limit; 0 uses all future_test images")
    parser.add_argument("--batch-size", type=int, default=8)
    parser.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=2)
    parser.add_argument("--epochs", type=int, default=2)
    parser.add_argument("--lr", type=float, default=1e-4)
    parser.add_argument("--force", action="store_true")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    if not torch.cuda.is_available():
        parser.error("this pilot requires a GPU")
    if args.batch_size < 1 or args.epochs < 1 or args.diagnostic_limit < 2:
        parser.error("invalid batch size, epochs or diagnostic limit")
    device = "cuda"
    random.seed(args.seed)
    np.random.seed(args.seed)
    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)
    revision = subprocess.check_output(["git", "rev-parse", "HEAD"], text=True).strip()
    config = {**vars(args), "manifest": str(args.manifest), "output": str(args.output),
              "git_head": revision, "backbone": BACKBONE}
    rows, categories = load_manifest(args.manifest)
    periods = {period: sorted((row for row in rows if row["period"] == period),
                              key=lambda row: row["local_path"])
               for period in ("historical_2010_13", "future_2016_17")}
    if args.limit_per_period:
        periods = {name: limit_rows(group, args.limit_per_period, args.seed)
                   for name, group in periods.items()}
    hist_train, hist_val = divide_groups(periods["historical_2010_13"], 0.1, args.seed)
    future_a, future_test = divide_groups(periods["future_2016_17"], 0.5, args.seed + 1)
    if args.test_limit:
        future_test = future_test[:args.test_limit]
    if min(map(len, (hist_train, hist_val, future_a, future_test))) < 2:
        parser.error("each split needs at least two images")
    args.output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    save_json(args.output / "config.json", {**config, "split_counts": {
        "hist_train": len(hist_train), "hist_val": len(hist_val),
        "future_a": len(future_a), "future_test": len(future_test)}})

    backbone = Dinov2Model.from_pretrained(BACKBONE).to(device).eval()
    for parameter in backbone.parameters():
        parameter.requires_grad = False
    head_path = args.output / "head.pt"
    if head_path.exists() and not args.force:
        head = nn.Linear(backbone.config.hidden_size, len(categories))
        head.load_state_dict(torch.load(head_path, map_location="cpu", weights_only=True))
    else:
        start = time.time()
        train_x, train_y = head_features(backbone, loader(hist_train, args.batch_size, args.workers, True), device)
        val_x, val_y = head_features(backbone, loader(hist_val, args.batch_size, args.workers, True), device)
        head, val_f1 = fit_head(train_x, train_y, val_x, val_y, len(categories), args.seed)
        torch.save(head.state_dict(), head_path)
        save_json(args.output / "head.json", {"seed": args.seed, "git_head": revision,
                   "validation_macro_f1": val_f1, "seconds": time.time() - start})
    head = head.to(device).eval()
    for parameter in head.parameters():
        parameter.requires_grad = False

    diagnostic_path = args.output / "diagnostic_pick.json"
    if diagnostic_path.exists() and not args.force:
        diagnostic = json.loads(diagnostic_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["profile"]
    else:
        start = time.time()
        rng = np.random.default_rng(args.seed)
        hist_rows = [hist_train[i] for i in rng.choice(len(hist_train),
                     min(args.diagnostic_limit, len(hist_train)), replace=False)]
        target_rows = [future_a[i] for i in rng.choice(len(future_a),
                       min(args.diagnostic_limit, len(future_a)), replace=False)]
        hist_tokens, hist_scores = diagnostic_features(
            backbone, head, loader(hist_rows, args.batch_size, args.workers, False), device)
        target_tokens, target_scores = diagnostic_features(
            backbone, head, loader(target_rows, args.batch_size, args.workers, False), device)
        diagnostic = profile(hist_tokens, target_tokens, hist_scores, target_scores, args.seed)
        save_json(diagnostic_path, {"seed": args.seed, "git_head": revision,
                   "profile": diagnostic, "seconds": time.time() - start,
                   "source_images": len(hist_rows), "target_images": len(target_rows),
                   "head": "frozen historical patch-mean linear head"})
    print("Locked choices:", {name: entry["pick"] for name, entry in diagnostic.items()}, flush=True)
    del backbone
    torch.cuda.empty_cache()

    test_data = loader(future_test, args.batch_size, args.workers, True)
    target_data = loader(future_a, args.batch_size, args.workers, False)
    for group in ("A0", "E", "M", "L", "ALL"):
        output = args.output / f"{group}.json"
        if output.exists() and not args.force:
            print(f"Skipping existing {output}", flush=True)
            continue
        torch.manual_seed(args.seed)
        model = Dinov2Model.from_pretrained(BACKBONE).to(device)
        for parameter in model.parameters():
            parameter.requires_grad = False
        start = time.time()
        count, history = 0, []
        if group != "A0":
            count = add_group_lora(model, group)
            model.to(device)
            history = adapt(model, head, target_data, device, args.epochs, args.lr)
        metrics, predictions = evaluate(model, head, test_data, device)
        save_json(output, {"seed": args.seed, "git_head": revision, "config": config,
                   "group": group, "trainable_parameters": count, "history": history,
                   "future_test": metrics, "predictions": predictions,
                   "seconds": time.time() - start})
        print(group, metrics, flush=True)
        del model
        torch.cuda.empty_cache()
    counts = [json.loads((args.output / f"{name}.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["trainable_parameters"]
              for name in ("E", "M", "L")]
    if len(set(counts)) != 1:
        raise AssertionError(f"E/M/L trainable parameter counts differ: {counts}")
    scores = {name: json.loads((args.output / f"{name}.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["future_test"]
              for name in ("A0", "E", "M", "L", "ALL")}
    selected = {mode: {"group": item["pick"],
                       "macro_f1": scores[item["pick"]]["macro_f1"]}
                for mode, item in diagnostic.items()}
    save_json(args.output / "summary.json", {"seed": args.seed, "git_head": revision,
              "config": config, "diagnostic_choices": {name: item["pick"] for name, item in diagnostic.items()},
              "selected": selected, "scores": scores})


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/fmow_study_b.py
================================================
"""Matched supervised FMoW adaptation with plain and saliency anchors."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import random
import subprocess
import time
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch
from torch import nn
from torch.nn import functional as F
from torch.utils.data import DataLoader, Dataset
from transformers import Dinov2Model

from fyp_drift.data.clear100 import MEAN, STD
from fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_layer_selection import add_group_lora
from fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_saliency_layer_pilot import divide_groups, evaluate
from fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_temporal_pilot import FMoWImages, load_manifest
from fyp_drift.models.vision_transformer import BACKBONE


class StudyImages(Dataset):
    def __init__(self, rows, shift, alpha, seed):
        self.base = FMoWImages(rows)
        self.rows = rows
        self.shift = shift
        self.alpha = alpha
        self.seed = seed

    def __len__(self):
        return len(self.base)

    def __getitem__(self, index):
        image, label, _ = self.base[index]
        if self.shift == "cloud":
            image = cloud_image(image, self.rows[index]["local_path"], self.alpha, self.seed)
        return image, label, index


def cloud_image(image, path, alpha, seed):
    """Fixed localized near-white overlay; same path gets the same overlay."""
    digest = hashlib.sha256(f"{seed}:{path}".encode()).digest()
    generator = torch.Generator().manual_seed(int.from_bytes(digest[:8], "little"))
    noise = torch.rand((1, 1, 16, 16), generator=generator)
    smooth = F.avg_pool2d(noise, 5, stride=1, padding=2)
    mask = (smooth >= torch.quantile(smooth, 0.8)).float()
    mask = F.interpolate(mask, size=image.shape[-2:], mode="bilinear", align_corners=False)[0]
    white = torch.tensor([(1 - mean) / std for mean, std in zip(MEAN, STD)])[:, None, None]
    return image * (1 - alpha * mask) + white * (alpha * mask)


def data_loader(rows, batch_size, workers, shift, alpha, seed, shuffle=False, epoch=0):
    return DataLoader(StudyImages(rows, shift, alpha, seed), batch_size=batch_size,
                      shuffle=shuffle, num_workers=workers,
                      generator=torch.Generator().manual_seed(seed + epoch),
                      pin_memory=True)


def anchor_weights(reference_tokens, head, mode, seed):
    """All modes sum to one; saliency and shuffled select equal patch counts."""
    predicted = head(reference_tokens.mean(1)).argmax(1)
    evidence = (reference_tokens * head.weight[predicted, None, :]).sum(-1)
    count = round(0.4 * evidence.shape[1])
    selected = evidence.topk(count, dim=1).indices
    weights = torch.zeros_like(evidence).scatter_(1, selected, 1 / count)
    if mode == "uniform":
        weights.fill_(1 / evidence.shape[1])
    elif mode == "shuffled":
        order = torch.rand(evidence.shape, generator=torch.Generator().manual_seed(seed)).argsort(1)
        weights = weights.gather(1, order.to(weights.device))
    elif mode != "saliency":
        raise ValueError(mode)
    return weights


def fit(model, reference, head, rows, val_rows, args, mode, alpha):
    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW((p for p in model.parameters() if p.requires_grad),
                                  lr=args.lr, weight_decay=0.01)
    scaler = torch.amp.GradScaler("cuda")
    history = []
    for epoch in range(args.epochs):
        model.train()
        losses, anchors, entropies, marginals = [], [], [], []
        batches = data_loader(rows, args.batch_size, args.workers, args.shift, alpha,
                              args.seed, shuffle=True, epoch=epoch)
        for batch_id, (images, labels, _) in enumerate(batches):
            images, labels = images.cuda(), labels.cuda()
            with torch.no_grad(), torch.autocast("cuda"):
                old = reference(pixel_values=images).last_hidden_state[:, 1:].float()
                if mode != "plain":
                    weights = anchor_weights(old, head, mode,
                                             args.seed + epoch * 100000 + batch_id)
            with torch.autocast("cuda"):
                new = model(pixel_values=images).last_hidden_state[:, 1:].float()
                logits = head(new.mean(1))
                cross_entropy = F.cross_entropy(logits, labels)
                anchor = (weights * (new - old).square().mean(-1)).sum(1).mean() if mode != "plain" else 0
                loss = cross_entropy + args.anchor_weight * anchor
                probabilities = logits.softmax(1)
                entropy = -(probabilities * probabilities.clamp_min(1e-8).log()).sum(1).mean()
                mean_prob = probabilities.mean(0)
                marginal = -(mean_prob * mean_prob.clamp_min(1e-8).log()).sum()
            optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
            scaler.scale(loss).backward()
            scaler.step(optimizer)
            scaler.update()
            losses.append(float(cross_entropy.detach()))
            anchors.append(float(anchor.detach()) if mode != "plain" else 0.0)
            entropies.append(float(entropy.detach()))
            marginals.append(float(marginal.detach()))
        val, _ = evaluate(model, head, data_loader(val_rows, args.batch_size, args.workers,
                                                  args.shift, alpha, args.seed), "cuda")
        point = {"epoch": epoch + 1, "cross_entropy": float(np.mean(losses)),
                 "anchor_mse": float(np.mean(anchors)),
                 "prediction_entropy": float(np.mean(entropies)),
                 "marginal_entropy": float(np.mean(marginals)),
                 "adapt_val_macro_f1": val["macro_f1"]}
        history.append(point)
        print(mode, point, flush=True)
    return history


def main(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--manifest", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--head", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=42)
    parser.add_argument("--group", choices=("E", "M", "L"), default="E")
    parser.add_argument("--shift", choices=("clean", "cloud"), default="clean")
    parser.add_argument("--cloud-alpha", type=float,
                        help="fixed value; omit to calibrate on historical validation")
    parser.add_argument("--anchor-weight", type=float, default=0.1)
    parser.add_argument("--epochs", type=int, default=2)
    parser.add_argument("--lr", type=float, default=1e-4)
    parser.add_argument("--batch-size", type=int, default=8)
    parser.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=2)
    parser.add_argument("--limit-train", type=int, default=0, help="bounded execution check")
    parser.add_argument("--limit-val", type=int, default=0, help="bounded execution check")
    parser.add_argument("--limit-test", type=int, default=0, help="bounded execution check")
    parser.add_argument("--force", action="store_true")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    if not torch.cuda.is_available():
        parser.error("GPU required")
    if (args.cloud_alpha is not None and not 0 <= args.cloud_alpha <= 1) or args.anchor_weight < 0:
        parser.error("invalid cloud alpha or anchor weight")
    args.output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    revision = subprocess.check_output(["git", "rev-parse", "HEAD"], text=True).strip()
    random.seed(args.seed)
    np.random.seed(args.seed)
    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)
    rows, categories = load_manifest(args.manifest)
    adapt = sorted((r for r in rows if r["period"] == "adapt_2014_15"), key=lambda r: r["local_path"])
    future = sorted((r for r in rows if r["period"] == "future_2016_17"), key=lambda r: r["local_path"])
    historical = sorted((r for r in rows if r["period"] == "historical_2010_13"),
                        key=lambda r: r["local_path"])
    train, val = divide_groups(adapt, 0.1, args.seed)
    _, test = divide_groups(future, 0.5, args.seed + 1)
    if args.limit_train:
        train = train[:args.limit_train]
    if args.limit_val:
        val = val[:args.limit_val]
    if args.limit_test:
        test = test[:args.limit_test]
    head = nn.Linear(384, len(categories)).cuda().eval()
    head.load_state_dict(torch.load(args.head, map_location="cuda", weights_only=True))
    for parameter in head.parameters():
        parameter.requires_grad = False
    reference = Dinov2Model.from_pretrained(BACKBONE).cuda().eval()
    for parameter in reference.parameters():
        parameter.requires_grad = False
    calibration = []
    alpha = 0.0
    if args.shift == "cloud":
        if args.cloud_alpha is None:
            _, hist_val = divide_groups(historical, 0.1, args.seed)
            for candidate in (0.0, 0.2, 0.35, 0.5, 0.7):
                score, _ = evaluate(reference, head,
                                    data_loader(hist_val, args.batch_size, args.workers,
                                                "cloud", candidate, args.seed), "cuda")
                calibration.append({"alpha": candidate, "macro_f1": score["macro_f1"]})
            clean = calibration[0]["macro_f1"]
            alpha = min(calibration[1:], key=lambda item:
                        abs((clean - item["macro_f1"]) - 0.075))["alpha"]
            selected_score = next(item["macro_f1"] for item in calibration if item["alpha"] == alpha)
            if not 0.05 <= clean - selected_score <= 0.10:
                raise RuntimeError("cloud candidates did not produce a 5–10 point historical-validation drop")
            print("cloud calibration", calibration, "selected", alpha, flush=True)
        else:
            alpha = args.cloud_alpha
    settings = {**vars(args), "manifest": str(args.manifest), "head": str(args.head),
                "output": str(args.output), "git_head": revision, "effective_cloud_alpha": alpha,
                "counts": {"adapt_train": len(train), "adapt_val": len(val), "future_test": len(test)},
                "cloud_calibration": calibration}
    config_path = args.output / "config.json"
    if config_path.exists() and not args.force:
        old_settings = json.loads(config_path.read_text())
        if old_settings != settings:
            raise RuntimeError("output directory contains a different configuration")
    config_path.write_text(json.dumps(settings, indent=2))
    test_data = data_loader(test, args.batch_size, args.workers, args.shift, alpha, args.seed)
    for mode in ("frozen", "plain", "uniform", "shuffled", "saliency"):
        path = args.output / f"{mode}.json"
        if path.exists() and not args.force:
            print("skip", path, flush=True)
            continue
        torch.manual_seed(args.seed)
        start = time.time()
        if mode == "frozen":
            model = reference
            count, history = 0, []
        else:
            model = Dinov2Model.from_pretrained(BACKBONE).cuda()
            count = add_group_lora(model, args.group)
            model.cuda()
            model.eval()
            images = next(iter(test_data))[0].cuda()
            with torch.inference_mode(), torch.autocast("cuda"):
                before = head(reference(pixel_values=images).last_hidden_state[:, 1:].mean(1).float())
                after = head(model(pixel_values=images).last_hidden_state[:, 1:].mean(1).float())
            if not torch.allclose(before, after, atol=1e-5, rtol=1e-5):
                raise RuntimeError(f"{mode} failed step-zero equivalence")
            history = fit(model, reference, head, train, val, args, mode, alpha)
        metrics, predictions = evaluate(model, head, test_data, "cuda")
        counts = np.bincount(predictions, minlength=len(categories))
        result = {"mode": mode, "seed": args.seed, "git_head": revision,
                  "trainable_parameters": count, "history": history,
                  "future_test": metrics, "prediction_histogram": counts.tolist(),
                  "predictions": predictions, "seconds": time.time() - start}
        path.write_text(json.dumps(result, indent=2))
        print(mode, {k: v for k, v in metrics.items() if k != "per_class_f1"}, flush=True)
        if mode != "frozen":
            del model
            torch.cuda.empty_cache()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/fmow_temporal_pilot.py
================================================
"""Chronological clean-drift audit and saliency/LoRA pilot on custom FMoW-RGB."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import json
import random
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw
from sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression
from sklearn.metrics import accuracy_score, f1_score, recall_score
from sklearn.model_selection import StratifiedGroupKFold, cross_val_predict
from sklearn.pipeline import make_pipeline
from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler
from torch import nn
from torch.nn import functional as F
from torch.utils.data import DataLoader, Dataset, Subset
from torchvision.transforms import functional as TVF
from tqdm import tqdm
from transformers import Dinov2Model

from fyp_drift.data.clear100 import MEAN, STD
from fyp_drift.data.corruptions import drift_transform
from fyp_drift.evaluation.drift_metrics import distribution_shift_metrics
from fyp_drift.data.temporal_sampling import matched_temporal_sample
from fyp_drift.evaluation.temporal_design import (
    crossed_corruption_grid,
    progressive_corruption_schedule,
)
from fyp_drift.models.vision_transformer import BACKBONE
from fyp_drift.training.saliency_lora_pilot import LoRALinear, teacher_saliency


PERIODS = ("historical_2010_13", "adapt_2014_15", "future_2016_17")
# FMoW includes verified JPEGs above Pillow's conservative pixel limit. Ask the
# JPEG decoder for a reduced-resolution decode before materializing RGB pixels.
Image.MAX_IMAGE_PIXELS = None


class FMoWImages(Dataset):
    def __init__(self, rows, corruption=None, seed=42):
        self.rows = rows
        self.corruption = corruption
        self.seed = seed

    def __len__(self):
        return len(self.rows)

    def __getitem__(self, index):
        row = self.rows[index]
        with Image.open(row["local_path"]) as opened:
            opened.draft("RGB", (224, 224))
            image = opened.convert("RGB")
        if self.corruption:
            name, severity = self.corruption
            image = apply_fmow_corruption(image, name, severity, self.seed, index)
        else:
            image = TVF.normalize(
                TVF.to_tensor(TVF.resize(image, (224, 224))), MEAN, STD
            )
        return image, int(row["class_id"]), index


def load_manifest(path):
    with Path(path).open(newline="", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        rows = list(csv.DictReader(handle))
    categories = sorted({row["category"] for row in rows})
    class_ids = {name: index for index, name in enumerate(categories)}
    for row in rows:
        row["class_id"] = class_ids[row["category"]]
    period_counts = {period: sum(row["period"] == period for row in rows) for period in PERIODS}
    if any(count < 2 for count in period_counts.values()):
        raise ValueError(f"Manifest is missing a required chronological window: {period_counts}")
    if len(categories) != 62:
        raise ValueError(f"Expected 62 FMoW classes, found {len(categories)}")
    return rows, categories


def limit_rows(rows, limit, seed):
    if limit is None or len(rows) <= limit:
        return rows
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    strata = {}
    for row in rows:
        strata.setdefault((row["category"], row["country"]), []).append(row)
    for candidates in strata.values():
        rng.shuffle(candidates)
    selected = []
    while len(selected) < limit:
        progressed = False
        for key in sorted(strata):
            if strata[key] and len(selected) < limit:
                selected.append(strata[key].pop())
                progressed = True
        if not progressed:
            break
    return selected


def make_loader(rows, batch_size, workers, corruption=None, seed=42):
    return DataLoader(
        FMoWImages(rows, corruption, seed),
        batch_size=batch_size,
        shuffle=False,
        num_workers=workers,
        pin_memory=torch.cuda.is_available(),
    )


def apply_fmow_corruption(image, scenario, severity, seed, index):
    """Use ImageNet-C Gaussian levels and project-defined non-noise transforms."""
    if scenario != "gaussian_noise":
        return drift_transform(scenario, severity, seed)(image, index)
    sigma = (0.08, 0.12, 0.18)[severity - 1]
    tensor = TVF.to_tensor(TVF.resize(image.convert("RGB"), (224, 224)))
    generator = torch.Generator().manual_seed((seed + index) % (2**63 - 1))
    noise = torch.randn(tensor.shape, generator=generator, dtype=tensor.dtype)
    return TVF.normalize((tensor + sigma * noise).clamp(0, 1), MEAN, STD)


def save_corruption_examples(rows, output_dir, seed=42, limit=4):
    """Save individual model-resolution clean/corrupted image pairs and a manifest."""
    examples, seen = [], set()
    for row in rows:
        if row["category"] not in seen:
            examples.append(row)
            seen.add(row["category"])
        if len(examples) == limit:
            break
    conditions = (
        ("clean", None),
        ("gaussian_noise_s1", ("gaussian_noise", 1)),
        ("gaussian_noise_s2", ("gaussian_noise", 2)),
        ("gaussian_noise_s3", ("gaussian_noise", 3)),
        ("sensor_noise_s3", ("sensor_noise", 3)),
        ("defocus_blur_s3", ("defocus_blur", 3)),
    )
    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    mean = torch.tensor(MEAN)[:, None, None]
    std = torch.tensor(STD)[:, None, None]
    manifest_rows = []
    for row_index, row in enumerate(examples):
        with Image.open(row["local_path"]) as opened:
            opened.draft("RGB", (224, 224))
            image = TVF.resize(opened.convert("RGB"), (224, 224))
        for title, corruption in conditions:
            stem = f"example{row_index + 1:02d}_{row['category']}_{title}"
            if corruption is None:
                destination = output_dir / f"{stem}.jpg"
                image.save(destination, format="JPEG", quality=95, subsampling=0)
            else:
                tensor = apply_fmow_corruption(image, *corruption, seed, row_index)
                display = (tensor * std + mean).clamp(0, 1)
                destination = output_dir / f"{stem}.png"
                TVF.to_pil_image(display).save(destination, format="PNG")
            manifest_rows.append({
                "file": destination.name,
                "category": row["category"],
                "period": row["period"],
                "timestamp": row["timestamp"],
                "country": row["country"],
                "utm": row["utm"],
                "scenario": "clean" if corruption is None else corruption[0],
                "severity": 0 if corruption is None else corruption[1],
                "label_check": "pending human visual review",
            })
    with (output_dir / "manifest.csv").open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(handle, fieldnames=list(manifest_rows[0]))
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(manifest_rows)


def save_corruption_preview(rows, output_path, seed=42, limit=4):
    """Save a contact sheet and individual label-preservation examples."""
    examples, seen = [], set()
    for row in rows:
        if row["category"] not in seen:
            examples.append(row)
            seen.add(row["category"])
        if len(examples) == limit:
            break
    save_corruption_examples(examples, output_path.parent / "examples", seed, limit)
    conditions = (
        ("clean", None),
        ("Gaussian σ=.08", ("gaussian_noise", 1)),
        ("Gaussian σ=.12", ("gaussian_noise", 2)),
        ("Gaussian σ=.18", ("gaussian_noise", 3)),
        ("sensor noise s3", ("sensor_noise", 3)),
        ("defocus blur s3", ("defocus_blur", 3)),
    )
    cell_size, label_width, header_height = 224, 150, 34
    row_height = cell_size + 28
    canvas = Image.new(
        "RGB", (label_width + cell_size * len(conditions), header_height + row_height * len(examples)),
        "white",
    )
    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(canvas)
    for column, (title, _) in enumerate(conditions):
        draw.text((label_width + column * cell_size + 4, 8), title, fill="black")
    mean = torch.tensor(MEAN)[:, None, None]
    std = torch.tensor(STD)[:, None, None]
    for row_index, row in enumerate(examples):
        with Image.open(row["local_path"]) as opened:
            opened.draft("RGB", (224, 224))
            image = opened.convert("RGB")
        for column, (title, corruption) in enumerate(conditions):
            tensor = (
                TVF.normalize(TVF.to_tensor(TVF.resize(image, (224, 224))), MEAN, STD)
                if corruption is None
                else apply_fmow_corruption(image, *corruption, seed, row_index)
            )
            display = (tensor * std + mean).clamp(0, 1)
            x = label_width + column * cell_size
            y = header_height + row_index * row_height
            canvas.paste(TVF.to_pil_image(display), (x, y))
        draw.text((5, header_height + row_index * row_height + 100), row["category"], fill="black")
    canvas.save(output_path)


@torch.inference_mode()
def extract(model, loader, device, desc):
    features, labels = [], []
    model.eval()
    for images, targets, _ in tqdm(loader, desc=desc, unit="batch"):
        tokens = model(pixel_values=images.to(device, non_blocking=True)).last_hidden_state
        features.append(tokens[:, 0].float().cpu().numpy())
        labels.append(targets.numpy())
    return np.concatenate(features), np.concatenate(labels)


def score_predictions(labels, logits):
    values = np.asarray(logits)
    prediction = values.astype(np.int64) if values.ndim == 1 else values.argmax(axis=1)
    return {
        "samples": int(len(labels)),
        "accuracy": float(accuracy_score(labels, prediction)),
        "macro_f1": float(f1_score(labels, prediction, average="macro", zero_division=0)),
        "balanced_accuracy": float(recall_score(labels, prediction, average="macro", zero_division=0)),
        "per_class_f1": f1_score(labels, prediction, average=None, zero_division=0).tolist(),
        "predictions": prediction.tolist(),
    }


def matched_metric_sample(rows_by_period, features_by_period, labels_by_period, limit, seed):
    rows = [dict(row) for group in rows_by_period.values() for row in group]
    selected = matched_temporal_sample(
        rows, period_key="period", strata_keys=("category", "country"),
        target_per_period=min(limit, *(len(group) for group in rows_by_period.values())),
        seed=seed,
    )
    index_by_period = {
        period: {row["local_path"]: index for index, row in enumerate(group)}
        for period, group in rows_by_period.items()
    }
    features, labels = {}, {}
    for period in rows_by_period:
        indices = np.asarray([
            index_by_period[period][row["local_path"]]
            for row in selected if row["_matched_period"] == period
        ], dtype=np.int64)
        features[period] = features_by_period[period][indices]
        labels[period] = labels_by_period[period][indices]
    return features, labels


def matched_period_sample(splits, limit, seed):
    """Choose equal-size, class-country-matched samples across all periods."""
    rows = [dict(row) for period in PERIODS for row in splits[period]]
    target = min(limit, *(len(splits[period]) for period in PERIODS))
    selected = matched_temporal_sample(
        rows, period_key="period", strata_keys=("category", "country"),
        target_per_period=target, seed=seed,
    )
    return {
        period: [row for row in selected if row["_matched_period"] == period]
        for period in PERIODS
    }


def fdr_bh(p_values):
    if not p_values:
        return []
    values = np.asarray(p_values, dtype=float)
    order = np.argsort(values)
    adjusted = np.empty_like(values)
    running = 1.0
    for rank in range(len(values), 0, -1):
        index = order[rank - 1]
        running = min(running, values[index] * len(values) / rank)
        adjusted[index] = min(1.0, running)
    return adjusted.tolist()


def clustered_bootstrap_delta(
    labels, reference_predictions, candidate_predictions, groups,
    repetitions=200, seed=42,
):
    """Paired score-difference intervals resampling geographic UTM clusters."""
    labels = np.asarray(labels)
    reference_predictions = np.asarray(reference_predictions)
    candidate_predictions = np.asarray(candidate_predictions)
    groups = np.asarray(groups)
    if not (len(labels) == len(reference_predictions) == len(candidate_predictions) == len(groups)):
        raise ValueError("labels, predictions, and groups must have equal lengths")
    buckets = [np.flatnonzero(groups == group) for group in np.unique(groups)]
    if len(buckets) < 2:
        raise ValueError("clustered bootstrap needs at least two groups")
    generator = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    differences = {"accuracy": [], "macro_f1": [], "balanced_accuracy": []}
    point = {}
    def score(truth, pred):
        return {
            "accuracy": float(accuracy_score(truth, pred)),
            "macro_f1": float(f1_score(truth, pred, average="macro", zero_division=0)),
            "balanced_accuracy": float(recall_score(truth, pred, average="macro", zero_division=0)),
        }
    ref_point = score(labels, reference_predictions)
    candidate_point = score(labels, candidate_predictions)
    for name in differences:
        point[name] = candidate_point[name] - ref_point[name]
    for _ in range(repetitions):
        sampled = generator.integers(0, len(buckets), size=len(buckets))
        indices = np.concatenate([buckets[index] for index in sampled])
        ref = score(labels[indices], reference_predictions[indices])
        candidate = score(labels[indices], candidate_predictions[indices])
        for name in differences:
            differences[name].append(candidate[name] - ref[name])
    return {
        name: {
            "candidate_minus_reference": point[name],
            "cluster_bootstrap_95_ci": [
                float(np.quantile(values, 0.025)),
                float(np.quantile(values, 0.975)),
            ],
            "repetitions": repetitions,
            "clusters": len(buckets),
        }
        for name, values in differences.items()
    }


class FMoWClassifier(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, classes, head_weight, head_bias, lora_rank=0):
        super().__init__()
        self.backbone = Dinov2Model.from_pretrained(BACKBONE)
        width = self.backbone.config.hidden_size
        self.classifier = nn.Linear(width, classes)
        with torch.no_grad():
            self.classifier.weight.copy_(torch.as_tensor(head_weight))
            self.classifier.bias.copy_(torch.as_tensor(head_bias))
        for parameter in self.backbone.parameters():
            parameter.requires_grad = False
        if lora_rank:
            for block in self.backbone.encoder.layer[-3:]:
                attention = block.attention.attention
                attention.query = LoRALinear(attention.query, rank=lora_rank)
                attention.value = LoRALinear(attention.value, rank=lora_rank)
        for parameter in self.classifier.parameters():
            parameter.requires_grad = True

    def forward(self, images, return_tokens=False):
        tokens = self.backbone(pixel_values=images).last_hidden_state
        logits = self.classifier(tokens[:, 0])
        return (logits, tokens) if return_tokens else logits


def mask_from_teacher(images, tokens, classifier_weight, labels, keep_fraction, mode):
    patches = tokens[:, 1:]
    if mode == "random":
        order = torch.rand(patches.shape[:2], device=images.device).argsort(dim=1)
        count = max(1, round(patches.shape[1] * keep_fraction))
        keep = torch.zeros_like(order, dtype=torch.bool)
        keep.scatter_(1, order[:, :count], True)
    else:
        with torch.no_grad():
            saliency = teacher_saliency(patches, classifier_weight, labels, keep_fraction)
        keep = saliency > 0
    side = round(patches.shape[1] ** 0.5)
    if side * side != patches.shape[1]:
        raise ValueError("DINO patch count must form a square grid")
    patch_mask = keep.reshape(-1, 1, side, side).to(images.dtype)
    return images * F.interpolate(patch_mask, size=images.shape[-2:], mode="nearest")


def train_adaptation(model, teacher, loader, device, mode, epochs, lr, keep_fraction, mask_weight, seed):
    trainable = [parameter for parameter in model.parameters() if parameter.requires_grad]
    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(trainable, lr=lr, weight_decay=0.01)
    history = []
    for epoch in range(epochs):
        model.train()
        model.backbone.eval()
        losses = []
        for images, labels, _ in tqdm(loader, desc=f"{mode} epoch {epoch + 1}", unit="batch"):
            images, labels = images.to(device), labels.to(device)
            logits = model(images)
            loss = F.cross_entropy(logits, labels)
            if mode in ("saliency_only", "saliency_lora", "random_mask_only", "random_mask_lora"):
                with torch.no_grad():
                    teacher_tokens = teacher.backbone(pixel_values=images).last_hidden_state
                    predicted = teacher.classifier(teacher_tokens[:, 0]).argmax(1)
                    mask_mode = "random" if mode.startswith("random") else "saliency"
                    masked = mask_from_teacher(
                        images, teacher_tokens, teacher.classifier.weight,
                        predicted, keep_fraction, mask_mode,
                    )
                loss = loss + mask_weight * F.cross_entropy(model(masked), labels)
            optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
            loss.backward()
            optimizer.step()
            losses.append(float(loss.detach()))
        history.append({"epoch": epoch + 1, "mean_loss": float(np.mean(losses))})
