    return history


def entropy_tta(head_weight, head_bias, classes, loader, device, rank, seed, lr=1e-5):
    """One label-free online entropy update per test batch, then score that batch."""
    torch.manual_seed(seed)
    model = FMoWClassifier(classes, head_weight, head_bias, lora_rank=rank).to(device).eval()
    for parameter in model.classifier.parameters():
        parameter.requires_grad = False
    parameters = [
        parameter for name, parameter in model.named_parameters()
        if parameter.requires_grad and (".down." in name or ".up." in name)
    ]
    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(parameters, lr=lr, weight_decay=0.0)
    predictions, targets_all = [], []
    for images, targets, _ in tqdm(loader, desc="label-free entropy TTA", unit="batch"):
        images = images.to(device)
        with torch.enable_grad():
            logits = model(images)
            probabilities = logits.softmax(dim=1)
            entropy = -(probabilities * logits.log_softmax(dim=1)).sum(dim=1).mean()
            optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
            entropy.backward()
            optimizer.step()
        with torch.no_grad():
            predictions.append(model(images).argmax(1).cpu().numpy())
        targets_all.append(targets.numpy())
    return {
        **score_predictions(np.concatenate(targets_all), np.concatenate(predictions)),
        "protocol": "one unlabeled entropy update on each batch before predicting it",
        "labels_used_for_adaptation": False,
        "adaptation_learning_rate": lr,
    }


@torch.inference_mode()
def evaluate_model(model, loader, device, desc):
    model.eval()
    labels, logits = [], []
    for images, targets, _ in tqdm(loader, desc=desc, unit="batch"):
        logits.append(model(images.to(device)).float().cpu().numpy())
        labels.append(targets.numpy())
    return score_predictions(np.concatenate(labels), np.concatenate(logits))


def parse_args(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--manifest", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=42)
    parser.add_argument("--batch-size", type=int, default=32)
    parser.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=8)
    parser.add_argument("--adapt-epochs", type=int, default=1)
    parser.add_argument("--rank", type=int, default=4)
    parser.add_argument("--feature-limit", type=int, default=1000)
    parser.add_argument("--stress-limit", type=int, default=1000)
    parser.add_argument("--permutations", type=int, default=199)
    parser.add_argument("--permutation-limit", type=int, default=200)
    parser.add_argument("--bootstrap-repetitions", type=int, default=200)
    parser.add_argument("--adaptation-limit", type=int)
    parser.add_argument("--checkpoint-dir", type=Path,
                        help="load trained adaptation arms and rerun evaluation/stress tests")
    parser.add_argument("--drift-only", action="store_true",
                        help="save the clean chronological drift audit and exit before adaptation")
    parser.add_argument("--keep-fraction", type=float, default=0.4)
    parser.add_argument("--mask-weight", type=float, default=0.5)
    parser.add_argument("--corruptions", nargs="+", default=("gaussian_noise", "sensor_noise", "defocus_blur"))
    parser.add_argument("--severities", nargs="+", type=int, default=(1, 2, 3))
    return parser.parse_args(argv)


def main(argv=None):
    args = parse_args(argv)
    if args.output.exists():
        raise FileExistsError(f"Output already exists: {args.output}")
    if args.adapt_epochs < 1 or args.rank < 1 or not 0 < args.keep_fraction < 1:
        raise ValueError("invalid training settings")
    random.seed(args.seed)
    np.random.seed(args.seed)
    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)
    if not torch.cuda.is_available():
        raise RuntimeError("FMoW pilot requires the server GPU")
    device = "cuda"
    rows, categories = load_manifest(args.manifest)
    splits = {period: [row for row in rows if row["period"] == period] for period in PERIODS}
    splits["adapt_2014_15"] = limit_rows(splits["adapt_2014_15"], args.adaptation_limit, args.seed)
    for name in PERIODS:
        splits[name] = limit_rows(splits[name], None, args.seed)
    loaders = {name: make_loader(splits[name], args.batch_size, args.workers) for name in PERIODS}

    backbone = Dinov2Model.from_pretrained(BACKBONE).to(device).eval()
    historical_features, historical_labels = extract(backbone, loaders[PERIODS[0]], device, "clean historical embeddings")
    adaptation_features, adaptation_labels = extract(backbone, loaders[PERIODS[1]], device, "clean adaptation embeddings")
    future_features, future_labels = extract(backbone, loaders[PERIODS[2]], device, "clean future embeddings")

    metric_n = min(args.feature_limit, *(len(splits[period]) for period in PERIODS))
    clean_metric_features, _ = matched_metric_sample(
        splits,
        dict(zip(PERIODS, (historical_features, adaptation_features, future_features))),
        dict(zip(PERIODS, (historical_labels, adaptation_labels, future_labels))),
        metric_n, args.seed,
    )
    null_rows = splits[PERIODS[0]].copy()
    random.Random(args.seed + 3).shuffle(null_rows)
    null_a_rows, null_b_rows = [], []
    buckets = {}
    for row in null_rows:
        buckets.setdefault((row["category"], row["country"]), []).append(row)
    for values in buckets.values():
        midpoint = len(values) // 2
        null_a_rows.extend(values[:midpoint])
        null_b_rows.extend(values[midpoint:])
    historical_index = {row["local_path"]: index for index, row in enumerate(splits[PERIODS[0]])}
    null_indices = matched_temporal_sample(
        [dict(row, _null_period=name) for name, group in (("null_a", null_a_rows), ("null_b", null_b_rows)) for row in group],
        period_key="_null_period", strata_keys=("category", "country"),
        target_per_period=min(args.feature_limit, len(null_a_rows), len(null_b_rows)),
        seed=args.seed + 4,
    )
    null_a_ix = np.asarray([historical_index[row["local_path"]] for row in null_indices if row["_matched_period"] == "null_a"])
    null_b_ix = np.asarray([historical_index[row["local_path"]] for row in null_indices if row["_matched_period"] == "null_b"])
    null_a, null_b = historical_features[null_a_ix], historical_features[null_b_ix]
    clean_pairs = ((0, 1), (1, 2), (0, 2))
    drift_rows = [
        {"comparison": f"natural_clean_{PERIODS[left]}_vs_{PERIODS[right]}",
         **distribution_shift_metrics(
            clean_metric_features[PERIODS[left]], clean_metric_features[PERIODS[right]],
            permutations=args.permutations, permutation_limit=args.permutation_limit,
            seed=args.seed + left * 11 + right,
        )}
        for left, right in clean_pairs
    ] + [
        {"comparison": "clean_historical_split_half_null", **distribution_shift_metrics(
            null_a, null_b, permutations=args.permutations,
            permutation_limit=args.permutation_limit, seed=args.seed + 3,
        )},
    ]
    q_values = fdr_bh([row["mmd_permutation_p"] for row in drift_rows])
    for row, q_value in zip(drift_rows, q_values):
        row["mmd_bh_q"] = q_value

    probe = make_pipeline(StandardScaler(), LogisticRegression(
        class_weight="balanced", max_iter=2000, random_state=args.seed,
    ))
    historical_groups = np.asarray([f"{row['country']}:{row['utm']}" for row in splits[PERIODS[0]]])
    fold_count = min(5, len(np.unique(historical_groups)))
    historical_cv = StratifiedGroupKFold(n_splits=fold_count, shuffle=True, random_state=args.seed)
    hist_probe_logits = cross_val_predict(
        probe, historical_features, historical_labels, cv=historical_cv,
        groups=historical_groups, method="decision_function",
    )
    probe.fit(historical_features, historical_labels)
    adaptation_probe_logits = probe.decision_function(adaptation_features)
    future_probe_logits = probe.decision_function(future_features)
    if hist_probe_logits.ndim == 1:
        hist_probe_logits = np.stack((-hist_probe_logits, hist_probe_logits), axis=1)
        adaptation_probe_logits = np.stack((-adaptation_probe_logits, adaptation_probe_logits), axis=1)
        future_probe_logits = np.stack((-future_probe_logits, future_probe_logits), axis=1)
    clean_scores = {
        "frozen_historical": {
            "historical_grouped_oof": score_predictions(historical_labels, hist_probe_logits),
            "adaptation": score_predictions(adaptation_labels, adaptation_probe_logits),
            "future": score_predictions(future_labels, future_probe_logits),
        }
    }
    scaler = probe.named_steps["standardscaler"]
    logistic = probe.named_steps["logisticregression"]
    head_weight = logistic.coef_ / scaler.scale_[None, :]
    head_bias = logistic.intercept_ - (scaler.mean_ / scaler.scale_) @ logistic.coef_.T
    if len(head_weight) == 1:
        head_weight = np.concatenate((-head_weight, head_weight), axis=0)
        head_bias = np.concatenate((-head_bias, head_bias), axis=0)
    if head_weight.shape[0] != len(categories):
        raise RuntimeError("linear-probe output dimension does not match FMoW classes")

    args.output.mkdir(parents=True)
    save_corruption_preview(splits[PERIODS[2]], args.output / "corruption_preview.png", args.seed)
    payload = {
        "dataset": "custom original FMoW-RGB chronological subset; not the official FMoW-Time benchmark",
        "feature_extractor": BACKBONE,
        "device": device,
        "seed": args.seed,
        "periods": {name: len(splits[name]) for name in PERIODS},
        "class_country_matched_manifest": str(args.manifest),
        "crossed_stress_design": {
            "periods": PERIODS,
            "scenarios": args.corruptions,
            "severities": args.severities,
            "matched_images_per_period": min(args.stress_limit, *(len(splits[p]) for p in PERIODS)),
            "class_country_composition_matched_across_periods": True,
        },
        "clean_drift_audit": drift_rows,
        "clean_classification": clean_scores,
        "adaptation_arms": {},
        "synthetic_stress": {},
    }
    (args.output / "clean_drift_audit.json").write_text(json.dumps({
        "dataset": payload["dataset"], "periods": payload["periods"],
        "drift_detection": drift_rows, "historical_only_performance": clean_scores,
        "note": "Saved before model adaptation and before synthetic corruption runs.",
    }, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    if args.drift_only:
        print(f"Saved clean-only drift audit: {args.output / 'clean_drift_audit.json'}")
        return

    teacher = FMoWClassifier(len(categories), head_weight, head_bias).to(device).eval()
    raw_test = clean_scores["frozen_historical"]["future"]
    payload["adaptation_arms"]["frozen_historical"] = {"future": raw_test}
    for mode in ("lora", "saliency_only", "saliency_lora", "random_mask_only", "random_mask_lora"):
        torch.manual_seed(args.seed)
        student = FMoWClassifier(
            len(categories), head_weight, head_bias,
            lora_rank=args.rank if mode in ("lora", "saliency_lora", "random_mask_lora") else 0,
        ).to(device)
        if args.checkpoint_dir:
            checkpoint = args.checkpoint_dir / f"{mode}.pt"
            student.load_state_dict(torch.load(checkpoint, map_location=device, weights_only=True))
            history = [{"loaded_checkpoint": str(checkpoint)}]
        else:
            adaptation_loader = make_loader(splits[PERIODS[1]], args.batch_size, args.workers)
            history = train_adaptation(
                student, teacher, adaptation_loader, device, mode,
                args.adapt_epochs, 1e-3, args.keep_fraction, args.mask_weight, args.seed,
            )
        future = evaluate_model(student, loaders[PERIODS[2]], device, f"{mode} clean future")
        payload["adaptation_arms"][mode] = {
            "history": history, "future": future,
            "trainable_parameters": sum(p.numel() for p in student.parameters() if p.requires_grad),
        }
        if not args.checkpoint_dir:
            torch.save(student.state_dict(), args.output / f"{mode}.pt")
        print(json.dumps({"arm": mode, "future": {k: future[k] for k in ("accuracy", "macro_f1", "balanced_accuracy")}}), flush=True)

    payload["adaptation_arms"]["entropy_tta"] = {
        "future": entropy_tta(
            head_weight, head_bias, len(categories), loaders[PERIODS[2]],
            device, args.rank, args.seed,
        )
    }
    print(json.dumps({"arm": "entropy_tta", "future": {
        key: payload["adaptation_arms"]["entropy_tta"]["future"][key]
        for key in ("accuracy", "macro_f1", "balanced_accuracy")
    }}), flush=True)

    future_groups = np.asarray([f"{row['country']}:{row['utm']}" for row in splits[PERIODS[2]]])
    raw_future_predictions = np.asarray(clean_scores["frozen_historical"]["future"]["predictions"])
    payload["clean_future_clustered_deltas"] = {}
    for mode in ("lora", "saliency_only", "saliency_lora", "random_mask_only", "random_mask_lora", "entropy_tta"):
        payload["clean_future_clustered_deltas"][f"{mode}_vs_frozen"] = clustered_bootstrap_delta(
            future_labels, raw_future_predictions,
            payload["adaptation_arms"][mode]["future"]["predictions"],
            future_groups, args.bootstrap_repetitions, args.seed,
        )
    for reference, candidate in (("lora", "saliency_lora"), ("random_mask_lora", "saliency_lora")):
        payload["clean_future_clustered_deltas"][f"{candidate}_vs_{reference}"] = clustered_bootstrap_delta(
            future_labels,
            payload["adaptation_arms"][reference]["future"]["predictions"],
            payload["adaptation_arms"][candidate]["future"]["predictions"],
            future_groups, args.bootstrap_repetitions, args.seed + 1,
        )

    stress_rows_by_period = matched_period_sample(splits, args.stress_limit, args.seed + 10)
    clean_stress_features = {}
    for period in PERIODS:
        period_index = {row["local_path"]: i for i, row in enumerate(splits[period])}
        indices = np.asarray([period_index[row["local_path"]] for row in stress_rows_by_period[period]])
        clean_stress_features[period] = {
            "features": clean_metric_features[period][:0],  # assigned from the full-period arrays below
            "rows": stress_rows_by_period[period],
            "indices": indices,
        }
    full_features = dict(zip(PERIODS, (historical_features, adaptation_features, future_features)))
    for period in PERIODS:
        clean_stress_features[period]["features"] = full_features[period][clean_stress_features[period]["indices"]]

    stress_p = []
    stress_feature_cache = {}
    crossed_grid = crossed_corruption_grid(PERIODS, args.corruptions, args.severities)
    checkpoint_root = args.checkpoint_dir or args.output
    adaptation_modes = ("lora", "saliency_only", "saliency_lora", "random_mask_only", "random_mask_lora")
    for cell in crossed_grid:
        period, scenario, severity = cell["period"], cell["scenario"], cell["severity"]
        rows_for_period = stress_rows_by_period[period]
        corruption = None if scenario == "clean" else (scenario, severity)
        condition_loader = make_loader(
            rows_for_period, args.batch_size, args.workers,
            corruption=corruption, seed=args.seed,
        )
        condition = f"{period}__{scenario}_s{severity}"
        if scenario == "clean":
            condition_features = clean_stress_features[period]["features"]
            feature_shift = None
        else:
            condition_features, _ = extract(backbone, condition_loader, device, f"{condition} embeddings")
            feature_shift = distribution_shift_metrics(
                clean_stress_features[period]["features"], condition_features,
                permutations=args.permutations, permutation_limit=args.permutation_limit,
                seed=args.seed + severity, paired=True,
            )
            stress_p.append((condition, feature_shift["mmd_permutation_p"]))
        stress_feature_cache[(period, scenario, severity)] = condition_features
        result = {
            "period": period, "scenario": scenario, "severity": severity,
            "feature_shift_vs_same_clean_images": feature_shift,
            "frozen_historical": evaluate_model(teacher, condition_loader, device, condition),
        }
        for mode in adaptation_modes:
            model = FMoWClassifier(
                len(categories), head_weight, head_bias,
                lora_rank=args.rank if mode in ("lora", "saliency_lora", "random_mask_lora") else 0,
            ).to(device)
            model.load_state_dict(torch.load(checkpoint_root / f"{mode}.pt", map_location=device, weights_only=True))
            result[mode] = evaluate_model(model, condition_loader, device, f"{condition} {mode}")
            del model
            torch.cuda.empty_cache()
        result["entropy_tta"] = entropy_tta(
            head_weight, head_bias, len(categories), condition_loader,
            device, args.rank, args.seed,
        )
        payload["synthetic_stress"][condition] = result
        print(json.dumps({"condition": condition, "frozen_accuracy": result["frozen_historical"]["accuracy"], "saliency_lora_accuracy": result["saliency_lora"]["accuracy"]}), flush=True)

    stress_q = fdr_bh([p_value for _, p_value in stress_p])
    for (condition, _), q_value in zip(stress_p, stress_q):
        payload["synthetic_stress"][condition]["feature_shift_vs_same_clean_images"]["mmd_bh_q"] = q_value

    payload["progressive_schedules"] = {}
    schedule_p = []
    for schedule_name, severities in (("mild_progression", (0, 1, 2)), ("stronger_future_sensitivity", (0, 1, 3))):
        schedule = progressive_corruption_schedule(PERIODS, "gaussian_noise", severities)
        baseline_features = stress_feature_cache[(PERIODS[0], "clean", 0)]
        cells = []
        for cell in schedule:
            period, scenario, severity = cell["period"], cell["scenario"], cell["severity"]
            condition = f"{period}__{scenario}_s{severity}"
            features = stress_feature_cache[(period, scenario, severity)]
            if period == PERIODS[0]:
                shift_from_initial = None
            else:
                shift_from_initial = distribution_shift_metrics(
                    baseline_features, features,
                    permutations=args.permutations, permutation_limit=args.permutation_limit,
                    seed=args.seed + severity + 100, paired=False,
                )
                schedule_p.append((schedule_name, period, shift_from_initial["mmd_permutation_p"]))
            grid_result = payload["synthetic_stress"][condition]
            stress_labels = np.asarray([row["class_id"] for row in stress_rows_by_period[period]])
            stress_groups = np.asarray([f"{row['country']}:{row['utm']}" for row in stress_rows_by_period[period]])
            cells.append({
                **cell, "condition": condition,
                "shift_vs_initial_clean_historical": shift_from_initial,
                "classification": {
                    mode: {key: grid_result[mode][key] for key in ("accuracy", "macro_f1", "balanced_accuracy")}
                    for mode in ("frozen_historical", *adaptation_modes, "entropy_tta")
                },
                "clustered_score_deltas": {
                    "saliency_lora_vs_lora": clustered_bootstrap_delta(
                        stress_labels,
                        grid_result["lora"]["predictions"],
                        grid_result["saliency_lora"]["predictions"],
                        stress_groups, args.bootstrap_repetitions, args.seed + severity + 1,
                    ),
                    "saliency_only_vs_frozen": clustered_bootstrap_delta(
                        stress_labels,
                        grid_result["frozen_historical"]["predictions"],
                        grid_result["saliency_only"]["predictions"],
                        stress_groups, args.bootstrap_repetitions, args.seed + severity + 2,
                    ),
                },
            })
        payload["progressive_schedules"][schedule_name] = cells
    schedule_q = fdr_bh([p_value for _, _, p_value in schedule_p])
    for (schedule_name, period, _), q_value in zip(schedule_p, schedule_q):
        for cell in payload["progressive_schedules"][schedule_name]:
            if cell["period"] == period:
                cell["shift_vs_initial_clean_historical"]["mmd_bh_q"] = q_value

    (args.output / "fmow_temporal_pilot.json").write_text(json.dumps(payload, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"Saved: {args.output / 'fmow_temporal_pilot.json'}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/fmow_token_dropping.py
================================================
"""Test-time evidence-guided token dropping on the frozen DINOv2-L fMoW model.

For each image: predict once, rank patch tokens by their evidence for that prediction,
remove the lowest-evidence fraction right after patch embedding (so they never enter
attention), and predict again. Controls drop the same number of random patches, or the
brightest patches (a saliency-free cloud detector). No training; future-test labels are
used only for scoring.

Conditions: clean images, realistic cumulus clouds off / on the labelled object box, and
real acquisition strata (recorded cloud cover, off-nadir angle, sun elevation).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch
from torch import nn

from fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_dinov2l_study import (
    DEFAULT_CACHE, DEFAULT_MANIFEST, Backbone, cluster_bootstrap, image_cache, image_loader, load_rows, log,
    scores, study_splits, write_json, _block_output,
)

DROP_MODES = ("evidence", "random", "brightest")


def encode_tokens(backbone, hidden):
    for block in backbone.frozen_blocks + backbone.adapted_blocks:
        hidden = _block_output(block, hidden)
    return backbone.model.layernorm(hidden)


def patch_brightness(images, patch=14):
    """Mean brightness of each patch in normalized-image units (higher = whiter)."""
    pooled = torch.nn.functional.avg_pool2d(images.mean(1, keepdim=True), patch)
    return pooled.flatten(1)


def tokens_to_keep(mode, evidence, images, keep, generator):
    if mode == "evidence":
        return evidence.topk(keep, dim=1).indices
    if mode == "brightest":
        return (-patch_brightness(images)).topk(keep, dim=1).indices
    order = torch.rand(evidence.shape, generator=generator).argsort(1)
    return order[:, :keep].to(evidence.device)


@torch.no_grad()
def predictions_with_dropping(backbone, head, data, fractions):
    """Predictions for the full model and for every (mode, fraction) of dropped tokens."""
    generator = torch.Generator().manual_seed(42)
    outputs = {"full": []} | {f"{m}_{f}": [] for m in DROP_MODES for f in fractions}
    labels = []
    for images, targets, _ in data:
        images = images.cuda(non_blocking=True)
        with torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=torch.float16):
            embedded = backbone.model.embeddings(images)
            tokens = encode_tokens(backbone, embedded)[:, 1:].float()
            logits = head(tokens.mean(1))
            evidence = (tokens * head.weight[logits.argmax(1)][:, None, :]).sum(-1)
            outputs["full"].append(logits.argmax(1).cpu())
            patches = embedded.shape[1] - 1
            for fraction in fractions:
                keep = patches - round(fraction * patches)
                for mode in DROP_MODES:
                    kept = tokens_to_keep(mode, evidence, images, keep, generator)
                    selected = torch.cat([embedded[:, :1], embedded[:, 1:].gather(1, kept[..., None].expand(-1, -1, embedded.shape[-1]))], 1)
                    pruned = encode_tokens(backbone, selected)[:, 1:].float()
                    outputs[f"{mode}_{fraction}"].append(head(pruned.mean(1)).argmax(1).cpu())
        labels.append(targets)
    return torch.cat(labels).numpy(), {k: torch.cat(v).numpy() for k, v in outputs.items()}


def real_strata(rows):
    """Masks over future-test rows for recorded acquisition nuisances (thresholds = future-test quartiles)."""
    cloud = np.array([r["cloud_cover"] for r in rows])
    nadir = np.array([r["off_nadir_angle"] for r in rows])
    sun = np.array([r["sun_elevation"] for r in rows])
    return {"all": np.ones(len(rows), bool), "cloud_cover_ge10": cloud >= 10, "cloud_free": cloud == 0,
            "off_nadir_top25": nadir >= np.quantile(nadir, 0.75), "off_nadir_bottom25": nadir <= np.quantile(nadir, 0.25),
            "low_sun_bottom25": sun <= np.quantile(sun, 0.25), "high_sun_top25": sun >= np.quantile(sun, 0.75)}


def summarize(labels, groups, outputs, strata, classes):
    table = {}
    for name, mask in strata.items():
        table[name] = {"n": int(mask.sum())} | {k: scores(labels[mask], v[mask], classes)["macro_f1"] for k, v in outputs.items()}
    paired = []
    for key in outputs:
        if not key.startswith("evidence_"):
            continue
        fraction = key.split("_", 1)[1]
        for control in ("random", "brightest", "full"):
            other = outputs["full"] if control == "full" else outputs[f"{control}_{fraction}"]
            for name, mask in strata.items():
                difference = (outputs[key][mask] == labels[mask]).astype(float) - (other[mask] == labels[mask]).astype(float)
                paired.append({"fraction": float(fraction), "vs": control, "stratum": name,
                               "delta_accuracy": cluster_bootstrap(difference, groups[mask], repetitions=1000)})
    return table, paired


def main(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    parser.add_argument("--run", type=Path, default=Path("/mnt/sdd/FYP/runs/today/main"), help="directory holding head.pt")
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, default=Path("/mnt/sdd/FYP/runs/today/token_dropping"))
    parser.add_argument("--backbone", default="facebook/dinov2-large")
    parser.add_argument("--cloud-strength", type=float, default=0.8)
    parser.add_argument("--fractions", type=float, nargs="+", default=[0.1, 0.25])
    parser.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=2)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    args.output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    rows, classes = load_rows(DEFAULT_MANIFEST)
    test = study_splits(rows)["future_test"]
    cache = image_cache(rows, DEFAULT_CACHE, args.workers)
    head_state = torch.load(args.run / "head.pt", weights_only=True)
    head = nn.Linear(head_state["weight"].shape[1], classes).cuda().eval()
    head.load_state_dict(head_state)
    backbone = Backbone(args.backbone).cuda().eval()
    groups = np.array([r["group"] for r in test])
    report = {}
    for condition, region in (("clean", None), ("cloud_off_object", "off_object"), ("cloud_on_object", "on_object")):
        path = args.output / f"{condition}.json"
        if path.exists():
            report[condition] = json.loads(path.read_text())
            continue
        alpha = 0.0 if region is None else args.cloud_strength
        labels, outputs = predictions_with_dropping(backbone, head, image_loader(cache, test, 64, args.workers, alpha, cloud_region=region), args.fractions)
        strata = real_strata(test) if condition == "clean" else {"all": np.ones(len(test), bool)}
        table, paired = summarize(labels, groups, outputs, strata, classes)
        report[condition] = {"cloud_strength": alpha, "macro_f1": table, "paired": paired,
                             "predictions": {k: v.tolist() for k, v in outputs.items()}}
        write_json(path, report[condition])
        log(condition, json.dumps(table["all"]))
    write_json(args.output / "summary.json", {c: {"macro_f1": r["macro_f1"], "paired": r["paired"]} for c, r in report.items()})


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/frozen_probe_screens.py
================================================
"""Frozen-backbone screens: does real shift harm a linear probe, across datasets and architectures?

For each (dataset, backbone): extract mean patch features from a frozen backbone, fit a
class-weighted linear head on the reference window, pick its checkpoint on a reference
validation set, and report
  - scores per window,
  - the reference-minus-target accuracy gap with a location-clustered 95% interval,
  - MMD / domain AUC between reference and target features (with a split-half null),
  - scores on real acquisition strata of the target window.

Datasets
  fmow      historical 2010-13 -> future 2016-17 (study splits); strata from recorded
            cloud cover, off-nadir angle and sun elevation.
  iwildcam  WILDS train cameras -> unseen test cameras; strata from the recorded capture
            hour (night 19:00-06:00, when camera traps switch to infrared) vs day.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import json
import random
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch

from fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_dinov2l_study import (
    DEFAULT_CACHE, DEFAULT_MANIFEST, cluster_bootstrap, fit_linear_head, image_cache, load_rows, log, scores,
    study_splits, write_json,
)

IMAGENET = ((0.485, 0.456, 0.406), (0.229, 0.224, 0.225))
BACKBONES = {
    "dinov2-small": ("facebook/dinov2-small", "Dinov2Model", IMAGENET, True),
    "dinov2-base": ("facebook/dinov2-base", "Dinov2Model", IMAGENET, True),
    "dinov2-large": ("facebook/dinov2-large", "Dinov2Model", IMAGENET, True),
    "clip-b16": ("openai/clip-vit-base-patch16", "CLIPVisionModel", ((0.4815, 0.4578, 0.4082), (0.2686, 0.2613, 0.2758)), True),
    "siglip-b16": ("google/siglip-base-patch16-224", "SiglipVisionModel", ((0.5, 0.5, 0.5), (0.5, 0.5, 0.5)), False),
}
IWILDCAM = Path("/mnt/sdd/FYP/data/wilds/iwildcam_v2.0")
IWILDCAM_CACHE = Path("/mnt/sdd/FYP/cache/iwildcam_u8_224.npy")


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- datasets
def fmow_windows():
    """Rows, class count, cache and the four windows; row['idx'] indexes the cache."""
    rows, classes = load_rows(DEFAULT_MANIFEST)
    splits = study_splits(rows)
    windows = {"fit": splits["hist_train"], "select": splits["hist_val"], "reference": splits["hist_val"], "target": splits["future_test"]}
    return rows, classes, DEFAULT_CACHE, windows


def iwildcam_windows(seed=42):
    """A seeded subsample of WILDS iWildCam: train cameras for fitting, unseen cameras as the target."""
    with open(IWILDCAM / "metadata.csv", newline="") as handle:
        everything = list(csv.DictReader(handle))
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    sizes = {"train": 15000, "id_val": 2000, "id_test": 4000, "test": 6000}
    picked = {split: rng.sample([r for r in everything if r["split"] == split], sizes[split]) for split in sizes}
    rows = [r for split in sizes for r in picked[split]]
    labels = sorted({int(r["y"]) for r in rows})
    remap = {y: i for i, y in enumerate(labels)}
    for i, row in enumerate(rows):
        hour = int(row["datetime"][11:13])
        row.update(idx=i, class_id=remap[int(row["y"])], group=row["location_remapped"],
                   local_path=str(IWILDCAM / "train" / row["filename"]), night=hour >= 19 or hour < 6)
    windows = {"fit": picked["train"], "select": picked["id_val"], "reference": picked["id_test"], "target": picked["test"]}
    return rows, len(labels), IWILDCAM_CACHE, windows


def target_strata(dataset, target):
    if dataset == "iwildcam":
        night = np.array([r["night"] for r in target])
        return {"night": night, "day": ~night}
    cloud = np.array([r["cloud_cover"] for r in target])
    nadir = np.array([r["off_nadir_angle"] for r in target])
    sun = np.array([r["sun_elevation"] for r in target])
    return {"cloud_cover_ge10": cloud >= 10, "cloud_free": cloud == 0,
            "off_nadir_top25": nadir >= np.quantile(nadir, 0.75), "off_nadir_bottom25": nadir <= np.quantile(nadir, 0.25),
            "low_sun_bottom25": sun <= np.quantile(sun, 0.25), "high_sun_top25": sun >= np.quantile(sun, 0.75)}


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- features
def frozen_mean_patch_features(name, cache, rows, batch_size=64, workers=2):
    import transformers
    hub_id, class_name, (mean, std), has_cls = BACKBONES[name]
    model = getattr(transformers, class_name).from_pretrained(hub_id).cuda().eval()
    mean, std = torch.tensor(mean)[:, None, None], torch.tensor(std)[:, None, None]

    class Images(torch.utils.data.Dataset):
        def __len__(self):
            return len(rows)

        def __getitem__(self, i):
            pixels = torch.from_numpy(np.array(cache[rows[i]["idx"]])).permute(2, 0, 1).float() / 255.0
            return (pixels - mean) / std

    features = []
    with torch.no_grad():
        for images in torch.utils.data.DataLoader(Images(), batch_size=batch_size, num_workers=workers):
            with torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=torch.float16):
                tokens = model(pixel_values=images.cuda(non_blocking=True)).last_hidden_state
            features.append((tokens[:, 1:] if has_cls else tokens).float().mean(1).cpu())
    del model
    torch.cuda.empty_cache()
    return torch.cat(features).numpy()


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- screen
def accuracy_gap(reference_correct, reference_groups, target_correct, target_groups, repetitions=1000):
    """Reference minus target accuracy, resampling location groups independently on each side."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
    sides = [(np.unique(g), {k: c[g == k] for k in np.unique(g)}) for c, g in
             ((reference_correct, reference_groups), (target_correct, target_groups))]
    draws = []
    for _ in range(repetitions):
        means = [np.concatenate([by[k] for k in rng.choice(unique, len(unique))]).mean() for unique, by in sides]
        draws.append(means[0] - means[1])
    return {"mean": float(np.mean(draws)), "ci95": [float(np.percentile(draws, 2.5)), float(np.percentile(draws, 97.5))]}


def drift(features, windows, permutations):
    from fyp_drift.evaluation.drift_metrics import distribution_shift_metrics
    rng = np.random.default_rng(42)
    reference = features[rng.choice(np.array([r["idx"] for r in windows["fit"]]), 2000, replace=False)]
    target = features[rng.choice(np.array([r["idx"] for r in windows["target"]]), 1000, replace=False)]
    results = {}
    for name, (a, b) in {"reference_vs_target": (reference[:1000], target), "split_half_null": (reference[:1000], reference[1000:])}.items():
        m = distribution_shift_metrics(a, b, permutations=permutations, permutation_limit=1000, seed=42)
        results[name] = {k: m[k] for k in ("mmd_squared", "mmd_permutation_p", "domain_classifier_roc_auc")}
    return results


def screen(dataset, backbone, rows, classes, cache, windows, output, permutations):
    path = output / f"{dataset}_{backbone}.json"
    if path.exists():
        return json.loads(path.read_text())
    features = frozen_mean_patch_features(backbone, cache, rows)
    index = {k: np.array([r["idx"] for r in v]) for k, v in windows.items()}
    labels = {k: np.array([r["class_id"] for r in v]) for k, v in windows.items()}
    head, select_f1 = fit_linear_head(features[index["fit"]], labels["fit"], classes, features[index["select"]], labels["select"])
    with torch.no_grad():
        predicted = {k: head(torch.tensor(features[index[k]]).cuda()).argmax(1).cpu().numpy() for k in index}
    correct = {k: predicted[k] == labels[k] for k in index}
    groups = {k: np.array([r["group"] for r in v]) for k, v in windows.items()}
    strata = target_strata(dataset, windows["target"])
    result = {"dataset": dataset, "backbone": backbone, "select_macro_f1": select_f1,
              "windows": {k: scores(labels[k], predicted[k], classes) for k in ("select", "reference", "target")},
              "gap_reference_minus_target": accuracy_gap(correct["reference"].astype(float), groups["reference"],
                                                         correct["target"].astype(float), groups["target"]),
              "drift": drift(features, windows, permutations),
              "target_strata": {name: {"n": int(mask.sum()), **scores(labels["target"][mask], predicted["target"][mask], classes)}
                                for name, mask in strata.items()}}
    write_json(path, result)
    log(dataset, backbone, "ref", round(result["windows"]["reference"]["accuracy"], 4), "target", round(result["windows"]["target"]["accuracy"], 4),
        "gap", result["gap_reference_minus_target"])
    return result


def main(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, default=Path("/mnt/sdd/FYP/runs/today/screens"))
    parser.add_argument("--datasets", nargs="+", default=["fmow", "iwildcam"])
    parser.add_argument("--backbones", nargs="+", default=list(BACKBONES))
    parser.add_argument("--permutations", type=int, default=499)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    args.output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    summary = []
    for dataset in args.datasets:
        rows, classes, cache_path, windows = fmow_windows() if dataset == "fmow" else iwildcam_windows()
        cache = image_cache(rows, cache_path, workers=4)
        for backbone in args.backbones:
            r = screen(dataset, backbone, rows, classes, cache, windows, args.output, args.permutations)
            summary.append({"dataset": dataset, "backbone": backbone,
                            "reference_acc": r["windows"]["reference"]["accuracy"], "target_acc": r["windows"]["target"]["accuracy"],
                            "reference_f1": r["windows"]["reference"]["macro_f1"], "target_f1": r["windows"]["target"]["macro_f1"],
                            "gap": r["gap_reference_minus_target"]["mean"], "gap_lo": r["gap_reference_minus_target"]["ci95"][0],
                            "gap_hi": r["gap_reference_minus_target"]["ci95"][1],
                            "mmd_p": r["drift"]["reference_vs_target"]["mmd_permutation_p"],
                            "auc": r["drift"]["reference_vs_target"]["domain_classifier_roc_auc"],
                            **{f"{k}_acc": v["accuracy"] for k, v in r["target_strata"].items()}})
            with open(args.output / "summary.csv", "w", newline="") as handle:
                writer = csv.DictWriter(handle, fieldnames=sorted({k for s in summary for k in s}, key=lambda k: (k not in ("dataset", "backbone"), k)))
                writer.writeheader()
                writer.writerows(summary)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/lora_rank_sweep.py
================================================
"""Parameter-matched LoRA: does LoRA still lead when it trains as few parameters as the saliency heads?

The main LoRA arm (rank 8, last 6 DINOv2-L blocks) trains 884,736 adapter weights with the historical head
frozen. The saliency attention heads train about 197k (scorer + classifier). Here LoRA is rerun, plain
cross-entropy only, at rank 2 (221,184) and rank 1 (110,592), bracketing that budget, with everything else
identical to the main arm: same historical head, cloud alpha, epochs, learning rate and seeds.

Each run is compared on the same 2016-17 test images, image by image, with the saliency heads from
saliency_attention_study (same seed and condition), using a location-clustered bootstrap of the accuracy gap.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import time
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch
from torch import nn

from fyp_drift.evaluation import fmow_dinov2l_study as study

COMPARE_ARMS = ("attention", "guided", "input", "gated", "prior")


def main(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    parser.add_argument("--run", type=Path, default=Path("/mnt/sdd/FYP/runs/today/main"))
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, default=Path("/mnt/sdd/FYP/runs/today/lora_rank_sweep"))
    parser.add_argument("--attention", type=Path, default=Path("/mnt/sdd/FYP/runs/today/saliency_attention"))
    parser.add_argument("--ranks", type=int, nargs="+", default=[2, 1])
    parser.add_argument("--seeds", type=int, nargs="+", default=[42, 43, 44])
    options = parser.parse_args(argv)
    options.output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    rows, classes = study.load_rows(study.DEFAULT_MANIFEST)
    splits = study.study_splits(rows)
    cache = study.image_cache(rows, study.DEFAULT_CACHE, 4)
    head_state = torch.load(options.run / "head.pt", weights_only=True)
    head = nn.Linear(head_state["weight"].shape[1], classes).cuda().eval()
    head.load_state_dict(head_state)
    head.requires_grad_(False)
    alphas = {"clean": 0.0, "cloud": json.loads((options.run / "calib.json").read_text())["selected_alpha"]}
    for rank in options.ranks:
        args = study.parse_args(["--output", str(options.run), "--rank", str(rank)])
        for seed in options.seeds:
            for condition, alpha in alphas.items():
                path = options.output / f"{condition}_plain_r{rank}_s{seed}.json"
                if path.exists():
                    continue
                started = time.time()
                result = study.train_arm(args, cache, splits, head, classes, "plain", 0.0, alpha, seed)
                study.write_json(path, {**result, "rank": rank, "condition": condition, "seconds": time.time() - started})
                study.log(f"done {path.name}: params={result['trainable_parameters']} test_f1={result['future_test']['macro_f1']:.4f}")
    summarize(options, splits)


def summarize(options, splits):
    labels = np.array([r["class_id"] for r in splits["future_test"]])
    groups = np.array([r["group"] for r in splits["future_test"]])
    lora = [json.loads(p.read_text()) for p in sorted(options.output.glob("*_plain_r*_s*.json"))]
    main_arms = {(json.loads(p.read_text())["condition"], json.loads(p.read_text())["seed"]): json.loads(p.read_text())
                 for p in sorted((options.run / "arms").glob("*_plain_l0.0_s*.json"))}
    for (condition, seed), r in main_arms.items():
        lora.append({**r, "rank": 8, "condition": condition, "seed": seed})
    table, paired = {}, []
    for rank in sorted({r["rank"] for r in lora}):
        for condition in ("clean", "cloud"):
            rs = [r for r in lora if (r["rank"], r["condition"]) == (rank, condition)]
            if not rs:
                continue
            table[f"{condition}/lora_r{rank}"] = {"seeds": len(rs), "trainable_parameters": rs[0]["trainable_parameters"],
                                                  "test_macro_f1": float(np.mean([r["future_test"]["macro_f1"] for r in rs])),
                                                  "test_accuracy": float(np.mean([r["future_test"]["accuracy"] for r in rs]))}
            for r in rs:
                for arm in COMPARE_ARMS:
                    other = options.attention / f"{condition}_{arm}_s{r['seed']}.json"
                    if not other.exists():
                        continue
                    o = json.loads(other.read_text())
                    difference = (np.array(o["predictions"]) == labels).astype(float) - (np.array(r["predictions"]) == labels).astype(float)
                    paired.append({"condition": condition, "rank": rank, "seed": r["seed"], "saliency_arm": arm,
                                   "delta_macro_f1_arm_minus_lora": o["future_test"]["macro_f1"] - r["future_test"]["macro_f1"],
                                   "delta_accuracy_arm_minus_lora": study.cluster_bootstrap(difference, groups, repetitions=1000)})
    study.write_json(options.output / "summary.json", {"table": table, "paired": paired})
    study.log("summary", json.dumps(table, indent=1))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/metrics.py
================================================
"""Shared classification, temporal, routing, and saliency agreement metrics."""

from collections import defaultdict

import numpy as np


def classification_metrics(truth, prediction, classes=100):
    truth = np.asarray(truth, dtype=np.int64)
    prediction = np.asarray(prediction, dtype=np.int64)
    if not len(truth):
        raise ValueError("Cannot score an empty evaluation set")
    matrix = np.zeros((classes, classes), dtype=np.int64)
    np.add.at(matrix, (truth, prediction), 1)
    tp = np.diag(matrix)
    support = matrix.sum(axis=1)
    predicted = matrix.sum(axis=0)
    precision = np.divide(tp, predicted, out=np.zeros(classes), where=predicted > 0)
    recall = np.divide(tp, support, out=np.zeros(classes), where=support > 0)
    f1 = np.divide(
        2 * precision * recall,
        precision + recall,
        out=np.zeros(classes),
        where=(precision + recall) > 0,
    )
    iou = np.divide(
        tp,
        support + predicted - tp,
        out=np.zeros(classes),
        where=(support + predicted - tp) > 0,
    )
    active = (support + predicted) > 0
    return {
        "count": len(truth),
        "accuracy": round(float(tp.sum() / len(truth)), 4),
        "macro_precision": round(float(precision[active].mean()), 4),
        "macro_recall": round(float(recall[active].mean()), 4),
        "macro_f1": round(float(f1[active].mean()), 4),
        "macro_iou": round(float(iou[active].mean()), 4),
        "weighted_f1": round(float(np.average(f1, weights=support)), 4),
        "per_class": {
            str(index): {
                "support": int(support[index]),
                "precision": round(float(precision[index]), 4),
                "recall": round(float(recall[index]), 4),
                "f1": round(float(f1[index]), 4),
                "iou": round(float(iou[index]), 4),
            }
            for index in range(classes)
            if active[index]
        },
        "confusion_matrix": matrix.tolist(),
    }


def grouped_classification(
    records, truth_key="true_class", prediction_key="prediction"
):
    groups = defaultdict(list)
    for record in records:
        groups["all"].append(record)
        groups[str(record["year"])].append(record)
    return {
        key: classification_metrics(
            [r[truth_key] for r in rows], [r[prediction_key] for r in rows]
        )
        for key, rows in groups.items()
    }


def classwise_model_comparison(
    records,
    prediction_keys=("old_class", "new_class", "routed_class"),
    classes=100,
    min_support=20,
):
    """Compare model variants per class and by historical difficulty.

    Per-class F1 and IoU are one-versus-rest scores. Classes below ``min_support``
    remain visible but are excluded from aggregate improvement claims.
    """
    if not records:
        raise ValueError("Cannot compare models on an empty evaluation set")
    if min_support < 1:
        raise ValueError("min_support must be at least 1")

    truth = np.asarray([row["true_class"] for row in records], dtype=np.int64)
    scores = {
        key: classification_metrics(
            truth,
            [row[key] for row in records],
            classes=classes,
        )
        for key in prediction_keys
    }
    baseline_key = prediction_keys[0]
    support = np.bincount(truth, minlength=classes)
    records_by_class = defaultdict(list)
    for record in records:
        records_by_class[record["true_class"]].append(record)
    rows = []
    for class_id in np.flatnonzero(support):
        class_id = int(class_id)
        class_scores = {
            key.removesuffix("_class"): scores[key]["per_class"][str(class_id)]
            for key in prediction_keys
        }
        baseline = class_scores[baseline_key.removesuffix("_class")]
        row = {
            "class_id": class_id,
            "support": int(support[class_id]),
            "sufficient_support": bool(support[class_id] >= min_support),
            "models": class_scores,
            "f1_delta_pp": {
                key.removesuffix("_class"): round(
                    100
                    * (class_scores[key.removesuffix("_class")]["f1"] - baseline["f1"]),
                    2,
                )
                for key in prediction_keys[1:]
            },
            "iou_delta_pp": {
                key.removesuffix("_class"): round(
                    100
                    * (
                        class_scores[key.removesuffix("_class")]["iou"]
                        - baseline["iou"]
                    ),
                    2,
                )
                for key in prediction_keys[1:]
            },
        }
        if "routed_to_new" in records[0]:
            class_records = records_by_class[class_id]
            row["routing"] = {
                "routed_to_new": sum(bool(r["routed_to_new"]) for r in class_records),
                "route_rate": round(
                    sum(bool(r["routed_to_new"]) for r in class_records)
                    / len(class_records),
                    4,
                ),
                "helpful": sum(
                    r["routed_to_new"]
                    and r[baseline_key] != class_id
                    and r[prediction_keys[1]] == class_id
                    for r in class_records
                ),
                "harmful": sum(
                    r["routed_to_new"]
                    and r[baseline_key] == class_id
                    and r[prediction_keys[1]] != class_id
                    for r in class_records
                ),
                "beneficial_available": sum(
                    r[baseline_key] != class_id and r[prediction_keys[1]] == class_id
                    for r in class_records
                ),
                "beneficial_missed": sum(
                    not r["routed_to_new"]
                    and r[baseline_key] != class_id
                    and r[prediction_keys[1]] == class_id
                    for r in class_records
                ),
                "harmful_available": sum(
                    r[baseline_key] == class_id and r[prediction_keys[1]] != class_id
                    for r in class_records
                ),
                "harmful_avoided": sum(
                    not r["routed_to_new"]
                    and r[baseline_key] == class_id
                    and r[prediction_keys[1]] != class_id
                    for r in class_records
                ),
            }
        rows.append(row)

    eligible = [row for row in rows if row["sufficient_support"]]
    comparisons = {}
    for key in prediction_keys[1:]:
        name = key.removesuffix("_class")
        deltas = np.asarray([row["f1_delta_pp"][name] for row in eligible])
        comparisons[name] = {
            "classes_improved": int(np.sum(deltas > 0)),
            "classes_unchanged": int(np.sum(deltas == 0)),
            "classes_worsened": int(np.sum(deltas < 0)),
            "mean_f1_delta_pp": round(float(deltas.mean()), 2) if len(deltas) else None,
            "median_f1_delta_pp": round(float(np.median(deltas)), 2)
            if len(deltas)
            else None,
        }

    ordered = sorted(
        eligible,
        key=lambda row: (
            row["models"][baseline_key.removesuffix("_class")]["f1"],
            row["class_id"],
        ),
    )
    strata = []
    stratum_count = min(4, len(ordered))
    partitions = np.array_split(ordered, stratum_count) if stratum_count else []
    for index, stratum_rows in enumerate(partitions, 1):
        stratum_rows = list(stratum_rows)
        if not stratum_rows:
            continue
        model_means = {
            key.removesuffix("_class"): round(
                float(
                    np.mean(
                        [
                            row["models"][key.removesuffix("_class")]["f1"]
                            for row in stratum_rows
                        ]
                    )
                ),
                4,
            )
            for key in prediction_keys
        }
        strata.append(
            {
                "stratum": f"Q{index}",
                "description": "weakest historical classes"
                if index == 1
                else "strongest historical classes"
                if index == stratum_count
                else "middle historical classes",
                "class_count": len(stratum_rows),
                "class_ids": [row["class_id"] for row in stratum_rows],
                "historical_f1_range": [
                    min(row["models"]["old"]["f1"] for row in stratum_rows),
                    max(row["models"]["old"]["f1"] for row in stratum_rows),
                ],
                "mean_f1": model_means,
                "mean_f1_delta_pp": {
                    name: round(100 * (value - model_means["old"]), 2)
                    for name, value in model_means.items()
                    if name != "old"
                },
            }
        )

    return {
        "minimum_support": min_support,
        "eligible_classes": len(eligible),
        "excluded_low_support_classes": len(rows) - len(eligible),
        "comparison": comparisons,
        "historical_f1_rank_strata": strata,
        "classes": rows,
    }


def binary_metrics(truth, prediction):
    truth = np.asarray(truth, dtype=bool)
    prediction = np.asarray(prediction, dtype=bool)
    tp = int(np.sum(truth & prediction))
    fp = int(np.sum(~truth & prediction))
    fn = int(np.sum(truth & ~prediction))
    return {
        "count": len(truth),
        "tp": tp,
        "fp": fp,
        "fn": fn,
        "precision": round(tp / (tp + fp), 4) if tp + fp else 0.0,
        "recall": round(tp / (tp + fn), 4) if tp + fn else 0.0,
        "f1": round(2 * tp / (2 * tp + fp + fn), 4) if 2 * tp + fp + fn else 0.0,
        "iou": round(tp / (tp + fp + fn), 4) if tp + fp + fn else 0.0,
    }


def topk_map_agreement(first, second, fraction=0.2):
    """Overlap of the top-attended patches. Agreement, not localization quality."""
    first = np.asarray(first)
    second = np.asarray(second)
    count = max(1, round(first.size * fraction))
    first_mask = np.zeros(first.size, dtype=bool)
    second_mask = np.zeros(second.size, dtype=bool)
    first_mask[np.argpartition(first, -count)[-count:]] = True
    second_mask[np.argpartition(second, -count)[-count:]] = True
    return binary_metrics(first_mask, second_mask)



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/patch_mechanism.py
================================================
"""Test whether a trained saliency map changes predictions on fixed image tokens.

This intervention does not retrain the model or tune on future labels. It
changes patch pooling inside the saved classifier while holding its backbone,
class token, and classifier weights fixed. The class-only and patch-only arms
are diagnostic masks, not separately trained model comparisons.
"""

import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch
from tqdm.auto import tqdm

from fyp_drift.data.clear100 import open_dataset, year_loader
from fyp_drift.data.corruptions import SCENARIOS, drift_transform
from fyp_drift.evaluation.metrics import classification_metrics
from fyp_drift.models.vision_transformer import TemporalViT
from fyp_drift.training.temporal_router import seed_everything

DEFAULT_SCENARIOS = (
    "clean",
    "motion_blur",
    "viewpoint_shift",
    "occlusion",
    "realistic_mix",
)


def parse_args(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--run-dir", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--data-root", type=Path, default=Path("data"))
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--year", type=int, default=2014)
    parser.add_argument("--batch-size", type=int, default=16)
    parser.add_argument("--limit", type=int)
    parser.add_argument("--scenarios", nargs="+", choices=SCENARIOS,
                        default=DEFAULT_SCENARIOS)
    parser.add_argument("--role", choices=("historical", "updated"),
                        default="updated")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    if args.batch_size < 1:
        parser.error("--batch-size must be positive")
    if args.limit is not None and args.limit < 1:
        parser.error("--limit must be positive")
    if args.output.exists():
        parser.error("--output already exists; use a new path")
    return args


def _checkpoint(run_dir, config, role):
    if role == "historical":
        return Path(config["base_checkpoint"])
    return run_dir / "models" / "best.pt"


@torch.no_grad()
def evaluate_condition(model, loader, device, classes):
    model.eval()
    truth = []
    predictions = {}
    true_logit_delta = {}
    for images, labels, _ in tqdm(loader, desc="patch mechanism", unit="batch"):
        logits = model.patch_weight_interventions(images.to(device))
        actual = labels.to(device)
        truth.extend(labels.tolist())
        reference_logit = logits["learned"].gather(1, actual[:, None]).squeeze(1)
        for name, values in logits.items():
            predictions.setdefault(name, []).extend(values.argmax(1).cpu().tolist())
            true_logit_delta.setdefault(name, []).extend(
                (values.gather(1, actual[:, None]).squeeze(1) - reference_logit)
                .cpu().tolist()
            )
    actual = np.asarray(truth)
    reference = np.asarray(predictions["learned"])
    results = {}
    for name, values in predictions.items():
        prediction = np.asarray(values)
        metrics = classification_metrics(actual, prediction, classes=classes)
        results[name] = {
            "accuracy": metrics["accuracy"],
            "macro_f1": metrics["macro_f1"],
            "top1_flip_rate_vs_learned": float(np.mean(prediction != reference)),
            "learned_correct_intervention_wrong": int(
                np.sum((reference == actual) & (prediction != actual))
            ),
            "learned_wrong_intervention_correct": int(
                np.sum((reference != actual) & (prediction == actual))
            ),
            "mean_true_class_logit_delta_vs_learned": float(
                np.mean(true_logit_delta[name])
            ),
        }
    return {"samples": len(truth), "interventions": results}


def main(argv=None):
    args = parse_args(argv)
    run_dir = args.run_dir.resolve()
    config = json.loads((run_dir / "config.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    if config.get("calibration_protocol") != "disjoint_class_stratified":
        raise ValueError("Use a corrected-split source run")
    if not config.get("long_horizon"):
        raise ValueError("Use the 2010/2011/2012-2014 horizon")
    seed = int(config["seed"])
    seed_everything(seed)
    checkpoint = _checkpoint(run_dir, config, args.role)
    pooling_mode = config.get("pooling_mode", "class_plus_patch")
    state = torch.load(checkpoint, map_location="cpu", weights_only=True)
    classes = state["classifier.weight"].shape[0]
    device = "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    model = TemporalViT("xai", classes, pooling_mode=pooling_mode).to(device)
    model.load_state_dict(state)
    if args.role == "historical" and config.get(
        "initialize_saliency_from_uniform", False
    ):
        with torch.no_grad():
            model.saliency_head[1].weight.zero_()
            model.saliency_head[1].bias.zero_()
    dataset = open_dataset(args.data_root)
    conditions = {}
    for scenario in args.scenarios:
        severity = max(1, args.year - 2011)
        loader, _ = year_loader(
            dataset, "test_source", (args.year,), args.batch_size,
            limit=args.limit, seed=seed,
            image_transform=drift_transform(scenario, severity, seed),
        )
        conditions[scenario] = evaluate_condition(model, loader, device, classes)
        print(json.dumps({"scenario": scenario, "year": args.year,
                          "result": conditions[scenario]}), flush=True)
    report = {
        "source_run": str(run_dir),
        "checkpoint": str(checkpoint),
        "checkpoint_role": args.role,
        "pooling_mode": pooling_mode,
        "training_performed": False,
        "future_labels_used_for_tuning": False,
        "year": args.year,
        "seed": seed,
        "sampling_limit": args.limit,
        "interpretation": (
            "Learned, uniform, permuted, and inverted patch weights share the same tokens, "
            "class token, and classifier. Class-only and patch-only mask one "
            "pathway and are not trained baselines. This tests prediction "
            "dependence on patch weights, not object localization or the "
            "generalization benefit of saliency-guided training."
        ),
        "conditions": conditions,
    }
    args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    args.output.write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/replication_summary.py
================================================
"""Summarise matched architecture screens without pooling different datasets."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import json
from collections import defaultdict
from pathlib import Path
from statistics import fmean, stdev


def parse_args(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Summarise external replication runs.")
    parser.add_argument("inputs", nargs="+", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True)
    return parser.parse_args(argv)


def load_row(path):
    result = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    screen = result["architecture_screen"]
    baseline = screen["baseline"]
    xai = screen["xai"]
    drift = result["drift_detection"]
    return {
        "dataset": result["dataset"],
        "seed": result["seed"],
        "mmd_squared": drift["mmd_squared"],
        "mmd_p": drift["mmd_permutation_p"],
        "domain_auc": drift["domain_classifier_roc_auc"],
        "baseline_id_macro_f1": baseline["in_distribution"]["macro_f1"],
        "baseline_ood_macro_f1": baseline["out_of_distribution"]["macro_f1"],
        "xai_id_macro_f1": xai["in_distribution"]["macro_f1"],
        "xai_ood_macro_f1": xai["out_of_distribution"]["macro_f1"],
        "xai_minus_baseline_ood_points": screen[
            "xai_minus_baseline_ood_macro_f1_points"
        ],
    }


def summarise(rows):
    grouped = defaultdict(list)
    for row in rows:
        grouped[row["dataset"]].append(row)

    summaries = []
    for dataset, dataset_rows in sorted(grouped.items()):
        differences = [row["xai_minus_baseline_ood_points"] for row in dataset_rows]
        summaries.append(
            {
                "dataset": dataset,
                "runs": len(dataset_rows),
                "baseline_ood_macro_f1_mean": fmean(
                    row["baseline_ood_macro_f1"] for row in dataset_rows
                ),
                "xai_ood_macro_f1_mean": fmean(
                    row["xai_ood_macro_f1"] for row in dataset_rows
                ),
                "xai_minus_baseline_ood_points_mean": fmean(differences),
                "xai_minus_baseline_ood_points_std": (
                    stdev(differences) if len(differences) > 1 else None
                ),
            }
        )
    return summaries


def main(argv=None):
    args = parse_args(argv)
    rows = [load_row(path) for path in args.inputs]
    payload = {"runs": rows, "dataset_summaries": summarise(rows)}

    args.output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (args.output / "multi_dataset_summary.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(payload, indent=2), encoding="utf-8"
    )
    with (args.output / "multi_dataset_runs.csv").open(
        "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8"
    ) as handle:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(handle, fieldnames=rows[0])
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(rows)
    print(json.dumps(payload["dataset_summaries"], indent=2))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/router_drift.py
================================================
"""Reevaluate saved historical and updated ViTs without retraining them."""

import argparse
import csv
import json
from pathlib import Path

import torch
from torch.utils.data import DataLoader, Subset

from fyp_drift.data.clear100 import balanced_indices, open_dataset
from fyp_drift.data.corruptions import SCENARIOS, drift_transform, scenario_components
from fyp_drift.training.temporal_router import (
    build_router,
    evaluate_stream,
    seed_everything,
)


def parse_args(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Evaluate an existing router checkpoint under named drift conditions."
    )
    parser.add_argument("--run-dir", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--data-root", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument(
        "--scenarios", nargs="+", choices=SCENARIOS, default=("realistic_mix",)
    )
    parser.add_argument("--batch-size", type=int)
    parser.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=0)
    return parser.parse_args(argv)


def _write_decisions(path, rows):
    if not rows:
        return
    with path.open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(handle, fieldnames=list(rows[0]))
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(rows)


def main(argv=None):
    args = parse_args(argv)
    config = json.loads((args.run_dir / "config.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    policy = json.loads(
        (args.run_dir / "metrics" / "router_policy.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    )
    seed = int(config.get("seed", 42))
    seed_everything(seed)

    device = "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    profile = config.get("adaptation_profile", "standard")
    router, _, _, _ = build_router(
        Path(config["base_checkpoint"]), device, profile,
        initialize_saliency_from_uniform=config.get(
            "initialize_saliency_from_uniform", False
        ),
    )
    updated_state = torch.load(
        args.run_dir / "models" / "best.pt", map_location=device, weights_only=True
    )
    router.new.load_state_dict(updated_state)

    data_root = args.data_root or Path(config.get("data_root", "data"))
    dataset = open_dataset(data_root)
    year_column = dataset.metadata_fields.index("year")
    test_split = "test_source" if config.get("long_horizon") else "test"
    test_years = tuple(config.get("test_years", (2014,)))
    limit_test = config.get("limit_test")
    batch_size = args.batch_size or int(config.get("batch_size", 8))

    results = {
        "source_run": str(args.run_dir),
        "checkpoint": "models/best.pt",
        "policy": "metrics/router_policy.json",
        "training_performed": False,
        "scenarios": {},
    }
    all_decisions = []
    for scenario in args.scenarios:
        results["scenarios"][scenario] = {}
        for severity, year in enumerate(test_years, 1):
            subset = dataset.get_subset(
                test_split,
                transform=drift_transform(scenario, severity, seed),
            )
            indices = torch.where(
                subset.metadata_array[:, year_column] == year - 2002
            )[0].tolist()
            if limit_test:
                indices = balanced_indices(
                    subset.rows, indices, min(int(limit_test), len(indices)), seed
                )
            loader = DataLoader(
                Subset(subset, indices),
                batch_size=batch_size,
                num_workers=args.workers,
            )
            report, decisions = evaluate_stream(
                router, loader, policy, device, year_column
            )
            for dataset_index, decision in zip(indices, decisions):
                all_decisions.append(
                    {
                        "scenario": scenario,
                        "severity": 0 if scenario == "clean" else severity,
                        "dataset_index": dataset_index,
                        "drift_components": "+".join(
                            scenario_components(scenario, dataset_index, severity, seed)
                        ),
                        **decision,
                    }
                )
            results["scenarios"][scenario][str(year)] = {
                "severity": 0 if scenario == "clean" else severity,
                "results": report,
            }
            summary = report["all"]
            print(
                json.dumps(
                    {
                        "scenario": scenario,
                        "year": year,
                        "historical_macro_f1": summary["old"]["macro_f1"],
                        "updated_macro_f1": summary["new"]["macro_f1"],
                        "routed_macro_f1": summary["routed"]["macro_f1"],
                        "helpful_routes": summary["helpful_routes"],
                        "harmful_routes": summary["harmful_routes"],
                    }
                ),
                flush=True,
            )

    args.output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (args.output / "router_drift_evaluation.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(results, indent=2), encoding="utf-8"
    )
    _write_decisions(args.output / "routing_decisions.csv", all_decisions)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/router_feature_ablation.py
================================================
"""Compare router evidence sets on one frozen, corrected-split model pair.

The source run supplies the historical/updated checkpoints and the logged future
features. Only the disjoint 2011 calibration images require model inference.
Neither model is trained or modified by this command.
"""

import argparse
import csv
import hashlib
import json
from collections import defaultdict
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch
from tqdm.auto import tqdm

from fyp_drift.data.clear100 import open_dataset
from fyp_drift.models.routing import FEATURE_SETS, FEATURES, feature_tensor, fit_policy
from fyp_drift.training.temporal_router import (
    build_router,
    build_temporal_loaders,
    seed_everything,
    validate_historical_checkpoint,
)

DEFAULT_COVERAGES = (0.10, 0.25, 0.50)


def parse_args(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Refit three routing feature sets on one frozen model pair."
    )
    parser.add_argument("--run-dir", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--data-root", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument(
        "--coverages",
        type=float,
        nargs="+",
        default=DEFAULT_COVERAGES,
        help="Fixed shares of disagreeing predictions routed in the ranking diagnostic.",
    )
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    if any(not 0 <= value <= 1 for value in args.coverages):
        parser.error("Every coverage must be between 0 and 1")
    return args


def _sha256(path):
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def _source_config(run_dir):
    config_path = run_dir / "config.json"
    config = json.loads(config_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    if config.get("calibration_protocol") != "disjoint_class_stratified":
        raise ValueError("Source run must use disjoint class-stratified calibration")
    if config.get("reuse_calibration_for_selection"):
        raise ValueError("Source run reused calibration labels for checkpoint selection")
    if not config.get("long_horizon"):
        raise ValueError("Source run must use the 2010/2011/2012-2014 long horizon")
    return config


@torch.no_grad()
def _calibration_arrays(router, loader, device):
    router.eval()
    features, truth, old, new = [], [], [], []
    for images, labels, _ in tqdm(loader, desc="2011 calibration", unit="batch"):
        comparison = router.compare(images.to(device))
        features.append(feature_tensor(comparison).cpu().numpy())
        truth.append(labels.numpy())
        old.append(comparison["old_logits"].argmax(1).cpu().numpy())
        new.append(comparison["new_logits"].argmax(1).cpu().numpy())
    return tuple(np.concatenate(parts) for parts in (features, truth, old, new))


def _fit_policies(features, truth, old, new):
    beneficial = ((old != truth) & (new == truth)).astype(np.int64)
    return {
        name: fit_policy(
            features, beneficial, old, new, truth, feature_names=feature_names
        )
        for name, feature_names in FEATURE_SETS.items()
    }


def _read_future_rows(path):
    required = {
        "scenario", "year", "dataset_index", "true_class", "old_class",
        "new_class", "routed_to_new", *FEATURES,
    }
    with path.open(newline="", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        reader = csv.DictReader(handle)
        missing = required - set(reader.fieldnames or ())
        if missing:
            raise ValueError(f"Future decisions are missing columns: {sorted(missing)}")
        groups = defaultdict(list)
        seen = set()
        for row in reader:
            key = (row["scenario"], int(row["year"]), int(row["dataset_index"]))
            if key in seen:
                raise ValueError(f"Duplicate future decision: {key}")
            seen.add(key)
            groups[key[:2]].append(row)
    if not groups:
        raise ValueError("Future decision log is empty")
    return groups


def _arrays(rows):
    features = np.asarray(
        [[float(row[name]) for name in FEATURES] for row in rows], dtype=np.float32
    )
    truth = np.asarray([int(row["true_class"]) for row in rows], dtype=np.int64)
    old = np.asarray([int(row["old_class"]) for row in rows], dtype=np.int64)
    new = np.asarray([int(row["new_class"]) for row in rows], dtype=np.int64)
    recorded = np.asarray(
        [row["routed_to_new"].lower() == "true" for row in rows], dtype=bool
    )
    return features, truth, old, new, recorded


def _policy_scores(features, policy):
    """Reproduce apply_policy's float32 score from a logged feature matrix."""
    selected = [FEATURES.index(name) for name in policy["feature_names"]]
    tensor = torch.from_numpy(features[:, selected])
    mean = tensor.new_tensor(policy["mean"])
    scale = tensor.new_tensor(policy["scale"])
    weights = tensor.new_tensor(policy["coefficients"])
    return torch.sigmoid(
        (((tensor - mean) / scale) * weights).sum(1) + policy["intercept"]
    ).numpy()


def _native_route(features, old, new, policy):
    probabilities = _policy_scores(features, policy)
    eligible = old != new
    if policy["saliency_floor"] is not None:
        eligible &= features[:, FEATURES.index("saliency_js")] >= policy[
            "saliency_floor"
        ]
    return probabilities, eligible & (
        probabilities >= policy["probability_threshold"]
    )


def _policy_reproduction(features, old, new, source, refitted):
    if source["feature_names"] != refitted["feature_names"]:
        raise ValueError("Refitted and source policies use different feature sets")
    _, source_routes = _native_route(features, old, new, source)
    _, refitted_routes = _native_route(features, old, new, refitted)
    source_floor, refitted_floor = source["saliency_floor"], refitted["saliency_floor"]
    floors_match = (source_floor is None and refitted_floor is None) or (
        source_floor is not None
        and refitted_floor is not None
        and abs(source_floor - refitted_floor) <= 1e-4
    )
    parameter_differences = [
        abs(source["intercept"] - refitted["intercept"]),
        *np.abs(np.asarray(source["mean"]) - refitted["mean"]),
        *np.abs(np.asarray(source["scale"]) - refitted["scale"]),
        *np.abs(np.asarray(source["coefficients"]) - refitted["coefficients"]),
    ]
    return {
        "feature_names_match": True,
        "max_parameter_abs_difference": float(max(parameter_differences)),
        "threshold_match": bool(
            source["probability_threshold"] == refitted["probability_threshold"]
        ),
        "floor_match_within_1e-4": bool(floors_match),
        "calibration_route_mismatches": int(np.sum(source_routes != refitted_routes)),
        "calibration_rows": len(features),
    }


def _fixed_coverage_route(probabilities, old, new, coverage):
    """Use a label-free route quota and a stable probability ranking."""
    candidates = np.flatnonzero(old != new)
    count = round(len(candidates) * coverage)
    routed = np.zeros(len(old), dtype=bool)
    if count:
        order = np.lexsort((candidates, -probabilities[candidates]))
        routed[candidates[order[:count]]] = True
    return routed


def _summary(truth, old, new, routed, num_classes):
    prediction = np.where(routed, new, old)

    def score(values):
        support = np.bincount(truth, minlength=num_classes)
        predicted = np.bincount(values, minlength=num_classes)
        true_positive = np.bincount(
            truth[truth == values], minlength=num_classes
        )
        denominator = support + predicted
        f1 = np.divide(
            2 * true_positive,
            denominator,
            out=np.zeros(num_classes, dtype=float),
            where=denominator > 0,
        )
        active = denominator > 0
        return {
            "accuracy": float(np.mean(truth == values)),
            "macro_f1": float(f1[active].mean()),
        }

    old_score, new_score, routed_score = score(old), score(new), score(prediction)
    disagreements = int(np.sum(old != new))
    return {
        "samples": len(truth),
        "disagreements": disagreements,
        "routed_to_new": int(np.sum(routed)),
        "disagreement_coverage": float(np.sum(routed) / disagreements)
        if disagreements else 0.0,
        "old": old_score,
        "new": new_score,
        "routed": routed_score,
        "routed_minus_old_f1_pp": 100 * (
            routed_score["macro_f1"] - old_score["macro_f1"]
        ),
        "routed_minus_new_f1_pp": 100 * (
            routed_score["macro_f1"] - new_score["macro_f1"]
        ),
        "beneficial_available": int(np.sum((old != truth) & (new == truth))),
        "beneficial_taken": int(
            np.sum(routed & (old != truth) & (new == truth))
        ),
        "harmful_available": int(np.sum((old == truth) & (new != truth))),
        "harmful_taken": int(np.sum(routed & (old == truth) & (new != truth))),
    }


def _evaluate_groups(groups, policies, source_policy, coverages, num_classes):
    results = {}
    reproduction_mismatches = 0
    total_rows = 0
    for (scenario, year), rows in sorted(groups.items()):
        features, truth, old, new, recorded = _arrays(rows)
        _, reproduced = _native_route(features, old, new, source_policy)
        reproduction_mismatches += int(np.sum(reproduced != recorded))
