        total_rows += len(rows)
        condition = {
            "native_policy": {},
            "fixed_disagreement_coverage": {},
        }
        for name, policy in policies.items():
            probabilities, route = _native_route(features, old, new, policy)
            condition["native_policy"][name] = _summary(
                truth, old, new, route, num_classes
            )
            for coverage in coverages:
                key = f"{coverage:.2f}"
                matched = _fixed_coverage_route(
                    probabilities, old, new, coverage
                )
                condition["fixed_disagreement_coverage"].setdefault(key, {})[
                    name
                ] = _summary(truth, old, new, matched, num_classes)
        results.setdefault(scenario, {})[str(year)] = condition
    return results, {"mismatches": reproduction_mismatches, "rows": total_rows}


def main(argv=None):
    args = parse_args(argv)
    if args.output.exists():
        raise FileExistsError(f"Ablation output already exists: {args.output}")
    run_dir = args.run_dir.resolve()
    config = _source_config(run_dir)
    config_path = run_dir / "config.json"
    decisions_path = run_dir / "logs" / "drift_routing_decisions.csv"
    source_policy_path = run_dir / "metrics" / "router_policy.json"
    updated_checkpoint = run_dir / "models" / "best.pt"
    for path in (decisions_path, source_policy_path, updated_checkpoint):
        if not path.is_file():
            raise FileNotFoundError(path)

    seed = int(config["seed"])
    seed_everything(seed)
    base_checkpoint = Path(config["base_checkpoint"])
    shared_uniform = bool(config.get("initialize_saliency_from_uniform", False))
    validate_historical_checkpoint(
        base_checkpoint, True, allow_uniform=shared_uniform
    )
    data_root = args.data_root or Path(config.get("data_root", "data"))
    device = "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    dataset = open_dataset(data_root)
    loaders = build_temporal_loaders(dataset, argparse.Namespace(**config))
    if loaders.calibration_protocol != "disjoint_class_stratified":
        raise ValueError("Reconstructed calibration split is not disjoint")
    router, _, num_classes, _ = build_router(
        base_checkpoint, device, config["adaptation_profile"],
        initialize_saliency_from_uniform=shared_uniform,
    )
    router.new.load_state_dict(
        torch.load(updated_checkpoint, map_location=device, weights_only=True)
    )
    features, truth, old, new = _calibration_arrays(
        router, loaders.calibration, device
    )
    policies = _fit_policies(features, truth, old, new)
    groups = _read_future_rows(decisions_path)
    source_policy = json.loads(source_policy_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    source_feature_set = config.get("router_features", "combined")
    if source_feature_set not in policies:
        raise ValueError(f"Unknown source router feature set: {source_feature_set}")
    policy_reproduction = _policy_reproduction(
        features, old, new, source_policy, policies[source_feature_set]
    )
    if (
        policy_reproduction["calibration_route_mismatches"]
        or not policy_reproduction["threshold_match"]
        or not policy_reproduction["floor_match_within_1e-4"]
        or policy_reproduction["max_parameter_abs_difference"] > 1e-4
    ):
        raise ValueError(
            "Refitted policy does not reproduce the source policy on 2011 "
            f"calibration rows: {policy_reproduction}"
        )
    conditions, reproduction = _evaluate_groups(
        groups, policies, source_policy, args.coverages, num_classes
    )
    if reproduction["mismatches"]:
        raise ValueError(
            "Logged future features fail to reproduce the source policy decisions "
            f"({reproduction['mismatches']}/{reproduction['rows']}); check provenance"
        )

    output = {
        "source_run": str(run_dir),
        "training_performed": False,
        "calibration_protocol": loaders.calibration_protocol,
        "calibration_samples": len(truth),
        "calibration_beneficial_switches": int(
            np.sum((old != truth) & (new == truth))
        ),
        "source_policy_refit_reproduction": policy_reproduction,
        "model_pair": {
            "historical_checkpoint": str(base_checkpoint),
            "updated_checkpoint": str(updated_checkpoint),
        },
        "future_features": {
            "path": str(decisions_path),
            "sha256": _sha256(decisions_path),
            "rounded_decimals": 6,
            "source_policy_reproduction": reproduction,
            "replay_exact": reproduction["mismatches"] == 0,
        },
        "calibration_config_sha256": _sha256(config_path),
        "policies": policies,
        "fixed_coverage_note": (
            "Transductive diagnostic ranking at a prespecified share of old/new "
            "disagreements; all feature sets route the same count, without "
            "saliency floors or future-label threshold fitting. It uses the full "
            "unlabelled test group to set the quota, so it is not a deployable "
            "streaming policy. It is separate from the native policy."
        ),
        "conditions": conditions,
    }
    args.output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
    (args.output / "router_feature_ablation.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(output, indent=2), encoding="utf-8"
    )
    print(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "output": str(args.output),
                "calibration_samples": len(truth),
                "source_policy_refit_reproduction": policy_reproduction,
                "source_policy_reproduction": reproduction,
                "conditions": {
                    scenario: list(years) for scenario, years in conditions.items()
                },
            },
            indent=2,
        ),
        flush=True,
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/saliency.py
================================================
"""Summarize whether transparent saliency features correspond to useful routes."""

import argparse
import csv
import json
from collections import defaultdict
from pathlib import Path

import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import numpy as np

FEATURES = (
    "saliency_js",
    "confidence_gain",
    "entropy_reduction",
    "evidence_gain",
    "route_probability",
)


def outcome(row):
    old_ok = int(row["old_class"]) == int(row["true_class"])
    new_ok = int(row["new_class"]) == int(row["true_class"])
    routed = row["routed_to_new"].lower() == "true"
    if routed and not old_ok and new_ok:
        return "helpful"
    if routed and old_ok and not new_ok:
        return "harmful"
    if routed:
        return "neutral_routed"
    return "kept_old"


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--run-dir", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    source = args.run_dir / "logs" / "drift_routing_decisions.csv"
    if not source.is_file():
        source = args.run_dir / "logs" / "routing_decisions.csv"
    with source.open(newline="", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        rows = list(csv.DictReader(handle))
    grouped = defaultdict(list)
    for row in rows:
        row["outcome"] = outcome(row)
        grouped[
            (row.get("scenario", "clean"), int(row["year"]), row["outcome"])
        ].append(row)
    summary = []
    for (scenario, year, result), values in sorted(grouped.items()):
        entry = {
            "scenario": scenario,
            "year": year,
            "outcome": result,
            "count": len(values),
        }
        for feature in FEATURES:
            numbers = np.asarray([float(row[feature]) for row in values])
            entry[f"mean_{feature}"] = round(float(numbers.mean()), 6)
            entry[f"median_{feature}"] = round(float(np.median(numbers)), 6)
        summary.append(entry)
    args.output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (args.output / "saliency_route_analysis.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(summary, indent=2), encoding="utf-8"
    )
    with (args.output / "saliency_route_analysis.csv").open(
        "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8"
    ) as handle:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(handle, fieldnames=list(summary[0]))
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(summary)

    combined_2014 = [
        row
        for row in rows
        if row.get("scenario") == "combined" and int(row["year"]) == 2014
    ]
    labels = ("helpful", "harmful", "neutral_routed", "kept_old")
    headline = {
        "scenario": "combined",
        "year": 2014,
        "rows": len(combined_2014),
        "outcomes": {},
    }
    for label in labels:
        selected = [row for row in combined_2014 if outcome(row) == label]
        headline["outcomes"][label] = {
            "count": len(selected),
            **(
                {
                    f"mean_{feature}": round(
                        float(np.mean([float(row[feature]) for row in selected])), 6
                    )
                    for feature in FEATURES
                }
                if selected
                else {}
            ),
        }
    helpful = headline["outcomes"]["helpful"]["count"]
    harmful = headline["outcomes"]["harmful"]["count"]
    headline["helpful_to_harmful_ratio"] = (
        round(helpful / harmful, 4) if harmful else None
    )
    (args.output / "saliency_route_headline.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(headline, indent=2), encoding="utf-8"
    )
    figure, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(9, 3.6))
    for axis, feature, title in zip(
        axes,
        ("saliency_js", "route_probability"),
        ("Saliency divergence", "Logistic routing score"),
    ):
        values = [
            [float(row[feature]) for row in combined_2014 if outcome(row) == label]
            for label in labels
        ]
        axis.boxplot(
            values,
            tick_labels=[label.replace("_", "\n") for label in labels],
            showfliers=False,
        )
        axis.set_title(f"2014 combined drift: {title}")
        axis.grid(axis="y", alpha=0.25)
    figure.tight_layout()
    figure.savefig(args.output / "saliency_route_outcomes.png", dpi=180)
    print(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "rows": len(rows),
                "groups": len(summary),
                "output": str(args.output),
                "combined_2014": headline,
            },
            indent=2,
        )
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/saliency_adapters.py
================================================
"""Saliency adapters on a frozen DINOv2-L: does saliency help when it decides which patches are read?

The backbone stays frozen; frozen final patch tokens are cached once per condition. Small
heads are then adapted on the 2014-15 adaptation set, checkpointed on adapt-val, and scored
on the 2016-17 test half. Every head starts from the historical linear head.

Arms (differ only in how patch tokens are pooled before the linear head):
  mean            equal weights                                       control
  evidence        top-40% patches by the frozen model's evidence for  pure saliency
                  its own predicted class (no labels at test time)
  shuffled        the same number of random patches                   control for evidence
  attention       learned per-patch weights (small MLP + softmax)     plain saliency adapter
  guided          attention plus a KL pull toward the frozen evidence saliency-guided adapter
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch
from torch import nn
from torch.nn import functional as F

from fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_dinov2l_study import (
    DEFAULT_CACHE, DEFAULT_MANIFEST, SALIENT_FRACTION, Backbone, cluster_bootstrap, image_cache, image_loader,
    load_rows, log, scores, study_splits, write_json,
)

ARMS = ("mean", "evidence", "shuffled", "attention", "guided")
SPLITS = ("adapt_train", "adapt_val", "future_test")


def token_cache(backbone, cache, rows, path, cloud_alpha, workers):
    """fp16 memmap of frozen final patch tokens, shape (images, patches, width)."""
    path = Path(path)
    done = path.with_suffix(".done")
    if done.exists():
        return np.load(path, mmap_mode="r")
    tokens = None
    start = 0
    with torch.no_grad():
        for images, _, _ in image_loader(cache, rows, 64, workers, cloud_alpha):
            with torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=torch.float16):
                batch = backbone(images.cuda(non_blocking=True))[:, 1:].half().cpu().numpy()
            if tokens is None:
                tokens = np.lib.format.open_memmap(path, mode="w+", dtype=np.float16, shape=(len(rows),) + batch.shape[1:])
            tokens[start:start + len(batch)] = batch
            start += len(batch)
    tokens.flush()
    done.write_text("ok")
    return np.load(path, mmap_mode="r")


class PooledHead(nn.Module):
    """Linear head over a weighted patch average; the weighting is the arm."""

    def __init__(self, arm, head_state, width, classes):
        super().__init__()
        self.arm = arm
        self.frozen = nn.Linear(width, classes)
        self.frozen.load_state_dict(head_state)
        self.frozen.requires_grad_(False)
        self.classifier = nn.Linear(width, classes)
        self.classifier.load_state_dict(head_state)
        self.scorer = nn.Sequential(nn.LayerNorm(width), nn.Linear(width, 128), nn.GELU(), nn.Linear(128, 1)) if arm in ("attention", "guided") else None

    def evidence(self, tokens):
        """Frozen-model evidence of each patch for the frozen model's own predicted class."""
        with torch.no_grad():
            predicted = self.frozen(tokens.mean(1)).argmax(1)
            return (tokens * self.frozen.weight[predicted][:, None, :]).sum(-1)

    def weights(self, tokens, generator=None):
        patches = tokens.shape[1]
        if self.arm == "mean":
            return torch.full(tokens.shape[:2], 1.0 / patches, device=tokens.device)
        if self.scorer is not None:
            return self.scorer(tokens).squeeze(-1).softmax(1)
        salient = round(SALIENT_FRACTION * patches)
        weights = torch.zeros(tokens.shape[:2], device=tokens.device).scatter_(1, self.evidence(tokens).topk(salient, 1).indices, 1.0 / salient)
        if self.arm == "shuffled":
            weights = weights.gather(1, torch.rand(weights.shape, generator=generator).argsort(1).to(weights.device))
        return weights

    def forward(self, tokens, generator=None):
        weights = self.weights(tokens, generator)
        return self.classifier((weights[..., None] * tokens).sum(1)), weights


def guidance_loss(model, tokens, weights):
    """KL(frozen-evidence distribution || learned weights), evidence standardised per image."""
    evidence = model.evidence(tokens)
    target = ((evidence - evidence.mean(1, keepdim=True)) / evidence.std(1, keepdim=True).clamp_min(1e-6)).softmax(1)
    return F.kl_div(weights.clamp_min(1e-8).log(), target, reduction="batchmean")


def batches(tokens, labels, batch_size, shuffle_seed=None):
    order = np.random.default_rng(shuffle_seed).permutation(len(labels)) if shuffle_seed is not None else np.arange(len(labels))
    for start in range(0, len(order), batch_size):
        chosen = np.sort(order[start:start + batch_size])
        yield torch.from_numpy(np.asarray(tokens[chosen])).cuda().float(), torch.from_numpy(labels[chosen]).cuda()


@torch.no_grad()
def predict(model, tokens, labels, generator):
    model.eval()
    return np.concatenate([model(x, generator)[0].argmax(1).cpu().numpy() for x, _ in batches(tokens, labels, 256)])


def train_arm(arm, seed, head_state, width, classes, data, args):
    torch.manual_seed(seed)
    model = PooledHead(arm, head_state, width, classes).cuda()
    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW([p for p in model.parameters() if p.requires_grad], lr=args.lr, weight_decay=1e-4)
    best = {"val_f1": -1.0}
    for epoch in range(args.epochs):
        model.train()
        generator = torch.Generator().manual_seed(seed * 1000 + epoch)
        for tokens, targets in batches(*data["adapt_train"], args.batch, shuffle_seed=seed * 1000 + epoch):
            logits, weights = model(tokens, generator)
            loss = F.cross_entropy(logits, targets)
            if arm == "guided":
                loss = loss + args.guide_weight * guidance_loss(model, tokens, weights)
            optimizer.zero_grad()
            loss.backward()
            optimizer.step()
        val_f1 = scores(data["adapt_val"][1], predict(model, *data["adapt_val"], torch.Generator().manual_seed(1)), classes)["macro_f1"]
        if val_f1 > best["val_f1"]:
            best = {"val_f1": val_f1, "epoch": epoch + 1, "state": {k: v.clone() for k, v in model.state_dict().items()}}
    model.load_state_dict(best["state"])
    predictions = predict(model, *data["future_test"], torch.Generator().manual_seed(2))
    return {"arm": arm, "seed": seed, "best_epoch": best["epoch"], "adapt_val_macro_f1": best["val_f1"],
            "future_test": scores(data["future_test"][1], predictions, classes), "predictions": predictions.tolist()}


def main(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    parser.add_argument("--run", type=Path, default=Path("/mnt/sdd/FYP/runs/today/main"))
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, default=Path("/mnt/sdd/FYP/runs/today/saliency_adapters"))
    parser.add_argument("--tokens", type=Path, default=Path("/mnt/sdd/FYP/cache/adapter_tokens"))
    parser.add_argument("--backbone", default="facebook/dinov2-large")
    parser.add_argument("--seeds", type=int, nargs="+", default=[42, 43, 44])
    parser.add_argument("--epochs", type=int, default=15)
    parser.add_argument("--batch", type=int, default=128)
    parser.add_argument("--lr", type=float, default=1e-3)
    parser.add_argument("--guide-weight", type=float, default=1.0)
    parser.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=2)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    args.output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    args.tokens.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    rows, classes = load_rows(DEFAULT_MANIFEST)
    splits = study_splits(rows)
    cache = image_cache(rows, DEFAULT_CACHE, args.workers)
    head_state = torch.load(args.run / "head.pt", weights_only=True)
    cloud_alpha = json.loads((args.run / "calib.json").read_text())["selected_alpha"]
    width = head_state["weight"].shape[1]
    groups = np.array([r["group"] for r in splits["future_test"]])
    backbone = None
    results = []
    for condition, alpha in (("clean", 0.0), ("cloud", cloud_alpha)):
        data = {}
        for split in SPLITS:
            path = args.tokens / f"{condition}_{split}.npy"
            if not path.with_suffix(".done").exists() and backbone is None:
                backbone = Backbone(args.backbone).cuda().eval()
            data[split] = (token_cache(backbone, cache, splits[split], path, alpha, args.workers), np.array([r["class_id"] for r in splits[split]]))
            log("tokens ready", condition, split)
        for seed in args.seeds:
            for arm in ARMS:
                path = args.output / f"{condition}_{arm}_s{seed}.json"
                if path.exists():
                    results.append(json.loads(path.read_text()))
                    continue
                result = {**train_arm(arm, seed, head_state, width, classes, data, args), "condition": condition}
                write_json(path, result)
                results.append(result)
                log(f"{condition} {arm} s{seed}: val={result['adapt_val_macro_f1']:.4f} test={result['future_test']['macro_f1']:.4f}")
    labels = np.array([r["class_id"] for r in splits["future_test"]])
    summary = {"means": {}, "paired": []}
    for condition in ("clean", "cloud"):
        for arm in ARMS:
            f1 = [r["future_test"]["macro_f1"] for r in results if r["condition"] == condition and r["arm"] == arm]
            summary["means"][f"{condition}/{arm}"] = {"seeds": len(f1), "mean": float(np.mean(f1)), "sd": float(np.std(f1, ddof=1)) if len(f1) > 1 else None}
        for a, b in (("evidence", "shuffled"), ("evidence", "mean"), ("guided", "attention"), ("attention", "mean"), ("guided", "mean")):
            for seed in args.seeds:
                ra = next(r for r in results if (r["condition"], r["arm"], r["seed"]) == (condition, a, seed))
                rb = next(r for r in results if (r["condition"], r["arm"], r["seed"]) == (condition, b, seed))
                difference = (np.array(ra["predictions"]) == labels).astype(float) - (np.array(rb["predictions"]) == labels).astype(float)
                summary["paired"].append({"condition": condition, "a": a, "b": b, "seed": seed,
                                          "delta_macro_f1": ra["future_test"]["macro_f1"] - rb["future_test"]["macro_f1"],
                                          "delta_accuracy": cluster_bootstrap(difference, groups, repetitions=1000)})
    write_json(args.output / "summary.json", summary)
    log("summary", json.dumps(summary["means"], indent=1))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/saliency_attention_study.py
================================================
"""Saliency-essential attention adapters on a frozen DINOv2-L, with faithfulness and grounding tests.

Frozen final patch tokens are reused from fyp_drift.evaluation.saliency_adapters. Every arm
is a small attention-pooling head over frozen tokens, initialised from the historical
linear head, adapted on 2014-15 images, checkpointed on adapt-val, scored on the 2016-17
test half. Saliency z is the frozen model's per-patch evidence for its own predicted class,
standardised per image, so no labels are needed at test time.

Arms (each saliency arm has a *_shuffled twin that receives z permuted within the image):
  attention  learned logits s(x)                        w = softmax(s)
  guided     attention + KL(softmax(z) || w)            saliency as a soft target
  prior      w = softmax(beta * z + s), s starts at 0    saliency is the starting attention
  gated      w ~ softmax(s) * sigmoid(gamma * z + delta) saliency gates what can be read
  input      w = softmax(s([token, z]))                 saliency is an input feature

Tests on the test half, per arm and seed:
  faithfulness  accuracy and prediction flips when the learned weights are replaced by
                uniform or within-image shuffled weights, and when the top / bottom / random
                10, 25, 50% of weighted patches are deleted
  grounding     attention mass inside the labelled fMoW object box versus the box's patch
                share, pointing-game hit rate, and (cloud condition) mass on cloud patches
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch
from torch import nn
from torch.nn import functional as F

from fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_dinov2l_study import (
    DEFAULT_MANIFEST, cloud_mask, cluster_bootstrap, load_rows, log, scores, study_splits, write_json,
)
from fyp_drift.evaluation.saliency_adapters import SPLITS, batches

SALIENCY_ARMS = ("guided", "prior", "gated", "input")
ARMS = ("attention",) + SALIENCY_ARMS + tuple(f"{a}_shuffled" for a in SALIENCY_ARMS)
from fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_dinov2l_study import IMAGE_SIZE
import os
GRID = int(os.environ.get("FYP_GRID", IMAGE_SIZE // 14))  # v2 pools 2x2 tokens -> 10x10 grid


class SaliencyAttentionHead(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, arm, head_state, width, classes):
        super().__init__()
        self.kind, self.shuffled = arm.replace("_shuffled", ""), arm.endswith("_shuffled")
        self.frozen = nn.Linear(width, classes)
        self.frozen.load_state_dict(head_state)
        self.frozen.requires_grad_(False)
        self.classifier = nn.Linear(width, classes)
        self.classifier.load_state_dict(head_state)
        inputs = width + (1 if self.kind == "input" else 0)
        self.scorer = nn.Sequential(nn.LayerNorm(inputs), nn.Linear(inputs, 128), nn.GELU(), nn.Linear(128, 1))
        if self.kind == "prior":
            nn.init.zeros_(self.scorer[-1].weight)
            nn.init.zeros_(self.scorer[-1].bias)
            self.beta = nn.Parameter(torch.tensor(1.0))
        if self.kind == "gated":
            self.gamma, self.delta = nn.Parameter(torch.tensor(2.0)), nn.Parameter(torch.tensor(0.0))

    def saliency(self, tokens, generator):
        """Standardised frozen evidence for the frozen prediction; permuted within image for *_shuffled."""
        with torch.no_grad():
            predicted = self.frozen(tokens.mean(1)).argmax(1)
            evidence = (tokens * self.frozen.weight[predicted][:, None, :]).sum(-1)
            z = (evidence - evidence.mean(1, keepdim=True)) / evidence.std(1, keepdim=True).clamp_min(1e-6)
            if self.shuffled:
                z = z.gather(1, torch.rand(z.shape, generator=generator).argsort(1).to(z.device))
        return z

    def weights(self, tokens, z):
        if self.kind == "input":
            return self.scorer(torch.cat([tokens, z[..., None]], -1)).squeeze(-1).softmax(1)
        logits = self.scorer(tokens).squeeze(-1)
        if self.kind == "prior":
            return (self.beta * z + logits).softmax(1)
        if self.kind == "gated":
            gated = logits.softmax(1) * torch.sigmoid(self.gamma * z + self.delta)
            return gated / gated.sum(1, keepdim=True).clamp_min(1e-8)
        return logits.softmax(1)

    def classify(self, tokens, weights):
        return self.classifier((weights[..., None] * tokens).sum(1))

    def forward(self, tokens, generator):
        z = self.saliency(tokens, generator)
        weights = self.weights(tokens, z)
        return self.classify(tokens, weights), weights, z


def train(arm, seed, head_state, width, classes, data, guide_weight, args):
    torch.manual_seed(seed)
    model = SaliencyAttentionHead(arm, head_state, width, classes).cuda()
    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW([p for p in model.parameters() if p.requires_grad], lr=args.lr, weight_decay=1e-4)
    best = {"val_f1": -1.0}
    for epoch in range(args.epochs):
        model.train()
        generator = torch.Generator().manual_seed(seed * 1000 + epoch)
        for tokens, targets in batches(*data["adapt_train"], args.batch, shuffle_seed=seed * 1000 + epoch):
            logits, weights, z = model(tokens, generator)
            loss = F.cross_entropy(logits, targets)
            if model.kind == "guided":
                loss = loss + guide_weight * F.kl_div(weights.clamp_min(1e-8).log(), z.softmax(1), reduction="batchmean")
            optimizer.zero_grad()
            loss.backward()
            optimizer.step()
        val = scores(data["adapt_val"][1], infer(model, data["adapt_val"][0], data["adapt_val"][1])[0], classes)["macro_f1"]
        if val > best["val_f1"]:
            best = {"val_f1": val, "epoch": epoch + 1, "state": {k: v.clone() for k, v in model.state_dict().items()}}
    model.load_state_dict(best["state"])
    return model.eval(), best


@torch.no_grad()
def infer(model, tokens, labels):
    """Predictions, attention weights and saliency for every image, in order."""
    model.eval()
    generator = torch.Generator().manual_seed(7)
    predictions, weights, saliency = [], [], []
    for x, _ in batches(tokens, labels, 256):
        logits, w, z = model(x, generator)
        predictions.append(logits.argmax(1).cpu())
        weights.append(w.cpu())
        saliency.append(z.cpu())
    return torch.cat(predictions).numpy(), torch.cat(weights), torch.cat(saliency)


@torch.no_grad()
def faithfulness(model, tokens, labels, weights, predictions):
    """Accuracy and flip rate when the head reads replaced or partially deleted weights."""
    generator = torch.Generator().manual_seed(11)
    results = {}
    def replaced(name, transform):
        out = []
        for start, (x, _) in zip(range(0, len(labels), 256), batches(tokens, labels, 256)):
            out.append(model.classify(x, transform(weights[start:start + len(x)].cuda())).argmax(1).cpu())
        p = torch.cat(out).numpy()
        results[name] = {"accuracy": float((p == labels).mean()), "flip_rate": float((p != predictions).mean())}
    replaced("uniform", lambda w: torch.full_like(w, 1.0 / w.shape[1]))
    replaced("shuffled_weights", lambda w: w.gather(1, torch.rand(w.shape, generator=generator).argsort(1).to(w.device)))
    for fraction in (0.1, 0.25, 0.5):
        k = round(fraction * GRID * GRID)
        def delete(order_fn):
            def transform(w):
                keep = torch.ones_like(w).scatter_(1, order_fn(w)[:, :k], 0.0)
                kept = w * keep
                return kept / kept.sum(1, keepdim=True).clamp_min(1e-8)
            return transform
        replaced(f"delete_top_{fraction}", delete(lambda w: w.argsort(1, descending=True)))
        replaced(f"delete_bottom_{fraction}", delete(lambda w: w.argsort(1)))
        replaced(f"delete_random_{fraction}", delete(lambda w: torch.rand(w.shape, generator=generator).argsort(1).to(w.device)))
    return results


def box_patch_mask(row):
    """16x16 boolean grid of patches whose centres fall inside the labelled box (at least the centre patch)."""
    centres = (torch.arange(GRID) + 0.5) / GRID
    inside_x = (centres - row["box_cx"]).abs() <= row["box_w"] / 2
    inside_y = (centres - row["box_cy"]).abs() <= row["box_h"] / 2
    mask = inside_y[:, None] & inside_x[None, :]
    if not mask.any():
        mask[min(GRID - 1, int(row["box_cy"] * GRID)), min(GRID - 1, int(row["box_cx"] * GRID))] = True
    return mask.flatten()


def grounding(weights, rows, cloudy):
    boxes = torch.stack([box_patch_mask(r) for r in rows]).float()
    mass = (weights * boxes).sum(1)
    chance = boxes.mean(1)
    hit = boxes.gather(1, weights.argmax(1, keepdim=True)).squeeze(1)
    result = {"box_mass": float(mass.mean()), "box_share": float(chance.mean()), "box_mass_over_share": float((mass / chance).mean()),
              "pointing_game": float(hit.mean())}
    if cloudy:
        clouds = torch.stack([(F.avg_pool2d(cloud_mask(r["local_path"])[None], IMAGE_SIZE // GRID)[0, 0] > 0.5).flatten() for r in rows]).float()
        has_cloud = clouds.sum(1) > 0
        result |= {"cloud_mass": float((weights * clouds).sum(1)[has_cloud].mean()), "cloud_share": float(clouds.mean(1)[has_cloud].mean())}
    return result


def main(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    parser.add_argument("--run", type=Path, default=Path("/mnt/sdd/FYP/runs/today/main"))
    parser.add_argument("--tokens", type=Path, default=Path("/mnt/sdd/FYP/cache/adapter_tokens"))
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, default=Path("/mnt/sdd/FYP/runs/today/saliency_attention"))
    parser.add_argument("--seeds", type=int, nargs="+", default=[42, 43, 44])
    parser.add_argument("--guide-weights", default='{"clean": 0.3, "cloud": 0.1}', help="adapt-val choices from the guidance sweep")
    parser.add_argument("--epochs", type=int, default=15)
    parser.add_argument("--batch", type=int, default=128)
    parser.add_argument("--lr", type=float, default=1e-3)
    parser.add_argument("--arms", nargs="+", default=list(ARMS), choices=ARMS)
    parser.add_argument("--conditions", nargs="+", default=["clean", "cloud"], choices=["clean", "cloud"])
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    args.output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    guide_weights = json.loads(args.guide_weights)
    rows, classes = load_rows(DEFAULT_MANIFEST)
    splits = study_splits(rows)
    test_rows = splits["future_test"]
    labels = np.array([r["class_id"] for r in test_rows])
    groups = np.array([r["group"] for r in test_rows])
    head_state = torch.load(args.run / "head.pt", weights_only=True)
    width = head_state["weight"].shape[1]
    for condition in args.conditions:
        data = {s: (np.load(args.tokens / f"{condition}_{s}.npy", mmap_mode="r"), np.array([r["class_id"] for r in splits[s]])) for s in SPLITS}
        for seed in args.seeds:
            for arm in args.arms:
                path = args.output / f"{condition}_{arm}_s{seed}.json"
                if path.exists():
                    continue
                model, best = train(arm, seed, head_state, width, classes, data, guide_weights[condition], args)
                predictions, weights, saliency = infer(model, *data["future_test"])
                result = {"arm": arm, "condition": condition, "seed": seed, "best_epoch": best["epoch"], "adapt_val_macro_f1": best["val_f1"],
                          "future_test": scores(labels, predictions, classes), "predictions": predictions.tolist(),
                          "faithfulness": faithfulness(model, *data["future_test"], weights, predictions),
                          "grounding": grounding(weights, test_rows, condition == "cloud"),
                          "saliency_grounding": grounding(saliency.softmax(1), test_rows, condition == "cloud"),
                          "learned_scalars": {n: float(getattr(model, n)) for n in ("beta", "gamma", "delta") if hasattr(model, n)}}
                write_json(path, result)
                log(f"{condition} {arm} s{seed}: test={result['future_test']['macro_f1']:.4f} uniform-flip={result['faithfulness']['uniform']['flip_rate']:.3f} "
                    f"box={result['grounding']['box_mass_over_share']:.2f}x point={result['grounding']['pointing_game']:.3f}")
    summarize(args, labels, groups)


def summarize(args, labels, groups):
    runs = [json.loads(p.read_text()) for p in sorted(args.output.glob("*_s*.json"))]
    table, paired = {}, []
    for condition in ("clean", "cloud"):
        for arm in ARMS:
            rs = [r for r in runs if (r["condition"], r["arm"]) == (condition, arm)]
            if not rs:
                continue
            mean = lambda f: float(np.mean([f(r) for r in rs]))
            table[f"{condition}/{arm}"] = {
                "seeds": len(rs), "test_macro_f1": mean(lambda r: r["future_test"]["macro_f1"]), "test_accuracy": mean(lambda r: r["future_test"]["accuracy"]),
                "uniform_flip": mean(lambda r: r["faithfulness"]["uniform"]["flip_rate"]),
                "uniform_acc_drop": mean(lambda r: r["future_test"]["accuracy"] - r["faithfulness"]["uniform"]["accuracy"]),
                "delete_top25_acc_drop": mean(lambda r: r["future_test"]["accuracy"] - r["faithfulness"]["delete_top_0.25"]["accuracy"]),
                "delete_bottom25_acc_drop": mean(lambda r: r["future_test"]["accuracy"] - r["faithfulness"]["delete_bottom_0.25"]["accuracy"]),
                "delete_random25_acc_drop": mean(lambda r: r["future_test"]["accuracy"] - r["faithfulness"]["delete_random_0.25"]["accuracy"]),
                "box_mass_over_share": mean(lambda r: r["grounding"]["box_mass_over_share"]), "pointing_game": mean(lambda r: r["grounding"]["pointing_game"]),
                **({"cloud_mass": mean(lambda r: r["grounding"]["cloud_mass"]), "cloud_share": mean(lambda r: r["grounding"]["cloud_share"])} if condition == "cloud" else {})}
            for r in rs:
                base = next((b for b in runs if (b["condition"], b["arm"], b["seed"]) == (condition, "attention", r["seed"])), None)
                if base and arm != "attention":
                    difference = (np.array(r["predictions"]) == labels).astype(float) - (np.array(base["predictions"]) == labels).astype(float)
                    paired.append({"condition": condition, "arm": arm, "seed": r["seed"], "delta_macro_f1_vs_attention": r["future_test"]["macro_f1"] - base["future_test"]["macro_f1"],
                                   "delta_accuracy_vs_attention": cluster_bootstrap(difference, groups, repetitions=1000)})
    write_json(args.output / "summary.json", {"table": table, "paired_vs_attention": paired})
    log("summary", json.dumps(table, indent=1))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/saliency_guidance_sweep.py
================================================
"""Guidance-strength sweep for the saliency-guided attention adapter (frozen DINOv2-L).

Reuses the cached frozen tokens from fyp_drift.evaluation.saliency_adapters. For each
condition the guidance weight is chosen by mean adapt-val macro-F1 across seeds; future-test
labels are read only to score the chosen weight against the unguided attention adapter.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch

from fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_dinov2l_study import DEFAULT_MANIFEST, cluster_bootstrap, load_rows, log, study_splits, write_json
from fyp_drift.evaluation.saliency_adapters import SPLITS, train_arm


def main(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--run", type=Path, default=Path("/mnt/sdd/FYP/runs/today/main"))
    parser.add_argument("--adapters", type=Path, default=Path("/mnt/sdd/FYP/runs/today/saliency_adapters"))
    parser.add_argument("--tokens", type=Path, default=Path("/mnt/sdd/FYP/cache/adapter_tokens"))
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, default=Path("/mnt/sdd/FYP/runs/today/guidance_sweep"))
    parser.add_argument("--weights", type=float, nargs="+", default=[0.03, 0.1, 0.3])
    parser.add_argument("--seeds", type=int, nargs="+", default=[42, 43, 44])
    parser.add_argument("--epochs", type=int, default=15)
    parser.add_argument("--batch", type=int, default=128)
    parser.add_argument("--lr", type=float, default=1e-3)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    args.output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    rows, classes = load_rows(DEFAULT_MANIFEST)
    splits = study_splits(rows)
    labels = np.array([r["class_id"] for r in splits["future_test"]])
    groups = np.array([r["group"] for r in splits["future_test"]])
    head_state = torch.load(args.run / "head.pt", weights_only=True)
    width = head_state["weight"].shape[1]
    report = {}
    for condition in ("clean", "cloud"):
        data = {s: (np.load(args.tokens / f"{condition}_{s}.npy", mmap_mode="r"), np.array([r["class_id"] for r in splits[s]])) for s in SPLITS}
        runs = {1.0: [json.loads((args.adapters / f"{condition}_guided_s{seed}.json").read_text()) for seed in args.seeds]}
        for weight in args.weights:
            runs[weight] = []
            for seed in args.seeds:
                path = args.output / f"{condition}_guided_w{weight}_s{seed}.json"
                if not path.exists():
                    args.guide_weight = weight
                    write_json(path, {**train_arm("guided", seed, head_state, width, classes, data, args), "condition": condition, "guide_weight": weight})
                runs[weight].append(json.loads(path.read_text()))
                log(condition, weight, seed, round(runs[weight][-1]["future_test"]["macro_f1"], 4))
        attention = [json.loads((args.adapters / f"{condition}_attention_s{seed}.json").read_text()) for seed in args.seeds]
        by_weight = {w: {"adapt_val": float(np.mean([r["adapt_val_macro_f1"] for r in rs])), "test": float(np.mean([r["future_test"]["macro_f1"] for r in rs]))} for w, rs in runs.items()}
        chosen = max(by_weight, key=lambda w: by_weight[w]["adapt_val"])
        paired = []
        for guided, plain in zip(runs[chosen], attention):
            difference = (np.array(guided["predictions"]) == labels).astype(float) - (np.array(plain["predictions"]) == labels).astype(float)
            paired.append({"seed": guided["seed"], "delta_macro_f1": guided["future_test"]["macro_f1"] - plain["future_test"]["macro_f1"],
                           "delta_accuracy": cluster_bootstrap(difference, groups, repetitions=1000)})
        report[condition] = {"by_weight": {str(w): v for w, v in by_weight.items()}, "chosen_weight": chosen,
                             "attention_test_mean": float(np.mean([r["future_test"]["macro_f1"] for r in attention])), "chosen_vs_attention": paired}
        write_json(args.output / "summary.json", report)
    log("sweep summary", json.dumps(report, indent=1))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/saliency_router_study.py
================================================
"""Auditable saliency router: frozen historical head versus a saliency-adapted head, on cached DINOv2-L tokens.

This is the v7 routing idea rebuilt on the fMoW study with its two known flaws removed:
  * checkpoint selection and router calibration use DISJOINT, location-grouped halves of adapt-val
    (v7 reused the same 2011 labels for both);
  * three seeds, with location-clustered bootstrap intervals (v7 had one seed).

For each image both models give a class distribution and a patch-weight distribution:
  historical  frozen linear head on mean-pooled tokens; patch weights = softmax of its own class evidence z
  updated     saliency-essential attention head (input or gated) from saliency_attention_study

Four named routing signals (as in v7):
  saliency_js        Jensen-Shannon divergence between the two patch-weight distributions
  confidence_gain    max softmax probability, updated minus historical
  entropy_reduction  predictive entropy, historical minus updated
  evidence_gain      selected-class logit from high-weight minus low-weight patches, updated minus historical

The updated prediction is used only when the two models disagree, saliency_js exceeds a stored floor and a
stored logistic probability exceeds a stored threshold. Every coefficient, mean, scale, floor and threshold is
written to policy JSON, and every test decision to a CSV row, so any single route can be recomputed by hand.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import json
from collections import defaultdict
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace

import numpy as np
import torch
from sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression
from sklearn.model_selection import GroupShuffleSplit

from fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_dinov2l_study import DEFAULT_MANIFEST, cluster_bootstrap, load_rows, log, scores, study_splits, write_json
from fyp_drift.evaluation.saliency_adapters import SPLITS, batches
from fyp_drift.evaluation.saliency_attention_study import train

FEATURES = ("saliency_js", "confidence_gain", "entropy_reduction", "evidence_gain")
POLICIES = {"combined": FEATURES, "confidence_only": ("confidence_gain", "entropy_reduction"), "saliency_only": ("saliency_js", "evidence_gain")}
DELETE_FRACTION = 0.2


def selected_vs_complement(head, tokens, weights, classes):
    """Logit of the chosen class from high-weight patches minus the same logit from the low-weight complement."""
    complement = (1 - weights) / (1 - weights).sum(1, keepdim=True)
    high = head((weights[..., None] * tokens).sum(1))
    low = head((complement[..., None] * tokens).sum(1))
    return (high - low).gather(1, classes[:, None]).squeeze(1)


@torch.no_grad()
def paired_views(model, tokens, labels):
    """Historical and updated probabilities, patch weights and class evidence for every image, in order."""
    generator, views = torch.Generator().manual_seed(7), defaultdict(list)
    for x, _ in batches(tokens, labels, 256):
        z = model.saliency(x, generator)
        weights_old, weights_new = z.softmax(1), model.weights(x, z)
        probs_old, probs_new = model.frozen(x.mean(1)).softmax(1), model.classify(x, weights_new).softmax(1)
        views["probs_old"].append(probs_old.cpu())
        views["probs_new"].append(probs_new.cpu())
        views["weights_old"].append(weights_old.cpu())
        views["weights_new"].append(weights_new.cpu())
        views["evidence_old"].append(selected_vs_complement(model.frozen, x, weights_old, probs_old.argmax(1)).cpu())
        views["evidence_new"].append(selected_vs_complement(model.classifier, x, weights_new, probs_new.argmax(1)).cpu())
    return {k: torch.cat(v) for k, v in views.items()}


def routing_signals(views):
    entropy = lambda p: -(p * p.clamp_min(1e-12).log()).sum(1)
    kl = lambda a, b: (a * (a.clamp_min(1e-12) / b.clamp_min(1e-12)).log()).sum(1)
    middle = 0.5 * (views["weights_old"] + views["weights_new"])
    signals = {
        "saliency_js": 0.5 * kl(views["weights_old"], middle) + 0.5 * kl(views["weights_new"], middle),
        "confidence_gain": views["probs_new"].max(1).values - views["probs_old"].max(1).values,
        "entropy_reduction": entropy(views["probs_old"]) - entropy(views["probs_new"]),
        "evidence_gain": views["evidence_new"] - views["evidence_old"],
    }
    return {k: v.numpy() for k, v in signals.items()}


def fit_policy(name, signals, old, new, labels, classes):
    """Logistic update score fitted where the models disagree; floor and threshold searched on the same calibration half."""
    names, disagree = POLICIES[name], old != new
    X = np.column_stack([signals[n] for n in names])
    mean, scale = X[disagree].mean(0), X[disagree].std(0) + 1e-8
    target = (old != labels) & (new == labels)
    policy = {"name": name, "features": list(names), "mean": mean.tolist(), "scale": scale.tolist(),
              "calibration_images": int(len(labels)), "calibration_disagreements": int(disagree.sum()), "calibration_helpful": int(target[disagree].sum())}
    if len(set(target[disagree])) < 2:
        policy |= {"coefficients": [0.0] * len(names), "intercept": 0.0 if target[disagree].any() else -10.0, "floor": 0.0, "threshold": 0.5, "note": "one class only in calibration"}
        return policy
    model = LogisticRegression(class_weight="balanced", max_iter=1000).fit(((X - mean) / scale)[disagree], target[disagree])
    policy |= {"coefficients": model.coef_[0].tolist(), "intercept": float(model.intercept_[0])}
    floors = [0.0] if name == "confidence_only" else [float(np.quantile(signals["saliency_js"][disagree], q)) for q in (0.0, 0.1, 0.25, 0.5)]
    best = None
    for floor in floors:
        for threshold in (0.3, 0.4, 0.5, 0.6, 0.7):
            routed = route(policy | {"floor": floor, "threshold": threshold}, signals, old, new)[0]
            final = np.where(routed, new, old)
            key = (scores(labels, final, classes)["macro_f1"], -int((routed & (old == labels) & (new != labels)).sum()))
            if best is None or key > best[0]:
                best = (key, floor, threshold)
    return policy | {"floor": best[1], "threshold": best[2], "calibration_macro_f1": best[0][0]}


def route(policy, signals, old, new):
    """Apply a stored policy: disagree AND saliency_js >= floor AND p(update) >= threshold."""
    z = (np.column_stack([signals[n] for n in policy["features"]]) - np.array(policy["mean"])) / np.array(policy["scale"])
    probability = 1 / (1 + np.exp(-(z @ np.array(policy["coefficients"]) + policy["intercept"])))
    eligible = (old != new) & (signals["saliency_js"] >= policy["floor"])
    return eligible & (probability >= policy["threshold"]), probability, z, eligible


@torch.no_grad()
def deletion_margin(model, tokens, labels, weights, historical, indices):
    """True-class probability drop after deleting the top 20% minus after deleting the bottom 20% of each model's own weights."""
    if len(indices) == 0:
        return np.array([])
    x = torch.from_numpy(np.asarray(tokens[np.sort(indices)])).cuda().float()
    order_index = np.argsort(np.argsort(indices))
    x, w, y = x[order_index], weights[indices].cuda(), torch.from_numpy(labels[indices]).cuda()
    k = round(DELETE_FRACTION * w.shape[1])

    def true_prob(keep):
        if historical:
            return model.frozen((keep[..., None] * x).sum(1) / keep.sum(1, keepdim=True)).softmax(1).gather(1, y[:, None]).squeeze(1)
        kept = w * keep
        return model.classify(x, kept / kept.sum(1, keepdim=True)).softmax(1).gather(1, y[:, None]).squeeze(1)

    full = true_prob(torch.ones_like(w))
    drop_top = full - true_prob(torch.ones_like(w).scatter_(1, w.argsort(1, descending=True)[:, :k], 0.0))
    drop_bottom = full - true_prob(torch.ones_like(w).scatter_(1, w.argsort(1)[:, :k], 0.0))
    return (drop_top - drop_bottom).cpu().numpy()


def outcome(old, new, final, label):
    if old != label and final == label:
        return "helpful_route"
    if old == label and final != label:
        return "harmful_route"
    if old != label and new == label:
        return "missed_correction"
    if old == label and new != label:
        return "harm_prevented"
    return "agree" if old == new else "both_wrong"


def main(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    parser.add_argument("--run", type=Path, default=Path("/mnt/sdd/FYP/runs/today/main"))
    parser.add_argument("--tokens", type=Path, default=Path("/mnt/sdd/FYP/cache/adapter_tokens"))
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, default=Path("/mnt/sdd/FYP/runs/today/saliency_router"))
    parser.add_argument("--seeds", type=int, nargs="+", default=[42, 43, 44])
    parser.add_argument("--arms", nargs="+", default=["input", "gated"])
    parser.add_argument("--conditions", nargs="+", default=["clean", "cloud"])
    parser.add_argument("--epochs", type=int, default=15)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    args.output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    train_args = SimpleNamespace(epochs=args.epochs, batch=128, lr=1e-3)
    rows, classes = load_rows(DEFAULT_MANIFEST)
    splits = study_splits(rows)
    test_rows = splits["future_test"]
    labels_test = np.array([r["class_id"] for r in test_rows])
    groups_test = np.array([r["group"] for r in test_rows])
    val_groups = np.array([r["group"] for r in splits["adapt_val"]])
    select_idx, calibrate_idx = next(GroupShuffleSplit(n_splits=1, test_size=0.5, random_state=7).split(val_groups, groups=val_groups))
    head_state = torch.load(args.run / "head.pt", weights_only=True)
    width = head_state["weight"].shape[1]
    for condition in args.conditions:
        tokens = {s: np.load(args.tokens / f"{condition}_{s}.npy", mmap_mode="r") for s in SPLITS}
        labels = {s: np.array([r["class_id"] for r in splits[s]]) for s in SPLITS}
        select = (np.asarray(tokens["adapt_val"][np.sort(select_idx)]), labels["adapt_val"][np.sort(select_idx)])
        calibrate = (np.asarray(tokens["adapt_val"][np.sort(calibrate_idx)]), labels["adapt_val"][np.sort(calibrate_idx)])
        for seed in args.seeds:
            for arm in args.arms:
                stem = f"{condition}_{arm}_s{seed}"
                if (args.output / f"{stem}.json").exists():
                    continue
                model, best = train(arm, seed, head_state, width, classes, {"adapt_train": (tokens["adapt_train"], labels["adapt_train"]), "adapt_val": select}, 0.0, train_args)
                cal_views = paired_views(model, *calibrate)
                cal_signals = routing_signals(cal_views)
                cal_old, cal_new = cal_views["probs_old"].argmax(1).numpy(), cal_views["probs_new"].argmax(1).numpy()
                test_views = paired_views(model, tokens["future_test"], labels_test)
                signals = routing_signals(test_views)
                old, new = test_views["probs_old"].argmax(1).numpy(), test_views["probs_new"].argmax(1).numpy()
                result = {"condition": condition, "arm": arm, "seed": seed, "best_epoch": best["epoch"],
                          "selection_images": int(len(select[1])), "calibration_images": int(len(calibrate[1])),
                          "historical": scores(labels_test, old, classes), "updated": scores(labels_test, new, classes), "policies": {}}
                for name in POLICIES:
                    policy = fit_policy(name, cal_signals, cal_old, cal_new, calibrate[1], classes)
                    write_json(args.output / f"{stem}_{name}_policy.json", policy)
                    routed, probability, z, eligible = route(policy, signals, old, new)
                    final = np.where(routed, new, old)
                    kinds = np.array([outcome(o, n, f, y) for o, n, f, y in zip(old, new, final, labels_test)])
                    routed_score = scores(labels_test, final, classes)
                    gain_new = result["updated"]["macro_f1"] - result["historical"]["macro_f1"]
                    entry = {"routed": routed_score, "coverage": float(routed.mean()),
                             "retained_gain": (routed_score["macro_f1"] - result["historical"]["macro_f1"]) / gain_new if abs(gain_new) > 1e-9 else None,
                             "counts": {k: int((kinds == k).sum()) for k in ("helpful_route", "harmful_route", "missed_correction", "harm_prevented")},
                             "routed_minus_updated_accuracy": cluster_bootstrap((final == labels_test).astype(float) - (new == labels_test).astype(float), groups_test, repetitions=1000),
                             "routed_minus_historical_accuracy": cluster_bootstrap((final == labels_test).astype(float) - (old == labels_test).astype(float), groups_test, repetitions=1000)}
                    if name == "combined":
                        for kind in ("helpful_route", "harmful_route"):
                            idx = np.flatnonzero(kinds == kind)
                            entry[f"deletion_margin_{kind}"] = {
                                "updated_map": float(np.mean(deletion_margin(model, tokens["future_test"], labels_test, test_views["weights_new"], False, idx))) if len(idx) else None,
                                "historical_map": float(np.mean(deletion_margin(model, tokens["future_test"], labels_test, test_views["weights_old"], True, idx))) if len(idx) else None,
                                "images": int(len(idx))}
                        with open(args.output / f"{stem}_route_log.csv", "w", newline="") as handle:
                            writer = csv.writer(handle)
                            writer.writerow(["image", "label", "historical_pred", "updated_pred", *FEATURES, *[f"z_{n}" for n in policy["features"]],
                                             "p_update", "disagree", "above_saliency_floor", "routed", "final_pred", "correct", "outcome"])
                            for i, r in enumerate(test_rows):
                                writer.writerow([r["local_path"], labels_test[i], old[i], new[i], *[f"{signals[n][i]:.6f}" for n in FEATURES],
                                                 *[f"{v:.4f}" for v in z[i]], f"{probability[i]:.4f}", int(old[i] != new[i]), int(eligible[i]),
                                                 int(routed[i]), final[i], int(final[i] == labels_test[i]), kinds[i]])
                    result["policies"][name] = entry
                write_json(args.output / f"{stem}.json", result)
                c = result["policies"]["combined"]
                log(f"{stem}: hist={result['historical']['macro_f1']:.4f} new={result['updated']['macro_f1']:.4f} routed={c['routed']['macro_f1']:.4f} "
                    f"cov={c['coverage']:.3f} counts={c['counts']}")
    summarize(args)


def summarize(args):
    runs = [json.loads(p.read_text()) for p in sorted(args.output.glob("*_s[0-9]*.json")) if "policy" not in p.name]
    table = {}
    for r in runs:
        for name, entry in r["policies"].items():
            key = f"{r['condition']}/{r['arm']}/{name}"
            t = table.setdefault(key, defaultdict(list))
            t["historical_f1"].append(r["historical"]["macro_f1"])
            t["updated_f1"].append(r["updated"]["macro_f1"])
            t["routed_f1"].append(entry["routed"]["macro_f1"])
            t["coverage"].append(entry["coverage"])
            if entry["retained_gain"] is not None:
                t["retained_gain"].append(entry["retained_gain"])
            for k, v in entry["counts"].items():
                t[k].append(v)
            for kind in ("helpful_route", "harmful_route"):
                margins = entry.get(f"deletion_margin_{kind}")
                if margins and margins["updated_map"] is not None:
                    t[f"updated_map_margin_{kind}"].append(margins["updated_map"])
                    t[f"historical_map_margin_{kind}"].append(margins["historical_map"])
    summary = {k: {m: float(np.mean(v)) for m, v in t.items()} | {"seeds": len(t["routed_f1"])} for k, t in table.items()}
    write_json(args.output / "summary.json", summary)
    log("summary", json.dumps(summary, indent=1))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/soft_saliency_heads.py
================================================
"""Softer saliency heads: let surrounding context through instead of locking attention onto the object.

The gated and prior heads in saliency_attention_study made the frozen DINOv2-L attend to the labelled object
(pointing 0.57-0.62) but lost 2-3.5 macro-F1 points, consistent with fMoW classes depending on context. These
three designs keep saliency in the attention path but bound how much it can suppress:

  soft_gated  a ~ softmax(s) * (rho + (1 - rho) * sigmoid(gamma z + delta)), floor rho = sigmoid(r), starts at 0.5
              every patch keeps at least rho of its learned weight, so context cannot be switched off
  mix         a = (1 - lam) softmax(s) + lam softmax(beta z), lam = sigmoid(l), starts at 0.3
              a learned blend of plain attention and the saliency map
  soft_prior  a = softmax(beta z + s), beta starts at 0.25 and the scorer is NOT zero-initialised
              saliency nudges the learned scores rather than defining them

Each has a *_shuffled twin (z permuted within the image). Training, selection, faithfulness and grounding are
identical to saliency_attention_study, and every run is compared image by image with learned attention.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch
from torch import nn
from torch.nn import functional as F

from fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_dinov2l_study import DEFAULT_MANIFEST, cluster_bootstrap, load_rows, log, scores, study_splits, write_json
from fyp_drift.evaluation.saliency_adapters import SPLITS, batches
from fyp_drift.evaluation.saliency_attention_study import SaliencyAttentionHead, faithfulness, grounding, infer

SOFT = ("soft_gated", "mix", "soft_prior")
ARMS = SOFT + tuple(f"{a}_shuffled" for a in SOFT)
import os
RUNS = Path(os.environ.get("FYP_RUNS", "/mnt/sdd/FYP/runs/today"))
TOKENS = Path(os.environ.get("FYP_TOKENS", "/mnt/sdd/FYP/cache/adapter_tokens"))


class SoftSaliencyHead(SaliencyAttentionHead):
    def __init__(self, arm, head_state, width, classes):
        super().__init__(arm, head_state, width, classes)
        if self.kind == "soft_gated":
            self.gamma, self.delta, self.floor_raw = nn.Parameter(torch.tensor(2.0)), nn.Parameter(torch.tensor(0.0)), nn.Parameter(torch.tensor(0.0))
        if self.kind == "mix":
            self.beta, self.mix_raw = nn.Parameter(torch.tensor(1.0)), nn.Parameter(torch.tensor(-0.85))
        if self.kind == "soft_prior":
            self.beta = nn.Parameter(torch.tensor(0.25))

    def weights(self, tokens, z):
        learned = self.scorer(tokens).squeeze(-1)
        if self.kind == "soft_gated":
            floor = torch.sigmoid(self.floor_raw)
            w = learned.softmax(1) * (floor + (1 - floor) * torch.sigmoid(self.gamma * z + self.delta))
            return w / w.sum(1, keepdim=True).clamp_min(1e-8)
        if self.kind == "mix":
            lam = torch.sigmoid(self.mix_raw)
            return (1 - lam) * learned.softmax(1) + lam * (self.beta * z).softmax(1)
        return (self.beta * z + learned).softmax(1)

    def scalars(self):
        out = {}
        if hasattr(self, "floor_raw"):
            out["floor"] = float(torch.sigmoid(self.floor_raw))
        if hasattr(self, "mix_raw"):
            out["mix"] = float(torch.sigmoid(self.mix_raw))
        for name in ("beta", "gamma", "delta"):
            if hasattr(self, name):
                out[name] = float(getattr(self, name))
        return out


def train(arm, seed, head_state, width, classes, data, args):
    torch.manual_seed(seed)
    model = SoftSaliencyHead(arm, head_state, width, classes).cuda()
    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW([p for p in model.parameters() if p.requires_grad], lr=args.lr, weight_decay=1e-4)
    best = {"val_f1": -1.0}
    for epoch in range(args.epochs):
        model.train()
        generator = torch.Generator().manual_seed(seed * 1000 + epoch)
        for tokens, targets in batches(*data["adapt_train"], args.batch, shuffle_seed=seed * 1000 + epoch):
            logits, _, _ = model(tokens, generator)
            loss = F.cross_entropy(logits, targets)
            optimizer.zero_grad()
            loss.backward()
            optimizer.step()
        val = scores(data["adapt_val"][1], infer(model, *data["adapt_val"])[0], classes)["macro_f1"]
        if val > best["val_f1"]:
            best = {"val_f1": val, "epoch": epoch + 1, "state": {k: v.clone() for k, v in model.state_dict().items()}}
    model.load_state_dict(best["state"])
    return model.eval(), best


def main(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, default=RUNS / "saliency_soft")
    parser.add_argument("--seeds", type=int, nargs="+", default=[42, 43, 44])
    parser.add_argument("--epochs", type=int, default=15)
    parser.add_argument("--batch", type=int, default=128)
    parser.add_argument("--lr", type=float, default=1e-3)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    args.output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    rows, classes = load_rows(DEFAULT_MANIFEST)
    splits = study_splits(rows)
    test_rows = splits["future_test"]
    labels = np.array([r["class_id"] for r in test_rows])
    groups = np.array([r["group"] for r in test_rows])
    head_state = torch.load(RUNS / "main/head.pt", weights_only=True)
    width = head_state["weight"].shape[1]
    for condition in ("clean", "cloud"):
        data = {s: (np.load(TOKENS / f"{condition}_{s}.npy", mmap_mode="r"), np.array([r["class_id"] for r in splits[s]])) for s in SPLITS}
        for seed in args.seeds:
            for arm in ARMS:
                path = args.output / f"{condition}_{arm}_s{seed}.json"
                if path.exists():
                    continue
                model, best = train(arm, seed, head_state, width, classes, data, args)
                predictions, weights, _ = infer(model, *data["future_test"])
                result = {"arm": arm, "condition": condition, "seed": seed, "best_epoch": best["epoch"], "adapt_val_macro_f1": best["val_f1"],
                          "future_test": scores(labels, predictions, classes), "predictions": predictions.tolist(),
                          "faithfulness": faithfulness(model, *data["future_test"], weights, predictions),
                          "grounding": grounding(weights, test_rows, condition == "cloud"), "learned_scalars": model.scalars()}
                write_json(path, result)
                log(f"{condition} {arm} s{seed}: test={result['future_test']['macro_f1']:.4f} point={result['grounding']['pointing_game']:.3f} "
                    f"box={result['grounding']['box_mass_over_share']:.2f}x scalars={result['learned_scalars']}")
    summarize(args, labels, groups)


def summarize(args, labels, groups):
    runs = [json.loads(p.read_text()) for p in sorted(args.output.glob("*_s*.json"))]
    table, paired = {}, []
    for condition in ("clean", "cloud"):
        for arm in ARMS:
            rs = [r for r in runs if (r["condition"], r["arm"]) == (condition, arm)]
            if not rs:
                continue
            mean = lambda f: float(np.mean([f(r) for r in rs]))
            table[f"{condition}/{arm}"] = {"seeds": len(rs), "test_macro_f1": mean(lambda r: r["future_test"]["macro_f1"]),
                                           "pointing_game": mean(lambda r: r["grounding"]["pointing_game"]), "box_mass_over_share": mean(lambda r: r["grounding"]["box_mass_over_share"]),
                                           "uniform_flip": mean(lambda r: r["faithfulness"]["uniform"]["flip_rate"]),
                                           **({"cloud_mass": mean(lambda r: r["grounding"]["cloud_mass"]), "cloud_share": mean(lambda r: r["grounding"]["cloud_share"])} if condition == "cloud" else {}),
                                           "scalars": {k: float(np.mean([r["learned_scalars"][k] for r in rs])) for k in rs[0]["learned_scalars"]}}
            for r in rs:
                for reference in ("attention", "gated"):
                    path = RUNS / "saliency_attention" / f"{condition}_{reference}_s{r['seed']}.json"
                    if path.exists():
                        base = json.loads(path.read_text())
                        difference = (np.array(r["predictions"]) == labels).astype(float) - (np.array(base["predictions"]) == labels).astype(float)
                        paired.append({"condition": condition, "arm": arm, "reference": reference, "seed": r["seed"],
                                       "delta_macro_f1": r["future_test"]["macro_f1"] - base["future_test"]["macro_f1"],
                                       "delta_accuracy": cluster_bootstrap(difference, groups, repetitions=1000)})
    write_json(args.output / "summary.json", {"table": table, "paired": paired})
    log("summary", json.dumps(table, indent=1))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/strict_saliency_pooling.py
================================================
"""Strict, training-free saliency pooling on the frozen DINOv2-L fMoW model.

Nothing is trained or tuned: the frozen backbone and the frozen historical head are used
as they are. The only change is which patch tokens the head reads. Saliency pooling keeps
the top fraction of patches by the frozen model's evidence for its own predicted class;
controls read every patch (the unmodified model) or the same number of random patches.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch
from torch import nn

from fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_dinov2l_study import (
    DEFAULT_CACHE, DEFAULT_MANIFEST, Backbone, cluster_bootstrap, image_cache, image_loader, load_rows, log, scores,
    study_splits, write_json,
)
from fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_token_dropping import real_strata


@torch.no_grad()
def pooled_predictions(backbone, head, data, fractions):
    """Predictions from all-patch pooling and from evidence / random top-k pooling at each fraction."""
    generator = torch.Generator().manual_seed(42)
    outputs = {"all_patches": []} | {f"{mode}_{f}": [] for mode in ("evidence", "random") for f in fractions}
    labels = []
    for images, targets, _ in data:
        with torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=torch.float16):
            tokens = backbone(images.cuda(non_blocking=True))[:, 1:].float()
        logits = head(tokens.mean(1))
        outputs["all_patches"].append(logits.argmax(1).cpu())
        evidence = (tokens * head.weight[logits.argmax(1)][:, None, :]).sum(-1)
        for fraction in fractions:
            keep = round(fraction * tokens.shape[1])
            for mode in ("evidence", "random"):
                order = evidence.topk(keep, 1).indices if mode == "evidence" else torch.rand(evidence.shape, generator=generator).argsort(1)[:, :keep].to(tokens.device)
                pooled = tokens.gather(1, order[..., None].expand(-1, -1, tokens.shape[-1])).mean(1)
                outputs[f"{mode}_{fraction}"].append(head(pooled).argmax(1).cpu())
        labels.append(targets)
    return torch.cat(labels).numpy(), {k: torch.cat(v).numpy() for k, v in outputs.items()}


def main(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--run", type=Path, default=Path("/mnt/sdd/FYP/runs/today/main"))
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, default=Path("/mnt/sdd/FYP/runs/today/strict_saliency"))
    parser.add_argument("--backbone", default="facebook/dinov2-large")
    parser.add_argument("--fractions", type=float, nargs="+", default=[0.2, 0.4, 0.6])
    parser.add_argument("--realistic-strength", type=float, default=0.8)
    parser.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=2)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    args.output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    rows, classes = load_rows(DEFAULT_MANIFEST)
    test = study_splits(rows)["future_test"]
    cache = image_cache(rows, DEFAULT_CACHE, args.workers)
    head_state = torch.load(args.run / "head.pt", weights_only=True)
    head = nn.Linear(head_state["weight"].shape[1], classes).cuda().eval()
    head.load_state_dict(head_state)
    backbone = Backbone(args.backbone).cuda().eval()
    main_cloud = json.loads((args.run / "calib.json").read_text())["selected_alpha"]
    groups = np.array([r["group"] for r in test])
    conditions = {"clean": (0.0, None), "main_cloud": (main_cloud, None),
                  "realistic_cloud_off_object": (args.realistic_strength, "off_object"),
                  "realistic_cloud_on_object": (args.realistic_strength, "on_object")}
    summary = {}
    for condition, (alpha, region) in conditions.items():
        labels, outputs = pooled_predictions(backbone, head, image_loader(cache, test, 64, args.workers, alpha, cloud_region=region), args.fractions)
        strata = real_strata(test) if condition == "clean" else {"all": np.ones(len(test), bool)}
        table = {name: {"n": int(mask.sum())} | {k: scores(labels[mask], v[mask], classes)["macro_f1"] for k, v in outputs.items()} for name, mask in strata.items()}
        paired = []
        for fraction in args.fractions:
            for control in ("random", "all_patches"):
                other = outputs["all_patches"] if control == "all_patches" else outputs[f"random_{fraction}"]
                for name, mask in strata.items():
                    difference = (outputs[f"evidence_{fraction}"][mask] == labels[mask]).astype(float) - (other[mask] == labels[mask]).astype(float)
                    paired.append({"fraction": fraction, "vs": control, "stratum": name, "delta_accuracy": cluster_bootstrap(difference, groups[mask], repetitions=1000)})
        summary[condition] = {"strength": alpha, "region": region, "macro_f1": table, "paired": paired}
        write_json(args.output / "summary.json", summary)
        log(condition, json.dumps({k: round(v, 4) for k, v in table["all"].items() if k != "n"}))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/temporal_design.py
================================================
"""Temporal-by-corruption experiment grids with unconfounded severity."""

from __future__ import annotations


def crossed_corruption_grid(periods, scenarios, severities=(1, 2, 3)):
    """Return clean and fixed-severity cells for every period and source.

    Severity is crossed with time: every period receives the same list of
    source/severity conditions. ``realistic_mix`` is rejected because its
    existing transform changes both severity and the number of sources.
    """
    periods = tuple(periods)
    scenarios = tuple(scenarios)
    severities = tuple(severities)
    if not periods or len(set(periods)) != len(periods):
        raise ValueError("periods must be a non-empty sequence without duplicates")
    if not scenarios or len(set(scenarios)) != len(scenarios):
        raise ValueError("scenarios must be a non-empty sequence without duplicates")
    if "clean" in scenarios:
        raise ValueError("clean is added automatically; omit it from scenarios")
    if "realistic_mix" in scenarios:
        raise ValueError(
            "realistic_mix changes source count with severity; evaluate it as a "
            "separate progression or define a fixed-source mixture"
        )
    if not severities or any(level not in (1, 2, 3) for level in severities):
        raise ValueError("severities must be selected from 1, 2, and 3")
    if len(set(severities)) != len(severities):
        raise ValueError("severities must not contain duplicates")

    cells = []
    for period in periods:
        cells.append({"period": period, "scenario": "clean", "severity": 0})
        for scenario in scenarios:
            for severity in severities:
                cells.append(
                    {"period": period, "scenario": scenario, "severity": severity}
                )
    return cells


def progressive_corruption_schedule(periods, scenario, severities):
    """Create a named progressive stress schedule, separate from the grid."""
    periods, severities = tuple(periods), tuple(severities)
    if not periods or len(periods) != len(severities):
        raise ValueError("one severity is required for each temporal period")
    if scenario == "realistic_mix":
        raise ValueError("use a fixed-source mixture to isolate severity progression")
    if any(level not in (0, 1, 2, 3) for level in severities):
        raise ValueError("progressive severities must be 0, 1, 2, or 3")
    if any(a > b for a, b in zip(severities, severities[1:])):
        raise ValueError("progressive severities must be non-decreasing")
    return [
        {
            "period": period,
            "scenario": "clean" if severity == 0 else scenario,
            "severity": severity,
        }
        for period, severity in zip(periods, severities)
    ]



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/wilds_replication.py
================================================
"""Screen for feature drift and performance loss on official WILDS splits."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import random
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch
from torch.nn import functional as F
from sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression
from sklearn.metrics import accuracy_score, f1_score
from sklearn.pipeline import make_pipeline
from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler
from torch.utils.data import DataLoader, Subset
from torchvision.transforms import Compose, Normalize, Resize, ToTensor
from tqdm import tqdm
from transformers import Dinov2Model
from wilds import get_dataset

from fyp_drift.data.clear100 import MEAN, STD
from fyp_drift.evaluation.drift_metrics import (
    DEFAULT_PERMUTATIONS,
    distribution_shift_metrics,
)
from fyp_drift.models.vision_transformer import BACKBONE, TemporalViT


DATASET_SPLITS = {
    "fmow": ("id_val", "val"),
    "iwildcam": ("id_val", "val"),
    "camelyon17": ("id_val", "val"),
}


def parse_args(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description=(
            "Fit a frozen-DINOv2 linear probe and compare official WILDS "
            "in-distribution and out-of-distribution validation splits."
        )
    )
    parser.add_argument("--dataset", choices=DATASET_SPLITS, default="fmow")
    parser.add_argument("--data-root", type=Path, default=Path("data/wilds"))
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--download", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--train-limit", type=int, default=5000)
    parser.add_argument("--eval-limit", type=int, default=1000)
    parser.add_argument("--batch-size", type=int, default=32)
    parser.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=0)
    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=42)
    parser.add_argument("--permutations", type=int, default=DEFAULT_PERMUTATIONS)
    parser.add_argument("--permutation-limit", type=int, default=200)
    parser.add_argument(
        "--architecture-screen",
        action="store_true",
        help="also train matched frozen-backbone baseline and saliency ViTs",
    )
    parser.add_argument("--epochs", type=int, default=2)
    parser.add_argument("--learning-rate", type=float, default=5e-5)
    parser.add_argument("--faithfulness-weight", type=float, default=0.1)
    return parser.parse_args(argv)


def stratified_sample(labels, limit, seed):
    """Return a repeatable class-balanced sample without duplicating rows."""
    labels = np.asarray(labels).reshape(-1)
    if limit < 1:
        raise ValueError("sample limit must be positive")
    buckets = {}
    for index, label in enumerate(labels):
        key = label.item() if isinstance(label, np.generic) else label
        buckets.setdefault(key, []).append(index)
    if limit < len(buckets):
        raise ValueError(
            f"sample limit {limit} cannot represent all {len(buckets)} classes"
        )

    generator = random.Random(seed)
    for indices in buckets.values():
        generator.shuffle(indices)

    selected = []
    target = min(limit, len(labels))
    while len(selected) < target:
        added = False
        for label in sorted(buckets):
            if buckets[label]:
                selected.append(buckets[label].pop())
                added = True
                if len(selected) == target:
                    break
        if not added:
            break
    return sorted(selected)


def stratified_group_sample(labels, groups, limit, seed):
    """Balance a sample across label and metadata groups."""
    labels = np.asarray(labels).reshape(-1)
    groups = np.asarray(groups).reshape(-1)
    if len(labels) != len(groups):
        raise ValueError("labels and groups must have equal lengths")
    combined = np.asarray(
        [f"{group}\0{label}" for group, label in zip(groups, labels)], dtype=str
    )
    return stratified_sample(combined, limit, seed)


def image_transform():
    return Compose(
        [
            Resize((224, 224)),
            ToTensor(),
            Normalize(mean=MEAN, std=STD),
        ]
    )


def make_loader(dataset, split, limit, seed, batch_size, workers):
    subset = dataset.get_subset(split, transform=image_transform())
    labels = subset.y_array.cpu().numpy()
    indices = stratified_sample(labels, limit, seed)
    return DataLoader(
        Subset(subset, indices),
        batch_size=batch_size,
        num_workers=workers,
        shuffle=False,
    )


@torch.inference_mode()
def extract_features(model, data_loader, device, label):
    feature_batches = []
    label_batches = []
    for images, labels, _ in tqdm(data_loader, desc=label, unit="batch"):
        tokens = model(pixel_values=images.to(device)).last_hidden_state[:, 0]
        feature_batches.append(tokens.cpu().numpy())
        label_batches.append(labels.cpu().numpy())
    return np.concatenate(feature_batches), np.concatenate(label_batches)


def fit_linear_probe(features, labels, seed):
    probe = make_pipeline(
        StandardScaler(),
        LogisticRegression(
            class_weight="balanced",
            max_iter=2000,
            random_state=seed,
        ),
    )
    probe.fit(features, labels)
    return probe


def classification_scores(probe, features, labels):
    predictions = probe.predict(features)
    return {
        "accuracy": float(accuracy_score(labels, predictions)),
        "macro_f1": float(f1_score(labels, predictions, average="macro", zero_division=0)),
        "samples": int(len(labels)),
    }


@torch.inference_mode()
def evaluate_architecture(model, data_loader, device, label):
    model.eval()
