    predictions = []
    targets = []
    for images, labels, _ in tqdm(data_loader, desc=label, unit="batch"):
        logits, _, _ = model(images.to(device))
        predictions.append(logits.argmax(1).cpu().numpy())
        targets.append(labels.cpu().numpy())
    predictions = np.concatenate(predictions)
    targets = np.concatenate(targets)
    return {
        "accuracy": float(accuracy_score(targets, predictions)),
        "macro_f1": float(
            f1_score(targets, predictions, average="macro", zero_division=0)
        ),
        "samples": int(len(targets)),
    }


def train_architecture(method, num_classes, loaders, args, device):
    """Train only the task heads so dataset comparisons remain affordable."""
    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)
    model = TemporalViT(method=method, num_classes=num_classes).to(device)
    for parameter in model.backbone.parameters():
        parameter.requires_grad = False
    if method == "baseline":
        for parameter in model.saliency_head.parameters():
            parameter.requires_grad = False
    optimiser = torch.optim.AdamW(
        (parameter for parameter in model.parameters() if parameter.requires_grad),
        lr=args.learning_rate,
    )

    epoch_losses = []
    for epoch in range(1, args.epochs + 1):
        model.train()
        model.backbone.eval()
        total_loss = 0.0
        generator = torch.Generator().manual_seed(args.seed + epoch)
        order = torch.randperm(
            len(loaders["train"].dataset), generator=generator
        ).tolist()
        training_loader = DataLoader(
            Subset(loaders["train"].dataset, order),
            batch_size=loaders["train"].batch_size,
            num_workers=loaders["train"].num_workers,
            shuffle=False,
        )
        progress = tqdm(
            training_loader,
            desc=f"{method} epoch {epoch}/{args.epochs}",
            unit="batch",
        )
        for images, labels, _ in progress:
            images = images.to(device)
            labels = labels.to(device, dtype=torch.long)
            logits, _, inverse_logits = model(
                images,
                return_counterfactual=method == "xai",
            )
            loss = F.cross_entropy(logits, labels)
            if inverse_logits is not None:
                selected = logits.gather(1, labels[:, None])
                inverse = inverse_logits.gather(1, labels[:, None])
                loss += args.faithfulness_weight * F.relu(
                    0.2 - (selected - inverse)
                ).mean()
            optimiser.zero_grad()
            loss.backward()
            optimiser.step()
            total_loss += loss.item() * len(labels)
            progress.set_postfix(loss=f"{loss.item():.4f}")
        epoch_losses.append(total_loss / len(loaders["train"].dataset))

    scores = {
        "method": method,
        "training_scope": (
            "classifier and saliency head" if method == "xai" else "classifier head"
        ),
        "epoch_losses": epoch_losses,
        "in_distribution": evaluate_architecture(
            model, loaders["id"], device, f"{method} ID"
        ),
        "out_of_distribution": evaluate_architecture(
            model, loaders["ood"], device, f"{method} OOD"
        ),
    }
    scores["ood_macro_f1_change_from_id_points"] = 100 * (
        scores["out_of_distribution"]["macro_f1"]
        - scores["in_distribution"]["macro_f1"]
    )
    del model
    if device == "cuda":
        torch.cuda.empty_cache()
    return scores


def main(argv=None):
    args = parse_args(argv)
    if args.train_limit < 100 or args.eval_limit < 100:
        raise ValueError("The train and evaluation limits must each be at least 100")

    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)
    np.random.seed(args.seed)
    device = "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    dataset = get_dataset(
        dataset=args.dataset,
        root_dir=str(args.data_root),
        download=args.download,
    )
    id_split, ood_split = DATASET_SPLITS[args.dataset]
    required = {"train", id_split, ood_split}
    missing = required.difference(dataset.split_dict)
    if missing:
        raise ValueError(f"Dataset is missing required splits: {sorted(missing)}")

    model = Dinov2Model.from_pretrained(BACKBONE).to(device).eval()
    loaders = {
        "train": make_loader(
            dataset,
            "train",
            args.train_limit,
            args.seed,
            args.batch_size,
            args.workers,
        ),
        "id": make_loader(
            dataset,
            id_split,
            args.eval_limit,
            args.seed + 1,
            args.batch_size,
            args.workers,
        ),
        "ood": make_loader(
            dataset,
            ood_split,
            args.eval_limit,
            args.seed + 2,
            args.batch_size,
            args.workers,
        ),
    }
    extracted = {
        name: extract_features(model, data_loader, device, name)
        for name, data_loader in loaders.items()
    }
    probe = fit_linear_probe(*extracted["train"], args.seed)
    id_scores = classification_scores(probe, *extracted["id"])
    ood_scores = classification_scores(probe, *extracted["ood"])
    drift = distribution_shift_metrics(
        extracted["id"][0],
        extracted["ood"][0],
        permutations=args.permutations,
        permutation_limit=args.permutation_limit,
        seed=args.seed,
    )

    payload = {
        "dataset": args.dataset,
        "feature_extractor": BACKBONE,
        "device": device,
        "seed": args.seed,
        "splits": {
            "training": "train",
            "in_distribution": id_split,
            "out_of_distribution": ood_split,
        },
        "limits": {
            "training": len(loaders["train"].dataset),
            "in_distribution": len(loaders["id"].dataset),
            "out_of_distribution": len(loaders["ood"].dataset),
        },
        "sampling": "class-balanced without replacement",
        "classification": {
            "in_distribution": id_scores,
            "out_of_distribution": ood_scores,
            "accuracy_change_percentage_points": 100
            * (ood_scores["accuracy"] - id_scores["accuracy"]),
            "macro_f1_change_percentage_points": 100
            * (ood_scores["macro_f1"] - id_scores["macro_f1"]),
        },
        "drift_detection": drift,
        "interpretation": (
            "MMD and domain-classifier AUC establish feature-distribution shift. "
            "The ID-to-OOD performance change measures whether that shift is harmful "
            "to this frozen-feature classifier."
        ),
    }
    if args.architecture_screen:
        architecture = {
            method: train_architecture(
                method,
                dataset.n_classes,
                loaders,
                args,
                device,
            )
            for method in ("baseline", "xai")
        }
        architecture["xai_minus_baseline_ood_macro_f1_points"] = 100 * (
            architecture["xai"]["out_of_distribution"]["macro_f1"]
            - architecture["baseline"]["out_of_distribution"]["macro_f1"]
        )
        payload["architecture_screen"] = architecture
    args.output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    output_path = args.output / f"{args.dataset}_replication.json"
    output_path.write_text(json.dumps(payload, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2))
    print(f"Saved: {output_path}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/yearbook_injected_drift.py
================================================
"""Paired drift screen for frozen Yearbook models and predeclared image shifts."""

import argparse
import csv
import json
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch
from torch.nn import functional as F

from fyp_drift.evaluation.drift_metrics import distribution_shift_metrics
from fyp_drift.evaluation.metrics import classification_metrics
from fyp_drift.models.vision_transformer import TemporalViT
from fyp_drift.training.saliency_lora_pilot import build_model
from fyp_drift.training.yearbook_saliency_mask_pilot import make_loaders


def shifted(images, name, batch_index):
    if name == "clean":
        return images
    if name == "blur":
        return F.gaussian_blur(images, (17, 17), sigma=(3.0, 3.0)) if hasattr(F, "gaussian_blur") else _blur(images)
    if name == "center_occlusion":
        result = images.clone()
        side = result.shape[-1]
        start = side // 2 - side // 6
        result[:, :, start:start + side // 3, start:start + side // 3] = 0
        return result
    if name == "noise":
        generator = torch.Generator(device="cpu").manual_seed(42000 + batch_index)
        noise = torch.randn(images.shape, generator=generator).to(images.device)
        return images + 0.6 * noise
    raise ValueError(name)


def _blur(images):
    from torchvision.transforms.functional import gaussian_blur
    return gaussian_blur(images, 17, sigma=3.0)


def parse_args(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--run", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--data-root", type=Path, default=Path("data/wild-time"))
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--permutations", type=int, default=499)
    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=42)
    return parser.parse_args(argv)


def main(argv=None):
    args = parse_args(argv)
    if args.output.exists():
        raise ValueError("output already exists")
    args.output.mkdir(parents=True)
    config = json.loads((args.run / "config.json").read_text())
    settings = argparse.Namespace(**config)
    settings.data_root = args.data_root
    loader = make_loaders(settings)["future"]
    device = "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    if device != "cuda":
        raise RuntimeError("GPU required")
    historical = TemporalViT("baseline", 2, pooling_mode="patch_only").to(device)
    historical.load_state_dict(torch.load(args.run / "historical.pt", map_location=device, weights_only=True))
    historical.eval()
    models = {"historical": historical}
    for name in ("none", "random", "saliency"):
        model = build_model(args.run / "historical.pt", device, settings.rank)
        model.load_state_dict(torch.load(args.run / f"{name}.pt", map_location=device, weights_only=True))
        models[name] = model.eval()
    payload = {"source_run": str(args.run), "seed": args.seed, "count": len(loader.dataset),
               "shift_parameters": {"blur": "Gaussian kernel 17 sigma 3 on 224 px", "center_occlusion": "central one-third square set to normalized mean", "noise": "Gaussian std 0.6 in normalized pixel units"},
               "scenarios": {}}
    clean_features = None
    for scenario in ("clean", "blur", "center_occlusion", "noise"):
        truth = []
        predictions = {name: [] for name in models}
        features = []
        years = []
        with torch.no_grad():
            for batch_index, (images, labels, batch_years) in enumerate(loader):
                images = shifted(images.to(device), scenario, batch_index)
                truth.extend(labels.tolist())
                years.extend(batch_years.squeeze(-1).tolist())
                tokens = historical.backbone(pixel_values=images).last_hidden_state[:, 1:]
                features.append(tokens.mean(1).cpu().numpy())
                predictions["historical"].extend(historical.classifier(tokens.mean(1)).argmax(1).cpu().tolist())
                for name in ("none", "random", "saliency"):
                    predictions[name].extend(models[name](images)[0].argmax(1).cpu().tolist())
        features = np.concatenate(features)
        result = {"models": {name: classification_metrics(truth, values, classes=2)
                             for name, values in predictions.items()}}
        if scenario == "clean":
            clean_features = features
            result["drift"] = None
            half = len(features) // 2
            random_order = np.random.default_rng(args.seed).permutation(len(features))
            payload["clean_split_half_control"] = distribution_shift_metrics(
                features[random_order[:half]], features[random_order[half:2 * half]],
                permutations=args.permutations, permutation_limit=half, seed=args.seed)
        else:
            result["drift"] = distribution_shift_metrics(
                clean_features, features, permutations=args.permutations,
                permutation_limit=200, seed=args.seed, paired=True)
            result["historical_accuracy_drop_points"] = 100 * (
                payload["scenarios"]["clean"]["models"]["historical"]["accuracy"]
                - result["models"]["historical"]["accuracy"])
        payload["scenarios"][scenario] = result
        with (args.output / f"{scenario}-predictions.csv").open("w", newline="") as handle:
            writer = csv.writer(handle)
            writer.writerow(("index", "year", "true_class", *models))
            writer.writerows((i, year, label, *(predictions[name][i] for name in models))
                             for i, (year, label) in enumerate(zip(years, truth)))
        print(json.dumps({"scenario": scenario, "result": result}), flush=True)
    (args.output / "summary.json").write_text(json.dumps(payload, indent=2))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/yearbook_replication.py
================================================
"""Run a lightweight temporal replication on the Wild-Time Yearbook data."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch
from torch.utils.data import ConcatDataset, DataLoader, Dataset, Subset
from torchvision.transforms import Compose, Lambda, Normalize, Resize, ToTensor
from transformers import Dinov2Model
from wild_time_data import load_dataset

from fyp_drift.data.clear100 import MEAN, STD
from fyp_drift.evaluation.drift_metrics import (
    DEFAULT_PERMUTATIONS,
    distribution_shift_metrics,
)
from fyp_drift.evaluation.wilds_replication import (
    classification_scores,
    extract_features,
    fit_linear_probe,
    stratified_group_sample,
    train_architecture,
)
from fyp_drift.models.vision_transformer import BACKBONE


class WithYear(Dataset):
    """Add year metadata to the two-value Wild-Time dataset interface."""

    def __init__(self, dataset, year):
        self.dataset = dataset
        self.year = year
        self.labels = np.asarray(dataset.y, dtype=np.int64)

    def __len__(self):
        return len(self.dataset)

    def __getitem__(self, index):
        image, label = self.dataset[index]
        return image, label, torch.tensor([self.year])


def parse_args(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Compare historical and later Wild-Time Yearbook periods."
    )
    parser.add_argument("--data-root", type=Path, default=Path("data/wild-time"))
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--train-start", type=int, default=1930)
    parser.add_argument("--train-end", type=int, default=1989)
    parser.add_argument("--id-start", type=int, default=1980)
    parser.add_argument("--id-end", type=int, default=1989)
    parser.add_argument("--ood-start", type=int, default=2004)
    parser.add_argument("--ood-end", type=int, default=2013)
    parser.add_argument("--train-limit", type=int, default=3000)
    parser.add_argument("--eval-limit", type=int, default=500)
    parser.add_argument("--batch-size", type=int, default=32)
    parser.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=0)
    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=42)
    parser.add_argument("--permutations", type=int, default=DEFAULT_PERMUTATIONS)
    parser.add_argument("--permutation-limit", type=int, default=200)
    parser.add_argument("--epochs", type=int, default=2)
    parser.add_argument("--learning-rate", type=float, default=5e-5)
    parser.add_argument("--faithfulness-weight", type=float, default=0.1)
    return parser.parse_args(argv)


def transform():
    return Compose(
        [
            ToTensor(),
            Resize((224, 224), antialias=True),
            Lambda(lambda image: image.repeat(3, 1, 1)),
            Normalize(mean=MEAN, std=STD),
        ]
    )


def year_range(data_root, start, end, split):
    datasets = []
    for year in range(start, end + 1):
        dataset = load_dataset(
            "yearbook",
            time_step=year,
            split=split,
            transform=transform(),
            data_dir=str(data_root),
        )
        datasets.append(WithYear(dataset, year))
    combined = ConcatDataset(datasets)
    combined.labels = np.concatenate([dataset.labels for dataset in datasets])
    combined.years = np.concatenate(
        [np.full(len(dataset), dataset.year, dtype=np.int64) for dataset in datasets]
    )
    return combined


def sampled_loader(dataset, limit, seed, batch_size, workers):
    indices = stratified_group_sample(dataset.labels, dataset.years, limit, seed)
    return DataLoader(
        Subset(dataset, indices),
        batch_size=batch_size,
        num_workers=workers,
        shuffle=False,
    )


def main(argv=None):
    args = parse_args(argv)
    if not (
        args.train_start <= args.id_start <= args.id_end <= args.train_end
        and args.ood_start <= args.ood_end
        and args.ood_start > args.train_end
    ):
        raise ValueError(
            "Yearbook periods must use an ID interval within training history and "
            "a strictly later OOD interval"
        )
    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)
    np.random.seed(args.seed)
    device = "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"

    datasets = {
        "train": year_range(
            args.data_root, args.train_start, args.train_end, "train"
        ),
        "id": year_range(args.data_root, args.id_start, args.id_end, "test"),
        "ood": year_range(args.data_root, args.ood_start, args.ood_end, "test"),
    }
    loaders = {
        "train": sampled_loader(
            datasets["train"], args.train_limit, args.seed, args.batch_size, args.workers
        ),
        "id": sampled_loader(
            datasets["id"],
            args.eval_limit,
            args.seed + 1,
            args.batch_size,
            args.workers,
        ),
        "ood": sampled_loader(
            datasets["ood"],
            args.eval_limit,
            args.seed + 2,
            args.batch_size,
            args.workers,
        ),
    }

    backbone = Dinov2Model.from_pretrained(BACKBONE).to(device).eval()
    extracted = {
        name: extract_features(backbone, data_loader, device, name)
        for name, data_loader in loaders.items()
    }
    del backbone
    if device == "cuda":
        torch.cuda.empty_cache()

    probe = fit_linear_probe(*extracted["train"], args.seed)
    id_scores = classification_scores(probe, *extracted["id"])
    ood_scores = classification_scores(probe, *extracted["ood"])
    drift = distribution_shift_metrics(
        extracted["id"][0],
        extracted["ood"][0],
        permutations=args.permutations,
        permutation_limit=args.permutation_limit,
        seed=args.seed,
    )
    architecture = {
        method: train_architecture(method, 2, loaders, args, device)
        for method in ("baseline", "xai")
    }
    architecture["xai_minus_baseline_ood_macro_f1_points"] = 100 * (
        architecture["xai"]["out_of_distribution"]["macro_f1"]
        - architecture["baseline"]["out_of_distribution"]["macro_f1"]
    )

    payload = {
        "dataset": "Wild-Time Yearbook",
        "feature_extractor": BACKBONE,
        "seed": args.seed,
        "periods": {
            "training": [args.train_start, args.train_end],
            "in_distribution_test": [args.id_start, args.id_end],
            "later_test": [args.ood_start, args.ood_end],
        },
        "sampling": "balanced across year and class without replacement",
        "linear_probe": {
            "in_distribution": id_scores,
            "later_period": ood_scores,
            "macro_f1_change_percentage_points": 100
            * (ood_scores["macro_f1"] - id_scores["macro_f1"]),
        },
        "drift_detection": drift,
        "architecture_screen": architecture,
        "scope": (
            "This low-resolution binary task checks temporal reproducibility and "
            "the evaluation path. It does not replace a complex-image FMoW run."
        ),
    }
    args.output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    output_path = args.output / "yearbook_replication.json"
    output_path.write_text(json.dumps(payload, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2))
    print(f"Saved: {output_path}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/fmow_v2/analysis.py
================================================
"""Post-hoc measures from saved runs: weight-map stability across seeds, real-cloud scores, example maps.

Example::  python -m fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_v2.analysis
"""

import glob
import hashlib
import json
import os
import random
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
from fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_dinov2l_study import (
    DEFAULT_CACHE,
    DEFAULT_MANIFEST,
    load_rows,
    study_splits,
)
from scipy.stats import rankdata
from sklearn.metrics import f1_score


def main():
    R = Path(os.environ["FYP_RUNS"])
    OUT = R / "analysis"
    OUT.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    rows, classes = load_rows(DEFAULT_MANIFEST)
    test = study_splits(rows)["future_test"]
    labels = np.array([r["class_id"] for r in test])
    res = {}
    rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
    sub = rng.choice(len(test), 1000, replace=False)
    stab = {}
    for f in sorted(glob.glob(str(R / "shift_test/*_s42_centred.npz"))):
        stem = Path(f).name.replace("_s42_centred.npz", "")
        ws = [
            np.load(str(R / f"shift_test/{stem}_s{s}_centred.npz"))["weights"][sub].astype(np.float32)
            for s in (42, 43, 44)
        ]
        rk = [np.apply_along_axis(rankdata, 1, w) for w in ws]
        corr = []
        for a, b in ((0, 1), (0, 2), (1, 2)):
            ra, rb = (rk[a] - rk[a].mean(1, keepdims=True), rk[b] - rk[b].mean(1, keepdims=True))
            corr.append(float(np.mean((ra * rb).sum(1) / np.sqrt((ra**2).sum(1) * (rb**2).sum(1)))))
        top = []
        for a, b in ((0, 1), (0, 2), (1, 2)):
            ta, tb = (np.argsort(-ws[a], 1)[:, :10], np.argsort(-ws[b], 1)[:, :10])
            top.append(float(np.mean([len(set(x) & set(y)) / 10 for x, y in zip(ta, tb, strict=True)])))
        stab[stem] = {"spearman": float(np.mean(corr)), "top10_overlap": float(np.mean(top))}
    res["stability"] = stab
    cloudy = np.array([float(r.get("cloud_cover", 0) or 0) >= 10 for r in test])
    res["real_cloud_n"] = int(cloudy.sum())

    def sub_f1(pred, mask):
        return float(
            f1_score(labels[mask], np.array(pred)[mask], labels=np.arange(classes), average="macro", zero_division=0)
        )

    rc = {}
    cands = {
        "frozen": [R / "main/arms/frozen_clean.json"],
        "lora_r8": sorted(R.glob("main/arms/clean_plain_l0.0_s*.json")),
        "lora_r2": sorted(R.glob("lora_rank_sweep/clean_plain_r2_s*.json")),
        "linear": sorted(R.glob("cloud_param/B_clean_attention_h0_s*.json")),
    }
    for arm in ("attention", "guided", "input", "gated", "prior", "gated_shuffled", "prior_shuffled"):
        cands[arm] = sorted(R.glob(f"saliency_attention/clean_{arm}_s[0-9]*.json"))
    for arm in ("soft_gated", "soft_prior", "soft_gated_shuffled"):
        cands[arm] = sorted(R.glob(f"saliency_soft/clean_{arm}_s[0-9]*.json"))
    for name, paths in cands.items():
        vals = []
        for p in paths:
            d = json.loads(Path(p).read_text())
            pred = d.get("predictions")
            if pred is None:
                continue
            vals.append((sub_f1(pred, cloudy), sub_f1(pred, ~cloudy)))
        if vals:
            rc[name] = {
                "seeds": len(vals),
                "real_cloudy_f1": float(np.mean([v[0] for v in vals])),
                "cloud_free_f1": float(np.mean([v[1] for v in vals])),
            }
    res["real_cloud"] = rc
    json.dump(res, open(OUT / "analysis.json", "w"), indent=1)
    sizes = json.loads(Path(DEFAULT_MANIFEST).with_name("image_sizes.json").read_text())

    def shifted_box(r):
        x0, y0, x1, y1 = r["crop"]
        side = x1 - x0
        cx, cy, bw, bh = (x0 + r["box_cx"] * side, y0 + r["box_cy"] * side, r["box_w"] * side, r["box_h"] * side)
        Wd, Hd = sizes[r["key"]]
        rng = random.Random(int(hashlib.sha256(r["key"].encode()).hexdigest()[:8], 16))
        bx, by = (min(0.5, bw / side / 2), min(0.5, bh / side / 2))
        best = (x0, y0)
        for _ in range(50):
            u, v = (rng.uniform(bx, 1 - bx), rng.uniform(by, 1 - by))
            if max(abs(u - 0.5), abs(v - 0.5)) < 0.15:
                continue
            nx0, ny0 = (min(max(cx - u * side, 0), Wd - side), min(max(cy - v * side, 0), Hd - side))
            if max(abs((cx - nx0) / side - 0.5), abs((cy - ny0) / side - 0.5)) >= 0.15:
                best = (nx0, ny0)
                break
        nx0, ny0 = best
        return [(cx - nx0) / side, (cy - ny0) / side, r["box_w"], r["box_h"]]

    cache = np.load(DEFAULT_CACHE, mmap_mode="r")
    scache = np.load("/mnt/sdd/FYP/cache/shift_test_v2/images_u8.npy", mmap_mode="r")
    W = {
        k: {
            v: np.load(str(R / f"shift_test/clean_{k}_s42_{v}.npz"))["weights"].astype(np.float32)
            for v in ("centred", "shifted")
        }
        for k in ("attention", "gated", "gated_shuffled")
    }
    P = {
        k: {v: np.load(str(R / f"shift_test/clean_{k}_s42_{v}.npz"))["predictions"] for v in ("centred", "shifted")}
        for k in ("gated",)
    }
    good = [
        i for i in range(len(test)) if P["gated"]["centred"][i] == labels[i] and P["gated"]["shifted"][i] == labels[i]
    ]
    pick = list(np.random.default_rng(3).choice(good, 6, replace=False))
    np.savez_compressed(
        OUT / "examples.npz",
        idx=np.array(pick),
        centred=np.stack([cache[test[i]["idx"]] for i in pick]),
        shifted=np.stack([scache[i] for i in pick]),
        **{f"{k}_{v}": W[k][v][pick] for k in W for v in ("centred", "shifted")},
        boxes=np.array([[test[i]["box_cx"], test[i]["box_cy"], test[i]["box_w"], test[i]["box_h"]] for i in pick]),
        classes=np.array([test[i]["category"] for i in pick]),
        sboxes=np.array([shifted_box(test[i]) for i in pick]),
    )
    print(json.dumps(res, indent=1)[:4000])


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/fmow_v2/cloud_pooling.py
================================================
"""Cloud-aware pooling heads (pixel cloud score gates attention) and the head-width parameter sweep.

Example::  python -m fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_v2.cloud_pooling
"""

import json
import os
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch
from fyp_drift.evaluation import saliency_attention_study as att
from fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_dinov2l_study import (
    DEFAULT_CACHE,
    DEFAULT_MANIFEST,
    IMAGE_SIZE,
    MEAN,
    STD,
    cloud_mask,
    image_cache,
    image_loader,
    load_rows,
    log,
    scores,
    study_splits,
    write_json,
)
from fyp_drift.evaluation.saliency_adapters import SPLITS
from torch import nn
from torch.nn import functional as F


def main():
    R, T = (Path(os.environ["FYP_RUNS"]), Path(os.environ["FYP_TOKENS"]))
    G = att.GRID
    CELL = IMAGE_SIZE // G
    OUT = R / "cloud_param"
    OUT.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    rows, classes = load_rows(DEFAULT_MANIFEST)
    splits = study_splits(rows)
    cache = image_cache(rows, DEFAULT_CACHE, 3)
    alpha = json.loads((R / "main/calib.json").read_text())["selected_alpha"]
    head_state = torch.load(R / "main/head.pt", weights_only=True)
    width = head_state["weight"].shape[1]

    def cloud_scores(cond, split):
        path = T / f"cloudscore_{cond}_{split}.npy"
        if path.exists():
            return np.load(path)
        out = []
        for images, _, _ in image_loader(cache, splits[split], 64, 3, alpha if cond == "cloud" else 0.0):
            px = (images * STD + MEAN).clamp(0, 1)
            cells = px.reshape(len(px), 3, G, CELL, G, CELL)
            white = cells.min(1).values.mean((2, 4))
            flat = 1 - (cells.mean(1).std((2, 4)) * 4).clamp(0, 1)
            out.append((white * flat).reshape(len(px), -1).half().numpy())
        out = np.concatenate(out)
        np.save(path, out)
        log("cloud scores", cond, split, out.shape)
        return out

    def detector_quality():
        c = cloud_scores("cloud", "future_test").astype(np.float32)
        truth = np.stack(
            [
                (F.avg_pool2d(cloud_mask(r["local_path"])[None], CELL)[0, 0] > 0.5).flatten().numpy()
                for r in splits["future_test"]
            ]
        )
        from sklearn.metrics import roc_auc_score

        clean = cloud_scores("clean", "future_test").astype(np.float32)
        res = {
            "cell_auc_cloud_vs_not": float(roc_auc_score(truth.flatten(), c.flatten())),
            "true_cloud_share": float(truth.mean()),
            "mean_score_cloud_cells": float(c[truth].mean()),
            "mean_score_other_cells": float(c[~truth].mean()),
            "mean_score_clean_images": float(clean.mean()),
        }
        write_json(OUT / "detector.json", res)
        log("detector", res)

    class Head(att.SaliencyAttentionHead):

        def __init__(self, kind, head_state, width, classes, hidden=128):
            base = {"cloud_gate": "attention", "cloud_gate_shuffled": "attention", "cloud_saliency_gate": "gated"}.get(
                kind, kind
            )
            super().__init__(base, head_state, width, classes)
            self.mode = kind
            self.hidden = hidden
            inputs = width
            self.scorer = (
                nn.Sequential(nn.LayerNorm(inputs), nn.Linear(inputs, hidden), nn.GELU(), nn.Linear(hidden, 1))
                if hidden > 0
                else None
            )
            if kind.startswith("cloud"):
                self.kappa, self.tau = (nn.Parameter(torch.tensor(5.0)), nn.Parameter(torch.tensor(0.5)))

        def full_weights(self, tokens, z, c, generator):
            logits = (
                self.scorer(tokens).squeeze(-1)
                if self.scorer is not None
                else torch.zeros(tokens.shape[:2], device=tokens.device)
            )
            w = logits.softmax(1)
            if self.kind == "gated":
                w = w * torch.sigmoid(self.gamma * z + self.delta)
            if self.mode.startswith("cloud"):
                if self.mode.endswith("shuffled"):
                    c = c.gather(1, torch.rand(c.shape, generator=generator).argsort(1).to(c.device))
                w = w * torch.sigmoid(self.kappa * (self.tau - c))
            return w / w.sum(1, keepdim=True).clamp_min(1e-08)

        def run(self, tokens, c, generator):
            z = self.saliency(tokens, generator)
            w = self.full_weights(tokens, z, c, generator)
            return (self.classify(tokens, w), w)

    def batches(tokens, cs, labels, bs, seed=None):
        order = np.random.default_rng(seed).permutation(len(labels)) if seed is not None else np.arange(len(labels))
        for s in range(0, len(order), bs):
            idx = np.sort(order[s : s + bs])
            yield (
                torch.from_numpy(np.asarray(tokens[idx])).cuda().float(),
                torch.from_numpy(cs[idx].astype(np.float32)).cuda(),
                torch.from_numpy(labels[idx]).cuda(),
            )

    @torch.no_grad()
    def infer(model, tokens, cs, labels):
        model.eval()
        g = torch.Generator().manual_seed(7)
        P, W = ([], [])
        for x, c, _ in batches(tokens, cs, labels, 256):
            lo, w = model.run(x, c, g)
            P.append(lo.argmax(1).cpu())
            W.append(w.cpu())
        return (torch.cat(P).numpy(), torch.cat(W))

    def train_eval(kind, hidden, seed, data, cond):
        torch.manual_seed(seed)
        model = Head(kind, head_state, width, classes, hidden).cuda()
        params = [p for p in model.parameters() if p.requires_grad]
        n_params = sum((p.numel() for p in params))
        opt = torch.optim.AdamW(params, lr=0.001, weight_decay=0.0001)
        best = (-1, None, 0)
        for epoch in range(15):
            model.train()
            g = torch.Generator().manual_seed(seed * 1000 + epoch)
            for x, c, y in batches(*data["adapt_train"], 128, seed * 1000 + epoch):
                lo, _ = model.run(x, c, g)
                loss = F.cross_entropy(lo, y)
                opt.zero_grad()
                loss.backward()
                opt.step()
            v = scores(data["adapt_val"][2], infer(model, *data["adapt_val"])[0], classes)["macro_f1"]
            if v > best[0]:
                best = (v, {k: t.clone() for k, t in model.state_dict().items()}, epoch + 1)
        model.load_state_dict(best[1])
        pred, w = infer(model, *data["future_test"])
        labels = data["future_test"][2]
        res = {
            "kind": kind,
            "hidden": hidden,
            "seed": seed,
            "condition": cond,
            "trainable_parameters": n_params,
            "adapt_val_macro_f1": best[0],
            "best_epoch": best[2],
            "future_test": scores(labels, pred, classes),
            "predictions": pred.tolist(),
            "grounding": att.grounding(w, splits["future_test"], cond == "cloud"),
            "scalars": {n: float(getattr(model, n)) for n in ("kappa", "tau", "gamma", "delta") if hasattr(model, n)},
        }
        if cond == "cloud":
            cs = torch.from_numpy(data["future_test"][1].astype(np.float32))
            res["weight_on_high_cloud_score_cells"] = float((w * (cs > 0.5)).sum(1).mean())
        return res

    def data_for(cond):
        return {
            s: (
                np.load(T / f"{cond}_{s}.npy", mmap_mode="r"),
                cloud_scores(cond, s),
                np.array([r["class_id"] for r in splits[s]]),
            )
            for s in SPLITS
        }

    detector_quality()
    jobs = [("A", k, 128) for k in ("cloud_gate", "cloud_gate_shuffled", "cloud_saliency_gate")] + [
        ("B", k, h) for h in (0, 8, 32) for k in ("attention", "gated", "gated_shuffled")
    ]
    for cond in ("cloud", "clean"):
        data = data_for(cond)
        for part, kind, hidden in jobs:
            for seed in (42, 43, 44):
                path = OUT / f"{part}_{cond}_{kind}_h{hidden}_s{seed}.json"
                if path.exists():
                    continue
                r = train_eval(kind, hidden, seed, data, cond)
                write_json(path, r)
                log(
                    f"{part} {cond} {kind} h{hidden} s{seed}: params={r['trainable_parameters']} F1={r['future_test']['macro_f1']:.4f} acc={r['future_test']['accuracy']:.4f} focus={r['grounding']['box_mass_over_share']:.2f} "
                    + (
                        f"cloudmass={r['grounding'].get('cloud_mass', 0):.3f}/{r['grounding'].get('cloud_share', 0):.3f} "
                        if cond == "cloud"
                        else ""
                    )
                    + str(r["scalars"])
                )
    log("DONE")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/fmow_v2/context.py
================================================
"""Shared run context (splits, image cache, cloud settings, frozen head), configured by scripts/fmow_v2/env.sh."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

import torch
from fyp_drift.evaluation import fmow_dinov2l_study as study
from torch import nn

BACKBONE = "facebook/dinov2-large"
WIDTH = 1024  # DINOv2-L token width


@dataclass
class Context:
    runs: Path  # root of the v2 outputs (main/, saliency_attention/, ...)
    rows: list  # manifest rows with class ids, crops and acquisition metadata
    classes: int
    splits: dict  # hist_train, hist_val, adapt_train, adapt_val, future_test
    cache: object  # memory-mapped uint8 image cache
    overlay_alpha: float  # training-overlay opacity chosen on 2010-13 validation
    cumulus: dict  # {"off_object": (strength, "off_object"), "on_object": (strength, "on_object")}

    def historical_head(self) -> nn.Linear:
        """The frozen 2010-13 linear head (on the GPU, gradients off)."""
        state = torch.load(self.runs / "main/head.pt", weights_only=True)
        head = nn.Linear(state["weight"].shape[1], self.classes).cuda().eval()
        head.load_state_dict(state)
        head.requires_grad_(False)
        return head

    def test_conditions(self) -> dict:
        """Every test condition as (cloud strength, cloud region): clean, training overlay and held-out cumulus."""
        return {"clean": (0.0, None), "overlay": (self.overlay_alpha, None), **self.cumulus}


def load_context(limit: int = 0) -> Context:
    """Load the manifest, splits and cache; ``limit`` caps every split for a quick smoke test."""
    runs = Path(os.environ["FYP_RUNS"])
    rows, classes = study.load_rows(study.DEFAULT_MANIFEST)
    splits = study.study_splits(rows)
    if limit:
        splits = {name: part[:limit] for name, part in splits.items()}
    cache = study.image_cache(rows, study.DEFAULT_CACHE, 4)
    overlay_alpha = json.loads((runs / "main/calib.json").read_text())["selected_alpha"]
    regions = json.loads((runs / "main/calib_regions.json").read_text())
    cumulus = {
        name: (cfg["selected_alpha"], name)
        for name, cfg in regions.items()
        if isinstance(cfg, dict) and "selected_alpha" in cfg
    }
    return Context(runs, rows, classes, splits, cache, overlay_alpha, cumulus)


def pool_2x2(tokens: torch.Tensor, grid: int = 20) -> torch.Tensor:
    """Average neighbouring 2 x 2 patch tokens: (B, grid*grid, D) -> (B, (grid/2)^2, D), row-major."""
    batch, _, width = tokens.shape
    half = grid // 2
    return tokens.reshape(batch, half, 2, half, 2, width).mean((2, 4)).reshape(batch, half * half, width)


def trainable(module: nn.Module) -> list:
    return [p for p in module.parameters() if p.requires_grad]



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/fmow_v2/equal_lora.py
================================================
"""LoRA trained with the heads' protocol: best-validation epoch of up to 5, early stopping, all test conditions.

Learning rate 3e-4 was chosen once on rank-2 clean seed 42 from {1e-4, 3e-4}.
Example::  python -m fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_v2.equal_lora --rank 2 --blocks 6 --condition clean --seed 43
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import time
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F
from fyp_drift.evaluation import fmow_dinov2l_study as study
from fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_v2.context import BACKBONE, load_context, trainable
from fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_v2.models import adapter_state, save_model

BATCH, ACCUMULATE, EVAL_BATCH, WORKERS = 16, 2, 64, 4  # effective batch 32


def output_name(condition: str, rank: int, lr: float, blocks: int, seed: int) -> str:
    """File name used in ``runs/v2/lora_fair`` (six-block runs carry no block tag)."""
    tag = "" if blocks == 6 else f"_b{blocks}"
    return f"{condition}_r{rank}_lr{lr:g}{tag}_s{seed}.json"


def train_and_score(ctx, rank, blocks, condition, seed, lr, max_epochs=5, patience=2):
    head = ctx.historical_head()  # LoRA keeps the old classifier frozen and changes only the features
    alpha = ctx.overlay_alpha if condition == "cloud" else 0.0
    torch.manual_seed(seed)
    np.random.seed(seed)
    backbone = study.Backbone(BACKBONE, blocks, rank).cuda()
    params = trainable(backbone)
    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(params, lr=lr, weight_decay=0.01)
    scaler = torch.amp.GradScaler("cuda")

    best_val, best_params, best_epoch, stale, history, start = -1.0, None, 0, 0, [], time.time()
    for epoch in range(max_epochs):
        backbone.train()
        optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
        loader = study.image_loader(
            ctx.cache, ctx.splits["adapt_train"], BATCH, WORKERS, alpha, shuffle_seed=seed + epoch
        )
        for step, (images, targets, _) in enumerate(loader):
            images, targets = images.cuda(non_blocking=True), targets.cuda(non_blocking=True)
            with torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=torch.float16):
                tokens = backbone.late_features(backbone.early_features(images))[:, 1:].float()
                loss = F.cross_entropy(head(tokens.mean(1)), targets) / ACCUMULATE
            scaler.scale(loss).backward()
            if (step + 1) % ACCUMULATE == 0:
                scaler.step(optimizer)
                scaler.update()
                optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
        backbone.eval()
        val, _ = study.evaluate(
            backbone,
            head,
            study.image_loader(ctx.cache, ctx.splits["adapt_val"], EVAL_BATCH, WORKERS, alpha),
            ctx.classes,
        )
        history.append({"epoch": epoch + 1, "val_f1": val["macro_f1"], "seconds": time.time() - start})
        study.log(f"lora r{rank} b{blocks} {condition} s{seed} epoch {epoch + 1}: val={val['macro_f1']:.4f}")
        if val["macro_f1"] > best_val:
            best_val, best_params, best_epoch, stale = (
                val["macro_f1"],
                [p.detach().clone() for p in params],
                epoch + 1,
                0,
            )
        else:
            stale += 1
            if stale >= patience:
                break
    for p, kept in zip(params, best_params, strict=True):
        p.data.copy_(kept)
    train_seconds = time.time() - start

    test, predictions = study.evaluate(
        backbone,
        head,
        study.image_loader(ctx.cache, ctx.splits["future_test"], EVAL_BATCH, WORKERS, alpha),
        ctx.classes,
    )
    extra = {}
    for name, (strength, region) in ctx.test_conditions().items():
        loader = study.image_loader(
            ctx.cache, ctx.splits["future_test"], EVAL_BATCH, WORKERS, strength, cloud_region=region
        )
        metrics, preds = study.evaluate(backbone, head, loader, ctx.classes)
        extra[name] = {"future_test": metrics, "predictions": preds.tolist()}
    result = dict(
        rank=rank,
        blocks=blocks,
        lr=lr,
        condition=condition,
        seed=seed,
        best_epoch=best_epoch,
        best_val=best_val,
        history=history,
        train_seconds=train_seconds,
        trainable_parameters=int(sum(p.numel() for p in params)),
        future_test=test,
        predictions=predictions.tolist(),
        extra=extra,
    )
    del optimizer
    return result, (backbone, head)


def main(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    parser.add_argument("--rank", type=int, default=2)
    parser.add_argument("--blocks", type=int, default=6, help="number of final backbone blocks that carry LoRA")
    parser.add_argument(
        "--condition", choices=("clean", "cloud"), default="clean", help="train on clean images or with the overlay"
    )
    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=42)
    parser.add_argument("--lr", type=float, default=3e-4)
    parser.add_argument("--max-epochs", type=int, default=5)
    parser.add_argument("--patience", type=int, default=2)
    parser.add_argument("--limit", type=int, default=0, help="cap every split (smoke test only)")
    parser.add_argument("--export", type=Path, help="also save the trained adapters here (safetensors)")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    ctx = load_context(args.limit)
    out = ctx.runs / "lora_fair"
    out.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    path = out / output_name(args.condition, args.rank, args.lr, args.blocks, args.seed)
    if path.exists() and not (args.limit or args.export):
        study.log("exists, skipping", path.name)
        return
    result, (backbone, head) = train_and_score(
        ctx, args.rank, args.blocks, args.condition, args.seed, args.lr, args.max_epochs, args.patience
    )
    if args.export:
        names = sorted({r["category"] for r in ctx.rows})
        tensors = {**adapter_state(backbone), "historical.weight": head.weight, "historical.bias": head.bias}
        save_model(
            args.export,
            "lora",
            tensors,
            names,
            rank=args.rank,
            blocks=args.blocks,
            seed=args.seed,
            val_f1=result["best_val"],
        )
    if not args.limit and not path.exists():
        study.write_json(path, result)
    study.log("done", path.name, {k: round(v["future_test"]["macro_f1"], 4) for k, v in result["extra"].items()})


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/fmow_v2/expand_subset.py
================================================
"""Grow the country-matched fMoW subset to 14k images per period, downloading images and metadata.

Example::  python -m fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_v2.expand_subset
"""

import collections
import concurrent.futures
import csv
import json
import random
import time
import urllib.request
from pathlib import Path


def main():
    ROOT = Path("/mnt/sdd/FYP/data/fmow_v1.1")
    SUB = ROOT / "subset_7k_country_matched"
    OUT = ROOT / "subset_14k_country_matched"
    OUT.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    URL = "https://spacenet-dataset.s3.amazonaws.com/Hosted-Datasets/fmow/fmow-rgb/"
    TARGET = 14000

    def log(*a):
        print(time.strftime("%H:%M:%S"), *a, flush=True)

    def period(ts):
        y = int(ts[:4])
        return (
            "historical_2010_13"
            if 2010 <= y <= 2013
            else "adapt_2014_15" if 2014 <= y <= 2015 else "future_2016_17" if 2016 <= y <= 2017 else None
        )

    old = list(csv.DictReader(open(SUB / "manifest_downloaded.csv")))
    old_keys = {r["key"] for r in old}
    pool = {}
    for fn in ("metadata_sample_trainval_20k.jsonl", "metadata_train_remaining.jsonl", "metadata_sample_5000.jsonl"):
        for line in open(ROOT / fn):
            r = json.loads(line)
            p = period(r["timestamp"])
            if p and r["key"] not in pool:
                pool[r["key"]] = {**r, "period": p}
    P = ("historical_2010_13", "adapt_2014_15", "future_2016_17")
    avail = {p: collections.defaultdict(list) for p in P}
    for k, r in pool.items():
        if k not in old_keys:
            avail[r["period"]][r["country"]].append(k)
    have = {p: collections.Counter((r["country"] for r in old if r["period"] == p)) for p in P}
    countries = set.intersection(*[set(have[p]) | set(avail[p]) for p in P])
    cap = {c: min((have[p][c] + len(avail[p][c]) for p in P)) for c in countries}
    base = {c: max((have[p][c] for p in P)) for c in countries}
    lo, hi = (0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(60):
        f = (lo + hi) / 2
        q = {c: min(cap[c], max(base[c], int(f * cap[c]))) for c in countries}
        lo, hi = (f, hi) if sum(q.values()) < TARGET else (lo, f)
    q = {c: min(cap[c], max(base[c], int(hi * cap[c]))) for c in countries}
    log("quota total per period", sum(q.values()), "countries", len(q))
    rng = random.Random(0)
    new = []
    for p in P:
        for c, n in q.items():
            extra = n - have[p][c]
            if extra > 0:
                ks = sorted(avail[p][c])
                rng.shuffle(ks)
                new += [(k, p) for k in ks[:extra]]
    log("new images to fetch", len(new))

    def fetch(item):
        k, p = item
        r = pool[k]
        parts = k.split("/")
        dest = SUB / p / parts[1] / parts[2] / parts[3]
        dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        if not dest.exists():
            with urllib.request.urlopen(URL + k, timeout=60) as resp:
                data = resp.read()
            dest.write_bytes(data)
        with urllib.request.urlopen(URL + k.replace(".jpg", ".json"), timeout=60) as resp:
            meta = json.load(resp)
        W, H = (meta["img_width"], meta["img_height"])
        x, y, w, h = meta["bounding_boxes"][0]["box"]
        row = {
            "key": k,
            "split": r["split"],
            "category": r["category"],
            "timestamp": r["timestamp"],
            "country": r["country"],
            "utm": r["utm"],
            "period": p,
            "size_bytes": dest.stat().st_size,
            "head_error": "",
            "local_path": str(dest),
            "downloaded_bytes": dest.stat().st_size,
        }
        md = {
            "key": k,
            "cloud_cover": meta.get("cloud_cover"),
            "off_nadir_angle": meta.get("off_nadir_angle_dbl"),
            "sun_elevation": meta.get("sun_elevation_dbl"),
            "sun_azimuth": meta.get("sun_azimuth_dbl"),
            "target_azimuth": meta.get("target_azimuth_dbl"),
            "gsd": meta.get("gsd"),
            "box_cx": (x + w / 2) / W,
            "box_cy": (y + h / 2) / H,
            "box_w": w / W,
            "box_h": h / H,
        }
        return (row, md)

    rows, mds, fails, t0, nbytes = ([], [], [], time.time(), 0)
    with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(48) as ex:
        futs = {ex.submit(fetch, it): it for it in new}
        for i, fu in enumerate(concurrent.futures.as_completed(futs)):
            try:
                r, m = fu.result()
                rows.append(r)
                mds.append(m)
                nbytes += r["downloaded_bytes"]
            except Exception as e:
                fails.append((futs[fu][0], repr(e)))
            if (i + 1) % 1000 == 0:
                dt = time.time() - t0
                log(
                    f"{i + 1}/{len(new)} done, {nbytes / 1000000.0 / dt:.1f} MB/s, eta {(len(new) - i - 1) * dt / (i + 1) / 60:.1f} min, fails {len(fails)}"
                )
    old_md = list(csv.DictReader(open(SUB / "acquisition_metadata.csv")))
    fields = list(old[0].keys())
    mfields = list(old_md[0].keys())
    with open(OUT / "manifest_downloaded.csv", "w", newline="") as h:
        w = csv.DictWriter(h, fieldnames=fields)
        w.writeheader()
        w.writerows(old + rows)
    with open(OUT / "acquisition_metadata.csv", "w", newline="") as h:
        w = csv.DictWriter(h, fieldnames=mfields)
        w.writeheader()
        w.writerows(old_md + mds)
    with open(OUT / "failures.csv", "w", newline="") as h:
        w = csv.writer(h)
        w.writerow(["key", "error"])
        w.writerows(fails)
    cnt = collections.Counter((r["period"] for r in old + rows))
    log("DONE", dict(cnt), "fails", len(fails), f"{nbytes / 1000000000.0:.1f} GB in {(time.time() - t0) / 60:.1f} min")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/fmow_v2/explain.py
================================================
"""Exact explanations: per-cell maps of the old and new heads over the test set, then 20x20 patch maps for the examples.

Example::  python -m fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_v2.explain            # both steps; or: explain maps / explain patches
"""

import argparse
import json
import os
import types
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch

from fyp_drift.evaluation import saliency_attention_study as sas
from fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_dinov2l_study import (
    DEFAULT_CACHE,
    DEFAULT_MANIFEST,
    load_rows,
    study_splits,
)


def maps():
    R = Path(os.environ["FYP_RUNS"])
    T = Path(os.environ["FYP_TOKENS"])
    OUT = R / "analysis"
    rows, classes = load_rows(DEFAULT_MANIFEST)
    sp = study_splits(rows)
    test = sp["future_test"]
    n = len(test)
    labels = np.array([r["class_id"] for r in test])
    names = {r["class_id"]: r["category"] for r in rows}
    boxes = np.array([[r["box_cx"], r["box_cy"], r["box_w"], r["box_h"]] for r in test])
    hs = torch.load(R / "main/head.pt", weights_only=True)
    W = hs["weight"].float().cuda()
    b = hs["bias"].float().cuda()

    def inbox(G):
        c = (np.arange(G) + 0.5) / G
        X, Y = np.meshgrid(c, c)
        return np.stack(
            [
                ((np.abs(X - cx) <= w / 2) & (np.abs(Y - cy) <= h / 2)).ravel()
                for cx, cy, w, h in boxes
            ]
        )

    def metrics(ev, mask):
        """ev: (n, N) signed contributions. Share of positive evidence in box, lift over box area, share of cells holding half of it."""
        pos = np.clip(ev, 0, None)
        tot = pos.sum(1) + 1e-09
        inside = (pos * mask).sum(1) / tot
        area = mask.mean(1).clip(0.001)
        srt = -np.sort(-pos, 1)
        cum = srt.cumsum(1) / tot[:, None]
        half = (cum < 0.5).sum(1) + 1
        return (inside, inside / area, half / ev.shape[1])

    res, keep = ({}, {})
    for G, path in (
        (20, Path("/mnt/sdd/FYP/cache/adapter_tokens_v2/clean_future_test.npy")),
        (10, T / "clean_future_test.npy"),
    ):
        tok = np.load(path, mmap_mode="r")
        EV, EVc, PR = ([], [], [])
        with torch.no_grad():
            for s in range(0, n, 128):
                h = torch.from_numpy(
                    np.asarray(tok[s : s + 128], dtype=np.float32)
                ).cuda()
                e = h @ W.T
                L = e.mean(1) + b
                p = L.argmax(1)
                r2 = L.topk(2, 1).indices
                ru = torch.where(r2[:, 0] == p, r2[:, 1], r2[:, 0])
                ar = torch.arange(len(p), device=h.device)
                EV.append((e[ar, :, p] - e.mean(2)).cpu().numpy() / e.shape[1])
                EVc.append((e[ar, :, p] - e[ar, :, ru]).cpu().numpy() / e.shape[1])
                PR.append(p.cpu().numpy())
        EV, EVc, PR = (np.concatenate(EV), np.concatenate(EVc), np.concatenate(PR))
        m = inbox(G)
        for nm, ev in (("old_evidence", EV), ("old_contrast", EVc)):
            i, lift, hf = metrics(ev, m)
            res[f"{nm}_G{G}"] = {
                "box_share_of_support": float(i.mean()),
                "lift": float(np.median(lift)),
                "cells_for_half": float(hf.mean()),
                "lift_correct": float(np.median(lift[PR == labels])),
                "lift_wrong": float(np.median(lift[PR != labels])),
            }
            keep[f"{nm}_G{G}"] = (i, lift, hf)
        keep[f"oldpred_G{G}"] = PR
        if G == 20:
            EV20, EVc20 = (EV, EVc)
    data = {
        s: (
            np.load(T / f"clean_{s}.npy", mmap_mode="r"),
            np.array([r["class_id"] for r in sp[s]]),
        )
        for s in ("adapt_train", "adapt_val", "future_test")
    }
    args = types.SimpleNamespace(lr=0.001, epochs=15, batch=128)
    width = hs["weight"].shape[1]
    m10 = inbox(10)
    NEW = {}
    for arm in ("attention", "gated", "gated_shuffled"):
        model, best = sas.train(arm, 42, hs, width, classes, data, 0.0, args)
        preds, wts, _ = sas.infer(model, *data["future_test"])
        Wn = model.classifier.weight.detach()
        bn = model.classifier.bias.detach()
        tok = data["future_test"][0]
        C, maxerr = ([], 0.0)
        with torch.no_grad():
            for s in range(0, n, 128):
                h = torch.from_numpy(
                    np.asarray(tok[s : s + 128], dtype=np.float32)
                ).cuda()
                w = wts[s : s + 128].cuda()
                e = h @ Wn.T
                p = torch.from_numpy(preds[s : s + 128]).cuda().long()
                ar = torch.arange(len(p), device=h.device)
                full = (w[..., None] * h).sum(1) @ Wn.T + bn
                maxerr = max(
                    maxerr, float(((w[..., None] * e).sum(1) + bn - full).abs().max())
                )
                C.append((w * (e[ar, :, p] - e.mean(2))).cpu().numpy())
        C = np.concatenate(C)
        i, lift, hf = metrics(C, m10)
        res[f"new_{arm}"] = {
            "test_f1_check": best["val_f1"],
            "completeness_max_err": maxerr,
            "box_share_of_support": float(i.mean()),
            "lift": float(np.median(lift)),
            "cells_for_half": float(hf.mean()),
            "lift_correct": float(np.median(lift[preds == labels])),
            "lift_wrong": float(np.median(lift[preds != labels])),
        }
        NEW[arm] = (C, preds, wts.numpy())
        keep[f"new_{arm}"] = (i, lift, hf)
    i, lift, _ = keep["old_evidence_G20"]
    ok = keep["oldpred_G20"] == labels
    il, ll, _ = keep["new_gated"]
    per = {}
    for c in range(classes):
        s = ok & (labels == c)
        if s.sum() >= 15:
            per[names[c]] = {
                "n": int(s.sum()),
                "old_lift": float(np.median(lift[s])),
                "old_box_share": float(i[s].mean()),
                "new_gated_lift": float(np.median(ll[s])),
                "box_area": float(inbox(20)[s].mean()),
            }
    res["per_class"] = dict(sorted(per.items(), key=lambda kv: -kv[1]["old_lift"]))
    area = inbox(20).mean(1)
    cand = np.where(ok & (area > 0.05) & (area < 0.4) & (NEW["gated"][1] == labels))[0]
    order = cand[np.argsort(-lift[cand])]

    def distinct(idx, k):
        out, seen = ([], set())
        for j in idx:
            if labels[j] not in seen:
                out.append(j)
                seen.add(labels[j])
            if len(out) == k:
                break
        return out

    good, bad = (distinct(order, 6), distinct(order[::-1], 6))
    old8 = [893, 5931, 859, 4517, 3956, 3229, 1124, 3529]
    sel = good + bad + old8
    cache = np.load(DEFAULT_CACHE, mmap_mode="r")
    np.savez_compressed(
        OUT / "explain_examples.npz",
        idx=np.array(sel),
        group=np.array(["good"] * 6 + ["bad"] * 6 + ["earlier"] * 8),
        images=np.stack([cache[test[j]["idx"]] for j in sel]),
        boxes=boxes[sel],
        labels=labels[sel],
        old=keep["oldpred_G20"][sel],
        old_ev20=EV20[sel].astype(np.float32),
        old_ct20=EVc20[sel].astype(np.float32),
        lift=lift[sel],
        **{f"new_{a}_contrib": NEW[a][0][sel].astype(np.float32) for a in NEW},
        **{f"new_{a}_pred": NEW[a][1][sel] for a in NEW},
        **{f"new_{a}_w": NEW[a][2][sel] for a in NEW},
        names=np.array([names[c] for c in range(classes)]),
    )
    json.dump(res, open(OUT / "explain_eval.json", "w"), indent=1)
    print(json.dumps({k: v for k, v in res.items() if k != "per_class"}, indent=1))
    pc = list(res["per_class"].items())
    print("TOP", pc[:8])
    print("BOTTOM", pc[-8:])


def patches():
    R = Path(os.environ["FYP_RUNS"])
    T = Path(os.environ["FYP_TOKENS"])
    OUT = R / "analysis"
    rows, classes = load_rows(DEFAULT_MANIFEST)
    sp = study_splits(rows)
    n = len(sp["future_test"])
    labels = np.array([r["class_id"] for r in sp["future_test"]])
    boxes = np.array(
        [[r["box_cx"], r["box_cy"], r["box_w"], r["box_h"]] for r in sp["future_test"]]
    )
    sel = np.load(OUT / "explain_examples.npz")["idx"]
    hs = torch.load(R / "main/head.pt", weights_only=True)
    data = {
        s: (
            np.load(T / f"clean_{s}.npy", mmap_mode="r"),
            np.array([r["class_id"] for r in sp[s]]),
        )
        for s in ("adapt_train", "adapt_val", "future_test")
    }
    t20 = np.load(
        "/mnt/sdd/FYP/cache/adapter_tokens_v2/clean_future_test.npy", mmap_mode="r"
    )
    args = types.SimpleNamespace(lr=0.001, epochs=15, batch=128)
    out = {}

    def inbox20():
        c = (np.arange(20) + 0.5) / 20
        X, Y = np.meshgrid(c, c)
        return np.stack(
            [
                ((np.abs(X - cx) <= w / 2) & (np.abs(Y - cy) <= h / 2)).ravel()
                for cx, cy, w, h in boxes
            ]
        )

    M = inbox20()
    stats = {}
    for arm in ("attention", "gated", "gated_shuffled"):
        model, _ = sas.train(
            arm, 42, hs, hs["weight"].shape[1], classes, data, 0.0, args
        )
        preds, wts, _ = sas.infer(model, *data["future_test"])
        Wn = model.classifier.weight.detach()
        bn = model.classifier.bias.detach()
        C20, err, top_in = ([], 0.0, [])
        with torch.no_grad():
            for s in range(0, n, 64):
                h = torch.from_numpy(
                    np.asarray(t20[s : s + 64], dtype=np.float32)
                ).cuda()
                w10 = wts[s : s + 64].cuda().reshape(-1, 10, 10)
                w20 = (
                    w10.repeat_interleave(2, 1).repeat_interleave(2, 2).reshape(-1, 400)
                    / 4
                )
                e = h @ Wn.T
                p = torch.from_numpy(preds[s : s + 64]).cuda().long()
                ar = torch.arange(len(p), device=h.device)
                full = (w20[..., None] * e).sum(1) + bn
                ref = model.classifier(
                    (
                        wts[s : s + 64].cuda()[..., None]
                        * torch.from_numpy(
                            np.asarray(
                                data["future_test"][0][s : s + 64], dtype=np.float32
                            )
                        ).cuda()
                    ).sum(1)
                )
                err = max(err, float((full - ref).abs().max()))
                c = (w20 * (e[ar, :, p] - e.mean(2))).cpu().numpy()
                C20.append(c)
        C20 = np.concatenate(C20)
        top = C20.argmax(1)
        top_in = M[np.arange(n), top]
        pos = np.clip(C20, 0, None)
        share = (pos * M).sum(1) / (pos.sum(1) + 1e-09)
        stats[arm] = {
            "reconstruction_err_vs_10x10_head": err,
            "top_cell_in_box": float(top_in.mean()),
            "box_share": float(share.mean()),
            "top_cell_in_box_correct": float(top_in[preds == labels].mean()),
            "top_cell_in_box_wrong": float(top_in[preds != labels].mean()),
        }
        out[f"{arm}_c20"] = C20[sel].astype(np.float32)
        out[f"{arm}_w"] = wts.numpy()[sel]
        out[f"{arm}_pred"] = preds[sel]
    np.savez_compressed(OUT / "explain20.npz", idx=sel, **out)
    print(json.dumps(stats, indent=1))


def main(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "step", nargs="?", choices=("maps", "patches", "all"), default="all"
    )
    step = parser.parse_args(argv).step
    if step in ("maps", "all"):
        maps()
    if step in ("patches", "all"):
        patches()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/fmow_v2/hybrid.py
================================================
"""LoRA + soft saliency gate trained end to end; the old classifier stays frozen, as in plain LoRA.

Saliency is the frozen head's standardised evidence on the unadapted tokens.
Example::  python -m fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_v2.hybrid --seed 43
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import time
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F
from fyp_drift.evaluation import fmow_dinov2l_study as study
from fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_v2.context import (
    BACKBONE,
    WIDTH,
    load_context,
    pool_2x2,
    trainable,
)
from fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_v2.models import adapter_state, save_model
from torch import nn

BATCH, ACCUMULATE, EVAL_BATCH, WORKERS = 16, 2, 64, 4


class GatedPooling(nn.Module):
    """Attention pooling over 10 x 10 cells, optionally soft-gated by saliency, then the frozen old classifier."""

    def __init__(self, historical: nn.Linear, kind: str = "soft_gated", width: int = 8):
        super().__init__()
        self.kind = kind
        self.classifier = nn.Linear(WIDTH, historical.out_features)
        self.classifier.load_state_dict(historical.state_dict())
        self.classifier.requires_grad_(False)
        self.scorer = nn.Sequential(nn.LayerNorm(WIDTH), nn.Linear(WIDTH, width), nn.GELU(), nn.Linear(width, 1))
        if kind == "soft_gated":
            self.gamma = nn.Parameter(torch.tensor(2.0))  # slope of the gate
            self.delta = nn.Parameter(torch.tensor(0.0))  # offset of the gate
            self.rho_logit = nn.Parameter(torch.tensor(0.0))  # floor rho = sigmoid(rho_logit), starts at 0.5

    def weights(self, cells, saliency):
        w = self.scorer(cells).squeeze(-1).softmax(1)
        if self.kind == "soft_gated":
            rho = torch.sigmoid(self.rho_logit)
            w = w * (rho + (1 - rho) * torch.sigmoid(self.gamma * saliency + self.delta))
            w = w / w.sum(1, keepdim=True).clamp_min(1e-8)
        return w

    def forward(self, cells, saliency):
        w = self.weights(cells, saliency)
        return self.classifier((w[..., None] * cells).sum(1)), w


class Hybrid(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, historical, rank, blocks, kind):
        super().__init__()
        self.historical = historical
        self.backbone = study.Backbone(BACKBONE, blocks, rank)
        self.pooling = GatedPooling(historical, kind)

    @torch.no_grad()
    def saliency(self, early):
        """Old model's standardised evidence for its own prediction, on the unadapted tokens."""
        self.backbone.use_adapters(False)
        reference = pool_2x2(self.backbone.late_features(early)[:, 1:].float())
        self.backbone.use_adapters(True)
        predicted = self.historical(reference.mean(1)).argmax(1)
        evidence = (reference * self.historical.weight[predicted][:, None, :]).sum(-1)
        return (evidence - evidence.mean(1, keepdim=True)) / evidence.std(1, keepdim=True).clamp_min(1e-6)

    def forward(self, images):
        with torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=torch.float16):
            early = self.backbone.early_features(images)
            cells = pool_2x2(self.backbone.late_features(early)[:, 1:].float())
        z = self.saliency(early) if self.pooling.kind != "attention" else None
        logits, w = self.pooling(cells.float(), z)
        return logits, w, cells


@torch.no_grad()
def evaluate(model, ctx, rows, strength, region=None, explain=False):
    """Macro-F1, predictions and (optionally) each cell's exact contribution to the predicted class."""
    model.eval()
    predictions, contributions, weights = [], [], []
    for images, _, _ in study.image_loader(ctx.cache, rows, EVAL_BATCH, WORKERS, strength, cloud_region=region):
        logits, w, cells = model(images.cuda(non_blocking=True))
        p = logits.argmax(1)
        predictions.append(p.cpu())
        if explain:  # logit_c = sum_i w_i * (W_c . h_i) + b_c, so w_i * (W_c . h_i) is cell i's exact share
            evidence = cells.float() @ model.pooling.classifier.weight.T
            idx = torch.arange(len(p), device=p.device)
            contributions.append((w * (evidence[idx, :, p] - evidence.mean(2))).cpu())
            weights.append(w.cpu())
    predictions = torch.cat(predictions).numpy()
    labels = np.array([r["class_id"] for r in rows])
    explained = (torch.cat(contributions).numpy(), torch.cat(weights).numpy()) if explain else (None, None)
    return study.scores(labels, predictions, ctx.classes), predictions, *explained


def box_mask(rows, grid=10):
    """(images, grid*grid) boolean: cell centre inside the labelled object box."""
    centres = (np.arange(grid) + 0.5) / grid
    xs, ys = np.meshgrid(centres, centres)
    return np.stack(
        [
            ((np.abs(xs - r["box_cx"]) <= r["box_w"] / 2) & (np.abs(ys - r["box_cy"]) <= r["box_h"] / 2)).ravel()
            for r in rows
        ]
    )


def train_and_score(ctx, kind, rank, blocks, seed, max_epochs=5, patience=2):
    torch.manual_seed(seed)
    np.random.seed(seed)
    model = Hybrid(ctx.historical_head(), rank, blocks, kind).cuda()
    lora, pooling = trainable(model.backbone), trainable(model.pooling)
    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(
        [{"params": lora, "lr": 3e-4, "weight_decay": 0.01}, {"params": pooling, "lr": 1e-3, "weight_decay": 1e-4}]
    )
    scaler = torch.amp.GradScaler("cuda")

    best_val, best_state, best_epoch, stale, history, start = -1.0, None, 0, 0, [], time.time()
    for epoch in range(max_epochs):
        model.train()
        optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
        for step, (images, targets, _) in enumerate(
            study.image_loader(ctx.cache, ctx.splits["adapt_train"], BATCH, WORKERS, 0.0, shuffle_seed=seed + epoch)
        ):
            logits, _, _ = model(images.cuda(non_blocking=True))
            scaler.scale(F.cross_entropy(logits, targets.cuda()) / ACCUMULATE).backward()
            if (step + 1) % ACCUMULATE == 0:
                scaler.step(optimizer)
                scaler.update()
                optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
        val = evaluate(model, ctx, ctx.splits["adapt_val"], 0.0)[0]["macro_f1"]
        history.append({"epoch": epoch + 1, "val_f1": val, "seconds": time.time() - start})
        study.log(f"hybrid {kind} r{rank} b{blocks} s{seed} epoch {epoch + 1}: val={val:.4f}")
        if val > best_val:
            best_val, best_epoch, stale = val, epoch + 1, 0
            best_state = (
                [p.detach().clone() for p in lora],
                {k: v.clone() for k, v in model.pooling.state_dict().items()},
            )
        else:
            stale += 1
            if stale >= patience:
                break
    for p, kept in zip(lora, best_state[0], strict=True):
        p.data.copy_(kept)
    model.pooling.load_state_dict(best_state[1])
    train_seconds = time.time() - start

    rows = ctx.splits["future_test"]
    tests = {}
    for name, (strength, region) in ctx.test_conditions().items():
        metrics, preds, contrib, _ = evaluate(model, ctx, rows, strength, region, explain=(name == "clean"))
        tests[name] = {"future_test": metrics, "predictions": preds.tolist()}
        if contrib is not None:
            positive = np.clip(contrib, 0, None)
            tests[name]["box_share_of_support"] = float(
                ((positive * box_mask(rows)).sum(1) / (positive.sum(1) + 1e-9)).mean()
            )
    scalars = {k: float(v.detach()) for k, v in model.pooling.named_parameters() if v.ndim == 0}
    result = dict(
        kind=kind,
        rank=rank,
        blocks=blocks,
        seed=seed,
        best_epoch=best_epoch,
        best_val=best_val,
        history=history,
        train_seconds=train_seconds,
        trainable_parameters=int(sum(p.numel() for p in lora + pooling)),
        scalars=scalars,
        tests=tests,
    )
    return result, model


def main(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    parser.add_argument("--kind", choices=("soft_gated", "attention"), default="soft_gated")
    parser.add_argument("--rank", type=int, default=1)
    parser.add_argument("--blocks", type=int, default=2)
    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=42)
    parser.add_argument("--limit", type=int, default=0, help="cap every split (smoke test only)")
    parser.add_argument("--export", type=Path, help="also save the trained model here (safetensors)")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    ctx = load_context(args.limit)
    out = ctx.runs / "hybrid"
    out.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    path = out / f"clean_{args.kind}_r{args.rank}_w8_b{args.blocks}_s{args.seed}.json"
    if path.exists() and not (args.limit or args.export):
        study.log("exists, skipping", path.name)
        return
    result, model = train_and_score(ctx, args.kind, args.rank, args.blocks, args.seed)
    if args.export:
        names = sorted({r["category"] for r in ctx.rows})
        tensors = {
            **adapter_state(model.backbone),
            **{f"pooling.{k}": v for k, v in model.pooling.state_dict().items()},
        }
        tensors |= {"historical.weight": model.historical.weight, "historical.bias": model.historical.bias}
        save_model(
            args.export,
            "hybrid",
            tensors,
            names,
            rank=args.rank,
            blocks=args.blocks,
            pooling=args.kind,
            seed=args.seed,
            val_f1=result["best_val"],
            scalars=result["scalars"],
        )
    if not args.limit and not path.exists():
        study.write_json(path, result)
    study.log("done", path.name, {k: round(v["future_test"]["macro_f1"], 4) for k, v in result["tests"].items()})


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/fmow_v2/models.py
================================================
"""Save, load, export and run trained v2 models (safetensors weights + JSON config).

Examples::
    python -m fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_v2.models predict models/fmow_v2/soft_gate site.jpg --map map.png
    python -m fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_v2.models export --out models/fmow_v2
    predictor = Predictor.load("models/fmow_v2/soft_gate"); predictor.predict("site.jpg")
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import os
import time
import types
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch
from PIL import Image
from safetensors.torch import load_file, save_file
from torch import nn

from fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_dinov2l_study import MEAN, STD, Backbone
from fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_v2.context import BACKBONE, WIDTH, pool_2x2

IMAGE_SIZE, GRID = 280, 10
KINDS = ("historical", "attention", "gated", "soft_gated", "lora", "hybrid")


def save_model(path, kind, tensors, classes, **config):
    """Write ``model.safetensors`` (weights only, no pickle) and ``config.json`` to ``path``."""
    path = Path(path)
    path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    save_file(
        {k: v.detach().cpu().contiguous() for k, v in tensors.items()},
        str(path / "model.safetensors"),
    )
    config = {
        "kind": kind,
        "classes": list(classes),
        "image_size": IMAGE_SIZE,
        "grid": GRID,
        "backbone": BACKBONE,
        **config,
    }
    (path / "config.json").write_text(json.dumps(config, indent=1))


def adapter_state(backbone):
    """Only the LoRA tensors of a backbone (the frozen DINOv2 weights come from the Hugging Face hub)."""
    return {
        k: v for k, v in backbone.state_dict().items() if ".down." in k or ".up." in k
    }


def build_head(config, tensors):
    """Rebuild a pooling head from its config and weights."""
    from fyp_drift.evaluation import saliency_attention_study as sas
    from fyp_drift.evaluation import soft_saliency_heads as soft
    from fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_v2.hybrid import GatedPooling

    kind, classes = config["kind"], len(config["classes"])
    frozen = (
        {"weight": tensors["frozen.weight"], "bias": tensors["frozen.bias"]}
        if "frozen.weight" in tensors
        else None
    )
    if kind == "historical":
        head = nn.Linear(WIDTH, classes)
        head.load_state_dict({"weight": tensors["weight"], "bias": tensors["bias"]})
        return head
    if kind in ("attention", "gated"):
        head = sas.SaliencyAttentionHead(kind, frozen, WIDTH, classes)
    elif kind == "soft_gated":
        head = soft.SoftSaliencyHead(kind, frozen, WIDTH, classes)
    else:  # lora / hybrid pooling on adapted tokens
        historical = nn.Linear(WIDTH, classes)
        historical.load_state_dict(
            {
                "weight": tensors["pooling.classifier.weight"],
                "bias": tensors["pooling.classifier.bias"],
            }
        )
        head = GatedPooling(historical, config.get("pooling", "soft_gated"))
        tensors = {
            k.removeprefix("pooling."): v
            for k, v in tensors.items()
            if k.startswith("pooling.")
        }
    head.load_state_dict(tensors)
    return head


def load_image(path, box=None):
    """Crop to twice the object's longer side (as in training) when ``box`` = (cx, cy, w, h) in [0, 1] is given."""
    image = Image.open(path).convert("RGB")
    if box is not None:
        width, height = image.size
        cx, cy, bw, bh = (
            box[0] * width,
            box[1] * height,
            box[2] * width,
            box[3] * height,
        )
        side = min(max(2 * max(bw, bh), 0.25 * min(width, height)), min(width, height))
        x0, y0 = min(max(cx - side / 2, 0), width - side), min(
            max(cy - side / 2, 0), height - side
        )
        image = image.crop((x0, y0, x0 + side, y0 + side))
    pixels = (
        np.asarray(
            image.resize((IMAGE_SIZE, IMAGE_SIZE), Image.BICUBIC), dtype=np.float32
        )
        / 255.0
    )
    return ((torch.from_numpy(pixels).permute(2, 0, 1) - MEAN) / STD).unsqueeze(0)


class Predictor:
    """A frozen (or LoRA-adapted) DINOv2-L plus a saved head, ready for inference."""

    def __init__(self, config, backbone, head, historical):
        self.config, self.backbone, self.head, self.historical = (
            config,
            backbone,
            head,
            historical,
        )
        self.classes = config["classes"]

    @classmethod
    def load(cls, path, device=None):
        path = Path(path)
        device = device or ("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
        config = json.loads((path / "config.json").read_text())
        tensors = load_file(str(path / "model.safetensors"))
        blocks, rank = config.get("blocks", 0), config.get("rank", 1)
        backbone = Backbone(config["backbone"], blocks, rank)
        if blocks:
            backbone.load_state_dict(
                {k: v for k, v in tensors.items() if ".down." in k or ".up." in k},
                strict=False,
            )
        head = build_head(config, tensors) if config["kind"] != "lora" else None
        historical = nn.Linear(WIDTH, len(config["classes"]))
        hist_keys = (
            ("frozen.weight", "frozen.bias")
            if "frozen.weight" in tensors
            else ("historical.weight", "historical.bias")
        )
        if hist_keys[0] in tensors:
            historical.load_state_dict(
                {"weight": tensors[hist_keys[0]], "bias": tensors[hist_keys[1]]}
            )
        elif config["kind"] == "historical":
            historical = head
        modules = [m for m in (backbone, head, historical) if m is not None]
        for module in modules:
            module.to(device).eval()
        return cls(config, backbone, head, historical)

    @torch.no_grad()
    def predict(self, image_path, box=None, top=5):
        """Top classes with probabilities and the 10 x 10 attention weights (None for heads without pooling)."""
        device = next(self.backbone.parameters()).device
        images = load_image(image_path, box).to(device)
        tokens = self.backbone(images)[:, 1:].float()
        cells = pool_2x2(tokens)
        kind, weights = self.config["kind"], None
        if kind == "historical":
            logits = self.head(cells.mean(1))
        elif kind == "lora":
            logits = self.historical(tokens.mean(1))
        elif kind == "hybrid":
            reference = self.saliency_reference(images)
            logits, weights = self.head(cells, reference)
        else:
