            logits, weights, _ = self.head(cells, torch.Generator().manual_seed(7))
        probs = logits.softmax(1)[0]
        values, indices = probs.topk(top)
        result = {
            "top": [
                (self.classes[i], float(p))
                for p, i in zip(values, indices, strict=True)
            ]
        }
        if weights is not None:
            result["weights"] = weights[0].reshape(GRID, GRID).cpu().numpy()
        return result

    def saliency_reference(self, images):
        """Old model's standardised evidence on the unadapted tokens (the hybrid's gate input)."""
        self.backbone.use_adapters(False)
        reference = pool_2x2(self.backbone(images)[:, 1:].float())
        self.backbone.use_adapters(True)
        predicted = self.historical(reference.mean(1)).argmax(1)
        evidence = (reference * self.historical.weight[predicted][:, None, :]).sum(-1)
        return (evidence - evidence.mean(1, keepdim=True)) / evidence.std(
            1, keepdim=True
        ).clamp_min(1e-6)


HEADS = {"attention": "attention", "hard_gate": "gated", "soft_gate": "soft_gated"}


def save_map(image_path, weights, out, box=None):
    import matplotlib

    matplotlib.use("Agg")
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
    from scipy.ndimage import zoom

    image = load_image(image_path, box)[0].permute(1, 2, 0).numpy()
    image = (image - image.min()) / (image.max() - image.min())
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(3.2, 3.2))
    ax.imshow(image.mean(-1), cmap="gray")
    ax.imshow(
        zoom(weights, IMAGE_SIZE / weights.shape[0], order=1), cmap="Oranges", alpha=0.6
    )
    ax.axis("off")
    fig.savefig(out, dpi=200, bbox_inches="tight")
    plt.close(fig)


def predict(args):
    """Print the top classes for each image and optionally save the first image's attention map."""
    box = tuple(float(v) for v in args.box.split(",")) if args.box else None
    start = time.time()
    predictor = Predictor.load(args.model)
    print(f"loaded {predictor.config['kind']} in {time.time() - start:.1f}s")
    for path in args.images:
        start = time.time()
        result = predictor.predict(path, box)
        top = ", ".join(f"{name} {p:.2f}" for name, p in result["top"])
        print(f"{path}: {top}  ({1000 * (time.time() - start):.0f} ms)")
        if args.map and "weights" in result:
            try:
                save_map(path, result["weights"], args.map, box)
                print(f"attention map saved to {args.map}")
            except ImportError:
                print("install matplotlib and scipy to save attention maps")
            args.map = None


def export(args):
    """Retrain the seed-42 attention, hard-gate and soft-gate heads exactly as in the experiments and save them."""
    from fyp_drift.evaluation import saliency_attention_study as sas
    from fyp_drift.evaluation import soft_saliency_heads as soft
    from fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_dinov2l_study import (
        DEFAULT_MANIFEST,
        load_rows,
        log,
        scores,
        study_splits,
    )

    runs, tokens = Path(os.environ["FYP_RUNS"]), Path(os.environ["FYP_TOKENS"])
    rows, classes = load_rows(DEFAULT_MANIFEST)
    splits = study_splits(rows)
    names = sorted({r["category"] for r in rows})
    data = {
        s: (
            np.load(tokens / f"clean_{s}.npy", mmap_mode="r"),
            np.array([r["class_id"] for r in splits[s]]),
        )
        for s in sas.SPLITS
    }
    historical = torch.load(runs / "main/head.pt", weights_only=True)
    save_model(args.out / "historical", "historical", historical, names)
    settings = types.SimpleNamespace(lr=1e-3, epochs=15, batch=128)
    for name, kind in HEADS.items():
        if kind == "soft_gated":
            model, best = soft.train(
                kind, args.seed, historical, WIDTH, classes, data, settings
            )
        else:
            model, best = sas.train(
                kind, args.seed, historical, WIDTH, classes, data, 0.0, settings
            )
        predictions = sas.infer(model, *data["future_test"])[0]
        test_f1 = scores(data["future_test"][1], predictions, classes)["macro_f1"]
        save_model(
            args.out / name,
            kind,
            model.state_dict(),
            names,
            seed=args.seed,
            val_f1=best["val_f1"],
            test_f1=test_f1,
        )
        log(f"exported {name}: val={best['val_f1']:.4f} test={test_f1:.4f}")


def main(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter
    )
    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
    p = sub.add_parser("predict", help="classify images with a saved model")
    p.add_argument("model", help="folder with model.safetensors and config.json")
    p.add_argument("images", nargs="+")
    p.add_argument(
        "--box",
        help="object box cx,cy,w,h as fractions of the image (crops like training)",
    )
    p.add_argument(
        "--map", help="save the attention map of the first image to this PNG"
    )
    e = sub.add_parser("export", help="train and save the heads used in the paper")
    e.add_argument("--out", type=Path, default=Path("models/fmow_v2"))
    e.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=42)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    if args.command == "predict":
        predict(args)
    else:
        export(args)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/fmow_v2/saliency_learning.py
================================================
"""Saliency in the training signal only: saliency-guided training and saliency-ranked patch dropping, with random controls.

Example::  python -m fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_v2.saliency_learning
"""

import hashlib
import json
import os
import random
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch
from fyp_drift.evaluation import saliency_attention_study as att
from fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_dinov2l_study import (
    DEFAULT_MANIFEST,
    load_rows,
    log,
    scores,
    study_splits,
    write_json,
)
from fyp_drift.evaluation.saliency_adapters import SPLITS, batches
from torch.nn import functional as F


def main():
    R, T = (Path(os.environ["FYP_RUNS"]), Path(os.environ["FYP_TOKENS"]))
    SHIFT = Path("/mnt/sdd/FYP/cache/shift_test_v2")
    OUT = R / "saliency_learning"
    OUT.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    rows, classes = load_rows(DEFAULT_MANIFEST)
    splits = study_splits(rows)
    test = splits["future_test"]
    sizes = json.loads(Path(DEFAULT_MANIFEST).with_name("image_sizes.json").read_text())
    head_state = torch.load(R / "main/head.pt", weights_only=True)
    width = head_state["weight"].shape[1]

    def shifted_row(r, k):
        x0, y0, x1, y1 = r["crop"]
        side = x1 - x0
        cx, cy, bw, bh = (x0 + r["box_cx"] * side, y0 + r["box_cy"] * side, r["box_w"] * side, r["box_h"] * side)
        W, H = sizes[r["key"]]
        rng = random.Random(int(hashlib.sha256(r["key"].encode()).hexdigest()[:8], 16))
        bx, by = (min(0.5, bw / side / 2), min(0.5, bh / side / 2))
        best = (x0, y0)
        for _ in range(50):
            u, v = (rng.uniform(bx, 1 - bx), rng.uniform(by, 1 - by))
            if max(abs(u - 0.5), abs(v - 0.5)) < 0.15:
                continue
            nx0, ny0 = (min(max(cx - u * side, 0), W - side), min(max(cy - v * side, 0), H - side))
            if max(abs((cx - nx0) / side - 0.5), abs((cy - ny0) / side - 0.5)) >= 0.15:
                best = (nx0, ny0)
                break
        nx0, ny0 = best
        return {**r, "box_cx": (cx - nx0) / side, "box_cy": (cy - ny0) / side}

    srows = [shifted_row(r, k) for k, r in enumerate(test)]

    def keep_mask(z, mode, generator):
        n = z.shape[1]
        if mode in ("sgt", "focus_aug"):
            order, frac = (z.argsort(1), 0.5)
        elif mode == "context_aug":
            order, frac = (z.argsort(1, descending=True), 0.25)
        else:
            order, frac = (torch.rand(z.shape, generator=generator).argsort(1).to(z.device), 0.5)
        k = round(frac * n)
        return torch.ones_like(z).scatter_(1, order[:, :k], 0.0)

    def masked_logits(model, tokens, z, keep):
        w = model.weights(tokens, z) * keep
        return model.classify(tokens, w / w.sum(1, keepdim=True).clamp_min(1e-08))

    def train(mode, seed, data):
        torch.manual_seed(seed)
        model = att.SaliencyAttentionHead("attention", head_state, width, classes).cuda()
        opt = torch.optim.AdamW([p for p in model.parameters() if p.requires_grad], lr=0.001, weight_decay=0.0001)
        best = {"val_f1": -1}
        for epoch in range(15):
            model.train()
            g = torch.Generator().manual_seed(seed * 1000 + epoch)
            for x, y in batches(*data["adapt_train"], 128, shuffle_seed=seed * 1000 + epoch):
                z = model.saliency(x, g)
                keep = keep_mask(z, mode, g)
                if mode in ("sgt", "sgt_random"):
                    full = model.classify(x, model.weights(x, z))
                    masked = masked_logits(model, x, z, keep)
                    loss = (
                        F.cross_entropy(masked, y)
                        + F.kl_div(masked.log_softmax(1), full.softmax(1).detach(), reduction="batchmean")
                        + F.cross_entropy(full, y)
                    )
                else:
                    loss = F.cross_entropy(masked_logits(model, x, z, keep), y)
                opt.zero_grad()
                loss.backward()
                opt.step()
            v = scores(data["adapt_val"][1], att.infer(model, *data["adapt_val"])[0], classes)["macro_f1"]
            if v > best["val_f1"]:
                best = {"val_f1": v, "epoch": epoch + 1, "state": {k: t.clone() for k, t in model.state_dict().items()}}
        model.load_state_dict(best["state"])
        return (model.eval(), best)

    MODES = ("sgt", "sgt_random", "focus_aug", "context_aug", "random_aug")
    for cond in ("clean", "cloud"):
        data = {
            s: (np.load(T / f"{cond}_{s}.npy", mmap_mode="r"), np.array([r["class_id"] for r in splits[s]]))
            for s in SPLITS
        }
        labels = data["future_test"][1]
        for seed in (42, 43, 44):
            for mode in MODES:
                path = OUT / f"{cond}_{mode}_s{seed}.json"
                if path.exists():
                    continue
                model, best = train(mode, seed, data)
                pred, w, z = att.infer(model, *data["future_test"])
                res = {
                    "mode": mode,
                    "condition": cond,
                    "seed": seed,
                    "best_epoch": best["epoch"],
                    "adapt_val_macro_f1": best["val_f1"],
                    "future_test": scores(labels, pred, classes),
                    "predictions": pred.tolist(),
                    "faithfulness": att.faithfulness(model, *data["future_test"], w, pred),
                    "grounding": att.grounding(w, test, cond == "cloud"),
                }
                sp = SHIFT / f"{cond}_shift_p.npy"
                if sp.with_suffix(".done").exists():
                    sp_pred, sw, _ = att.infer(model, np.load(sp, mmap_mode="r"), labels)
                    res["shifted"] = {
                        "scores": scores(labels, sp_pred, classes),
                        "grounding": att.grounding(sw, srows, False),
                    }
                write_json(path, res)
                log(
                    f"{cond} {mode} s{seed}: F1={res['future_test']['macro_f1']:.4f} focus={res['grounding']['box_mass_over_share']:.2f} point={res['grounding']['pointing_game']:.2f} top25del={res['future_test']['accuracy'] - res['faithfulness']['delete_top_0.25']['accuracy']:.3f}"
                    + (
                        f" | shifted F1={res['shifted']['scores']['macro_f1']:.4f} point={res['shifted']['grounding']['pointing_game']:.2f}"
                        if "shifted" in res
                        else ""
                    )
                )
    log("DONE")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/fmow_v2/shift_test.py
================================================
"""Moved-object test: score heads on centred crops and on crops moved so the object sits off-centre.

Example::  python -m fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_v2.shift_test
"""

import hashlib
import json
import os
import random
import types
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch
from fyp_drift.evaluation import saliency_attention_study as att
from fyp_drift.evaluation import soft_saliency_heads as soft
from fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_dinov2l_study import (
    DEFAULT_MANIFEST,
    IMAGE_SIZE,
    Backbone,
    load_rows,
    log,
    scores,
    study_splits,
    write_json,
)
from fyp_drift.evaluation.saliency_adapters import SPLITS, token_cache
from PIL import Image
from torch.utils.data import DataLoader, Dataset


def main():
    R, T = (Path(os.environ["FYP_RUNS"]), Path(os.environ["FYP_TOKENS"]))
    OUT = R / "shift_test"
    OUT.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    FULL = Path("/mnt/sdd/FYP/cache/shift_test_v2")
    FULL.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    G = att.GRID
    rows, classes = load_rows(DEFAULT_MANIFEST)
    splits = study_splits(rows)
    test = splits["future_test"]
    alpha = json.loads((R / "main/calib.json").read_text())["selected_alpha"]
    sizes = json.loads(Path(DEFAULT_MANIFEST).with_name("image_sizes.json").read_text())

    def shifted_row(r, k):
        x0, y0, x1, y1 = r["crop"]
        side = x1 - x0
        cx, cy, bw, bh = (x0 + r["box_cx"] * side, y0 + r["box_cy"] * side, r["box_w"] * side, r["box_h"] * side)
        W, H = sizes[r["key"]]
        rng = random.Random(int(hashlib.sha256(r["key"].encode()).hexdigest()[:8], 16))
        bx, by = (min(0.5, bw / side / 2), min(0.5, bh / side / 2))
        best = (x0, y0)
        for _ in range(50):
            u, v = (rng.uniform(bx, 1 - bx), rng.uniform(by, 1 - by))
            if max(abs(u - 0.5), abs(v - 0.5)) < 0.15:
                continue
            nx0, ny0 = (min(max(cx - u * side, 0), W - side), min(max(cy - v * side, 0), H - side))
            if max(abs((cx - nx0) / side - 0.5), abs((cy - ny0) / side - 0.5)) >= 0.15:
                best = (nx0, ny0)
                break
        nx0, ny0 = best
        return {
            **r,
            "idx": k,
            "crop": (nx0, ny0, nx0 + side, ny0 + side),
            "box_cx": (cx - nx0) / side,
            "box_cy": (cy - ny0) / side,
        }

    srows = [shifted_row(r, k) for k, r in enumerate(test)]
    moved = np.array([max(abs(s["box_cx"] - 0.5), abs(s["box_cy"] - 0.5)) for s in srows])
    log(
        f"shifted test: {len(srows)} images, {np.mean(moved >= 0.15):.3f} moved >= 0.15 off-centre, median offset {np.median(moved):.3f}"
    )
    cache_path = FULL / "images_u8.npy"
    if not cache_path.with_suffix(".done").exists():
        cache = np.lib.format.open_memmap(
            cache_path, mode="w+", dtype=np.uint8, shape=(len(srows), IMAGE_SIZE, IMAGE_SIZE, 3)
        )

        class D(Dataset):

            def __len__(self):
                return len(srows)

            def __getitem__(self, i):
                with Image.open(srows[i]["local_path"]) as im:
                    im = (
                        im.convert("RGB")
                        .crop(tuple((int(round(v)) for v in srows[i]["crop"])))
                        .resize((IMAGE_SIZE, IMAGE_SIZE), Image.BICUBIC)
                    )
                return (i, torch.from_numpy(np.asarray(im, dtype=np.uint8).copy()))

        for ids, ims in DataLoader(D(), batch_size=64, num_workers=3):
            cache[ids.numpy()] = ims.numpy()
        cache.flush()
        cache_path.with_suffix(".done").write_text("ok")
    cache = np.load(cache_path, mmap_mode="r")

    def pooled(cond):
        out = FULL / f"{cond}_shift_p.npy"
        if out.with_suffix(".done").exists():
            return np.load(out, mmap_mode="r")
        backbone = Backbone("facebook/dinov2-large").cuda().eval()
        full = token_cache(backbone, cache, srows, FULL / f"{cond}_shift.npy", alpha if cond == "cloud" else 0.0, 3)
        del backbone
        torch.cuda.empty_cache()
        n, p, d = full.shape
        g = int(round(p**0.5))
        y = np.lib.format.open_memmap(out, mode="w+", dtype=np.float16, shape=(n, (g // 2) ** 2, d))
        for s in range(0, n, 256):
            b = np.asarray(full[s : s + 256], dtype=np.float32).reshape(-1, g // 2, 2, g // 2, 2, d).mean((2, 4))
            y[s : s + 256] = b.reshape(len(b), -1, d).astype(np.float16)
        y.flush()
        out.with_suffix(".done").write_text("ok")
        (FULL / f"{cond}_shift.npy").unlink()
        log("pooled shifted tokens", cond)
        return np.load(out, mmap_mode="r")

    centres = (torch.arange(G) + 0.5) / G
    yy, xx = torch.meshgrid(centres, centres, indexing="ij")
    centre_prior = torch.exp(-((yy - 0.5) ** 2 + (xx - 0.5) ** 2) / (2 * 0.2**2)).flatten()
    centre_prior /= centre_prior.sum()
    ring = torch.zeros(G, G, dtype=torch.bool)
    ring[0, :] = ring[-1, :] = ring[:, 0] = ring[:, -1] = True
    ring = ring.flatten()

    def robust(weights, rs):
        boxes = torch.stack([att.box_patch_mask(r) for r in rs]).float()
        k = boxes.sum(1).long()
        ranks = weights.argsort(1, descending=True).argsort(1)
        topk = (ranks < k[:, None]).float()
        mass = (weights * boxes).sum(1)
        prior_mass = (centre_prior[None] * boxes).sum(1)
        top = weights.argmax(1)
        return {
            "pointing_game": float(boxes.gather(1, top[:, None]).mean()),
            "box_mass_over_share": float((mass / boxes.mean(1)).mean()),
            "topk_precision": float(((topk * boxes).sum(1) / k).mean()),
            "box_mass": float(mass.mean()),
            "box_mass_minus_centre_prior": float((mass - prior_mass).mean()),
            "top_patch_on_border": float(ring[top].float().mean()),
            "centre_prior_pointing": float(boxes.gather(1, centre_prior.argmax().repeat(len(rs))[:, None]).mean()),
        }

    ARMS = {
        "clean": list(att.ARMS) + ["soft_gated", "soft_prior", "soft_gated_shuffled", "soft_prior_shuffled"],
        "cloud": [
            "attention",
            "gated",
            "gated_shuffled",
            "prior",
            "prior_shuffled",
            "soft_gated",
            "soft_gated_shuffled",
        ],
    }
    args = types.SimpleNamespace(lr=0.001, epochs=15, batch=128)
    head_state = torch.load(R / "main/head.pt", weights_only=True)
    width = head_state["weight"].shape[1]
    guide = {"clean": 0.3, "cloud": 0.1}
    for cond in ("clean", "cloud"):
        data = {
            s: (np.load(T / f"{cond}_{s}.npy", mmap_mode="r"), np.array([r["class_id"] for r in splits[s]]))
            for s in SPLITS
        }
        shift_tokens = pooled(cond)
        labels = data["future_test"][1]
        for seed in (42, 43, 44):
            for arm in ARMS[cond]:
                path = OUT / f"{cond}_{arm}_s{seed}.json"
                if path.exists():
                    continue
                if arm.startswith("soft"):
                    model, best = soft.train(arm, seed, head_state, width, classes, data, args)
                else:
                    model, best = att.train(arm, seed, head_state, width, classes, data, guide[cond], args)
                res = {"arm": arm, "condition": cond, "seed": seed, "adapt_val_macro_f1": best["val_f1"]}
                for name, toks, rs in (("centred", data["future_test"][0], test), ("shifted", shift_tokens, srows)):
                    pred, w, z = att.infer(model, toks, labels)
                    res[name] = {
                        "scores": scores(labels, pred, classes),
                        "grounding": robust(w, rs),
                        "saliency_grounding": robust(z.softmax(1), rs),
                    }
                    np.savez_compressed(
                        OUT / f"{cond}_{arm}_s{seed}_{name}.npz", weights=w.numpy().astype(np.float16), predictions=pred
                    )
                write_json(path, res)
                c, s_ = (res["centred"], res["shifted"])
                log(
                    f"{cond} {arm} s{seed}: F1 {c['scores']['macro_f1']:.3f}->{s_['scores']['macro_f1']:.3f} | point {c['grounding']['pointing_game']:.2f}->{s_['grounding']['pointing_game']:.2f} | topk {c['grounding']['topk_precision']:.2f}->{s_['grounding']['topk_precision']:.2f} | border {c['grounding']['top_patch_on_border']:.2f}"
                )
    log("DONE")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/evaluation/fmow_v2/tokens.py
================================================
"""Build the frozen-token caches: 2x2 pooling, the cloud-overlay condition and the held-out cumulus conditions.

Example::  python -m fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_v2.tokens cloud    # then: pool, heldout
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch

from fyp_drift.evaluation import fmow_dinov2l_study as study
from fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_v2.context import BACKBONE, WIDTH, load_context, pool_2x2
from fyp_drift.evaluation.saliency_adapters import SPLITS, token_cache


def pool():
    """Average 2x2 patch tokens (20x20 -> 10x10) so the head-training caches fit in RAM."""
    src, dst = (
        Path("/mnt/sdd/FYP/cache/adapter_tokens_v2"),
        Path("/mnt/sdd/FYP/cache/adapter_tokens_v2p"),
    )
    dst.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    for f in sorted(src.glob("*.npy")):
        out = dst / f.name
        if out.with_suffix(".done").exists():
            continue
        x = np.load(f, mmap_mode="r")
        n, p, d = x.shape
        g = int(round(p**0.5))
        assert g * g == p and g % 2 == 0
        y = np.lib.format.open_memmap(
            out, mode="w+", dtype=np.float16, shape=(n, (g // 2) ** 2, d)
        )
        for s in range(0, n, 256):
            b = (
                np.asarray(x[s : s + 256], dtype=np.float32)
                .reshape(-1, g // 2, 2, g // 2, 2, d)
                .mean((2, 4))
            )
            y[s : s + 256] = b.reshape(len(b), -1, d).astype(np.float16)
        y.flush()
        out.with_suffix(".done").write_text("ok")
        print("pooled", f.name, x.shape, "->", y.shape, flush=True)


def cloud():
    """Cache 20x20 tokens for every split under the training cloud overlay (clean caches come from saliency_adapters)."""
    R, T = (Path(os.environ["FYP_RUNS"]), Path(os.environ["FYP_TOKENS"]))
    rows, _ = study.load_rows(study.DEFAULT_MANIFEST)
    splits = study.study_splits(rows)
    cache = study.image_cache(rows, study.DEFAULT_CACHE, 4)
    alpha = json.loads((R / "main/calib.json").read_text())["selected_alpha"]
    backbone = study.Backbone(BACKBONE).cuda().eval()
    for split in SPLITS:
        token_cache(backbone, cache, splits[split], T / f"cloud_{split}.npy", alpha, 4)
        study.log("tokens ready cloud", split)


def heldout():
    """Cache 10x10 cell tokens of the test images under each held-out cumulus condition."""
    ctx = load_context()
    out_dir = Path(
        os.environ.get(
            "FYP_TOKENS_EXTRA", "/mnt/sdd/FYP/cache/adapter_tokens_v2p_extra"
        )
    )
    out_dir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    backbone = study.Backbone(BACKBONE).cuda().eval()
    rows = ctx.splits["future_test"]
    for name, (strength, region) in ctx.cumulus.items():
        path = out_dir / f"{name}_future_test.npy"
        if path.with_suffix(".done").exists():
            continue
        cache = np.lib.format.open_memmap(
            path, mode="w+", dtype=np.float16, shape=(len(rows), 100, WIDTH)
        )
        written = 0
        with torch.no_grad():
            for images, _, _ in study.image_loader(
                ctx.cache, rows, 64, 4, strength, cloud_region=region
            ):
                with torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=torch.float16):
                    tokens = backbone(images.cuda(non_blocking=True))[:, 1:].float()
                cells = pool_2x2(tokens)
                cache[written : written + len(cells)] = cells.half().cpu().numpy()
                written += len(cells)
        assert written == len(rows)
        cache.flush()
        path.with_suffix(".done").write_text("ok")
        study.log("cached held-out tokens", name, strength)


def main(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter
    )
    parser.add_argument("step", choices=("cloud", "pool", "heldout"))
    {"cloud": cloud, "pool": pool, "heldout": heldout}[parser.parse_args(argv).step]()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/models/routing.py
================================================
"""Interpretable calibration policy for deciding when the newer ViT should be used."""

import numpy as np
import torch
from sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression

from fyp_drift.evaluation.metrics import classification_metrics

FEATURES = ("saliency_js", "confidence_gain", "entropy_reduction", "evidence_gain")
FEATURE_SETS = {
    "confidence_only": ("confidence_gain", "entropy_reduction"),
    "saliency_only": ("saliency_js", "evidence_gain"),
    "combined": FEATURES,
}


def _feature_indices(feature_names):
    unknown = set(feature_names) - set(FEATURES)
    if unknown:
        raise ValueError(f"Unknown routing features: {sorted(unknown)}")
    if not feature_names:
        raise ValueError("At least one routing feature is required")
    return [FEATURES.index(name) for name in feature_names]


def feature_tensor(comparison):
    old_p = comparison["old_logits"].softmax(1)
    new_p = comparison["new_logits"].softmax(1)
    old_conf = old_p.max(1).values
    new_conf = new_p.max(1).values
    old_entropy = -(old_p * old_p.clamp_min(1e-8).log()).sum(1)
    new_entropy = -(new_p * new_p.clamp_min(1e-8).log()).sum(1)
    return torch.stack(
        (
            comparison["saliency_gap"],
            new_conf - old_conf,
            old_entropy - new_entropy,
            comparison["new_evidence"] - comparison["old_evidence"],
        ),
        1,
    )


def fit_policy(
    features,
    labels,
    old_predictions,
    new_predictions,
    truth,
    feature_names=FEATURES,
):
    feature_names = tuple(feature_names)
    all_features = np.asarray(features, dtype=np.float64)
    features = all_features[:, _feature_indices(feature_names)]
    labels = np.asarray(labels, dtype=np.int64)
    mean, scale = features.mean(0), features.std(0).clip(1e-8)
    unique_labels = np.unique(labels)
    if not set(unique_labels).issubset({0, 1}):
        raise ValueError("Routing targets must be binary")
    if len(unique_labels) == 1:
        coefficients = np.zeros(features.shape[1], dtype=np.float64)
        intercept = 20.0 if unique_labels[0] == 1 else -20.0
        probabilities = np.full(
            len(features),
            1.0 if unique_labels[0] == 1 else 0.0,
            dtype=np.float64,
        )
        fit_kind = "constant"
    else:
        model = LogisticRegression(
            class_weight="balanced", max_iter=1000, random_state=42
        )
        model.fit((features - mean) / scale, labels)
        probabilities = model.predict_proba((features - mean) / scale)[:, 1]
        coefficients = model.coef_[0]
        intercept = float(model.intercept_[0])
        fit_kind = "logistic_regression"
    best = None
    saliency_index = FEATURES.index("saliency_js")
    floor_options = (
        [
            (quantile, float(np.quantile(all_features[:, saliency_index], quantile)))
            for quantile in (0.20, 0.35, 0.50, 0.65)
        ]
        if "saliency_js" in feature_names
        else [(None, None)]
    )
    for quantile, saliency_floor in floor_options:
        for threshold in np.linspace(0.1, 0.9, 33):
            saliency_eligible = (
                np.ones(len(features), dtype=bool)
                if saliency_floor is None
                else all_features[:, saliency_index] >= saliency_floor
            )
            use_new = (
                (old_predictions != new_predictions)
                & saliency_eligible
                & (probabilities >= threshold)
            )
            routed = np.where(use_new, new_predictions, old_predictions)
            score = classification_metrics(truth, routed)["macro_f1"]
            harmful = int(
                np.sum(
                    use_new & (old_predictions == truth) & (new_predictions != truth)
                )
            )
            floor_preference = 0.0 if quantile is None else -quantile
            candidate = (
                score,
                -harmful,
                floor_preference,
                float(threshold),
                saliency_floor,
                quantile,
            )
            if best is None or candidate > best:
                best = candidate
    return {
        "feature_names": list(feature_names),
        "mean": mean.tolist(),
        "scale": scale.tolist(),
        "coefficients": coefficients.tolist(),
        "intercept": intercept,
        "fit_kind": fit_kind,
        "beneficial_switch_rate": float(labels.mean()),
        "probability_threshold": best[3],
        "saliency_floor": best[4],
        "saliency_quantile": best[5],
        "calibration_macro_f1": best[0],
        "calibration_harmful_routes": -best[1],
    }


def apply_policy(comparison, policy):
    all_features = feature_tensor(comparison)
    feature_names = tuple(policy.get("feature_names", FEATURES))
    features = all_features[:, _feature_indices(feature_names)]
    mean = features.new_tensor(policy["mean"])
    scale = features.new_tensor(policy["scale"])
    weights = features.new_tensor(policy["coefficients"])
    probability = torch.sigmoid(
        ((features - mean) / scale * weights).sum(1) + policy["intercept"]
    )
    old_class = comparison["old_logits"].argmax(1)
    new_class = comparison["new_logits"].argmax(1)
    saliency_floor = policy.get("saliency_floor")
    saliency_gate = (
        torch.ones_like(old_class, dtype=torch.bool)
        if saliency_floor is None
        else all_features[:, FEATURES.index("saliency_js")] >= saliency_floor
    )
    needs_update = (old_class != new_class) & saliency_gate
    use_new = needs_update & (probability >= policy["probability_threshold"])
    return (
        torch.where(use_new, new_class, old_class),
        needs_update,
        use_new,
        probability,
        all_features,
    )



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/models/siamese.py
================================================
"""Twin ViT comparison and class-conditional, saliency-only routing."""

import torch
from torch import nn

from fyp_drift.models.vision_transformer import TemporalViT


def divergence(first, second):
    """Jensen-Shannon divergence of the two learned patch saliency maps."""
    midpoint = 0.5 * (first + second)
    return 0.5 * (
        (first * (first.clamp_min(1e-8) / midpoint.clamp_min(1e-8)).log()).sum(1)
        + (second * (second.clamp_min(1e-8) / midpoint.clamp_min(1e-8)).log()).sum(1)
    )


class ViTTwinRouter(nn.Module):
    """Compare frozen historical and drift-adapted XAI ViT snapshots."""

    def __init__(self, historical: TemporalViT, updated: TemporalViT):
        super().__init__()
        if historical.method != "xai" or updated.method != "xai":
            raise ValueError("Both towers must use the saliency-gated ViT")
        self.historical = historical
        self.updated = updated

    @property
    def old(self):
        """Compatibility alias used by archived analysis code."""
        return self.historical

    @property
    def new(self):
        """Compatibility alias used by archived checkpoints and reports."""
        return self.updated

    def compare(self, images):
        with torch.no_grad():
            old_logits, old_map, old_inverse = self.historical(
                images, return_counterfactual=True
            )
        new_logits, new_map, new_inverse = self.updated(
            images, return_counterfactual=True
        )
        saliency_gap = divergence(old_map, new_map)
        old_class = old_logits.argmax(1)
        new_class = new_logits.argmax(1)
        old_evidence = (
            (old_logits.softmax(1) - old_inverse.softmax(1))
            .gather(1, old_class[:, None])
            .squeeze(1)
        )
        new_evidence = (
            (new_logits.softmax(1) - new_inverse.softmax(1))
            .gather(1, new_class[:, None])
            .squeeze(1)
        )
        return {
            "old_logits": old_logits,
            "new_logits": new_logits,
            "new_inverse": new_inverse,
            "old_map": old_map,
            "new_map": new_map,
            "saliency_gap": saliency_gap,
            "old_evidence": old_evidence,
            "new_evidence": new_evidence,
            "score": saliency_gap,
        }

    @staticmethod
    def route(comparison, thresholds):
        old_logits, new_logits = comparison["old_logits"], comparison["new_logits"]
        old_class = old_logits.argmax(1)
        new_class = new_logits.argmax(1)
        boundary = torch.tensor(
            [thresholds.get(int(c), thresholds["global"]) for c in old_class],
            device=old_logits.device,
        )
        needs_update = (comparison["score"] > boundary) & (old_class != new_class)
        use_new = needs_update & (
            comparison["new_evidence"] > comparison["old_evidence"]
        )
        prediction = torch.where(use_new, new_class, old_class)
        return prediction, needs_update, use_new


def fit_thresholds(class_scores, quantile=0.9, minimum=5):
    """Calibrate on correctly classified old-model examples from the calibration window."""
    values = [score for scores in class_scores.values() for score in scores]
    if not values:
        raise ValueError("No correct calibration examples were available")
    global_threshold = float(torch.tensor(values).quantile(quantile))
    thresholds = {"global": global_threshold}
    for class_id, scores in class_scores.items():
        if len(scores) >= minimum:
            thresholds[class_id] = float(torch.tensor(scores).quantile(quantile))
    return thresholds



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/models/vision_transformer.py
================================================
"""DINOv2 ViT classifier with an optional saliency gate inside the forward pass."""

import torch
from torch import nn
from transformers import Dinov2Model

BACKBONE = "facebook/dinov2-small"


class TemporalViT(nn.Module):
    """Classify images from a patch summary with an optional direct CLS path.

    The baseline uses equal patch weights; XAI learns one positive weight per
    patch. ``patch_only`` removes the direct class-token-to-head bypass while
    retaining the backbone's class/patch attention interactions.
    """

    def __init__(self, method="baseline", num_classes=100,
                 pooling_mode="class_plus_patch"):
        super().__init__()
        if method not in ("baseline", "xai"):
            raise ValueError(method)
        if pooling_mode not in ("class_plus_patch", "patch_only"):
            raise ValueError(pooling_mode)
        self.method = method
        self.pooling_mode = pooling_mode
        self.backbone = Dinov2Model.from_pretrained(BACKBONE)
        width = self.backbone.config.hidden_size
        self.saliency_head = nn.Sequential(nn.LayerNorm(width), nn.Linear(width, 1))
        self.classifier = nn.Linear(
            width * (2 if pooling_mode == "class_plus_patch" else 1), num_classes
        )

    def encode(self, images):
        """Return the classified representation and its patch weights."""
        tokens = self.backbone(pixel_values=images).last_hidden_state
        class_token = tokens[:, 0]
        patch_tokens = tokens[:, 1:]
        patch_count = patch_tokens.size(1)
        if self.method == "xai":
            patch_gates = self.saliency_head(patch_tokens).squeeze(-1).sigmoid()
            saliency = patch_gates / patch_gates.sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True).clamp_min(
                1e-8
            )
        else:
            patch_gates = patch_tokens.new_ones((patch_tokens.size(0), patch_count))
            saliency = patch_tokens.new_full(
                (patch_tokens.size(0), patch_count), 1 / patch_count
            )
        selected_patches = torch.sum(patch_tokens * saliency.unsqueeze(-1), dim=1)
        representation = (
            torch.cat((class_token, selected_patches), dim=1)
            if self.pooling_mode == "class_plus_patch"
            else selected_patches
        )
        return representation, saliency, patch_tokens, patch_gates

    def forward(self, images, return_counterfactual=False):
        representation, saliency, patch_tokens, patch_gates = self.encode(images)
        logits = self.classifier(representation)
        counterfactual = None
        if return_counterfactual and self.method == "xai":
            inverse_gates = 1 - patch_gates
            inverse_saliency = inverse_gates / inverse_gates.sum(
                dim=-1, keepdim=True
            ).clamp_min(1e-8)
            complementary_patches = torch.sum(
                patch_tokens * inverse_saliency.unsqueeze(-1), dim=1
            )
            complement_representation = (
                torch.cat(
                    (representation[:, : patch_tokens.size(-1)], complementary_patches),
                    dim=1,
                )
                if self.pooling_mode == "class_plus_patch"
                else complementary_patches
            )
            counterfactual = self.classifier(complement_representation)
        return logits, saliency, counterfactual

    def patch_weight_interventions(self, images, permutation=None):
        """Score the same tokens with learned and controlled patch weights.

        This is an inference diagnostic. It holds the backbone and classifier
        fixed, so any learned-versus-uniform difference comes from patch
        weighting. Class-only and patch-only scores reveal bypass capacity but
        are out-of-training-distribution interventions, not trained controls.
        """
        if self.method != "xai":
            raise ValueError("Patch-weight interventions require an XAI model")
        representation, saliency, patch_tokens, patch_gates = self.encode(images)
        patch_count = patch_tokens.size(1)
        if permutation is None:
            permutation = torch.randperm(patch_count, device=patch_tokens.device)
        if permutation.ndim != 1 or permutation.numel() != patch_count:
            raise ValueError("Permutation must have one index per patch")
        if not torch.equal(
            torch.sort(permutation.detach().cpu()).values,
            torch.arange(patch_count),
        ):
            raise ValueError("Permutation must contain every patch index once")
        permutation = permutation.to(device=patch_tokens.device)
        width = patch_tokens.size(-1)
        class_token = representation[:, :width] if self.pooling_mode == "class_plus_patch" else None
        learned_patches = (
            representation[:, width:]
            if self.pooling_mode == "class_plus_patch"
            else representation
        )
        uniform_patches = patch_tokens.mean(dim=1)
        permuted_patches = torch.sum(
            patch_tokens * saliency[:, permutation].unsqueeze(-1), dim=1
        )
        inverse_gates = 1 - patch_gates
        inverse_saliency = inverse_gates / inverse_gates.sum(
            dim=-1, keepdim=True
        ).clamp_min(1e-8)
        inverted_patches = torch.sum(
            patch_tokens * inverse_saliency.unsqueeze(-1), dim=1
        )

        def classify(patches):
            data = (
                torch.cat((class_token, patches), dim=1)
                if class_token is not None
                else patches
            )
            return self.classifier(data)

        results = {
            "learned": classify(learned_patches),
            "uniform": classify(uniform_patches),
            "permuted": classify(permuted_patches),
            "inverted": classify(inverted_patches),
        }
        if class_token is not None:
            results["class_only"] = self.classifier(
                torch.cat((class_token, torch.zeros_like(learned_patches)), dim=1)
            )
            results["patch_only"] = self.classifier(
                torch.cat((torch.zeros_like(class_token), learned_patches), dim=1)
            )
        return results

================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/system/hardware.py
================================================
"""Inspect the local compute device without loading data or model weights."""

import argparse
import json
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tomllib
from pathlib import Path

import torch


def report():
    project_root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
    with (project_root / "uv.lock").open("rb") as handle:
        lock = tomllib.load(handle)
    locked_torch = next(
        package["version"] for package in lock["package"] if package["name"] == "torch"
    )
    devices = []
    if torch.cuda.is_available():
        for index in range(torch.cuda.device_count()):
            properties = torch.cuda.get_device_properties(index)
            devices.append(
                {
                    "index": index,
                    "name": properties.name,
                    "memory_gib": round(properties.total_memory / 1024**3, 2),
                }
            )
    try:
        command = subprocess.run(
            ["nvidia-smi", "--query-gpu=name,memory.total", "--format=csv,noheader"],
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
            timeout=5,
            check=False,
        )
        driver_view = command.stdout.strip() if command.returncode == 0 else None
    except (FileNotFoundError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
        driver_view = None
    data_root = "/mnt/sdd" if os.path.isdir("/mnt/sdd") else os.getcwd()
    space = shutil.disk_usage(data_root)
    return {
        "torch_version": torch.__version__,
        "locked_torch_version": locked_torch,
        "cuda_build": torch.version.cuda,
        "rocm_build": torch.version.hip,
        "gpu_available": bool(devices),
        "gpus": devices,
        "nvidia_smi": driver_view,
        "cpu_count": os.cpu_count(),
        "data_volume": data_root,
        "data_free_gib": round(space.free / 1024**3, 2),
    }


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--require-gpu", action="store_true")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    result = report()
    print(json.dumps(result, indent=2))
    if args.require_gpu and not result["gpu_available"]:
        print("No GPU is accessible to PyTorch.", file=sys.stderr)
        raise SystemExit(2)
    if args.require_gpu:
        if torch.__version__.split("+")[0] != result["locked_torch_version"]:
            print(
                "Installed PyTorch differs from uv.lock; run 'bash deploy/server.sh setup'.",
                file=sys.stderr,
            )
            raise SystemExit(2)
        major, minor = (
            int(part) for part in torch.__version__.split("+")[0].split(".")[:2]
        )
        if (major, minor) < (2, 3) or not torch.version.cuda:
            print("Expected a CUDA-enabled PyTorch >= 2.3.", file=sys.stderr)
            raise SystemExit(2)
        try:
            sample = torch.randn(32, 32, device="cuda")
            torch.cuda.synchronize()
            if not torch.isfinite(sample @ sample).all():
                raise RuntimeError("non-finite CUDA result")
        except Exception as error:
            print(f"CUDA computation failed: {error}", file=sys.stderr)
            raise SystemExit(2) from error


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/system/smoke.py
================================================
"""Tiny end-to-end runtime check. Synthetic inputs; no CLEAR-100 download."""

import json
from pathlib import Path

import torch
from torch.nn import functional as F
from torch.utils.data import DataLoader, Dataset

from fyp_drift.artifacts.run_store import RunStore, manual_snapshot
from fyp_drift.evaluation.classifier import evaluate
from fyp_drift.evaluation.metrics import binary_metrics, topk_map_agreement
from fyp_drift.models.siamese import ViTTwinRouter
from fyp_drift.models.vision_transformer import TemporalViT


class TinyImages(Dataset):
    def __init__(self, year):
        self.year = year

    def __len__(self):
        return 2

    def __getitem__(self, index):
        image = torch.rand(
            (3, 224, 224), generator=torch.Generator().manual_seed(10 + index)
        )
        return image, torch.tensor(index), torch.tensor([0, self.year - 2002, index, 1])


def one_step(model, device):
    model.train()
    for parameter in model.backbone.parameters():
        parameter.requires_grad = False
    for parameter in model.backbone.encoder.layer[-1].parameters():
        parameter.requires_grad = True
    image, label, _ = TinyImages(2012)[0]
    logits, saliency, inverse = model(
        image[None].to(device), return_counterfactual=True
    )
    loss = F.cross_entropy(logits, label[None].to(device))
    if inverse is not None:
        loss = loss + 0.1 * F.relu(0.02 - (logits[0, label] - inverse[0, label]))
    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(
        (p for p in model.parameters() if p.requires_grad), lr=1e-5
    )
    optimizer.zero_grad()
    loss.backward()
    optimizer.step()
    assert logits.shape == (1, 100)
    assert saliency.shape == (1, 256)
    assert torch.isfinite(loss)
    return round(loss.detach().item(), 4)


def main():
    device = "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    torch.manual_seed(42)
    loaders = {
        name: DataLoader(TinyImages(year), batch_size=1)
        for name, year in (("id_val", 2011), ("test", 2014))
    }
    losses = {}
    xai = None
    for method in ("baseline", "xai"):
        model = TemporalViT(method).to(device)
        losses[method] = one_step(model, device)
        report, records = evaluate(
            model, loaders, year_column=1, device=device, splits=("id_val", "test")
        )
        assert report["test"]["all"]["count"] == 2
        assert len(records) == 4
        if method == "xai":
            xai = model
        del model
    newer = TemporalViT("xai").to(device)
    newer.load_state_dict(xai.state_dict())
    with torch.no_grad():
        newer.saliency_head[-1].bias.add_(0.1)
    router = ViTTwinRouter(xai.eval(), newer.eval())
    image, _, _ = TinyImages(2014)[0]
    comparison = router.compare(image[None].to(device))
    prediction, flag, route = router.route(comparison, {"global": 0.0})
    forced = {
        "old_logits": torch.tensor([[4.0, 0.0]], device=device),
        "new_logits": torch.tensor([[0.0, 4.0]], device=device),
        "score": torch.tensor([0.5], device=device),
        "old_evidence": torch.tensor([0.1], device=device),
        "new_evidence": torch.tensor([0.3], device=device),
    }
    forced_prediction, forced_flag, forced_route = router.route(forced, {"global": 0.2})
    assert (
        bool(forced_flag[0])
        and bool(forced_route[0])
        and int(forced_prediction[0]) == 1
    )
    forced["new_evidence"] = torch.tensor([0.05], device=device)
    blocked_prediction, _, blocked_route = router.route(forced, {"global": 0.2})
    assert not bool(blocked_route[0]) and int(blocked_prediction[0]) == 0
    overlap = topk_map_agreement(
        comparison["old_map"][0].detach().cpu().numpy(),
        comparison["new_map"][0].detach().cpu().numpy(),
    )
    trigger = binary_metrics([False, True], [False, bool(flag[0])])
    assert prediction.shape == (1,)
    assert 0 <= overlap["iou"] <= 1
    store = RunStore(Path("runs"), "smoke")
    store.log({"event": "smoke", "losses": losses})
    store.save_model(
        newer, "best", 1, "synthetic_macro_f1", report["test"]["all"]["macro_f1"]
    )
    snapshot = manual_snapshot(store.path, "verified")
    assert snapshot.exists()
    print(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "status": "passed",
                "device": device,
                "losses": losses,
                "metrics": {
                    "macro_f1": report["test"]["all"]["macro_f1"],
                    "macro_iou": report["test"]["all"]["macro_iou"],
                },
                "saliency_agreement_iou": overlap["iou"],
                "trigger_f1": trigger["f1"],
                "routed": bool(route[0]),
                "run": str(store.path),
            },
            indent=2,
        )
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
