"""Training workflows for historical and drift-adapted models."""



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/training/classifier.py
================================================
"""Train the historical baseline or saliency-gated ViT classifier."""

import argparse
import csv
import json
import random
import time
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch
from torch.nn import functional as F
from tqdm.auto import tqdm

from fyp_drift.artifacts.run_store import RunStore
from fyp_drift.data.clear100 import NUM_CLASSES, make_loaders, open_dataset, year_loader
from fyp_drift.evaluation.classifier import evaluate
from fyp_drift.models.vision_transformer import TemporalViT


def parse_args(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Train a DINOv2 classifier on chronological CLEAR-100 splits."
    )
    parser.add_argument("--method", choices=("baseline", "xai"), required=True)
    parser.add_argument(
        "--pooling-mode", choices=("class_plus_patch", "patch_only"),
        default="class_plus_patch",
        help="Use a patch-only head to remove the direct class-token bypass.",
    )
    parser.add_argument("--epochs", type=int, default=3)
    parser.add_argument("--batch-size", type=int, default=8)
    parser.add_argument("--data-root", default="data")
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, default=Path("runs"))
    parser.add_argument("--run-id")
    parser.add_argument("--limit-train", type=int)
    parser.add_argument("--limit-eval", type=int)
    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=42)
    parser.add_argument("--faithfulness-weight", type=float, default=0.1)
    parser.add_argument(
        "--cv-val-year",
        type=int,
        help="Train on earlier years and validate on this train-source year.",
    )
    parser.add_argument("--log-every", type=int, default=25)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    if args.epochs < 1:
        parser.error("--epochs must be at least 1")
    if args.log_every < 1:
        parser.error("--log-every must be at least 1")
    return args


def seed_everything(seed):
    random.seed(seed)
    np.random.seed(seed)
    torch.manual_seed(seed)


def build_loaders(args):
    """Build the standard split or one fixed-history temporal backtest."""
    if not args.cv_val_year:
        return make_loaders(
            args.data_root,
            args.batch_size,
            seed=args.seed,
            limit_train=args.limit_train,
            limit_eval=args.limit_eval,
        )

    dataset = open_dataset(args.data_root)
    year_column = dataset.metadata_fields.index("year")
    train_metadata = dataset.get_subset("train").metadata_array
    available_years = sorted(
        {
            2002 + int(value)
            for value in train_metadata[:, year_column].tolist()
            if value >= 0
        }
    )
    history = [year for year in available_years if year < args.cv_val_year]
    if len(history) < 2 or args.cv_val_year not in available_years:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Need at least two earlier train years and validation year "
            f"{args.cv_val_year}; available: {available_years}"
        )
    train_loader, _ = year_loader(
        dataset,
        "train",
        history,
        args.batch_size,
        shuffle=True,
        limit=args.limit_train,
        seed=args.seed,
    )
    validation_loader, _ = year_loader(
        dataset,
        "train",
        (args.cv_val_year,),
        args.batch_size,
        limit=args.limit_eval,
    )
    return {"train": train_loader, "id_val": validation_loader}, year_column


def build_model(method, device, pooling_mode="class_plus_patch"):
    model = TemporalViT(
        method, num_classes=NUM_CLASSES, pooling_mode=pooling_mode
    ).to(device)
    for parameter in model.backbone.parameters():
        parameter.requires_grad = False
    for parameter in model.backbone.encoder.layer[-1].parameters():
        parameter.requires_grad = True
    return model


def train_epoch(model, loader, optimizer, store, args, epoch, device):
    model.train()
    loss_sum = 0.0
    if device == "cuda":
        torch.cuda.synchronize()
        gpu_start = torch.cuda.Event(enable_timing=True)
        gpu_end = torch.cuda.Event(enable_timing=True)
        gpu_start.record()
    wall_start = time.perf_counter()

    progress = tqdm(
        loader,
        desc=f"{args.method} epoch {epoch}/{args.epochs}",
        unit="batch",
    )
    for step, (images, labels, _) in enumerate(progress, 1):
        images = images.to(device)
        labels = labels.to(device)
        logits, _, inverse_logits = model(
            images, return_counterfactual=args.method == "xai"
        )
        loss = F.cross_entropy(logits, labels)
        if inverse_logits is not None:
            selected_score = logits.gather(1, labels[:, None])
            inverse_score = inverse_logits.gather(1, labels[:, None])
            evidence_margin = selected_score - inverse_score
            loss += args.faithfulness_weight * F.relu(0.2 - evidence_margin).mean()

        optimizer.zero_grad()
        loss.backward()
        optimizer.step()
        loss_sum += loss.item() * len(labels)
        progress.set_postfix(loss=f"{loss.item():.4f}")
        if step % args.log_every == 0 or step == len(loader):
            store.log(
                {
                    "event": "train_batch",
                    "epoch": epoch,
                    "batch": step,
                    "batches": len(loader),
                    "loss": round(loss.item(), 6),
                }
            )

    if device == "cuda":
        gpu_end.record()
        torch.cuda.synchronize()
        gpu_seconds = gpu_start.elapsed_time(gpu_end) / 1000
    else:
        gpu_seconds = None
    wall_seconds = time.perf_counter() - wall_start
    timing = {
        "train_wall_seconds": round(wall_seconds, 4),
        "train_gpu_seconds": round(gpu_seconds, 4) if gpu_seconds is not None else None,
        "train_images_per_second": round(len(loader.dataset) / wall_seconds, 2),
    }
    return loss_sum / len(loader.dataset), timing


def write_predictions(path, records):
    with path.open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(handle, fieldnames=list(records[0]))
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(records)


def main(argv=None):
    args = parse_args(argv)
    seed_everything(args.seed)
    loaders, year_column = build_loaders(args)
    if not len(loaders["train"].dataset) or not len(loaders["id_val"].dataset):
        raise ValueError("Training or validation split is empty")

    device = "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    model = build_model(args.method, device, args.pooling_mode)
    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(
        (parameter for parameter in model.parameters() if parameter.requires_grad),
        lr=2e-5,
    )
    store = RunStore(args.output, args.method, args.run_id)
    store.write_json(
        "config.json",
        vars(args)
        | {
            "dataset": "CLEAR-100",
            "num_classes": NUM_CLASSES,
            "device": device,
            "torch_version": torch.__version__,
            "cuda_build": torch.version.cuda,
        },
    )

    best_score = -1.0
    for epoch in range(1, args.epochs + 1):
        train_loss, train_timing = train_epoch(
            model, loaders["train"], optimizer, store, args, epoch, device
        )
        validation, _ = evaluate(
            model, loaders, year_column, device, splits=("id_val",)
        )
        score = validation["id_val"]["all"]["macro_f1"]
        row = {
            "epoch": epoch,
            "train_loss": round(train_loss, 4),
            "id_val_accuracy": validation["id_val"]["all"]["accuracy"],
            "id_val_macro_f1": score,
            "id_val_macro_iou": validation["id_val"]["all"]["macro_iou"],
            "timing": train_timing | {"validation": validation["id_val"]["_timing"]},
        }
        store.log(row)
        store.save_model(model, "last", epoch, "id_val_macro_f1", score)
        if score > best_score:
            best_score = score
            store.save_model(model, "best", epoch, "id_val_macro_f1", score)
        print(json.dumps(row), flush=True)

    best_state = torch.load(
        store.path / "models" / "best.pt",
        map_location=device,
        weights_only=True,
    )
    model.load_state_dict(best_state)
    final_splits = (
        ("id_val",)
        if args.cv_val_year
        else (
            "id_val",
            "id_test",
            "val",
            "test",
        )
    )
    report, records = evaluate(model, loaders, year_column, device, splits=final_splits)
    store.write_json("metrics/final.json", report)
    write_predictions(store.path / "logs" / "predictions.csv", records)
    store.log(
        {
            "event": "final",
            "selected_checkpoint": "models/best.pt",
            "selection_metric": "id_val_macro_f1",
            "best_score": best_score,
        }
    )

    summary_split = "id_val" if args.cv_val_year else "test"
    print(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "run": str(store.path),
                "best_id_val_macro_f1": best_score,
                "reported_split": summary_split,
                "scores": {
                    key: report[summary_split]["all"][key]
                    for key in ("accuracy", "macro_f1", "macro_iou")
                },
            },
            indent=2,
        )
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/training/fine_tune.py
================================================
"""Conventionally fine-tune a uniform-pooling ViT on the temporal adaptation window."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import json
import time
from pathlib import Path

import torch
from torch.nn import functional as F
from torch.utils.data import DataLoader, Subset
from tqdm.auto import tqdm

from fyp_drift.artifacts.run_store import RunStore
from fyp_drift.data.clear100 import balanced_indices, open_dataset
from fyp_drift.data.corruptions import SCENARIOS, drift_transform, scenario_components
from fyp_drift.evaluation.metrics import classification_metrics
from fyp_drift.models.vision_transformer import TemporalViT
from fyp_drift.training.temporal_router import (
    build_temporal_loaders,
    seed_everything,
    validate_resume_protocol,
)


def parse_args(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description=(
            "Fine-tune a DINOv2 ViT with uniform patch pooling and classification "
            "loss only."
        )
    )
    parser.add_argument("--base-checkpoint", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--data-root", default="data")
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, default=Path("runs"))
    parser.add_argument("--run-id")
    parser.add_argument("--epochs", type=int, default=5)
    parser.add_argument("--batch-size", type=int, default=8)
    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=42)
    parser.add_argument("--log-every", type=int, default=50)
    parser.add_argument("--limit-adapt", type=int)
    parser.add_argument("--limit-calibrate", type=int)
    parser.add_argument("--limit-test", type=int)
    parser.add_argument(
        "--selection-fraction", type=float, default=0.5,
        help="Class-stratified share of 2011 used for checkpoint selection.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--drift-scenarios",
        nargs="+",
        choices=SCENARIOS,
        default=("clean", "realistic_mix"),
    )
    parser.add_argument("--resume-run-dir", type=Path)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    if args.epochs < 1:
        parser.error("--epochs must be at least 1")
    if args.log_every < 1:
        parser.error("--log-every must be at least 1")
    if not 0 < args.selection_fraction < 1:
        parser.error("--selection-fraction must be strictly between 0 and 1")

    # These fields reproduce the established long-horizon protocol.
    args.long_horizon = True
    args.adapt_year = 2010
    args.calibration_year = 2011
    args.test_years = (2012, 2013, 2014)
    args.adaptation_profile = "realistic"
    args.reuse_calibration_for_selection = False
    return args


def validate_checkpoint(path):
    """Require a uniform-pooling model selected before the adaptation year."""
    config_path = path.resolve().parent.parent / "config.json"
    if not config_path.is_file():
        raise ValueError("Historical run config is missing beside the checkpoint")
    config = json.loads(config_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    if config.get("method") != "baseline" or config.get("cv_val_year") != 2009:
        raise ValueError(
            "Conventional fine-tuning requires a baseline checkpoint selected on "
            "2009 and trained only on earlier years"
        )
    return config


def build_model(checkpoint, device, pooling_mode="class_plus_patch"):
    state = torch.load(checkpoint, map_location="cpu", weights_only=True)
    classes = state["classifier.weight"].shape[0]
    model = TemporalViT(
        "baseline", classes, pooling_mode=pooling_mode
    ).to(device)
    model.load_state_dict(state)

    for parameter in model.parameters():
        parameter.requires_grad = False
    for block in model.backbone.encoder.layer[-3:]:
        for parameter in block.parameters():
            parameter.requires_grad = True
    for parameter in model.classifier.parameters():
        parameter.requires_grad = True

    backbone = [
        parameter for parameter in model.backbone.parameters() if parameter.requires_grad
    ]
    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(
        [
            {"params": backbone, "lr": 1e-5},
            {"params": model.classifier.parameters(), "lr": 5e-5},
        ],
        weight_decay=0.01,
    )
    return model, optimizer, classes


@torch.no_grad()
def evaluate(model, loader, device, year_column):
    model.eval()
    truth = []
    predictions = []
    records = []
    for images, labels, metadata in tqdm(loader, desc="evaluate fine-tuned ViT", unit="batch"):
        predicted = model(images.to(device))[0].argmax(1).cpu()
        truth.extend(labels.tolist())
        predictions.extend(predicted.tolist())
        for label, prediction, fields in zip(labels, predicted, metadata):
            records.append(
                {
                    "true_class": int(label),
                    "predicted_class": int(prediction),
                    "year": 2002 + int(fields[year_column]),
                }
            )
    return classification_metrics(truth, predictions), records


def train(model, optimizer, loaders, store, args, device, start_epoch, best_score):
    metric_name = "selection_2011_macro_f1"
    for epoch in range(start_epoch, args.epochs + 1):
        model.train()
        loss_sum = 0.0
        started = time.perf_counter()
        progress = tqdm(
            loaders.adaptation,
            desc=f"conventional fine-tune epoch {epoch}/{args.epochs}",
            unit="batch",
        )
        for step, (images, labels, _) in enumerate(progress, 1):
            labels = labels.to(device)
            logits = model(images.to(device))[0]
            loss = F.cross_entropy(logits, labels, label_smoothing=0.05)
            optimizer.zero_grad()
            loss.backward()
            optimizer.step()
            loss_sum += loss.item() * len(labels)
            progress.set_postfix(loss=f"{loss.item():.4f}")
            if step % args.log_every == 0 or step == len(loaders.adaptation):
                store.log(
                    {
                        "event": "fine_tune_batch",
                        "epoch": epoch,
                        "batch": step,
                        "batches": len(loaders.adaptation),
                        "loss": round(loss.item(), 6),
                    }
                )

        selection, _ = evaluate(model, loaders.selection, device, loaders.year_column)
        score = selection["macro_f1"]
        elapsed = time.perf_counter() - started
        row = {
            "epoch": epoch,
            "train_loss": round(loss_sum / len(loaders.adaptation.dataset), 4),
            metric_name: score,
            "train_wall_seconds": round(elapsed, 4),
            "train_images_per_second": round(len(loaders.adaptation.dataset) / elapsed, 2),
        }
        store.log(row)
        store.save_model(model, "last", epoch, metric_name, score)
        if score > best_score:
            best_score = score
            store.save_model(model, "best", epoch, metric_name, score)
        store.save_training_checkpoint(model, optimizer, epoch, best_score)
        print(json.dumps(row), flush=True)
    return best_score


def evaluate_horizon(model, dataset, loaders, store, args, device):
    results = {}
    records = []
    for scenario in args.drift_scenarios:
        results[scenario] = {}
        for severity, year in enumerate(args.test_years, 1):
            subset = dataset.get_subset(
                loaders.test_split,
                transform=drift_transform(scenario, severity, args.seed),
            )
            indices = torch.where(
                subset.metadata_array[:, loaders.year_column] == year - 2002
            )[0].tolist()
            if args.limit_test:
                indices = balanced_indices(
                    subset.rows,
                    indices,
                    min(args.limit_test, len(indices)),
                    args.seed,
                )
            loader = DataLoader(Subset(subset, indices), batch_size=args.batch_size)
            metrics, rows = evaluate(model, loader, device, loaders.year_column)
            results[scenario][str(year)] = {
                "severity": 0 if scenario == "clean" else severity,
                "metrics": metrics,
            }
            components = "+".join(scenario_components(scenario, 0, severity, args.seed))
            reported_severity = 0 if scenario == "clean" else severity
            records.extend(
                {
                    "scenario": scenario,
                    "severity": reported_severity,
                    "components": components,
                    **row,
                }
                for row in rows
            )
            print(
                json.dumps(
                    {
                        "event": "fine_tune_evaluation",
                        "scenario": scenario,
                        "year": year,
                        "macro_f1": metrics["macro_f1"],
                    }
                ),
                flush=True,
            )
    store.write_json("metrics/future_drift.json", {"scenarios": results})
    with (store.path / "logs" / "predictions.csv").open(
        "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8"
    ) as handle:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(handle, fieldnames=list(records[0]))
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(records)
    return results


def main(argv=None):
    args = parse_args(argv)
    seed_everything(args.seed)
    historical_config = validate_checkpoint(args.base_checkpoint)
    args.pooling_mode = historical_config.get("pooling_mode", "class_plus_patch")
    device = "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    dataset = open_dataset(args.data_root)
    loaders = build_temporal_loaders(dataset, args)
    if args.resume_run_dir:
        validate_resume_protocol(args.resume_run_dir, loaders.calibration_protocol)
    model, optimizer, classes = build_model(
        args.base_checkpoint, device, args.pooling_mode
    )

    if args.resume_run_dir:
        store = RunStore.from_existing(args.resume_run_dir)
        checkpoint = torch.load(
            store.path / "models" / "resume.pt",
            map_location=device,
            weights_only=False,
        )
        model.load_state_dict(checkpoint["model"])
        optimizer.load_state_dict(checkpoint["optimizer"])
        start_epoch = int(checkpoint["epoch"]) + 1
        best_score = float(checkpoint["best_score"])
    else:
        store = RunStore(args.output, "finetune", args.run_id)
        store.write_json(
            "config.json",
            vars(args)
            | {
                "method": "baseline",
                "dataset": "CLEAR-100",
                "num_classes": classes,
                "device": device,
                "objective": "classification_only",
                "pooling": (
                    "uniform_patch_mean_plus_class_token"
                    if args.pooling_mode == "class_plus_patch"
                    else "uniform_patch_mean_only"
                ),
                "trainable_backbone_blocks": 3,
                "calibration_protocol": loaders.calibration_protocol,
                "selection_samples": len(loaders.selection.dataset),
                "held_out_router_calibration_samples": len(loaders.calibration.dataset),
                "torch_version": torch.__version__,
                "cuda_build": torch.version.cuda,
            },
        )
        start_epoch = 1
        best_score = -1.0

    best_score = train(
        model, optimizer, loaders, store, args, device, start_epoch, best_score
    )
    model.load_state_dict(
        torch.load(store.path / "models" / "best.pt", map_location=device, weights_only=True)
    )
    results = evaluate_horizon(model, dataset, loaders, store, args, device)
    store.log(
        {
            "event": "final",
            "selected_checkpoint": "models/best.pt",
            "selection_metric": "selection_2011_macro_f1",
            "best_score": best_score,
        }
    )
    print(json.dumps({"run": str(store.path), "results": results}, indent=2))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/training/saliency_lora_pilot.py
================================================
"""Matched LoRA pilot of fixed-teacher saliency-weighted view consistency.

This is an exploratory mechanism experiment, separate from the frozen main
protocol. All arms see the same paired clean/shifted images and train the same
query/value LoRA modules and patch-only classifier. Only consistency weighting
differs. The historical teacher and its class-evidence masks stay frozen.
"""

import argparse
import csv
import json
import random
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch
from torch import nn
from torch.nn import functional as F
from torch.utils.data import DataLoader, Dataset

from fyp_drift.data.clear100 import open_dataset, year_loader, stratified_year_partition_loaders
from fyp_drift.data.corruptions import drift_transform, realistic_mixed_drift_transform
from fyp_drift.evaluation.metrics import classification_metrics
from fyp_drift.models.vision_transformer import TemporalViT


class LoRALinear(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, base, rank=4, alpha=8):
        super().__init__()
        self.base = base
        self.down = nn.Linear(base.in_features, rank, bias=False)
        self.up = nn.Linear(rank, base.out_features, bias=False)
        nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.down.weight, a=5 ** 0.5)
        nn.init.zeros_(self.up.weight)
        self.scale = alpha / rank
        for parameter in base.parameters():
            parameter.requires_grad = False

    def forward(self, x):
        return self.base(x) + self.up(self.down(x)) * self.scale


class PairedDataset(Dataset):
    def __init__(self, clean, shifted):
        if clean.indices != shifted.indices:
            raise ValueError("Paired views must have identical manifest indices")
        self.clean, self.shifted = clean, shifted

    def __len__(self):
        return len(self.clean)

    def __getitem__(self, index):
        clean, label, _ = self.clean[index]
        shifted, other_label, _ = self.shifted[index]
        if label != other_label:
            raise ValueError("Paired views have different labels")
        return clean, shifted, label


def teacher_saliency(tokens, class_weights, labels, top_fraction=0.2):
    """Normalized top-patch evidence for the known training class."""
    scores = torch.einsum("bpd,bd->bp", tokens, class_weights[labels])
    count = max(1, round(scores.shape[1] * top_fraction))
    top = scores.topk(count, dim=1).indices
    weights = torch.zeros_like(scores).scatter_(1, top, 1.0 / count)
    return weights


def consistency_weights(mode, saliency):
    if mode == "uniform":
        return torch.full_like(saliency, 1.0 / saliency.shape[1])
    if mode == "saliency":
        return saliency
    if mode == "shuffled":
        return saliency[:, torch.randperm(saliency.shape[1], device=saliency.device)]
    raise ValueError(mode)


def build_model(checkpoint, device, rank):
    state = torch.load(checkpoint, map_location="cpu", weights_only=True)
    classes = state["classifier.weight"].shape[0]
    model = TemporalViT("baseline", classes, pooling_mode="patch_only")
    model.load_state_dict(state)
    for parameter in model.parameters():
        parameter.requires_grad = False
    for block in model.backbone.encoder.layer[-3:]:
        attention = block.attention.attention
        attention.query = LoRALinear(attention.query, rank=rank)
        attention.value = LoRALinear(attention.value, rank=rank)
    for parameter in model.classifier.parameters():
        parameter.requires_grad = True
    return model.to(device)


@torch.no_grad()
def evaluate(model, loader, device):
    model.eval()
    truth, predictions, rows = [], [], []
    for images, labels, _ in loader:
        predicted = model(images.to(device))[0].argmax(1).cpu().tolist()
        truth.extend(labels.tolist())
        predictions.extend(predicted)
        ids = loader.dataset.indices[len(rows):len(rows) + len(predicted)]
        rows.extend(zip(ids, labels.tolist(), predicted))
    return classification_metrics(truth, predictions), rows


def parse_args(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--base-checkpoint", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--data-root", type=Path, default=Path("data"))
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=42)
    parser.add_argument("--epochs", type=int, default=1)
    parser.add_argument("--batch-size", type=int, default=8)
    parser.add_argument("--rank", type=int, default=4)
    parser.add_argument("--consistency-weight", type=float, default=0.25)
    parser.add_argument("--lora-lr", type=float, default=1e-3)
    parser.add_argument("--classifier-lr", type=float, default=5e-5)
    parser.add_argument("--limit-adapt", type=int, default=600)
    parser.add_argument("--limit-select", type=int, default=400)
    parser.add_argument("--limit-test", type=int, default=500)
    parser.add_argument("--modes", nargs="+", choices=("none", "uniform", "shuffled", "saliency"),
                        default=("none", "uniform", "shuffled", "saliency"))
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    if args.output.exists():
        parser.error("--output already exists")
    if args.epochs < 1 or args.batch_size < 1 or args.rank < 1:
        parser.error("epochs, batch size, and rank must be positive")
    if args.consistency_weight < 0 or args.lora_lr <= 0 or args.classifier_lr <= 0:
        parser.error("consistency weight must be nonnegative and rates positive")
    return args


def main(argv=None):
    args = parse_args(argv)
    device = "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    if device != "cuda":
        raise RuntimeError("This pilot requires a visible GPU")
    dataset = open_dataset(args.data_root)
    clean, _ = year_loader(dataset, "train_source", (2010,), args.batch_size,
                           limit=args.limit_adapt, seed=args.seed,
                           image_transform=drift_transform("clean", 1, args.seed))
    shifted, _ = year_loader(dataset, "train_source", (2010,), args.batch_size,
                             limit=args.limit_adapt, seed=args.seed,
                             image_transform=realistic_mixed_drift_transform((1, 2, 3), args.seed))
    paired = PairedDataset(clean.dataset, shifted.dataset)
    selection, _, _ = stratified_year_partition_loaders(
        dataset, "train_source", 2011, args.batch_size, fraction=0.5,
        limit=args.limit_select, seed=args.seed,
        image_transform=realistic_mixed_drift_transform(2, args.seed),
    )
    test_loaders = {
        name: year_loader(dataset, "test_source", (2014,), args.batch_size,
                          limit=args.limit_test, seed=args.seed,
                          image_transform=drift_transform(name, 3, args.seed))[0]
        for name in ("clean", "viewpoint_shift", "occlusion", "realistic_mix")
    }
    historical = build_model(args.base_checkpoint, device, args.rank)
    # Teacher uses the original uniform checkpoint; LoRA is zero at initialization.
    historical.eval()
    for parameter in historical.parameters():
        parameter.requires_grad = False
    args.output.mkdir(parents=True)
    (args.output / "config.json").write_text(json.dumps({
        **vars(args), "base_checkpoint": str(args.base_checkpoint), "device": device,
        "adaptation_year": 2010, "selection_year": 2011,
        "evaluation_year": 2014, "selection_protocol": "disjoint_class_stratified",
        "teacher_map": "frozen_true_class_top20_patch_contribution",
    }, indent=2, default=str))
    summary = {}
    for mode in args.modes:
        random.seed(args.seed)
        np.random.seed(args.seed)
        torch.manual_seed(args.seed)
        torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(args.seed)
        model = build_model(args.base_checkpoint, device, args.rank)
        lora_parameters = [p for name, p in model.named_parameters()
                           if p.requires_grad and (".down." in name or ".up." in name)]
        optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW([
            {"params": lora_parameters, "lr": args.lora_lr},
            {"params": model.classifier.parameters(), "lr": args.classifier_lr},
        ], weight_decay=0.01)
        generator = torch.Generator().manual_seed(args.seed)
        train_loader = DataLoader(paired, batch_size=args.batch_size, shuffle=True,
                                  generator=generator)
        best_score = -1.0
        history = []
        for epoch in range(1, args.epochs + 1):
            model.train()
            losses, classification_losses, consistency_losses = [], [], []
            for clean_images, shifted_images, labels in train_loader:
                clean_images = clean_images.to(device)
                shifted_images = shifted_images.to(device)
                labels = labels.to(device)
                with torch.no_grad():
                    teacher_tokens = historical.backbone(
                        pixel_values=clean_images
                    ).last_hidden_state[:, 1:]
                    saliency = teacher_saliency(
                        teacher_tokens, historical.classifier.weight, labels
                    )
                clean_tokens = model.backbone(
                    pixel_values=clean_images
                ).last_hidden_state[:, 1:]
                shifted_tokens = model.backbone(
                    pixel_values=shifted_images
                ).last_hidden_state[:, 1:]
                clean_logits = model.classifier(clean_tokens.mean(1))
                shifted_logits = model.classifier(shifted_tokens.mean(1))
                classification_loss = (
                    F.cross_entropy(clean_logits, labels, label_smoothing=0.05)
                    + F.cross_entropy(shifted_logits, labels, label_smoothing=0.05)
                ) / 2
                loss = classification_loss
                consistency_loss = torch.zeros((), device=device)
                if mode != "none":
                    weights = consistency_weights(mode, saliency)
                    distance = (F.normalize(clean_tokens, dim=-1)
                                - F.normalize(shifted_tokens, dim=-1)).square().sum(-1)
                    consistency_loss = (weights * distance).sum(1).mean()
                    loss = loss + args.consistency_weight * consistency_loss
                optimizer.zero_grad()
                loss.backward()
                optimizer.step()
                losses.append(float(loss.detach()))
                classification_losses.append(float(classification_loss.detach()))
                consistency_losses.append(float(consistency_loss.detach()))
            selected, _ = evaluate(model, selection, device)
            history.append({"epoch": epoch, "mean_loss": float(np.mean(losses)),
                            "mean_classification_loss": float(np.mean(classification_losses)),
                            "mean_consistency_loss": float(np.mean(consistency_losses)),
                            "selection_macro_f1": selected["macro_f1"]})
            print(json.dumps({"mode": mode, **history[-1]}), flush=True)
            if selected["macro_f1"] > best_score:
                best_score = selected["macro_f1"]
                torch.save(model.state_dict(), args.output / f"{mode}.pt")
        model.load_state_dict(torch.load(args.output / f"{mode}.pt", map_location=device,
                                         weights_only=True))
        scores = {}
        with (args.output / f"{mode}-predictions.csv").open("w", newline="") as handle:
            writer = csv.writer(handle)
            writer.writerow(("scenario", "dataset_index", "true_class", "predicted_class"))
            for scenario, loader in test_loaders.items():
                metrics, rows = evaluate(model, loader, device)
                scores[scenario] = {"count": metrics["count"],
                                    "accuracy": metrics["accuracy"],
                                    "macro_f1": metrics["macro_f1"]}
                writer.writerows((scenario, *row) for row in rows)
        summary[mode] = {"history": history, "scores": scores,
                         "trainable_parameters": sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters()
                                                     if p.requires_grad)}
        print(json.dumps({"mode": mode, "scores": scores}), flush=True)
    (args.output / "summary.json").write_text(json.dumps(summary, indent=2))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/training/temporal_router.py

"""Adapt a ViT snapshot and calibrate the transparent temporal router."""

import argparse
import csv
import json
import random
import time
from collections import defaultdict
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch
from torch.nn import functional as F
from torch.utils.data import DataLoader, Subset
from tqdm.auto import tqdm

from fyp_drift.artifacts.run_store import RunStore
from fyp_drift.data.clear100 import (
    balanced_indices,
    open_dataset,
    stratified_year_partition_loaders,
    year_loader,
)
from fyp_drift.data.corruptions import (
    REALISTIC_BENCHMARK_SCENARIOS,
    SCENARIOS,
    drift_transform,
    mixed_drift_transform,
    realistic_mixed_drift_transform,
    scenario_components,
)
from fyp_drift.evaluation.metrics import (
    binary_metrics,
    classification_metrics,
    classwise_model_comparison,
    topk_map_agreement,
)
from fyp_drift.models.routing import (
    FEATURE_SETS,
    FEATURES,
    apply_policy,
    feature_tensor,
    fit_policy,
)
from fyp_drift.models.siamese import ViTTwinRouter
from fyp_drift.models.vision_transformer import TemporalViT


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TemporalLoaders:
    """Data loaders and metadata needed by the temporal experiment."""

    adaptation: DataLoader
    selection: DataLoader
    calibration: DataLoader
    future: DataLoader
    year_column: int
    test_split: str
    calibration_protocol: str


def parse_args(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Adapt an XAI ViT and calibrate its temporal routing policy."
    )
    parser.add_argument("--base-checkpoint", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--data-root", default="data")
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, default=Path("runs"))
    parser.add_argument("--run-id")
    parser.add_argument("--epochs", type=int, default=2)
    parser.add_argument("--batch-size", type=int, default=8)
    parser.add_argument("--limit-adapt", type=int)
    parser.add_argument("--limit-calibrate", type=int)
    parser.add_argument("--limit-test", type=int)
    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=42)
    parser.add_argument("--log-every", type=int, default=25)
    parser.add_argument(
        "--evaluate-drift",
        action="store_true",
        help="Evaluate the frozen router under controlled future corruptions.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--drift-scenarios",
        nargs="+",
        choices=SCENARIOS,
        default=list(REALISTIC_BENCHMARK_SCENARIOS),
        help="Drift conditions to evaluate after the clean future stream.",
    )
    parser.add_argument("--adapt-year", type=int, default=2012)
    parser.add_argument("--calibration-year", type=int, default=2013)
    parser.add_argument(
        "--selection-fraction",
        type=float,
        default=0.5,
        help="Class-stratified calibration-year share used for checkpoint selection.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--reuse-calibration-for-selection",
        action="store_true",
        help="Reproduce archived runs that selected and calibrated on the same data.",
    )
    parser.add_argument("--test-years", type=int, nargs="+", default=(2014,))
    parser.add_argument(
        "--long-horizon",
        action="store_true",
        help="Adapt in 2010, calibrate in 2011, and test the frozen router in 2012-2014.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--adaptation-profile",
        choices=("standard", "enhanced", "realistic"),
        default="standard",
        help="Choose the adaptation curriculum and trainable backbone depth.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--disable-saliency-loss",
        action="store_true",
        help="Ablate the pairwise and counterfactual saliency losses.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--initialize-saliency-from-uniform",
        action="store_true",
        help=(
            "Start the XAI pair from one patch-only uniform checkpoint, "
            "initialising its gate to exact uniform weights."
        ),
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--router-features",
        choices=tuple(FEATURE_SETS),
        default="combined",
        help="Fit the route policy with prediction, explanation, or combined evidence.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--resume-run-dir",
        type=Path,
        help="Continue from models/resume.pt inside an interrupted run.",
    )
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)

    if args.epochs < 1:
        parser.error("--epochs must be at least 1")
    if args.log_every < 1:
        parser.error("--log-every must be at least 1")
    if not 0 < args.selection_fraction < 1:
        parser.error("--selection-fraction must be strictly between 0 and 1")
    return args


def seed_everything(seed):
    random.seed(seed)
    np.random.seed(seed)
    torch.manual_seed(seed)


def _resolve_timeline(args):
    """Return split names and transforms for the requested protocol."""
    if not args.long_horizon:
        clean = drift_transform("clean", 1, args.seed)
        return "val", "val", "test", clean, clean

    args.adapt_year = 2010
    args.calibration_year = 2011
    args.test_years = (2012, 2013, 2014)
    if args.adaptation_profile == "realistic":
        adaptation_transform = realistic_mixed_drift_transform((1, 2, 3), args.seed)
        calibration_transform = realistic_mixed_drift_transform(2, args.seed)
    elif args.adaptation_profile == "enhanced":
        adaptation_transform = mixed_drift_transform(
            (1, 2, 3), args.seed, clean_repeats=1
        )
        calibration_transform = mixed_drift_transform(2, args.seed)
    else:
        adaptation_transform = mixed_drift_transform(1, args.seed)
        calibration_transform = mixed_drift_transform(2, args.seed)
    return (
        "train_source",
        "train_source",
        "test_source",
        adaptation_transform,
        calibration_transform,
    )


def build_temporal_loaders(dataset, args):
    """Build disjoint selection, calibration, and future data streams."""
    (
        adaptation_split,
        calibration_split,
        test_split,
        adaptation_transform,
        calibration_transform,
    ) = _resolve_timeline(args)

    adaptation, _ = year_loader(
        dataset,
        adaptation_split,
        (args.adapt_year,),
        args.batch_size,
        shuffle=True,
        limit=args.limit_adapt,
        seed=args.seed,
        image_transform=adaptation_transform,
    )
    if args.reuse_calibration_for_selection:
        calibration, _ = year_loader(
            dataset,
            calibration_split,
            (args.calibration_year,),
            args.batch_size,
            limit=args.limit_calibrate,
            image_transform=calibration_transform,
        )
        selection = calibration
        calibration_protocol = "shared_legacy"
    else:
        selection, calibration, _ = stratified_year_partition_loaders(
            dataset,
            calibration_split,
            args.calibration_year,
            args.batch_size,
            fraction=args.selection_fraction,
            limit=args.limit_calibrate,
            seed=args.seed,
            image_transform=calibration_transform,
        )
        calibration_protocol = "disjoint_class_stratified"

    future, year_column = year_loader(
        dataset,
        test_split,
        args.test_years,
        args.batch_size,
        limit=args.limit_test,
        image_transform=drift_transform("clean", 1, args.seed),
    )
    loaders = TemporalLoaders(
        adaptation=adaptation,
        selection=selection,
        calibration=calibration,
        future=future,
        year_column=year_column,
        test_split=test_split,
        calibration_protocol=calibration_protocol,
    )
    if any(
        len(loader.dataset) == 0
        for loader in (
            loaders.adaptation,
            loaders.selection,
            loaders.calibration,
            loaders.future,
        )
    ):
        raise ValueError("One temporal split is empty; check the CLEAR-100 manifest")
    return loaders


def validate_resume_protocol(run_dir, requested_protocol):
    config_path = run_dir / "config.json"
    if not config_path.is_file():
        return
    saved_config = json.loads(config_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    saved_protocol = saved_config.get("calibration_protocol", "shared_legacy")
    if saved_protocol != requested_protocol:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Resume protocol mismatch: run uses {saved_protocol}, "
            f"requested {requested_protocol}."
        )


def validate_historical_checkpoint(checkpoint, long_horizon, allow_uniform=False):
    """Verify that a long-horizon checkpoint stops before the adaptation year."""
    if not long_horizon:
        return
    checkpoint = Path(checkpoint).resolve()
    config_path = checkpoint.parent.parent / "config.json"
    if not config_path.is_file():
        raise ValueError(
            "Long-horizon runs require the historical run config beside the checkpoint"
        )
    config = json.loads(config_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    expected_method = "baseline" if allow_uniform else "xai"
    if config.get("method") != expected_method or config.get("cv_val_year") != 2009:
        raise ValueError(
            f"The long-horizon historical checkpoint must be a {expected_method} "
            "run trained before 2009 and selected on 2009 (--cv-val-year 2009)."
        )
    if allow_uniform and config.get("pooling_mode") != "patch_only":
        raise ValueError(
            "Shared uniform-to-saliency initialization requires a patch-only head"
        )


def build_router(base_checkpoint, device, adaptation_profile,
                 initialize_saliency_from_uniform=False):
    """Load frozen historical and trainable updated copies of the XAI ViT."""
    config_path = Path(base_checkpoint).resolve().parent.parent / "config.json"
    config = (
        json.loads(config_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        if config_path.is_file()
        else {}
    )
    pooling_mode = config.get("pooling_mode", "class_plus_patch")
    if initialize_saliency_from_uniform and (
        config.get("method") != "baseline" or pooling_mode != "patch_only"
    ):
        raise ValueError("Shared initialization requires a patch-only baseline")
    state = torch.load(base_checkpoint, map_location="cpu", weights_only=True)
    num_classes = state["classifier.weight"].shape[0]
    historical_model = TemporalViT(
        "xai", num_classes, pooling_mode=pooling_mode
    ).to(device)
    updated_model = TemporalViT(
        "xai", num_classes, pooling_mode=pooling_mode
    ).to(device)
    historical_model.load_state_dict(state)
    updated_model.load_state_dict(state)
    if initialize_saliency_from_uniform:
        with torch.no_grad():
            for model in (historical_model, updated_model):
                model.saliency_head[1].weight.zero_()
                model.saliency_head[1].bias.zero_()

    historical_model.eval()
    for parameter in historical_model.parameters():
        parameter.requires_grad = False
    for parameter in updated_model.backbone.parameters():
        parameter.requires_grad = False

    uses_strong_profile = adaptation_profile in ("enhanced", "realistic")
    trainable_blocks = 3 if uses_strong_profile else 1
    for block in updated_model.backbone.encoder.layer[-trainable_blocks:]:
        for parameter in block.parameters():
            parameter.requires_grad = True

    router = ViTTwinRouter(historical_model, updated_model)
    backbone_parameters = [
        parameter
        for parameter in updated_model.backbone.parameters()
        if parameter.requires_grad
    ]
    head_parameters = list(updated_model.saliency_head.parameters()) + list(
        updated_model.classifier.parameters()
    )
    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(
        [
            {"params": backbone_parameters, "lr": 1e-5},
            {
                "params": head_parameters,
                "lr": 5e-5 if uses_strong_profile else 1e-5,
            },
        ],
        weight_decay=0.01,
    )
    return router, optimizer, num_classes, uses_strong_profile


@torch.no_grad()
def model_metrics(model, loader, device):
    model.eval()
    truth = []
    predictions = []
    for images, labels, _ in tqdm(
        loader, desc="select updated ViT", unit="batch", leave=False
    ):
        logits = model(images.to(device))[0]
        truth.extend(labels.tolist())
        predictions.extend(logits.argmax(1).cpu().tolist())
    return classification_metrics(truth, predictions)


@torch.no_grad()
def calibrate_router(router, loader, device, feature_set="combined"):
    """Fit the transparent policy to beneficial model switches."""
    router.eval()
    feature_rows = []
    beneficial_switches = []
    historical_predictions = []
    updated_predictions = []
    truth = []
    for images, labels, _ in tqdm(
        loader, desc="calibrate routing", unit="batch", leave=False
    ):
        comparison = router.compare(images.to(device))
        historical = comparison["old_logits"].argmax(1).cpu().numpy()
        updated = comparison["new_logits"].argmax(1).cpu().numpy()
        actual = labels.numpy()
        feature_rows.extend(feature_tensor(comparison).cpu().numpy().tolist())
        beneficial_switches.extend(
            ((historical != actual) & (updated == actual)).astype(int).tolist()
        )
        historical_predictions.extend(historical.tolist())
        updated_predictions.extend(updated.tolist())
        truth.extend(actual.tolist())
    return fit_policy(
        feature_rows,
        beneficial_switches,
        np.asarray(historical_predictions),
        np.asarray(updated_predictions),
        np.asarray(truth),
        feature_names=FEATURE_SETS[feature_set],
    )


def _summarize_decisions(rows):
    truth = [row["true_class"] for row in rows]
    beneficial = [
        row["old_class"] != row["true_class"] and row["new_class"] == row["true_class"]
        for row in rows
    ]
    flagged = [row["needs_update"] for row in rows]
    return {
        "old": classification_metrics(truth, [row["old_class"] for row in rows]),
        "new": classification_metrics(truth, [row["new_class"] for row in rows]),
        "routed": classification_metrics(truth, [row["routed_class"] for row in rows]),
        "classwise_comparison": classwise_model_comparison(rows),
        "update_trigger_vs_beneficial_switch": binary_metrics(beneficial, flagged),
        "update_flags": sum(flagged),
        "routed_to_new": sum(row["routed_to_new"] for row in rows),
        "helpful_routes": sum(
            row["routed_to_new"]
            and row["old_class"] != row["true_class"]
            and row["new_class"] == row["true_class"]
            for row in rows
        ),
        "harmful_routes": sum(
            row["routed_to_new"]
            and row["old_class"] == row["true_class"]
            and row["new_class"] != row["true_class"]
            for row in rows
        ),
        "mean_saliency_js": round(
            float(np.mean([row["saliency_js"] for row in rows])), 6
        ),
        "mean_saliency_top20_iou": round(
            float(np.mean([row["saliency_top20_iou"] for row in rows])), 4
        ),
        "mean_saliency_top20_f1": round(
            float(np.mean([row["saliency_top20_f1"] for row in rows])), 4
        ),
    }


@torch.no_grad()
def evaluate_stream(router, loader, policy, device, year_column):
    """Evaluate each routing decision and retain its interpretable inputs."""
    router.eval()
    decisions = []
    if device == "cuda":
        torch.cuda.synchronize()
        gpu_start = torch.cuda.Event(enable_timing=True)
        gpu_end = torch.cuda.Event(enable_timing=True)
        gpu_start.record()
    wall_start = time.perf_counter()

    for images, labels, metadata in tqdm(loader, desc="future routing", unit="batch"):
        comparison = router.compare(images.to(device))
        routed, needs_update, use_updated, route_probability, features = apply_policy(
            comparison, policy
        )
        historical = comparison["old_logits"].argmax(1)
        updated = comparison["new_logits"].argmax(1)
        for index, year_id in enumerate(metadata[:, year_column].tolist()):
            overlap = topk_map_agreement(
                comparison["old_map"][index].cpu().numpy(),
                comparison["new_map"][index].cpu().numpy(),
            )
            decisions.append(
                {
                    "year": 2002 + year_id,
                    "true_class": int(labels[index]),
                    "old_class": int(historical[index]),
                    "new_class": int(updated[index]),
                    "routed_class": int(routed[index]),
                    "saliency_js": round(float(comparison["saliency_gap"][index]), 6),
                    "saliency_top20_iou": overlap["iou"],
                    "saliency_top20_f1": overlap["f1"],
                    "route_probability": round(float(route_probability[index]), 6),
                    "route_threshold": policy["probability_threshold"],
                    "old_evidence": round(float(comparison["old_evidence"][index]), 6),
                    "new_evidence": round(float(comparison["new_evidence"][index]), 6),
                    **{
                        name: round(float(features[index, position]), 6)
                        for position, name in enumerate(FEATURES)
                    },
                    "needs_update": bool(needs_update[index]),
                    "routed_to_new": bool(use_updated[index]),
                }
            )

    if device == "cuda":
        gpu_end.record()
        torch.cuda.synchronize()
        gpu_seconds = gpu_start.elapsed_time(gpu_end) / 1000
    else:
        gpu_seconds = None
    wall_seconds = time.perf_counter() - wall_start
    grouped = defaultdict(list)
    for decision in decisions:
        grouped["all"].append(decision)
        grouped[str(decision["year"])].append(decision)
    report = {name: _summarize_decisions(rows) for name, rows in grouped.items()}
    report["_timing"] = {
        "samples": len(decisions),
        "batches": len(loader),
        "wall_seconds": round(wall_seconds, 4),
        "gpu_seconds": round(gpu_seconds, 4) if gpu_seconds is not None else None,
        "images_per_second": round(len(decisions) / wall_seconds, 2),
        "milliseconds_per_image": round(1000 * wall_seconds / len(decisions), 4),
    }
    return report, decisions


def create_run_store(args, loaders, num_classes, device):
    if args.resume_run_dir:
        validate_resume_protocol(args.resume_run_dir, loaders.calibration_protocol)
        return RunStore.from_existing(args.resume_run_dir)

    store = RunStore(args.output, "siamese", args.run_id)
    store.write_json(
        "config.json",
        vars(args)
        | {
            "dataset": "CLEAR-100",
            "num_classes": num_classes,
            "device": device,
            "torch_version": torch.__version__,
            "cuda_build": torch.version.cuda,
            "calibration_protocol": loaders.calibration_protocol,
            "selection_samples": len(loaders.selection.dataset),
            "router_calibration_samples": len(loaders.calibration.dataset),
        },
    )
    return store


def restore_training_state(args, store, updated_model, optimizer, device):
    if not args.resume_run_dir:
        return 1, -1.0
    resume = torch.load(
        store.path / "models" / "resume.pt",
        map_location=device,
        weights_only=False,
    )
    updated_model.load_state_dict(resume["model"])
    optimizer.load_state_dict(resume["optimizer"])
    start_epoch = int(resume["epoch"]) + 1
    best_score = float(resume["best_score"])
    store.log(
        {"event": "resume", "from_epoch": resume["epoch"], "next_epoch": start_epoch}
    )
    return start_epoch, best_score


def adapt_updated_model(
    router,
    optimizer,
    loaders,
    store,
    args,
    device,
    uses_strong_profile,
    start_epoch,
    best_score,
):
    """Train the updated tower and select its best temporal checkpoint."""
    updated_model = router.new
    saliency_weight = (
        0.0 if args.disable_saliency_loss else (0.25 if uses_strong_profile else 0.1)
    )
    saliency_margin = 0.08 if uses_strong_profile else 0.05
    label_smoothing = 0.05 if uses_strong_profile else 0.0
    metric_name = f"selection_{args.calibration_year}_macro_f1"

    for epoch in range(start_epoch, args.epochs + 1):
        updated_model.train()
        loss_sum = 0.0
        if device == "cuda":
            torch.cuda.synchronize()
            gpu_start = torch.cuda.Event(enable_timing=True)
            gpu_end = torch.cuda.Event(enable_timing=True)
            gpu_start.record()
        wall_start = time.perf_counter()
        progress = tqdm(
            loaders.adaptation,
            desc=f"siamese epoch {epoch}/{args.epochs}",
            unit="batch",
        )
        for step, (images, labels, _) in enumerate(progress, 1):
            labels = labels.to(device)
            comparison = router.compare(images.to(device))
            updated_logits = comparison["new_logits"]
            historical_correct = comparison["old_logits"].argmax(1).eq(labels)
            pairwise_loss = torch.where(
                historical_correct,
                comparison["saliency_gap"],
                F.relu(saliency_margin - comparison["saliency_gap"]),
            ).mean()
            true_evidence = (
                updated_logits.softmax(1) - comparison["new_inverse"].softmax(1)
            ).gather(1, labels[:, None])
            loss = (
                F.cross_entropy(updated_logits, labels, label_smoothing=label_smoothing)
                + saliency_weight * pairwise_loss
                + saliency_weight * F.relu(0.03 - true_evidence).mean()
            )
            optimizer.zero_grad()
            loss.backward()
            optimizer.step()
            loss_sum += loss.item() * len(labels)
            progress.set_postfix(loss=f"{loss.item():.4f}")
            if step % args.log_every == 0 or step == len(loaders.adaptation):
                store.log(
                    {
                        "event": "adapt_batch",
                        "epoch": epoch,
                        "batch": step,
                        "batches": len(loaders.adaptation),
                        "loss": round(loss.item(), 6),
                    }
                )

        if device == "cuda":
            gpu_end.record()
            torch.cuda.synchronize()
            gpu_seconds = gpu_start.elapsed_time(gpu_end) / 1000
        else:
            gpu_seconds = None
        wall_seconds = time.perf_counter() - wall_start
        validation = model_metrics(updated_model, loaders.selection, device)
        score = validation["macro_f1"]
        row = {
            "epoch": epoch,
            "adapt_loss": round(loss_sum / len(loaders.adaptation.dataset), 4),
            metric_name: score,
            f"selection_{args.calibration_year}_macro_iou": validation["macro_iou"],
            "timing": {
                "adapt_wall_seconds": round(wall_seconds, 4),
                "adapt_gpu_seconds": round(gpu_seconds, 4)
                if gpu_seconds is not None
                else None,
                "adapt_images_per_second": round(
                    len(loaders.adaptation.dataset) / wall_seconds, 2
                ),
            },
        }
        store.log(row)
        store.save_model(updated_model, "last", epoch, metric_name, score)
        if score > best_score:
            best_score = score
            store.save_model(updated_model, "best", epoch, metric_name, score)
        store.save_training_checkpoint(updated_model, optimizer, epoch, best_score)
        print(json.dumps(row), flush=True)
    return best_score


def write_decisions(path, decisions):
    with path.open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(handle, fieldnames=list(decisions[0]))
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(decisions)


def evaluate_drift_scenarios(router, policy, dataset, loaders, store, args, device):
    scenario_results = {}
    all_decisions = []
    for scenario in args.drift_scenarios:
        scenario_results[scenario] = {}
        for severity, year in enumerate(args.test_years, 1):
            reported_severity = 0 if scenario == "clean" else severity
            started = {
                "event": "drift_eval_start",
                "scenario": scenario,
                "year": year,
                "severity": reported_severity,
            }
            store.log(started)
            print(json.dumps(started), flush=True)
            subset = dataset.get_subset(
                loaders.test_split,
                transform=drift_transform(scenario, severity, args.seed),
            )
            indices = torch.where(
                subset.metadata_array[:, loaders.year_column] == year - 2002
            )[0].tolist()
            if args.limit_test:
                indices = balanced_indices(
                    subset.rows,
                    indices,
                    min(args.limit_test, len(indices)),
                    args.seed,
                )
            stream = DataLoader(Subset(subset, indices), batch_size=args.batch_size)
            report, decisions = evaluate_stream(
                router, stream, policy, device, loaders.year_column
            )
            for dataset_index, decision in zip(indices, decisions):
                all_decisions.append(
                    {
                        "scenario": scenario,
                        "severity": reported_severity,
                        "dataset_index": dataset_index,
                        "drift_components": "+".join(
                            scenario_components(
                                scenario, dataset_index, severity, args.seed
                            )
                        ),
                        **decision,
                    }
                )
            scenario_results[scenario][str(year)] = {
                "severity": reported_severity,
                "results": report,
            }
            completed = {
                "event": "drift_eval_complete",
                "scenario": scenario,
                "year": year,
                "severity": reported_severity,
                "old_macro_f1": report["all"]["old"]["macro_f1"],
                "new_macro_f1": report["all"]["new"]["macro_f1"],
                "routed_macro_f1": report["all"]["routed"]["macro_f1"],
                "helpful_routes": report["all"]["helpful_routes"],
                "harmful_routes": report["all"]["harmful_routes"],
            }
            store.log(completed)
            print(json.dumps(completed), flush=True)
            store.write_json(
                "metrics/future_drift.partial.json",
                {
                    "compound_shift_held_out": True,
                    "test_years": args.test_years,
                    "scenarios": scenario_results,
                },
            )

    store.write_json(
        "metrics/future_drift.json",
        {
            "compound_shift_held_out": True,
            "test_years": args.test_years,
            "scenarios": scenario_results,
        },
    )
    write_decisions(store.path / "logs" / "drift_routing_decisions.csv", all_decisions)


def main(argv=None):
    args = parse_args(argv)
    seed_everything(args.seed)
    validate_historical_checkpoint(
        args.base_checkpoint, args.long_horizon,
        allow_uniform=args.initialize_saliency_from_uniform,
    )
    device = "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    dataset = open_dataset(args.data_root)
    loaders = build_temporal_loaders(dataset, args)
    router, optimizer, num_classes, uses_strong_profile = build_router(
        args.base_checkpoint, device, args.adaptation_profile,
        initialize_saliency_from_uniform=args.initialize_saliency_from_uniform,
    )
    args.pooling_mode = router.old.pooling_mode
    store = create_run_store(args, loaders, num_classes, device)
    start_epoch, best_score = restore_training_state(
        args, store, router.new, optimizer, device
    )
    best_score = adapt_updated_model(
        router,
        optimizer,
        loaders,
        store,
        args,
        device,
        uses_strong_profile,
        start_epoch,
        best_score,
    )

    best_state = torch.load(
        store.path / "models" / "best.pt",
        map_location=device,
        weights_only=True,
    )
    router.new.load_state_dict(best_state)
    policy = calibrate_router(
        router, loaders.calibration, device, feature_set=args.router_features
    )
    store.write_json("metrics/router_policy.json", policy)
    result, decisions = evaluate_stream(
        router, loaders.future, policy, device, loaders.year_column
    )
    store.write_json("metrics/future_test.json", result)
    write_decisions(store.path / "logs" / "routing_decisions.csv", decisions)
    store.log(
        {
            "event": "final",
            "selected_checkpoint": "models/best.pt",
            "selection_metric": f"selection_{args.calibration_year}_macro_f1",
            "best_score": best_score,
            "calibration_protocol": loaders.calibration_protocol,
            "future_routed_macro_f1": result["all"]["routed"]["macro_f1"],
        }
    )
    if args.evaluate_drift:
        evaluate_drift_scenarios(router, policy, dataset, loaders, store, args, device)
    print(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "run": str(store.path),
                "future": {
                    name: result["all"][name]["macro_f1"]
                    for name in ("old", "new", "routed")
                },
            },
            indent=2,
        )
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/training/yearbook_saliency_mask_pilot.py
================================================
"""Chronological Yearbook pilot of saliency-guided masking during LoRA adaptation.

The historical head is trained through 1989. LoRA adapts on 1990-1999, selects
an epoch on 2000-2003, and evaluates once on 2004-2013. Saliency and random
masking preserve the same number of image patches. No future labels tune masks.
"""

import argparse
import csv
import json
import random
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch
from torch import nn
from torch.nn import functional as F
from torch.utils.data import DataLoader

from fyp_drift.evaluation.metrics import classification_metrics
from fyp_drift.models.vision_transformer import TemporalViT
from fyp_drift.training.saliency_lora_pilot import build_model, teacher_saliency


def mask_images(images, weights):
    side = round(weights.shape[1] ** 0.5)
    if side * side != weights.shape[1]:
        raise ValueError("patch weights require a square grid")
    keep = (weights > 0).reshape(-1, 1, side, side).to(images.dtype)
    keep = F.interpolate(keep, size=images.shape[-2:], mode="nearest")
    return images * keep  # zero is the normalized RGB channel mean


def make_loaders(args):
    from fyp_drift.evaluation.yearbook_replication import year_range, sampled_loader

    periods = {
        "historical_train": (1930, 1989, "train", args.historical_limit, args.seed),
        "historical_select": (1980, 1989, "test", args.selection_limit, args.seed + 1),
        "adaptation": (1990, 1999, "train", args.adapt_limit, args.seed + 2),
        "updated_select": (2000, 2003, "test", args.selection_limit, args.seed + 3),
        "future": (2004, 2013, "test", args.future_limit, args.seed + 4),
    }
    loaders = {}
    for name, (start, end, split, limit, seed) in periods.items():
        dataset = year_range(args.data_root, start, end, split)
        loaders[name] = sampled_loader(dataset, limit, seed, args.batch_size, 0)
    return loaders


@torch.no_grad()
def evaluate(model, loader, device):
    model.eval()
    truth, predictions, rows = [], [], []
    for images, labels, years in loader:
        predicted = model(images.to(device))[0].argmax(1).cpu().tolist()
        truth.extend(labels.tolist())
        predictions.extend(predicted)
        rows.extend((int(year), int(label), int(prediction)) for year, label, prediction
                    in zip(years.squeeze(-1).tolist(), labels.tolist(), predicted))
    metrics = classification_metrics(truth, predictions, classes=2)
    return {"count": metrics["count"], "accuracy": metrics["accuracy"],
            "macro_f1": metrics["macro_f1"]}, rows


def parse_args(argv=None):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--data-root", type=Path, default=Path("data/wild-time"))
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=42)
    parser.add_argument("--historical-limit", type=int, default=3000)
    parser.add_argument("--selection-limit", type=int, default=300)
    parser.add_argument("--adapt-limit", type=int, default=1000)
    parser.add_argument("--future-limit", type=int, default=1000)
    parser.add_argument("--batch-size", type=int, default=16)
    parser.add_argument("--historical-epochs", type=int, default=2)
    parser.add_argument("--adapt-epochs", type=int, default=2)
    parser.add_argument("--rank", type=int, default=4)
    parser.add_argument("--keep-fraction", type=float, default=0.4)
    parser.add_argument("--mask-loss-weight", type=float, default=0.5)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    if args.output.exists():
        parser.error("output already exists")
    if not 0 < args.keep_fraction < 1 or args.mask_loss_weight < 0:
        parser.error("invalid mask parameters")
    return args


def main(argv=None):
    args = parse_args(argv)
    random.seed(args.seed)
    np.random.seed(args.seed)
    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)
    device = "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    if device != "cuda":
        raise RuntimeError("Yearbook pilot requires a GPU")
    loaders = make_loaders(args)
    args.output.mkdir(parents=True)
    (args.output / "config.json").write_text(json.dumps(vars(args), indent=2, default=str))

    historical = TemporalViT("baseline", 2, pooling_mode="patch_only").to(device)
    for parameter in historical.backbone.parameters():
        parameter.requires_grad = False
    for parameter in historical.saliency_head.parameters():
        parameter.requires_grad = False
    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(historical.classifier.parameters(), lr=5e-4)
    best = -1.0
    historical_history = []
    for epoch in range(1, args.historical_epochs + 1):
        historical.train()
        historical.backbone.eval()
        for images, labels, _ in loaders["historical_train"]:
            logits = historical(images.to(device))[0]
            loss = F.cross_entropy(logits, labels.to(device))
            optimizer.zero_grad()
            loss.backward()
            optimizer.step()
        score, _ = evaluate(historical, loaders["historical_select"], device)
        historical_history.append({"epoch": epoch, **score})
        print(json.dumps({"stage": "historical", **historical_history[-1]}), flush=True)
        if score["macro_f1"] > best:
            best = score["macro_f1"]
            torch.save(historical.state_dict(), args.output / "historical.pt")
    historical.load_state_dict(torch.load(args.output / "historical.pt",
                                          map_location=device, weights_only=True))
    historical.eval()
    for parameter in historical.parameters():
        parameter.requires_grad = False
    historical_future, historical_rows = evaluate(historical, loaders["future"], device)
    historical_selection, _ = evaluate(historical, loaders["updated_select"], device)

    summary = {"historical_history": historical_history,
               "historical_future": historical_future,
               "historical_2000_2003": historical_selection, "arms": {}}
    for mode in ("none", "random", "saliency"):
        random.seed(args.seed)
        np.random.seed(args.seed)
        torch.manual_seed(args.seed)
        model = build_model(args.output / "historical.pt", device, args.rank)
        lora_parameters = [p for name, p in model.named_parameters()
                           if p.requires_grad and (".down." in name or ".up." in name)]
        optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW([
            {"params": lora_parameters, "lr": 1e-3},
            {"params": model.classifier.parameters(), "lr": 5e-5},
        ], weight_decay=0.01)
        best = -1.0
        history = []
        for epoch in range(1, args.adapt_epochs + 1):
            model.train()
            losses = []
            generator = torch.Generator().manual_seed(args.seed + epoch)
            order = torch.randperm(len(loaders["adaptation"].dataset), generator=generator)
            train_loader = DataLoader(
                torch.utils.data.Subset(loaders["adaptation"].dataset, order.tolist()),
                batch_size=args.batch_size,
            )
            for images, labels, _ in train_loader:
                images, labels = images.to(device), labels.to(device)
                original_logits = model(images)[0]
                loss = F.cross_entropy(original_logits, labels)
                if mode != "none":
                    with torch.no_grad():
                        teacher_tokens = historical.backbone(
                            pixel_values=images).last_hidden_state[:, 1:]
                        predicted_class = historical.classifier(
                            teacher_tokens.mean(1)).argmax(1)
                        weights = teacher_saliency(
                            teacher_tokens, historical.classifier.weight,
                            predicted_class, args.keep_fraction,
                        )
                        if mode == "random":
                            weights = weights[:, torch.randperm(
                                weights.shape[1], device=device)]
                        masked_images = mask_images(images, weights)
                    loss = loss + args.mask_loss_weight * F.cross_entropy(
                        model(masked_images)[0], labels
                    )
                optimizer.zero_grad()
                loss.backward()
                optimizer.step()
                losses.append(float(loss.detach()))
            score, _ = evaluate(model, loaders["updated_select"], device)
            history.append({"epoch": epoch, "mean_loss": float(np.mean(losses)), **score})
            print(json.dumps({"mode": mode, **history[-1]}), flush=True)
            if score["macro_f1"] > best:
                best = score["macro_f1"]
                torch.save(model.state_dict(), args.output / f"{mode}.pt")
        model.load_state_dict(torch.load(args.output / f"{mode}.pt",
                                         map_location=device, weights_only=True))
        future, rows = evaluate(model, loaders["future"], device)
        summary["arms"][mode] = {"history": history, "future": future,
                                  "trainable_parameters": sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters()
                                                              if p.requires_grad)}
        with (args.output / f"{mode}-future.csv").open("w", newline="") as handle:
            writer = csv.writer(handle)
            writer.writerow(("year", "true_class", "predicted_class"))
            writer.writerows(rows)
        print(json.dumps({"mode": mode, "future": future}), flush=True)
    with (args.output / "historical-future.csv").open("w", newline="") as handle:
        writer = csv.writer(handle)
        writer.writerow(("year", "true_class", "predicted_class"))
        writer.writerows(historical_rows)
    (args.output / "summary.json").write_text(json.dumps(summary, indent=2))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
