================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/visualization/drift.py
================================================
"""Save a source-labelled grid of the v2 drift transforms before model runs."""

import argparse
import json
import math
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw

from fyp_drift.data.clear100 import MEAN, STD, open_dataset
from fyp_drift.data.corruptions import (
    REALISTIC_BENCHMARK_SCENARIOS,
    drift_transform,
    scenario_components,
)


def rgb(tensor):
    value = (
        tensor.cpu() * torch.tensor(STD)[:, None, None]
        + torch.tensor(MEAN)[:, None, None]
    )
    return Image.fromarray(
        (value.clamp(0, 1).permute(1, 2, 0).numpy() * 255).astype(np.uint8)
    )


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--data-root", default="data")
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--year", type=int, default=2014)
    parser.add_argument("--offset", type=int, default=777)
    parser.add_argument("--severity", type=int, choices=(1, 2, 3), default=3)
    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=42)
    args = parser.parse_args()

    dataset = open_dataset(args.data_root)
    reference = dataset.get_subset(
        "test_source", transform=drift_transform("clean", 1, args.seed)
    )
    year_indices = torch.where(reference.metadata_array[:, 0] == args.year - 2002)[
        0
    ].tolist()
    index = year_indices[args.offset % len(year_indices)]
    panels, records = [], []
    for scenario in REALISTIC_BENCHMARK_SCENARIOS:
        subset = dataset.get_subset(
            "test_source", transform=drift_transform(scenario, args.severity, args.seed)
        )
        image, label, _ = subset[index]
        components = scenario_components(scenario, index, args.severity, args.seed)
        title = scenario.replace("_", " ")
        if scenario == "realistic_mix":
            title += "\n" + ", ".join(name.replace("_", " ") for name in components)
        panels.append((title, rgb(image)))
        records.append({"scenario": scenario, "components": list(components)})

    columns, cell_width, cell_height = 4, 224, 270
    rows = math.ceil(len(panels) / columns)
    canvas = Image.new("RGB", (columns * cell_width, rows * cell_height), "white")
    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(canvas)
    for position, (title, panel) in enumerate(panels):
        left, top = position % columns * cell_width, position // columns * cell_height
        canvas.paste(panel, (left, top + 42))
        draw.multiline_text((left + 4, top + 4), title, fill="black", spacing=2)
    args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    canvas.save(args.output, optimize=True)
    metadata = {
        "dataset_index": index,
        "year": args.year,
        "severity": args.severity,
        "true_class": int(label),
        "panels": records,
    }
    args.output.with_suffix(".json").write_text(
        json.dumps(metadata, indent=2), encoding="utf-8"
    )
    print(json.dumps(metadata, indent=2))
    print(f"Saved: {args.output}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/visualization/router.py
================================================
"""Visualize the two saliency maps and the transparent routing decision."""

import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch
from PIL import Image

from fyp_drift.data.clear100 import MEAN, STD, open_dataset, year_loader
from fyp_drift.models.siamese import ViTTwinRouter
from fyp_drift.models.vision_transformer import TemporalViT

SALIENCY_OVERLAY_STRENGTH = 0.28


def overlay(base, weights):
    heat = weights.reshape(16, 16).detach().cpu().numpy()
    heat = Image.fromarray((255 * heat / max(heat.max(), 1e-8)).astype(np.uint8))
    heat = heat.resize(base.size, Image.Resampling.BILINEAR)
    return Image.composite(
        Image.new("RGB", base.size, (213, 94, 0)),
        base,
        heat.point(lambda value: int(value * SALIENCY_OVERLAY_STRENGTH)),
    )


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--base-checkpoint", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--run-dir", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--data-root", default="data")
    parser.add_argument("--index", type=int, default=0)
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.output is None:
        args.output = args.run_dir / "metrics" / "siamese_maps.png"
    if args.index < 0:
        raise ValueError("--index must be nonnegative")
    thresholds = json.loads((args.run_dir / "metrics" / "thresholds.json").read_text())
    thresholds = {
        int(key) if key != "global" else key: value for key, value in thresholds.items()
    }
    device = "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    base = torch.load(args.base_checkpoint, map_location=device, weights_only=True)
    num_classes = base["classifier.weight"].shape[0]
    old = TemporalViT("xai", num_classes=num_classes).to(device)
    new = TemporalViT("xai", num_classes=num_classes).to(device)
    old.load_state_dict(base)
    new.load_state_dict(
        torch.load(
            args.run_dir / "models" / "best.pt", map_location=device, weights_only=True
        )
    )
    router = ViTTwinRouter(old, new).eval()
    dataset = open_dataset(args.data_root)
    loader, _ = year_loader(
        dataset, "test", (2014,), batch_size=1, limit=args.index + 1
    )
    if len(loader.dataset) <= args.index:
        raise IndexError("--index exceeds the future test set")
    for image, label, _ in loader:
        pass
    with torch.no_grad():
        comparison = router.compare(image.to(device))
        prediction, needs_update, use_new = router.route(comparison, thresholds)
    rgb = (
        image[0] * torch.tensor(STD)[:, None, None] + torch.tensor(MEAN)[:, None, None]
    )
    base = Image.fromarray(
        (rgb.clamp(0, 1).permute(1, 2, 0).numpy() * 255).astype(np.uint8)
    )
    canvas = Image.new("RGB", (672, 224))
    canvas.paste(base, (0, 0))
    canvas.paste(overlay(base, comparison["old_map"][0]), (224, 0))
    canvas.paste(overlay(base, comparison["new_map"][0]), (448, 0))
    args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    canvas.save(args.output)
    old_class = int(comparison["old_logits"].argmax(1)[0])
    print(
        json.dumps(
            {
                "image": str(args.output),
                "panels": ["image", "old map", "new map"],
                "true_class": int(label[0]),
                "old_class": old_class,
                "new_class": int(comparison["new_logits"].argmax(1)[0]),
                "saliency_js": float(comparison["saliency_gap"][0]),
                "class_threshold": thresholds.get(old_class, thresholds["global"]),
                "old_evidence": float(comparison["old_evidence"][0]),
                "new_evidence": float(comparison["new_evidence"][0]),
                "needs_update": bool(needs_update[0]),
                "routed_to_new": bool(use_new[0]),
                "routed_class": int(prediction[0]),
            },
            indent=2,
        )
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/visualization/saliency.py
================================================
"""Export a held-out image and the XAI gate's 16x16 saliency overlay."""

import argparse
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch
from PIL import Image

from fyp_drift.data.clear100 import MEAN, STD, make_loaders
from fyp_drift.models.vision_transformer import TemporalViT

SALIENCY_OVERLAY_STRENGTH = 0.28


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--checkpoint", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--data-root", default="data")
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.output is None:
        args.output = args.checkpoint.parent.parent / "metrics" / "saliency.png"
    device = "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    checkpoint = torch.load(args.checkpoint, map_location=device, weights_only=True)
    model = TemporalViT("xai", num_classes=checkpoint["classifier.weight"].shape[0]).to(
        device
    )
    model.load_state_dict(checkpoint)
    model.eval()
    loaders, _ = make_loaders(args.data_root, batch_size=1, limit_eval=1)
    image, label, _metadata = next(iter(loaders["test"]))
    with torch.no_grad():
        logits, saliency, _ = model(image.to(device))
    rgb = (
        image[0] * torch.tensor(STD)[:, None, None] + torch.tensor(MEAN)[:, None, None]
    )
    rgb = (rgb.clamp(0, 1).permute(1, 2, 0).numpy() * 255).astype(np.uint8)
    base = Image.fromarray(rgb)
    heat = saliency[0].reshape(16, 16).cpu().numpy()
    heat = (255 * heat / max(heat.max(), 1e-8)).astype(np.uint8)
    heat = Image.fromarray(heat).resize(base.size, Image.Resampling.BILINEAR)
    red = Image.new("RGB", base.size, (213, 94, 0))
    overlay = Image.composite(
        red,
        base,
        heat.point(lambda value: int(value * SALIENCY_OVERLAY_STRENGTH)),
    )
    canvas = Image.new("RGB", (448, 224))
    canvas.paste(base, (0, 0))
    canvas.paste(overlay, (224, 0))
    args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    canvas.save(args.output)
    print(
        f"saved={args.output} true={int(label[0])} predicted={int(logits.argmax(1)[0])}"
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

================================================
FILE: src/fyp_drift/workflows/experiment.py
================================================
"""Run the logged GPU checks, temporal repeats, and final model training."""

import argparse
import json
import os
import statistics
import subprocess
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from pathlib import Path

from fyp_drift.data.clear100 import open_dataset


def run_command(uv, executable, args, log):
    command = [str(uv), "run", "--no-sync", executable, *map(str, args)]
    print("$ " + " ".join(command), flush=True)
    with log.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        handle.write("$ " + " ".join(command) + "\n")
        with subprocess.Popen(
            command,
            stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
            stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
            bufsize=0,
            env=os.environ | {"PYTHONUNBUFFERED": "1"},
        ) as process:
            while chunk := os.read(process.stdout.fileno(), 4096):
                output = chunk.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
                print(output, end="", flush=True)
                handle.write(output)
                handle.flush()
            code = process.wait()
        handle.flush()
    if code:
        raise RuntimeError(f"{executable} failed with exit code {code}; see {log}")


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument(
        "--temporal-repeats",
        "--cv-folds",
        dest="temporal_repeats",
        type=int,
        default=0,
        help="Repeated fixed-window temporal backtests (the old --cv-folds name is retained as an alias)",
    )
    parser.add_argument("--cv-only", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--epochs", type=int, default=3)
    parser.add_argument("--batch-size", type=int, default=8)
    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=42)
    parser.add_argument("--limit-train", type=int)
    parser.add_argument("--limit-eval", type=int)
    parser.add_argument("--skip-smoke", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument(
        "--evaluate-drift",
        action="store_true",
        help="Opt in to controlled corruption evaluation after final training",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--smoke-only",
        action="store_true",
        help="Check CUDA and the model without dataset ingestion",
    )
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.temporal_repeats < 0 or args.epochs < 1 or args.batch_size < 1:
        parser.error(
            "temporal-repeats must be nonnegative; epochs and batch-size must be positive"
        )
    if args.cv_only and args.temporal_repeats == 0:
        parser.error("--cv-only needs --temporal-repeats")
    if args.cv_only and args.evaluate_drift:
        parser.error("--evaluate-drift requires the final models; omit --cv-only")
    if args.smoke_only and (
        args.cv_only or args.temporal_repeats or args.skip_smoke or args.evaluate_drift
    ):
        parser.error(
            "--smoke-only cannot be combined with backtesting, drift evaluation, or --skip-smoke"
        )
    uv = Path("tools/uv").resolve()
    if not uv.is_file():
        parser.error("Run bash deploy/server.sh setup first")
    stamp = datetime.now(UTC).strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ")
    experiment = Path("runs/experiments") / stamp
    experiment.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
    log = experiment / "console.log"
    (experiment / "config.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(vars(args), indent=2), encoding="utf-8"
    )
    results = []
    try:
        run_command(uv, "fyp-hardware", ["--require-gpu"], log)
        if args.smoke_only:
            run_command(uv, "fyp-smoke", [], log)
            (experiment / "summary.json").write_text(
                json.dumps({"status": "completed", "smoke_only": True}, indent=2),
                encoding="utf-8",
            )
            print(f"Completed. Summary: {experiment / 'summary.json'}")
            return
        try:
            open_dataset("data")
        except FileNotFoundError as error:
            raise RuntimeError(
                "CLEAR-100 data is missing. Run 'bash deploy/server.sh ingest' before the experiment."
            ) from error
        if not args.skip_smoke:
            run_command(uv, "fyp-smoke", [], log)
        common = [
            "--epochs",
            args.epochs,
            "--batch-size",
            args.batch_size,
            "--seed",
            args.seed,
        ]
        if args.limit_train:
            common += ["--limit-train", args.limit_train]
        if args.limit_eval:
            common += ["--limit-eval", args.limit_eval]
        # Every backtest uses the same historical fit window. Later years are
        # evaluation windows only, so future samples never migrate into training.
        for repeat in range(1, args.temporal_repeats + 1):
            for method in ("baseline", "xai"):
                run_id = f"{stamp}-backtest-r{repeat}"
                repeat_common = [value for value in common]
                repeat_common[repeat_common.index("--seed") + 1] = (
                    args.seed + repeat - 1
                )
                run_command(
                    uv,
                    "fyp-train",
                    ["--method", method, "--run-id", run_id, *repeat_common],
                    log,
                )
                run_dir = Path("runs") / method / run_id
                metrics = json.loads((run_dir / "metrics/final.json").read_text())
                for year in (2012, 2013):
                    yearly = metrics["val"][str(year)]
                    results.append(
                        {
                            "method": method,
                            "repeat": repeat,
                            "training_years": "2004-2010",
                            "selection_year": 2011,
                            "validation_year": year,
                            "run": str(run_dir),
                            "macro_f1": yearly["macro_f1"],
                            "macro_iou": yearly["macro_iou"],
                        }
                    )
        if not args.cv_only:
            for method in ("baseline", "xai"):
                run_id = f"{stamp}-final"
                run_command(
                    uv,
                    "fyp-train",
                    ["--method", method, "--run-id", run_id, *common],
                    log,
                )
                results.append(
                    {"method": method, "run": str(Path("runs") / method / run_id)}
                )
                if args.evaluate_drift:
                    run_dir = Path("runs") / method / run_id
                    drift_args = [
                        "--method",
                        method,
                        "--checkpoint",
                        run_dir / "models/best.pt",
                        "--output",
                        run_dir / "metrics/drift",
                        "--batch-size",
                        args.batch_size,
                        "--seed",
                        args.seed,
                    ]
                    if args.limit_eval:
                        drift_args += ["--limit", args.limit_eval]
                    run_command(uv, "fyp-evaluate-drift", drift_args, log)
            checkpoint = Path("runs/xai") / f"{stamp}-final" / "models/best.pt"
            twin_args = [
                "--base-checkpoint",
                checkpoint,
                "--run-id",
                f"{stamp}-final",
                "--epochs",
                args.epochs,
                "--batch-size",
                args.batch_size,
                "--seed",
                args.seed,
            ]
            if args.limit_train:
                twin_args += ["--limit-adapt", args.limit_train]
            if args.limit_eval:
                twin_args += [
                    "--limit-calibrate",
                    args.limit_eval,
                    "--limit-test",
                    args.limit_eval,
                ]
            if args.evaluate_drift:
                twin_args += ["--evaluate-drift"]
            run_command(uv, "fyp-train-router", twin_args, log)
            results.append(
                {
                    "method": "siamese",
                    "run": str(Path("runs/siamese") / f"{stamp}-final"),
                }
            )
        cv_summary = {}
        for method in ("baseline", "xai"):
            folds = [
                item
                for item in results
                if item["method"] == method and "validation_year" in item
            ]
            if folds:
                cv_summary[method] = {
                    "protocol": "fixed 2004-2010 fit; 2011 selection; 2012-2013 temporal backtest",
                    "repeats": args.temporal_repeats,
                    "yearly_scores": len(folds),
                    "macro_f1_mean": statistics.mean(
                        item["macro_f1"] for item in folds
                    ),
                    "macro_f1_std": statistics.pstdev(
                        item["macro_f1"] for item in folds
                    ),
                    "macro_iou_mean": statistics.mean(
                        item["macro_iou"] for item in folds
                    ),
                }
        (experiment / "summary.json").write_text(
            json.dumps(
                {"status": "completed", "runs": results, "cv_summary": cv_summary},
                indent=2,
            ),
            encoding="utf-8",
        )
        print(f"Completed. Summary: {experiment / 'summary.json'}")
    except Exception as error:
        (experiment / "summary.json").write_text(
            json.dumps(
                {"status": "failed", "error": str(error), "completed_runs": results},
                indent=2,
            ),
            encoding="utf-8",
        )
        print(f"Experiment stopped: {error}", flush=True)
        raise SystemExit(1) from error


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

from fyp_drift.data.acdc import scan_acdc, validate_rows, write_manifest


class AcdcManifestTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_scanner_matches_adverse_reference_label_and_invalid_mask(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temporary_directory:
            root = Path(temporary_directory)
            for condition in ("fog", "night", "rain", "snow"):
                recording = f"GOPR-{condition}"
                stem = f"{recording}_frame_000001"
                paths = (
                    root / "rgb_anon" / condition / "train" / recording / f"{stem}_rgb_anon.png",
                    root / "rgb_anon" / condition / "train_ref" / recording / f"{stem}_rgb_ref_anon.png",
                    root / "gt" / condition / "train" / recording / f"{stem}_gt_labelTrainIds.png",
                    root / "gt" / condition / "train" / recording / f"{stem}_gt_invIds.png",
                )
                for path in paths:
                    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
                    path.touch()

            rows = scan_acdc(root, splits=("train",))
            manifest = write_manifest(rows, root / "paired_manifest.csv")

            self.assertEqual(len(rows), 4)
            self.assertTrue(manifest.is_file())
            self.assertTrue(all(row["normal_image"] for row in rows))
            self.assertTrue(all(row["invalid_mask"] for row in rows))

            duplicated = [dict(row) for row in rows]
            duplicated[1]["normal_image"] = duplicated[0]["normal_image"]
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "duplicate normal_image"):
                validate_rows(duplicated)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()



================================================
FILE: tests/test_adaptation_evidence.py
================================================
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch

from fyp_drift.evaluation.adaptation_evidence import (
    compare_checkpoints,
    compare_feature_archives,
)


class AdaptationEvidenceTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_aligned_features_reduce_measured_condition_shift(self):
        generator = np.random.default_rng(12)
        normal = generator.normal(size=(80, 12))
        historical = {
            "normal_features": normal,
            "adverse_features": normal
            + 1.2
            + generator.normal(0, 0.15, normal.shape),
        }
        adapted = {
            "normal_features": normal,
            "adverse_features": normal + generator.normal(0, 0.08, normal.shape),
        }

        report = compare_feature_archives(
            historical,
            adapted,
            permutations=39,
            bootstrap_repetitions=100,
            seed=12,
        )

        self.assertGreater(report["change"]["mmd_squared_reduction"], 0)
        self.assertGreater(report["change"]["domain_auc_reduction"], 0)
        self.assertEqual(
            report["historical"]["distribution_shift"]["mmd_bandwidth"],
            report["adapted"]["distribution_shift"]["mmd_bandwidth"],
        )
        paired = report["change"]["paired_feature_distance"]
        self.assertGreater(paired["historical_minus_adapted_mean"], 0)
        self.assertGreater(paired["bootstrap_95_ci"][0], 0)
        self.assertTrue(report["conclusions"]["baseline_drift"]["detected"])
        self.assertTrue(
            report["conclusions"]["representation_mitigation"]["supported"]
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            report["conclusions"]["overall"],
            "representation_mitigation_supported_task_not_assessed",
        )

    def test_task_evidence_requires_adverse_gain_and_clean_retention(self):
        generator = np.random.default_rng(4)
        normal = generator.normal(size=(40, 6))
        labels = np.arange(40) % 2

        def probabilities(predictions):
            values = np.full((40, 2), 0.1)
            values[np.arange(40), predictions] = 0.9
            return values

        historical_predictions = labels.copy()
        historical_predictions[:12] = 1 - historical_predictions[:12]
        adapted_predictions = labels.copy()
        adapted_predictions[:4] = 1 - adapted_predictions[:4]
        shared = {
            "normal_features": normal,
            "normal_labels": labels,
            "adverse_labels": labels,
            "normal_probabilities": probabilities(labels),
        }
        historical = {
            **shared,
            "adverse_features": normal + 1,
            "adverse_probabilities": probabilities(historical_predictions),
        }
        adapted = {
            **shared,
            "adverse_features": normal + generator.normal(0, 0.05, normal.shape),
            "adverse_probabilities": probabilities(adapted_predictions),
        }

        report = compare_feature_archives(
            historical,
            adapted,
            permutations=19,
            bootstrap_repetitions=50,
            seed=4,
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            report["conclusions"]["task_mitigation"]["status"], "supported"
        )
        self.assertGreater(
            report["conclusions"]["task_mitigation"][
                "adverse_macro_f1_change"
            ],
            0,
        )

    def test_checkpoint_comparison_reports_parameter_movement(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temporary_directory:
            root = Path(temporary_directory)
            historical = torch.nn.Linear(4, 2)
            adapted = torch.nn.Linear(4, 2)
            adapted.load_state_dict(historical.state_dict())
            with torch.no_grad():
                adapted.weight.add_(0.1)
            first = root / "historical.pt"
            second = root / "adapted.pt"
            torch.save(historical.state_dict(), first)
            torch.save(adapted.state_dict(), second)

            report = compare_checkpoints(first, second)

            self.assertGreater(report["relative_l2_change"], 0)
            self.assertGreater(report["changed_parameter_fraction"], 0)
            self.assertEqual(
                report["largest_relative_changes"][0]["parameter"], "weight"
            )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

FILE: tests/test_camelyon_replication.py
================================================
import unittest

from fyp_drift.evaluation.camelyon_replication import (
    balanced_rows,
    balanced_source_rows,
)


class FakeStream:
    def __init__(self, rows):
        self.rows = rows

    def shuffle(self, **_):
        return self.rows


class FakeImage:
    def convert(self, _):
        return self

    def copy(self):
        return self


class CamelyonReplicationTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_balanced_rows_returns_equal_classes(self):
        rows = [
            {"image": FakeImage(), "label": label}
            for label in (0, 0, 0, 1, 1, 1)
        ]
        selected = balanced_rows(FakeStream(rows), limit=4, seed=42)
        self.assertEqual([label for _, label in selected], [0, 1, 0, 1])

    def test_balanced_rows_rejects_odd_limit(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            balanced_rows(FakeStream([]), limit=3, seed=42)

    def test_source_splits_are_balanced(self):
        rows = [
            {
                "image": FakeImage(),
                "label": label,
                "center": centre,
                "patient": patient,
            }
            for patient in range(100)
            for centre in (0, 3, 4)
            for label in (0, 1)
        ]
        selected = balanced_source_rows(
            FakeStream(rows), train_limit=20, eval_limit=10, seed=42
        )
        self.assertEqual(sum(label for _, label in selected["train"]), 10)
        self.assertEqual(sum(label for _, label in selected["id"]), 5)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

import json
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

from fyp_drift.training.temporal_router import validate_historical_checkpoint


class HistoricalCheckpointProtocolTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_long_horizon_accepts_only_pre_adaptation_checkpoint(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(dir=Path.cwd()) as temporary_directory:
            run = Path(temporary_directory)
            checkpoint = run / "models" / "best.pt"
            checkpoint.parent.mkdir()
            checkpoint.touch()
            (run / "config.json").write_text(
                json.dumps({"method": "xai", "cv_val_year": 2009}),
                encoding="utf-8",
            )
            validate_historical_checkpoint(checkpoint, long_horizon=True)

    def test_long_horizon_rejects_checkpoint_trained_through_2010(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(dir=Path.cwd()) as temporary_directory:
            run = Path(temporary_directory)
            checkpoint = run / "models" / "best.pt"
            checkpoint.parent.mkdir()
            checkpoint.touch()
            (run / "config.json").write_text(
                json.dumps({"method": "xai", "cv_val_year": None}),
                encoding="utf-8",
            )
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "cv-val-year 2009"):
                validate_historical_checkpoint(checkpoint, long_horizon=True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()



================================================
FILE: tests/test_classwise_metrics.py
================================================
import unittest

from fyp_drift.evaluation.metrics import classwise_model_comparison


def _row(truth, old, new, routed, routed_to_new):
    return {
        "true_class": truth,
        "old_class": old,
        "new_class": new,
        "routed_class": routed,
        "routed_to_new": routed_to_new,
    }


class ClasswiseMetricTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_reports_support_and_model_deltas(self):
        records = [
            _row(0, 0, 0, 0, False),
            _row(0, 1, 0, 0, True),
            _row(1, 1, 1, 1, False),
            _row(1, 1, 0, 1, False),
            _row(2, 2, 2, 2, False),
        ]

        report = classwise_model_comparison(records, classes=3, min_support=2)

        self.assertEqual(report["eligible_classes"], 2)
        self.assertEqual(report["excluded_low_support_classes"], 1)
        self.assertEqual(report["comparison"]["new"]["classes_improved"], 1)
        self.assertEqual(report["comparison"]["new"]["classes_worsened"], 1)
        # Correcting class 0 also removes a false positive from class 1.
        self.assertEqual(report["comparison"]["routed"]["classes_improved"], 2)
        self.assertEqual(report["comparison"]["routed"]["classes_unchanged"], 0)
        self.assertEqual(report["classes"][0]["routing"]["helpful"], 1)
        self.assertEqual(report["classes"][0]["routing"]["harmful"], 0)
        self.assertEqual(report["classes"][0]["routing"]["beneficial_available"], 1)
        self.assertEqual(report["classes"][0]["routing"]["beneficial_missed"], 0)

    def test_does_not_claim_unsupported_aggregate_gains(self):
        report = classwise_model_comparison(
            [_row(0, 0, 0, 0, False)], classes=2, min_support=2
        )

        self.assertEqual(report["eligible_classes"], 0)
        self.assertEqual(report["historical_f1_rank_strata"], [])
        self.assertIsNone(report["comparison"]["new"]["mean_f1_delta_pp"])

    def test_rejects_invalid_support_threshold(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "at least 1"):
            classwise_model_comparison(
                [_row(0, 0, 0, 0, False)], classes=1, min_support=0
            )

FILE: tests/test_data_manifest.py
================================================
import copy
import unittest

from fyp_drift.data.manifest import expected_split, validate_manifest_rows


def complete_manifest():
    rows = []
    for source, archive_root in (("train", "train_image_only"), ("test", "test")):
        for bucket in range(11):
            for label in range(100):
                class_name = f"class-{label:03d}"
                rows.append(
                    {
                        "path": (
                            f"{archive_root}/labeled_images/{bucket}/{class_name}/"
                            f"{source}-{bucket}-{label}.jpg"
                        ),
                        "source": source,
                        "bucket": str(bucket),
                        "year": str(2004 + bucket),
                        "class_name": class_name,
                        "label": str(label),
                        "split": expected_split(source, bucket),
                    }
                )
    return rows


class ClearManifestTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.rows = complete_manifest()

    def test_complete_temporal_manifest_is_valid(self):
        validate_manifest_rows(self.rows)

    def test_missing_class_in_one_bucket_is_rejected(self):
        incomplete = [
            row
            for row in self.rows
            if not (
                row["source"] == "test"
                and row["bucket"] == "8"
                and row["label"] == "99"
            )
        ]
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "missing classes"):
            validate_manifest_rows(incomplete)

    def test_metadata_that_disagrees_with_path_is_rejected(self):
        inconsistent = copy.deepcopy(self.rows)
        inconsistent[0]["year"] = "2005"
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "year/bucket mismatch"):
            validate_manifest_rows(inconsistent)

    def test_cross_period_image_reuse_is_rejected(self):
        duplicated = copy.deepcopy(self.rows)
        duplicated[100]["path"] = duplicated[100]["path"].replace(
            "train-1-0.jpg", "train-0-0.jpg"
        )
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "reuses an image identifier"):
            validate_manifest_rows(duplicated)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()



================================================
FILE: tests/test_drift.py
================================================
import unittest

import numpy as np
import torch
from PIL import Image

from fyp_drift.data.corruptions import (
    EVIDENCE_RANKED_MIX_SOURCES,
    REALISTIC_BENCHMARK_SCENARIOS,
    drift_transform,
    realistic_mixed_drift_transform,
    scenario_components,
)


class RealisticDriftTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        horizontal = np.linspace(20, 235, 224, dtype=np.uint8)[None, :, None]
        vertical = np.linspace(235, 20, 224, dtype=np.uint8)[:, None, None]
        image = np.concatenate(
            (
                np.broadcast_to(horizontal, (224, 224, 1)),
                np.broadcast_to(vertical, (224, 224, 1)),
                np.full((224, 224, 1), 128, dtype=np.uint8),
            ),
            axis=2,
        )
        self.image = Image.fromarray(image, "RGB")

    def test_every_benchmark_source_is_finite_and_deterministic(self):
        for scenario in REALISTIC_BENCHMARK_SCENARIOS:
            with self.subTest(scenario=scenario):
                transform = drift_transform(scenario, severity=3, seed=42)
                first = transform(self.image, 123)
                second = transform(self.image, 123)
                self.assertEqual(first.shape, (3, 224, 224))
                self.assertTrue(torch.isfinite(first).all())
                self.assertTrue(torch.equal(first, second))

    def test_realistic_mix_uses_the_frozen_evidence_ranking(self):
        for severity in (1, 2, 3):
            composition = scenario_components("realistic_mix", 123, severity, 42)
            self.assertEqual(composition, EVIDENCE_RANKED_MIX_SOURCES[:severity])

    def test_adaptation_stream_contains_clean_and_each_individual_source(self):
        transform = realistic_mixed_drift_transform((1, 2, 3), seed=42)
        outputs = [transform(self.image, index) for index in range(30)]
        self.assertTrue(all(output.shape == (3, 224, 224) for output in outputs))
        self.assertGreater(len({output.numpy().tobytes() for output in outputs}), 10)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()



================================================
FILE: tests/test_drift_metrics.py
================================================
import unittest

import numpy as np

from fyp_drift.evaluation.drift_metrics import (
    distribution_shift_metrics,
    maximum_mean_discrepancy,
    mmd_permutation_test,
)


class DriftMetricTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        generator = np.random.default_rng(7)
        self.reference = generator.normal(size=(80, 8))
        self.shifted = self.reference + 1.25

    def test_identical_features_have_zero_distances(self):
        mmd, _ = maximum_mean_discrepancy(self.reference, self.reference)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(mmd, 0.0, places=10)

    def test_shifted_features_are_detectable(self):
        metrics = distribution_shift_metrics(
            self.reference,
            self.shifted,
            permutations=39,
            seed=7,
            paired=True,
        )
        self.assertGreater(metrics["mmd_squared"], 0.05)
        self.assertLessEqual(metrics["mmd_permutation_p"], 0.05)
        self.assertGreater(metrics["domain_classifier_roc_auc"], 0.9)
        self.assertEqual(
            metrics["mmd_permutation_scheme"], "paired_within_image_swap"
        )
        self.assertEqual(metrics["mmd_permutation_resolution"], 1 / 40)
        self.assertEqual(metrics["domain_classifier_cv"], "pair_grouped_stratified")

    def test_paired_test_rejects_unequal_pair_counts(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "same number of rows"):
            mmd_permutation_test(
                self.reference,
                self.shifted[:-1],
                permutations=19,
                paired=True,
            )

    def test_fixed_bandwidth_is_reported_and_reused(self):
        metrics = mmd_permutation_test(
            self.reference,
            self.shifted,
            permutations=19,
            paired=True,
            bandwidth=0.75,
        )
        self.assertEqual(metrics["mmd_bandwidth"], 0.75)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

import importlib
import tomllib
import unittest
from pathlib import Path


class EntryPointTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_every_console_command_resolves_to_a_callable(self):
        project_root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
        with (project_root / "pyproject.toml").open("rb") as handle:
            scripts = tomllib.load(handle)["project"]["scripts"]

        self.assertGreaterEqual(len(scripts), 10)
        for command, target in scripts.items():
            with self.subTest(command=command):
                module_name, function_name = target.split(":", maxsplit=1)
                module = importlib.import_module(module_name)
                self.assertTrue(callable(getattr(module, function_name)))

import json
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

from fyp_drift.training.fine_tune import parse_args, validate_checkpoint


class FineTuneProtocolTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_defaults_match_the_long_horizon_comparison(self):
        args = parse_args(["--base-checkpoint", "historical.pt"])
        self.assertEqual(args.adapt_year, 2010)
        self.assertEqual(args.calibration_year, 2011)
        self.assertEqual(args.test_years, (2012, 2013, 2014))
        self.assertEqual(args.drift_scenarios, ("clean", "realistic_mix"))
        self.assertEqual(args.selection_fraction, 0.5)
        self.assertFalse(args.reuse_calibration_for_selection)

    def test_selection_fraction_is_configurable_and_bounded(self):
        args = parse_args(
            ["--base-checkpoint", "historical.pt", "--selection-fraction", "0.4"]
        )
        self.assertEqual(args.selection_fraction, 0.4)
        with self.assertRaises(SystemExit):
            parse_args(
                ["--base-checkpoint", "historical.pt", "--selection-fraction", "1"]
            )

    def test_checkpoint_must_be_pre_adaptation_uniform_baseline(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(dir=Path.cwd()) as temporary_directory:
            run = Path(temporary_directory)
            checkpoint = run / "models" / "best.pt"
            checkpoint.parent.mkdir()
            checkpoint.touch()
            (run / "config.json").write_text(
                json.dumps({"method": "baseline", "cv_val_year": 2009}),
                encoding="utf-8",
            )
            validate_checkpoint(checkpoint)

            (run / "config.json").write_text(
                json.dumps({"method": "xai", "cv_val_year": 2009}),
                encoding="utf-8",
            )
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "baseline checkpoint"):
                validate_checkpoint(checkpoint)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()



================================================
FILE: tests/test_fmow_dinov2l_study.py
================================================
import numpy as np
import torch
from torch import nn

from fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_dinov2l_study import add_cloud, anchor_weights, cluster_bootstrap, object_box_mask, realistic_cloud, split_by_group


def rows(n=60):
    return [{"group": f"g{i % 12}", "local_path": f"p{i}"} for i in range(n)]


def test_group_split_keeps_locations_disjoint():
    kept, held = split_by_group(rows(), 0.25, 42)
    assert not {r["group"] for r in kept} & {r["group"] for r in held}
    assert len(kept) + len(held) == 60


def test_cloud_is_deterministic_per_image_and_local():
    image = torch.zeros(3, 224, 224)
    first, again, other = add_cloud(image, "a", 0.5), add_cloud(image, "a", 0.5), add_cloud(image, "b", 0.5)
    assert torch.equal(first, again)
    assert not torch.equal(first, other)
    changed = (first != image).any(0).float().mean()
    assert 0.05 < changed < 0.6


def test_anchor_modes_sum_to_one_and_differ():
    torch.manual_seed(0)
    tokens, head, labels = torch.randn(4, 256, 16), nn.Linear(16, 5), torch.tensor([0, 1, 2, 3])
    weights = {m: anchor_weights(tokens, head, labels, m, torch.Generator().manual_seed(1)) for m in ("uniform", "shuffled", "saliency")}
    for w in weights.values():
        assert torch.allclose(w.sum(1), torch.ones(4))
    assert (weights["saliency"] > 0).sum(1).eq(round(0.4 * 256)).all()
    assert (weights["shuffled"] > 0).sum(1).eq(round(0.4 * 256)).all()
    assert not torch.equal(weights["saliency"], weights["shuffled"])


def test_cluster_bootstrap_interval_contains_mean():
    values = np.random.default_rng(0).normal(0.02, 0.1, 500)
    groups = np.repeat(np.arange(50), 10)
    result = cluster_bootstrap(values, groups, repetitions=300)
    assert result["ci95"][0] <= result["mean"] <= result["ci95"][1]


def scene():
    row = {"key": "train/x/x_1/x_1_1_rgb.jpg", "box_cx": 0.5, "box_cy": 0.5, "box_w": 0.2, "box_h": 0.2,
           "sun_elevation": 30.0, "sun_azimuth": 160.0}
    return torch.full((3, 224, 224), 0.4), row


def test_realistic_cloud_respects_the_object_box():
    pixels, row = scene()
    box = object_box_mask(row).bool()
    off = realistic_cloud(pixels, row, 1.0, "off_object")
    on = realistic_cloud(pixels, row, 1.0, "on_object")
    brighter_off = (off > pixels + 1e-4).any(0)
    brighter_on = (on > pixels + 1e-4).any(0)
    assert brighter_off[box].float().mean() < 0.15
    assert brighter_on[box].float().mean() > brighter_on[~box].float().mean()
    assert brighter_off.float().mean() > 0.03


def test_realistic_cloud_is_deterministic_and_casts_a_shadow():
    pixels, row = scene()
    first, again = realistic_cloud(pixels, row, 1.0, "off_object"), realistic_cloud(pixels, row, 1.0, "off_object")
    assert torch.equal(first, again)
    assert (first < pixels - 1e-4).any()

FILE: tests/test_fmow_layer_selection.py
================================================
import unittest

import numpy as np
import torch

from transformers import Dinov2Config, Dinov2Model

from fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_layer_selection import (
    add_group_lora, evidence_weights, layer_features, profile,
)


class LayerSelectionTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_equal_area_controls(self):
        scores = np.random.default_rng(3).normal(size=(8, 20))
        saliency = evidence_weights(scores, "saliency", 42)
        shuffled = evidence_weights(scores, "shuffled", 42)
        np.testing.assert_allclose(saliency.sum(1), 1)
        np.testing.assert_allclose(shuffled.sum(1), 1)
        np.testing.assert_array_equal((saliency > 0).sum(1), np.full(8, 8))
        np.testing.assert_array_equal((shuffled > 0).sum(1), np.full(8, 8))
        np.testing.assert_allclose(evidence_weights(scores, "uniform", 42).sum(1), 1)

    def test_profile_uses_salient_patch_shift_to_pick_group(self):
        rng = np.random.default_rng(5)
        source = rng.normal(size=(24, 12, 8, 2)).astype(np.float32)
        target = source.copy()
        target[:, :4, :3] += 3.0
        scores = np.tile(np.array([5, 5, 5, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0]), (24, 1))
        result = profile(source, target, scores, scores)
        self.assertEqual(result["saliency"]["pick"], "E")
        self.assertEqual(len(result["saliency"]["layer_mmd_squared"]), 12)

    def test_layer_features_shape(self):
        tokens = np.ones((2, 12, 4, 3))
        pooled = layer_features(tokens, np.full((2, 4), 0.25))
        self.assertEqual(pooled.shape, (2, 12, 3))
        np.testing.assert_allclose(pooled, 1)

    def test_lora_groups_have_equal_parameter_counts_and_forward(self):
        config = Dinov2Config(image_size=28, patch_size=14, hidden_size=24,
                              num_hidden_layers=12, num_attention_heads=4,
                              intermediate_size=48)
        counts = []
        for group in ("E", "M", "L"):
            model = Dinov2Model(config)
            counts.append(add_group_lora(model, group))
            output = model(pixel_values=torch.randn(1, 3, 28, 28))
            self.assertEqual(output.last_hidden_state.shape, (1, 5, 24))
        self.assertEqual(len(set(counts)), 1)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

FILE: tests/test_fmow_temporal_pilot.py
================================================
import unittest
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
from PIL import Image

from fyp_drift.evaluation.fmow_temporal_pilot import (
    apply_fmow_corruption,
    clustered_bootstrap_delta,
    fdr_bh,
    matched_metric_sample,
    matched_period_sample,
    save_corruption_preview,
    score_predictions,
)


class FMoWTemporalPilotTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_metric_sample_matches_class_country_composition(self):
        rows_by_period = {}
        features_by_period = {}
        labels_by_period = {}
        for period, offset in (("history", 0), ("future", 100)):
            rows = []
            labels = []
            for class_name, country, count in (
                ("airport", "USA", 6),
                ("airport", "CAN", 4),
                ("port", "USA", 2),
            ):
                for item in range(count):
                    rows.append({
                        "period": period,
                        "category": class_name,
                        "country": country,
                        "local_path": f"{period}-{class_name}-{country}-{item}",
                    })
                    labels.append(0 if class_name == "airport" else 1)
            rows_by_period[period] = rows
            labels_by_period[period] = np.asarray(labels)
            features_by_period[period] = np.arange(offset, offset + len(rows))[:, None]

        features, labels = matched_metric_sample(
            rows_by_period, features_by_period, labels_by_period, 6, 7
        )
        self.assertEqual(len(features["history"]), 6)
        self.assertEqual(len(features["future"]), 6)
        self.assertEqual(
            np.bincount(labels["history"], minlength=2).tolist(),
            np.bincount(labels["future"], minlength=2).tolist(),
        )
        self.assertTrue(np.all(features["history"][:, 0] < 100))
        self.assertTrue(np.all(features["future"][:, 0] >= 100))

    def test_benjamini_hochberg_adjustment_is_monotonic_by_rank(self):
        adjusted = fdr_bh([0.04, 0.001, 0.02])
        self.assertAlmostEqual(adjusted[1], 0.003)
        self.assertLessEqual(adjusted[1], adjusted[2])
        self.assertLessEqual(adjusted[2], adjusted[0])

    def test_task_scores_include_accuracy_macro_f1_and_balanced_accuracy(self):
        result = score_predictions(np.asarray([0, 0, 1, 1]), np.asarray([
            [2.0, 0.0], [2.0, 0.0], [2.0, 0.0], [0.0, 2.0],
        ]))
        self.assertEqual(result["accuracy"], 0.75)
        self.assertIn("macro_f1", result)
        self.assertIn("balanced_accuracy", result)

    def test_gaussian_stress_uses_deterministic_increasing_levels(self):
        image = Image.new("RGB", (32, 32), (127, 127, 127))
        outputs = [
            apply_fmow_corruption(image, "gaussian_noise", level, 17, 4)
            for level in (1, 2, 3)
        ]
        repeated = apply_fmow_corruption(image, "gaussian_noise", 2, 17, 4)
        self.assertTrue(np.array_equal(outputs[1].numpy(), repeated.numpy()))
        clean = apply_fmow_corruption(image, "clean", 1, 17, 4)
        errors = [float(((value - clean) ** 2).mean()) for value in outputs]
        self.assertLess(errors[0], errors[1])
        self.assertLess(errors[1], errors[2])

    def test_task_scoring_accepts_precomputed_tta_predictions(self):
        result = score_predictions(np.asarray([0, 1, 1]), np.asarray([0, 0, 1]))
        self.assertAlmostEqual(result["accuracy"], 2 / 3)

    def test_cross_period_stress_sample_matches_every_period(self):
        splits = {}
        for period in ("historical_2010_13", "adapt_2014_15", "future_2016_17"):
            splits[period] = [
                {"period": period, "category": "airport", "country": "USA", "local_path": f"{period}-{i}"}
                for i in range(5)
            ] + [
                {"period": period, "category": "port", "country": "CAN", "local_path": f"{period}-p-{i}"}
                for i in range(3)
            ]
        selected = matched_period_sample(splits, 4, 12)
        self.assertEqual({period: len(rows) for period, rows in selected.items()}, {
            "historical_2010_13": 4, "adapt_2014_15": 4, "future_2016_17": 4,
        })
        compositions = [
            sorted((row["category"], row["country"]) for row in selected[period])
            for period in selected
        ]
        self.assertEqual(compositions[0], compositions[1])
        self.assertEqual(compositions[1], compositions[2])

    def test_clustered_bootstrap_returns_paired_score_interval(self):
        result = clustered_bootstrap_delta(
            [0, 1, 0, 1], [0, 0, 0, 0], [0, 1, 0, 1],
            ["a", "a", "b", "b"], repetitions=20, seed=3,
        )
        self.assertEqual(result["accuracy"]["candidate_minus_reference"], 0.5)
        self.assertEqual(len(result["accuracy"]["cluster_bootstrap_95_ci"]), 2)

    def test_corruption_preview_is_saved_with_pillow(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            source = Path(directory) / "sample.jpg"
            output = Path(directory) / "preview.png"
            Image.new("RGB", (32, 32), (120, 90, 70)).save(source)
            save_corruption_preview(
                [{
                    "category": "airport", "local_path": str(source),
                    "period": "future_2016_17", "timestamp": "2017-01-01",
                    "country": "USA", "utm": "10T",
                }], output
            )
            self.assertTrue(output.is_file())
            examples = Path(directory) / "examples"
            self.assertTrue((examples / "manifest.csv").is_file())
            saved_images = list(examples.glob("*.jpg")) + list(examples.glob("*.png"))
            self.assertEqual(len(saved_images), 6)
            with Image.open(output) as preview:
                self.assertEqual(preview.size[1], 224 + 28 + 34)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()



================================================
FILE: tests/test_patch_mechanism.py
================================================
import json
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace
from unittest.mock import patch

import torch
from torch import nn

from fyp_drift.models.vision_transformer import TemporalViT
from fyp_drift.training.temporal_router import build_router


class TinyBackbone(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.config = SimpleNamespace(hidden_size=1)
        self.encoder = SimpleNamespace(layer=nn.ModuleList([nn.Linear(1, 1)] * 3))

    def forward(self, pixel_values):
        return SimpleNamespace(last_hidden_state=pixel_values)


class PatchMechanismTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def make_model(self):
        with patch(
            "fyp_drift.models.vision_transformer.Dinov2Model.from_pretrained",
            return_value=TinyBackbone(),
        ):
            model = TemporalViT("xai", num_classes=2)
        model.saliency_head = nn.Linear(1, 1, bias=False)
        with torch.no_grad():
            model.saliency_head.weight.fill_(1)
        return model

    def test_intervention_exposes_class_token_bypass(self):
        model = self.make_model()
        tokens = torch.tensor([[[2.0], [1.0], [3.0]]])
        permutation = torch.tensor([1, 0])

        with torch.no_grad():
            model.classifier.weight.zero_()
            model.classifier.bias.zero_()
            model.classifier.weight[0, 0] = 1  # Class-token-only head.
            cls = model.patch_weight_interventions(tokens, permutation)
        self.assertTrue(torch.equal(cls["learned"], cls["uniform"]))
        self.assertTrue(torch.equal(cls["learned"], cls["permuted"]))

        with torch.no_grad():
            model.classifier.weight.zero_()
            model.classifier.weight[0, 1] = 1  # Patch-only head.
            patches = model.patch_weight_interventions(tokens, permutation)
            _, _, complement = model(tokens, return_counterfactual=True)
        self.assertNotEqual(float(patches["learned"][0, 0]),
                            float(patches["uniform"][0, 0]))
        self.assertNotEqual(float(patches["learned"][0, 0]),
                            float(patches["permuted"][0, 0]))
        self.assertLess(float(patches["inverted"][0, 0]),
                        float(patches["uniform"][0, 0]))
        inverse = 1 - torch.sigmoid(torch.tensor([1.0, 3.0]))
        expected_patch = (inverse * torch.tensor([1.0, 3.0])).sum() / inverse.sum()
        self.assertTrue(torch.allclose(
            patches["inverted"], model.classifier(
                torch.tensor([[2.0, expected_patch]])
            ), atol=1e-6
        ))
        self.assertTrue(torch.equal(patches["inverted"], complement))
        self.assertTrue(torch.equal(patches["learned"], patches["patch_only"]))

    def test_rejects_repeated_patch_index(self):
        model = self.make_model()
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "every patch index"):
            model.patch_weight_interventions(
                torch.tensor([[[2.0], [1.0], [3.0]]]), torch.tensor([0, 0])
            )

    def test_patch_only_head_has_no_direct_class_token_path(self):
        with patch(
            "fyp_drift.models.vision_transformer.Dinov2Model.from_pretrained",
            return_value=TinyBackbone(),
        ):
            model = TemporalViT("xai", num_classes=2, pooling_mode="patch_only")
        model.saliency_head = nn.Linear(1, 1, bias=False)
        with torch.no_grad():
            model.saliency_head.weight.fill_(1)
            model.classifier.weight.fill_(1)
            model.classifier.bias.zero_()
            first = torch.tensor([[[2.0], [1.0], [3.0]]])
            changed_class_token = torch.tensor([[[20.0], [1.0], [3.0]]])
            first_logits, _, first_complement = model(
                first, return_counterfactual=True
            )
            second_logits, _, second_complement = model(
                changed_class_token, return_counterfactual=True
            )
            interventions = model.patch_weight_interventions(
                first, torch.tensor([1, 0])
            )
        self.assertTrue(torch.equal(first_logits, second_logits))
        self.assertTrue(torch.equal(first_complement, second_complement))
        self.assertNotIn("class_only", interventions)

    def test_shared_uniform_checkpoint_starts_with_same_predictions(self):
        with patch(
            "fyp_drift.models.vision_transformer.Dinov2Model.from_pretrained",
            side_effect=lambda *_: TinyBackbone(),
        ):
            baseline = TemporalViT(
                "baseline", num_classes=2, pooling_mode="patch_only"
            )
            with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temporary:
                run = Path(temporary)
                (run / "models").mkdir()
                checkpoint = run / "models" / "best.pt"
                torch.save(baseline.state_dict(), checkpoint)
                (run / "config.json").write_text(json.dumps({
                    "method": "baseline", "cv_val_year": 2009,
                    "pooling_mode": "patch_only",
                }))
                router, _, _, _ = build_router(
                    checkpoint, "cpu", "realistic",
                    initialize_saliency_from_uniform=True,
                )
                tokens = torch.tensor([[[2.0], [1.0], [3.0]]])
                with torch.no_grad():
                    baseline_logits = baseline(tokens)[0]
                    old_logits, old_map, _ = router.old(tokens)
                self.assertTrue(torch.allclose(old_logits, baseline_logits))
                self.assertTrue(torch.allclose(
                    old_map, torch.full_like(old_map, 0.5)
                ))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()



================================================
FILE: tests/test_router_feature_ablation.py
================================================
import json
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

from fyp_drift.evaluation.router_feature_ablation import (
    _fixed_coverage_route,
    _native_route,
    _policy_reproduction,
    _source_config,
    _summary,
)
from fyp_drift.evaluation.metrics import classification_metrics


class RouterFeatureAblationTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_source_must_use_corrected_disjoint_split(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            run_dir = Path(directory)
            config_path = run_dir / "config.json"
            base = {"long_horizon": True, "reuse_calibration_for_selection": False}
            for protocol in ("shared_legacy", None):
                config_path.write_text(
                    json.dumps(base | {"calibration_protocol": protocol}),
                    encoding="utf-8",
                )
                with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                    _source_config(run_dir)
            config_path.write_text(
                json.dumps(base | {"calibration_protocol": "disjoint_class_stratified"}),
                encoding="utf-8",
            )
            self.assertTrue(_source_config(run_dir)["long_horizon"])

    def test_native_floor_and_fixed_coverage_answer_different_questions(self):
        features = np.array(
            [
                [0.05, 0.9, 0, 0],
                [0.80, 0.8, 0, 0],
                [0.90, 0.7, 0, 0],
                [0.95, 0.6, 0, 0],
                [0.01, 0.5, 0, 0],
            ],
            dtype=np.float32,
        )
        old = np.zeros(5, dtype=np.int64)
        new = np.ones(5, dtype=np.int64)
        policy = {
            "feature_names": ["confidence_gain"],
            "mean": [0],
            "scale": [1],
            "coefficients": [1],
            "intercept": 0,
            "saliency_floor": 0.5,
            "probability_threshold": 0.5,
        }
        probabilities, native = _native_route(features, old, new, policy)
        self.assertEqual(native.tolist(), [False, True, True, True, False])
        matched = _fixed_coverage_route(probabilities, old, new, 0.4)
        self.assertEqual(matched.tolist(), [True, True, False, False, False])

    def test_same_route_quota_and_macro_f1_matches_project_metric(self):
        truth = np.array([0, 0, 1, 1, 2, 2])
        old = np.array([0, 1, 1, 0, 2, 1])
        new = np.array([1, 0, 0, 1, 1, 2])
        probabilities = np.array([0.9, 0.8, 0.7, 0.6, 0.5, 0.4])
        route = _fixed_coverage_route(probabilities, old, new, 0.5)
        self.assertEqual(int(route.sum()), 3)
        result = _summary(truth, old, new, route, num_classes=3)
        prediction = np.where(route, new, old)
        expected = classification_metrics(truth, prediction, classes=3)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(result["routed"]["macro_f1"], expected["macro_f1"], places=4)
        self.assertEqual(result["routed_to_new"], 3)

    def test_refit_check_detects_calibration_route_change(self):
        features = np.array([[0, -1, 0, 0], [0, 1, 0, 0]], dtype=np.float32)
        old = np.zeros(2, dtype=np.int64)
        new = np.ones(2, dtype=np.int64)
        source = {
            "feature_names": ["confidence_gain"],
            "mean": [0], "scale": [1], "coefficients": [1],
            "intercept": 0, "saliency_floor": None,
            "probability_threshold": 0.5,
        }
        refitted = source | {"probability_threshold": 0.9}
        check = _policy_reproduction(features, old, new, source, refitted)
        self.assertFalse(check["threshold_match"])
        self.assertEqual(check["calibration_route_mismatches"], 1)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()



================================================
FILE: tests/test_routing_policy.py
================================================
import unittest

import numpy as np

from fyp_drift.models.routing import FEATURES, FEATURE_SETS, fit_policy


class RoutingFeatureAblationTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.features = np.array(
            [
                [0.01, -0.20, -0.10, -0.15],
                [0.02, -0.10, -0.05, -0.08],
                [0.03, 0.05, 0.03, 0.02],
                [0.04, 0.10, 0.08, 0.06],
                [0.08, 0.20, 0.15, 0.12],
                [0.09, 0.25, 0.18, 0.16],
                [0.11, 0.30, 0.22, 0.20],
                [0.12, 0.35, 0.28, 0.25],
            ]
        )
        self.labels = np.array([0, 0, 0, 0, 1, 1, 1, 1])
        self.old = np.array([0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0])
        self.new = np.array([1, 1, 1, 1, 1, 1, 1, 1])
        self.truth = np.array([0, 0, 0, 0, 1, 1, 1, 1])

    def fit(self, feature_set):
        return fit_policy(
            self.features,
            self.labels,
            self.old,
            self.new,
            self.truth,
            feature_names=FEATURE_SETS[feature_set],
        )

    def test_feature_sets_are_explicit_and_recorded(self):
        for feature_set, expected in FEATURE_SETS.items():
            with self.subTest(feature_set=feature_set):
                policy = self.fit(feature_set)
                self.assertEqual(policy["feature_names"], list(expected))
                self.assertEqual(len(policy["coefficients"]), len(expected))

    def test_prediction_only_policy_has_no_saliency_floor(self):
        policy = self.fit("confidence_only")
        self.assertIsNone(policy["saliency_floor"])
        self.assertIsNone(policy["saliency_quantile"])

    def test_saliency_policies_retain_the_floor(self):
        for feature_set in ("saliency_only", "combined"):
            with self.subTest(feature_set=feature_set):
                policy = self.fit(feature_set)
                self.assertIsNotNone(policy["saliency_floor"])
                self.assertIn("saliency_js", policy["feature_names"])

    def test_declared_features_match_model_order(self):
        self.assertEqual(FEATURE_SETS["combined"], FEATURES)

    def test_no_beneficial_switches_produces_safe_constant_policy(self):
        policy = fit_policy(
            self.features,
            np.zeros(len(self.labels), dtype=int),
            self.old,
            self.new,
            self.truth,
        )
        self.assertEqual(policy["fit_kind"], "constant")
        self.assertEqual(policy["beneficial_switch_rate"], 0.0)
        self.assertTrue(all(value == 0.0 for value in policy["coefficients"]))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()



================================================
FILE: tests/test_run_store.py
================================================
import json
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

import torch

from fyp_drift.artifacts.run_store import RunStore


class RunStoreCheckpointTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_checkpoint_round_trip_preserves_resume_state(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temporary_directory:
            root = Path(temporary_directory)
            store = RunStore(root, "siamese", "round-trip")
            model = torch.nn.Linear(3, 2)
            optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=0.01)

            loss = model(torch.ones(1, 3)).sum()
            loss.backward()
            optimizer.step()
            expected = {
                name: value.detach().clone()
                for name, value in model.state_dict().items()
            }

            store.save_training_checkpoint(model, optimizer, epoch=2, best_score=0.75)
            payload = torch.load(
                store.path / "models" / "resume.pt", weights_only=False
            )
            metadata = json.loads((store.path / "models" / "resume.json").read_text())

            self.assertEqual(payload["epoch"], 2)
            self.assertEqual(payload["best_score"], 0.75)
            self.assertIn("state", payload["optimizer"])
            self.assertEqual(metadata["epoch"], 2)
            self.assertEqual(metadata["best_score"], 0.75)
            self.assertIn("saved_utc", metadata)
            for name, value in expected.items():
                self.assertTrue(torch.equal(payload["model"][name], value))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()



================================================
FILE: tests/test_saliency_lora_pilot.py
================================================
import unittest

import torch
from torch import nn

from fyp_drift.training.saliency_lora_pilot import (
    LoRALinear, consistency_weights, teacher_saliency,
)


class SaliencyLoRAPilotTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_zero_initialized_lora_preserves_historical_projection(self):
        base = nn.Linear(3, 2)
        lora = LoRALinear(base, rank=2)
        inputs = torch.randn(4, 5, 3)
        self.assertTrue(torch.equal(base(inputs), lora(inputs)))
        self.assertFalse(any(p.requires_grad for p in base.parameters()))
        self.assertTrue(all(p.requires_grad for p in lora.up.parameters()))

    def test_class_evidence_selects_top_patches_and_normalizes(self):
        tokens = torch.tensor([[[4.0], [1.0], [3.0], [0.0], [2.0]]])
        weights = torch.tensor([[1.0], [-1.0]])
        saliency = teacher_saliency(tokens, weights, torch.tensor([0]), 0.4)
        self.assertEqual(saliency.tolist(), [[0.5, 0.0, 0.5, 0.0, 0.0]])
        self.assertTrue(torch.equal(saliency.sum(1), torch.ones(1)))
        self.assertTrue(torch.equal(
            consistency_weights("uniform", saliency), torch.full_like(saliency, 0.2)
        ))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()



================================================
FILE: tests/test_temporal_design.py
================================================
import unittest
from collections import Counter

from fyp_drift.data.temporal_sampling import matched_temporal_sample
from fyp_drift.evaluation.temporal_design import (
    crossed_corruption_grid,
    progressive_corruption_schedule,
)


class TemporalDesignTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_crossed_grid_applies_each_severity_in_every_period(self):
        cells = crossed_corruption_grid(
            ("history", "adapt", "future"),
            ("gaussian_noise", "sensor_noise"),
        )
        self.assertEqual(len(cells), 21)
        for period in ("history", "adapt", "future"):
            period_cells = [cell for cell in cells if cell["period"] == period]
            self.assertEqual(
                Counter(cell["scenario"] for cell in period_cells),
                Counter({"clean": 1, "gaussian_noise": 3, "sensor_noise": 3}),
            )
            self.assertEqual(
                [cell["severity"] for cell in period_cells if cell["scenario"] == "gaussian_noise"],
                [1, 2, 3],
            )

    def test_crossed_grid_rejects_a_mix_whose_source_count_changes_with_severity(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "changes source count"):
            crossed_corruption_grid(("history", "future"), ("realistic_mix",))

    def test_progression_is_explicit_and_monotonic(self):
        cells = progressive_corruption_schedule(
            ("history", "adapt", "future"), "gaussian_noise", (0, 1, 2)
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            [(cell["scenario"], cell["severity"]) for cell in cells],
            [("clean", 0), ("gaussian_noise", 1), ("gaussian_noise", 2)],
        )
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "non-decreasing"):
            progressive_corruption_schedule(
                ("history", "adapt", "future"), "gaussian_noise", (0, 2, 1)
            )

    def test_matched_sample_has_identical_class_country_counts(self):
        rows = []
        for period in ("history", "adapt", "future"):
            for category, country, count in (
                ("crop", "USA", 5),
                ("crop", "CAN", 3),
                ("wind", "USA", 2),
            ):
                for item in range(count):
                    rows.append(
                        {
                            "period": period,
                            "category": category,
                            "country": country,
                            "sequence": f"{period}-{category}-{country}-{item}",
                        }
                    )
        selected = matched_temporal_sample(
            rows,
            period_key="period",
            strata_keys=("category", "country"),
            target_per_period=6,
            seed=9,
            group_key="sequence",
        )
        counts = Counter(
            (row["_matched_period"], row["category"], row["country"])
            for row in selected
        )
        for stratum in (("crop", "USA"), ("crop", "CAN"), ("wind", "USA")):
            observed = [counts[(period, *stratum)] for period in ("history", "adapt", "future")]
            self.assertEqual(len(set(observed)), 1)
        self.assertEqual(
            Counter(row["_matched_period"] for row in selected),
            Counter({"history": 6, "adapt": 6, "future": 6}),
        )

    def test_matched_sampler_rejects_insufficient_common_support(self):
        rows = [
            {"period": "history", "class": "a", "country": "US"},
            {"period": "future", "class": "b", "country": "US"},
        ]
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "matched capacity 0"):
            matched_temporal_sample(
                rows,
                period_key="period",
                strata_keys=("class", "country"),
                target_per_period=1,
            )

    def test_matched_sampler_rejects_sequence_leakage_across_periods(self):
        rows = [
            {"period": "history", "class": "a", "country": "US", "sequence": "same"},
            {"period": "adapt", "class": "a", "country": "US", "sequence": "same"},
            {"period": "future", "class": "a", "country": "US", "sequence": "future"},
        ]
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "appears in periods"):
            matched_temporal_sample(
                rows,
                period_key="period",
                strata_keys=("class", "country"),
                target_per_period=1,
                group_key="sequence",
            )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()



================================================
FILE: tests/test_temporal_partition.py
================================================
import unittest
from typing import ClassVar

from fyp_drift.data.clear100 import (
    ClearSubset,
    balanced_indices,
    stratified_year_partition_loaders,
    year_loader,
)
from fyp_drift.evaluation.drift_verification import stratified_indices


class _Dataset:
    metadata_fields: ClassVar = ["year"]

    def __init__(self, rows):
        self.rows = rows

    def get_subset(self, split, transform=None):
        return ClearSubset(".", self.rows, transform)


class TemporalPartitionTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_limited_sample_represents_classes_and_years(self):
        rows = [
            {
                "path": f"unused-{year}-{label}-{item}.jpg",
                "label": str(label),
                "year": str(year),
            }
            for year in (2008, 2009)
            for label in range(4)
            for item in range(5)
        ]
        selected = balanced_indices(rows, range(len(rows)), 16, seed=11)
        self.assertEqual({int(rows[index]["year"]) for index in selected}, {2008, 2009})
        self.assertEqual({int(rows[index]["label"]) for index in selected}, set(range(4)))

    def test_selection_and_calibration_are_disjoint_stratified_and_deterministic(self):
        rows = [
            {"path": f"unused-{label}-{item}.jpg", "label": str(label), "year": "2011"}
            for label in range(100)
            for item in range(7)
        ]
        dataset = _Dataset(rows)
        first = stratified_year_partition_loaders(
            dataset, "train_source", 2011, seed=17
        )
        second = stratified_year_partition_loaders(
            dataset, "train_source", 2011, seed=17
        )
        selection = first[0].dataset.indices
        calibration = first[1].dataset.indices

        self.assertEqual(selection, second[0].dataset.indices)
        self.assertEqual(calibration, second[1].dataset.indices)
        self.assertFalse(set(selection) & set(calibration))
        self.assertEqual(set(selection) | set(calibration), set(range(len(rows))))
        for label in range(100):
            selected = sum(int(rows[index]["label"]) == label for index in selection)
            calibrated = sum(
                int(rows[index]["label"]) == label for index in calibration
            )
            self.assertLessEqual(abs(selected - calibrated), 1)

    def test_missing_requested_year_is_rejected(self):
        rows = [
            {"path": f"unused-{label}.jpg", "label": str(label), "year": "2011"}
            for label in range(100)
        ]
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "2012"):
            year_loader(_Dataset(rows), "train_source", (2011, 2012))

    def test_drift_sample_cannot_silently_drop_classes(self):
        rows = [
            {"path": f"unused-{label}.jpg", "label": str(label), "year": "2014"}
            for label in range(100)
        ]
        subset = ClearSubset(".", rows)
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "cannot represent all"):
            stratified_indices(subset, 2014, limit=99, seed=42)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()



================================================
FILE: tests/test_wilds_replication.py
================================================
import unittest

from fyp_drift.evaluation.wilds_replication import (
    stratified_group_sample,
    stratified_sample,
)


class WildsReplicationTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_stratified_sample_represents_each_class(self):
        labels = [0] * 20 + [1] * 4 + [2] * 2
        indices = stratified_sample(labels, limit=9, seed=7)
        selected_labels = {labels[index] for index in indices}
        self.assertEqual(selected_labels, {0, 1, 2})
        self.assertEqual(len(indices), 9)
        self.assertEqual(len(indices), len(set(indices)))

    def test_limit_smaller_than_class_count_is_rejected(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "cannot represent all"):
            stratified_sample([0, 1, 2], limit=2, seed=7)

    def test_group_sample_represents_each_year_and_class(self):
        labels = [0, 1] * 8
        years = [2000] * 4 + [2001] * 4 + [2002] * 4 + [2003] * 4
        indices = stratified_group_sample(labels, years, limit=8, seed=7)
        represented = {(years[index], labels[index]) for index in indices}
        self.assertEqual(len(represented), 8)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

FILE: tests/test_yearbook_saliency_mask_pilot.py

import unittest

import torch

from fyp_drift.training.yearbook_saliency_mask_pilot import mask_images


class YearbookMaskTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_mask_keeps_only_selected_equal_area_patches(self):
        image = torch.arange(16, dtype=torch.float32).reshape(1, 1, 4, 4)
        weights = torch.tensor([[0.5, 0.0, 0.0, 0.5]])
        masked = mask_images(image, weights)
        expected = image.clone()
        expected[:, :, :2, 2:] = 0
        expected[:, :, 2:, :2] = 0
        self.assertTrue(torch.equal(masked, expected))

    def test_rejects_non_square_patch_grid(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "square grid"):
            mask_images(torch.ones(1, 1, 4, 4), torch.ones(1, 3))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
